  ], '#fef3c7');
}

function userNoteCleanV790_(value) {
  return String(value === null || value === undefined ? '' : value).replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
}

function userNotePhoneV790_(value) {
  return String(value || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
}

function userNoteIsAdminV790_(role) {
  var raw = String(role || '').toLowerCase();
  return raw.indexOf('admin') !== -1 || raw.indexOf('super') !== -1 || raw.indexOf('master') !== -1;
}

function userNoteBoolV790_(value) {
  var raw = String(value === null || value === undefined ? '' : value).toLowerCase();
  return value === true || raw === 'true' || raw === 'yes' || raw === '1' || raw === 'ใช่';
}

function userNoteTagsV790_(value) {
  return userNoteCleanV790_(value)
    .split(',')
    .map(function(tag){ return tag.trim(); })
    .filter(function(tag){ return !!tag; });
}

function userNoteMakeIdV790_() {
  return 'NOTE-' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyyMMdd') + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 6).toUpperCase();
}

function userNoteFormatDateTimeV790_(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  try {
    if (Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === '[object Date]' && !isNaN(value.getTime())) {
      return Utilities.formatDate(value, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
    }
    var parsed = new Date(value);
    if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime()) && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(String(value))) {
      return Utilities.formatDate(parsed, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
    }
  } catch(ignore) {}
  return userNoteCleanV790_(value);
}

function userNoteRowToObjectV790_(row) {
  var item = {
    noteId: row[0],
    title: row[1],
    content: row[2],
    scope: row[3] || 'personal',
    tags: row[4],
    pinned: userNoteBoolV790_(row[5]),
    archived: userNoteBoolV790_(row[6]),
    ownerPhone: userNotePhoneV790_(row[7]),
    ownerName: row[8],
    department: row[9],
    linkedType: row[10],
    linkedId: row[11],
    fileUrl: row[12],
    createdAt: userNoteFormatDateTimeV790_(row[13]),
    updatedAt: userNoteFormatDateTimeV790_(row[14])
  };
  item.tagsList = userNoteTagsV790_(item.tags);
  return item;
}

function userNoteActorProfileV790_(phone) {
  var profile = {};
  try { profile = getUserProfileByPhone_(phone) || {}; } catch(ignore) {}
  return {
    phone: userNotePhoneV790_(phone),
    name: userNoteCleanV790_(profile.fullName || profile.name || getUserNameByPhone_(phone) || ''),
    department: userNoteCleanV790_(profile.department || profile.departmentName || '')
  };
}

function userNoteCanViewV790_(item, actorPhone, role, department) {
  if (userNoteIsAdminV790_(role)) return true;
  var phone = userNotePhoneV790_(actorPhone);
  if (item.ownerPhone === phone) return true;
  var scope = String(item.scope || 'personal').toLowerCase();
  if (scope === 'department') {
    return userNoteCleanV790_(item.department).toLowerCase() === userNoteCleanV790_(department).toLowerCase();
  }
  return false;
}

function userNoteCanManageV790_(item, actorPhone, role) {
  return userNoteIsAdminV790_(role) || item.ownerPhone === userNotePhoneV790_(actorPhone);
}

function getUserNotesV790(phone, role, department, filters) {
  try {
    var sh = getUserNotesSheetV790_();
    var values = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var actorPhone = userNotePhoneV790_(phone);
    var actorDept = userNoteCleanV790_(department);
    if (!actorDept && actorPhone) actorDept = userNoteActorProfileV790_(actorPhone).department;
    filters = filters || {};
    var q = userNoteCleanV790_(filters.q || filters.query || '').toLowerCase();
    var scopeFilter = userNoteCleanV790_(filters.scope || '').toLowerCase();
    var tagFilter = userNoteCleanV790_(filters.tag || '').toLowerCase();
    var pinnedOnly = userNoteBoolV790_(filters.pinnedOnly || filters.pinned);
    var includeArchived = userNoteBoolV790_(filters.includeArchived);
    var out = [];
    for (var i = values.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      var item = userNoteRowToObjectV790_(values[i]);
      if (!includeArchived && item.archived) continue;
      if (!userNoteCanViewV790_(item, actorPhone, role, actorDept)) continue;
      var scope = String(item.scope || 'personal').toLowerCase();
      if (scopeFilter && scopeFilter !== 'all' && scope !== scopeFilter) continue;
      if (pinnedOnly && !item.pinned) continue;
      if (tagFilter && item.tagsList.join(' ').toLowerCase().indexOf(tagFilter) === -1) continue;
      if (q) {
        var hay = [item.title, item.content, item.tags, item.department, item.ownerName, item.linkedType, item.linkedId].join(' ').toLowerCase();
        if (hay.indexOf(q) === -1) continue;
      }
      item.canManage = userNoteCanManageV790_(item, actorPhone, role);
      out.push(item);
      if (out.length >= 300) break;
    }
    out.sort(function(a, b) {
      if (a.pinned !== b.pinned) return a.pinned ? -1 : 1;
      return new Date(b.updatedAt || b.createdAt || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.updatedAt || a.createdAt || 0).getTime();
    });
    return { success:true, data:out };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[] };
  }
}

function saveUserNoteV790(payload, actorPhone, role) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var sh = getUserNotesSheetV790_();
    var values = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var actor = userNoteActorProfileV790_(actorPhone || payload.ownerPhone || '');
    if (!actor.phone) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบข้อมูลผู้ใช้งาน กรุณาเข้าสู่ระบบใหม่' };
    var noteId = userNoteCleanV790_(payload.noteId || '');
    var rowIndex = -1;
    var oldItem = null;
    for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (String(values[i][0]) === String(noteId)) {
        rowIndex = i + 1;
        oldItem = userNoteRowToObjectV790_(values[i]);
        break;
      }
    }
    if (oldItem && !userNoteCanManageV790_(oldItem, actor.phone, role)) {
      return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์แก้ไข Note นี้' };
    }
    if (!noteId) noteId = userNoteMakeIdV790_();
    var now = new Date();
    var nowText = userNoteFormatDateTimeV790_(now);
    var content = userNoteCleanV790_(payload.content || (oldItem && oldItem.content) || '');
    var title = userNoteCleanV790_(payload.title || (oldItem && oldItem.title) || '');
    if (!title) title = content ? content.split('\n')[0].substring(0, 80) : 'Note ใหม่';
    var scope = String(payload.scope || (oldItem && oldItem.scope) || 'personal').toLowerCase() === 'department' ? 'department' : 'personal';
    var item = {
      noteId: noteId,
      title: title,
      content: content,
      scope: scope,
      tags: userNoteTagsV790_(payload.tags || (oldItem && oldItem.tags) || '').join(', '),
      pinned: userNoteBoolV790_(payload.pinned !== undefined ? payload.pinned : (oldItem && oldItem.pinned)),
      archived: userNoteBoolV790_(payload.archived !== undefined ? payload.archived : (oldItem && oldItem.archived)),
      ownerPhone: oldItem && oldItem.ownerPhone ? oldItem.ownerPhone : actor.phone,
      ownerName: oldItem && oldItem.ownerName ? oldItem.ownerName : actor.name,
      department: userNoteCleanV790_(payload.department || (oldItem && oldItem.department) || actor.department),
      linkedType: userNoteCleanV790_(payload.linkedType || (oldItem && oldItem.linkedType) || ''),
      linkedId: userNoteCleanV790_(payload.linkedId || (oldItem && oldItem.linkedId) || ''),
      fileUrl: userNoteCleanV790_(payload.fileUrl || (oldItem && oldItem.fileUrl) || ''),
      createdAt: oldItem && oldItem.createdAt ? oldItem.createdAt : nowText,
      updatedAt: nowText
    };
    var row = [
      item.noteId, item.title, item.content, item.scope, item.tags, item.pinned, item.archived,
      "'" + item.ownerPhone, item.ownerName, item.department, item.linkedType, item.linkedId, item.fileUrl,
      item.createdAt, item.updatedAt
    ];
    if (rowIndex > 0) sh.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    try {
      logAudit_(actor.phone, actor.name, rowIndex > 0 ? 'UPDATE_USER_NOTE' : 'CREATE_USER_NOTE', 'UserNote', noteId, 'บันทึก Note: ' + item.title, oldItem, item);
    } catch(ignore) {}
    item.canManage = true;
    item.tagsList = userNoteTagsV790_(item.tags);
    return { success:true, data:item, message:'บันทึก Note แล้ว' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success:false, message:e.toString() };
  }
}

function toggleUserNotePinV790(noteId, actorPhone, role, pinned) {
  try {
    var sh = getUserNotesSheetV790_();
    var values = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var actor = userNoteActorProfileV790_(actorPhone || '');
    for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (String(values[i][0]) !== String(noteId)) continue;
      var item = userNoteRowToObjectV790_(values[i]);
      if (!userNoteCanManageV790_(item, actor.phone, role)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ปักหมุด Note นี้' };
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 6).setValue(!!pinned);
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 15).setValue(new Date());
      return { success:true, message:pinned ? 'ปักหมุด Note แล้ว' : 'ยกเลิกปักหมุดแล้ว' };
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบ Note' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success:false, message:e.toString() };
  }
}

function archiveUserNoteV790(noteId, actorPhone, role, archived) {
  try {
    var sh = getUserNotesSheetV790_();
    var values = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var actor = userNoteActorProfileV790_(actorPhone || '');
    for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (String(values[i][0]) !== String(noteId)) continue;
      var item = userNoteRowToObjectV790_(values[i]);
      if (!userNoteCanManageV790_(item, actor.phone, role)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์จัดเก็บ Note นี้' };
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 7).setValue(!!archived);
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 15).setValue(new Date());
      return { success:true, message:archived ? 'เก็บ Note เข้าคลังแล้ว' : 'นำ Note กลับมาแล้ว' };
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบ Note' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success:false, message:e.toString() };
  }
}

function deleteUserNoteV790(noteId, actorPhone, role) {
  try {
    var sh = getUserNotesSheetV790_();
    var values = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var actor = userNoteActorProfileV790_(actorPhone || '');
    for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (String(values[i][0]) !== String(noteId)) continue;
      var item = userNoteRowToObjectV790_(values[i]);
      if (!userNoteCanManageV790_(item, actor.phone, role)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ลบ Note นี้' };
      sh.deleteRow(i + 1);
      try {
        logAudit_(actor.phone, actor.name, 'DELETE_USER_NOTE', 'UserNote', noteId, 'ลบ Note: ' + (item.title || noteId), item, null);
      } catch(ignore) {}
      return { success:true, message:'ลบ Note แล้ว' };
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบ Note' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success:false, message:e.toString() };
  }
}

function createScheduleNoteFromPayloadV793_(data, scheduleId) {
  data = data || {};
  var actorPhone = userNotePhoneV790_(data.ownerPhone || data.userPhone || '');
  if (!actorPhone) return null;
  var title = 'ตารางงาน: ' + userNoteCleanV790_(data.eventName || data.title || 'กำหนดการ');
  var lines = [];
  lines.push(title);
  if (data.startTime || data.endTime) lines.push('เวลา: ' + userNoteCleanV790_(data.startTime || '-') + (data.endTime ? ' ถึง ' + userNoteCleanV790_(data.endTime) : ''));
  if (data.location) lines.push('สถานที่: ' + userNoteCleanV790_(data.location));
  if (data.meetingLink) lines.push('ลิงก์ประชุม: ' + userNoteCleanV790_(data.meetingLink));
  if (data.workStatus) lines.push('สถานะ: ' + userNoteCleanV790_(data.workStatus));
  if (data.priority) lines.push('ความสำคัญ: ' + userNoteCleanV790_(data.priority));
  if (data.details) {
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('รายละเอียด');
    lines.push(userNoteCleanV790_(data.details));
  }
  var tags = Array.isArray(data.tags) ? data.tags.join(', ') : userNoteCleanV790_(data.tags || '');
  var payload = {
    title: title,
    content: lines.join('\n'),
    scope: String(data.scheduleScope || '').toLowerCase() === 'department' ? 'department' : 'personal',
    tags: tags,
    fileUrl: userNoteCleanV790_(data.fileUrl || ''),
    linkedType: 'schedule',
    linkedId: scheduleId || data.eventEditId || '',
    pinned: false
  };
  return saveUserNoteV790(payload, actorPhone, data.role || data.userRole || 'User');
}

function testListGeminiModels() {
  var apiKey = getGeminiApiKey_();
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า GEMINI_API_KEY ใน Script Properties");
  var url = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=" + encodeURIComponent(apiKey);
  var res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
    method: "get",
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  });
  Logger.log(res.getResponseCode());
  Logger.log(res.getContentText());
}


// ==========================================
// Webhook & Automation
// ==========================================
function setupTelegramWebhook(webAppUrl) {
  if (!webAppUrl || webAppUrl === "") {
    return { success: false, message: "ไม่พบ Web App URL" };
  }
  var token = getTelegramBotToken_();
  if (!token) return { success: false, message: "ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า BOT_TOKEN ใน Script Properties" };
  UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://api.telegram.org/bot" + token + "/setWebhook?url=" + webAppUrl);
  return { success: true, message: "ผูก Webhook สำเร็จ!" };
}

function jsonOutput_(obj) {
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(obj)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}



// ==========================================
// Demo / Database Utilities (Super Admin only)
// ==========================================
const DEMO_USER_PHONES = ['0990001001', '0990001002', '0990001003', '0990001004', '0990001005'];

function isSuperAdmin_(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return false;
    var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) return false;
    var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 12)).getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rowPhone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (rowPhone === target && role === 'Super Admin' && status !== 'Inactive') return true;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

function getUserNameByPhone_(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) return String(phone || 'Super Admin');
    var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 12)).getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '') === target) return data[i][2] || target;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return String(phone || 'Super Admin');
}


function getUserPositionByPhone_(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) return '';
    var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 12)).getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '') === target) return data[i][3] || '';
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return '';
}

function clearSheetBody_(sheetName) {
  var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) return 0;
  var count = Math.max(sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 0);
  if (count > 0) sheet.deleteRows(2, count);
  return count;
}

function deleteDemoUsers_() {
  var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  if (!sheet) return 0;
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var deleted = 0;
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    var phone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var name = String(data[i][2] || '');
    if (DEMO_USER_PHONES.indexOf(phone) !== -1 || name.indexOf('ทดสอบ') === 0) {
      sheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      deleted++;
    }
  }
  return deleted;
}

function clearDemoDatabase(adminPhone, mode) {
  try {
    if (!isSuperAdmin_(adminPhone)) return { success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin เท่านั้น' };
    var actorName = getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone);
    var cleared = {};

    cleared.schedules = clearSheetBody_(SHEET_NAME);
    cleared.dailyReports = clearSheetBody_(SHEET_REPORTS);
    cleared.notifications = clearSheetBody_(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS);
    cleared.attachments = clearSheetBody_(SHEET_ATTACHMENTS);
    cleared.recurringRules = clearSheetBody_(SHEET_RECURRING);
    cleared.approvalHistory = clearSheetBody_('ApprovalHistory');
    cleared.demoUsers = deleteDemoUsers_();

    logAudit_(adminPhone, actorName, 'CLEAR_DATABASE', 'System', 'DEMO_RESET', 'เคลียร์ข้อมูลสำหรับ Demo/Test', null, cleared);

    return { success:true, message:'เคลียร์ข้อมูลทดสอบและข้อมูลปฏิบัติงานเรียบร้อยแล้ว', data: cleared };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success:false, message:e.toString() };
  }
}

        'Private',

        approvals[s],

        s % 2 === 0 ? 'ห้องประชุม สสจ.นครนายก' : 'ประชุมออนไลน์',

      ]);

    }

    var leaveTypes = ['ปฏิบัติงานปกติ', 'ปฏิบัติงานนอกสถานที่', 'ปฏิบัติงานปกติ', 'ลาพักร้อน', 'ปฏิบัติงานปกติ'];

    for (var r = 0; r < DEMO_USER_PHONES.length; r++) {
      reportSheet.appendRow([
        'REP-DEMO-' + (r + 1),
        Utilities.formatDate(now, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd'),
        '08:30 - 16:30',
        "'" + DEMO_USER_PHONES[r],
        names[r],
        departments[r],
        leaveTypes[r],
        'รายงานทดสอบสำหรับสาธิตระบบ Health Assistant OS\n- บันทึกรายงานประจำวัน\n- ตรวจสอบสถานะทีม\n- ใช้ทดสอบ AI สรุปรายงาน',
        'ข้อมูลตัวอย่างสำหรับ Demo',
        '',
        new Date()
      ]);
      createdReports++;
    }

    notifSheet.appendRow(['NTF-DEMO-1', "'" + DEMO_USER_PHONES[0], 'แจ้งเตือนทดสอบ: คุณมีงาน Demo ใหม่', false, new Date()]);
    notifSheet.appendRow(['NTF-DEMO-2', "'" + DEMO_USER_PHONES[1], 'แจ้งเตือนทดสอบ: มีงานรออนุมัติ', false, new Date()]);

    var result = { users:createdUsers, schedules:createdSchedules, dailyReports:createdReports, pin:'123456' };
    logAudit_(adminPhone, actorName, 'SEED_DEMO_DATA', 'System', 'DEMO_DATA', 'สร้างข้อมูลทดสอบ 5 users สำหรับนำเสนอ', null, result);
    return { success:true, message:'สร้างข้อมูลทดสอบเรียบร้อยแล้ว: 5 Users, 5 ตารางงาน, 5 รายงานประจำวัน (PIN: 123456)', data:result };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success:false, message:e.toString() };
  }
}

function resetAndSeedDemoData(adminPhone) {
  var cleared = clearDemoDatabase(adminPhone, 'demo');
  if (!cleared.success) return cleared;
  var seeded = seedDemoData(adminPhone);
  if (!seeded.success) return seeded;
  return { success:true, message:'รีเซ็ตฐานข้อมูล Demo และสร้างข้อมูลทดสอบใหม่เรียบร้อยแล้ว', cleared:cleared.data, seeded:seeded.data };
}

function getVercelApiSecret_() {
  return getScriptProp_('VERCEL_API_SECRET', FALLBACK_VERCEL_API_SECRET);
}



function getDashboardAnalytics(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var ss = getDB();
    var schedSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME);
    var reportSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_REPORTS);
    if (!schedSheet || !reportSheet) return { success:true, data:{} };
    ensureHeaders_(schedSheet, ['ID','Event Name','Start Time','End Time','Meeting Link','Visibility','User Email','Telegram Chat ID','Notify Lead Time','Status','Owner Phone','Resource ID','Approval Status','Approver Phone','File URL','Details','Location','Public Enabled','Public Expires At','Public Created At','Schedule Scope','Owner Department','Priority','Tags'], '#e8f0fe');
    var sdata = schedSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var rdata = reportSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var targetPhone = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var statusCounts={}, priorityCounts={}, tagCounts={}, monthlyCounts={}, approvalCounts={};
    var totalSchedules=0, totalReports=0;
    var ownerDeptCol = getScheduleColumn_(schedSheet, 'Owner Department');
    var priorityCol = getScheduleColumn_(schedSheet, 'Priority');
    var tagsCol = getScheduleColumn_(schedSheet, 'Tags');
    for (var i=1;i<sdata.length;i++) {
      var owner = String(sdata[i][10] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      var rowDept = ownerDeptCol ? String(sdata[i][ownerDeptCol-1] || '') : '';
      var visible = owner === targetPhone || ((role === 'Head' || role === 'Super Admin') && (!department || rowDept === department));
      if (!visible && role !== 'Super Admin') continue;
      totalSchedules++;
      var st = sdata[i][9] || 'ไม่ระบุ'; statusCounts[st]=(statusCounts[st]||0)+1;
      var ap = sdata[i][12] || 'ไม่ระบุ'; approvalCounts[ap]=(approvalCounts[ap]||0)+1;
      var pr = priorityCol ? (sdata[i][priorityCol-1] || 'ปกติ') : 'ปกติ'; priorityCounts[pr]=(priorityCounts[pr]||0)+1;
      var tags = tagsCol ? String(sdata[i][tagsCol-1] || '') : '';
      tags.split(',').map(function(x){return x.trim();}).filter(Boolean).forEach(function(t){ tagCounts[t]=(tagCounts[t]||0)+1; });
      try { var m=Utilities.formatDate(new Date(sdata[i][2]), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM'); monthlyCounts[m]=(monthlyCounts[m]||0)+1; } catch(e) {}
    }
    for (var j=1;j<rdata.length;j++) {
      var rp = String(rdata[j][3] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      var rd = String(rdata[j][5] || '');
      if (rp === targetPhone || ((role === 'Head' || role === 'Super Admin') && (!department || rd === department)) || role === 'Super Admin') totalReports++;
    }
    return { success:true, data:{ totalSchedules:totalSchedules, totalReports:totalReports, statusCounts:statusCounts, priorityCounts:priorityCounts, tagCounts:tagCounts, monthlyCounts:monthlyCounts, approvalCounts:approvalCounts } };
  } catch(e) { logError_(phone, 'getDashboardAnalytics', e, {role:role, department:department}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}


// ==========================================
// Collaborative Workspaces v2: Scope / Expiry / Preview / Checklist / Online Form
// ==========================================
function workspaceSetup_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_WORKSPACES, [
    'Workspace ID', 'Title', 'Description', 'Owner Phone', 'Owner Name', 'Owner Department',
    'Columns JSON', 'Rows JSON', 'Public Token', 'Access Mode', 'Require Login To Edit',
    'Active', 'Created At', 'Updated At', 'Scope', 'Expires At', 'Workspace Type',
    'Google Sheet URL', 'Google Form URL', 'Response Sheet URL', 'External ID'
  ], '#ccfbf1');
}

function getWorkspaceCol_(sheet, name) {
  var idx = getColumnIndex_(sheet, name);
  return idx > 0 ? idx : -1;
}

function workspaceValue_(row, idx0, fallback) {
  return row[idx0] == null || row[idx0] === '' ? fallback : row[idx0];
}

function getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone) {
  var usersSheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  var actor = { phone: String(actorPhone || '').replace(/'/g,''), name: '', department: '', role: '' };
  if (!actor.phone || !usersSheet) return actor;
  var users = usersSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var u=1; u<users.length; u++) {
    if (String(users[u][0]).replace(/'/g,'') === actor.phone) {
      actor.name = users[u][2] || '';
      actor.department = users[u][4] || '';
      actor.role = users[u][7] || '';
      break;
    }
  }
  return actor;
}

function getDefaultWorkspaceColumns_(type) {
  type = String(type || 'table');
  if (type === 'checklist') {
    return [
      'เสร็จแล้ว [checkbox]', 'ลำดับ', 'รายการตรวจสอบ', 'ผู้รับผิดชอบ', 'กำหนดเสร็จ',
      'สถานะ [dropdown: ยังไม่เริ่ม, กำลังดำเนินการ, เสร็จสิ้น, ติดปัญหา]',
      'หลักฐานรูปภาพ [รูปภาพ]', 'หมายเหตุ'
    ];
  }
  if (type === 'form') {
    return [
      'Timestamp', 'ชื่อผู้กรอก', 'หน่วยงาน [dropdown: สสจ., รพ., สสอ., รพ.สต., อื่นๆ]',
      'ประเภทคำขอ [ตัวเลือก: ขอข้อมูล, แจ้งปัญหา, เสนอแนะ, อื่นๆ]',
      'หัวข้อ/เรื่อง', 'รายละเอียด', 'ระดับความเร่งด่วน [dropdown: ปกติ, ด่วน, ด่วนมาก]',
      'แนบรูปภาพ [รูปภาพ]', 'หมายเหตุ'
    ];
  }
  return [
    'ลำดับ',
    'ระดับตัวชี้วัด/สั่งการ [dropdown: สั่งการ, ตัวชี้วัด, ติดตาม, ทั่วไป]',
    'ประเภทงาน [dropdown: Dashboard, HosXP XE, GIS Map, Smart Hospital, Cyber Security, Telemedicine, MOPH, HDC, อื่นๆ]',
    'หัวข้อ/เรื่อง', 'กิจกรรม/รายละเอียด', 'กลุ่มเป้าหมาย/หน่วยงานที่เกี่ยวข้อง',
    'ระยะเวลา/ช่วงเวลา', 'ผู้รับผิดชอบ',
    'สถานะ [ตัวเลือก: ยังไม่เริ่ม, กำลังดำเนินการ, เสร็จสิ้น, ติดปัญหา]',
    'ไฟล์/รูปภาพ [รูปภาพ]', 'หมายเหตุ'
  ];
}

function normalizeWorkspaceColumns_(columns, type) {
  if (!columns || !columns.length) columns = getDefaultWorkspaceColumns_(type);
  return columns.map(function(c){ return String(c || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 30);
}

function normalizeWorkspaceRows_(rows, columns) {
  rows = rows || [];
  var maxRows = 500;
  var maxCols = Math.max(columns.length, 1);
  return rows.slice(0, maxRows).map(function(row){
    row = row || [];
    var clean = [];
    for (var i=0; i<maxCols; i++) clean.push(String(row[i] == null ? '' : row[i]).slice(0, 3000));
    return clean;
  });
}

function normalizeWorkspaceScope_(scope) {
  return String(scope || 'personal') === 'department' ? 'department' : 'personal';
}

function normalizeWorkspaceType_(type) {
  type = String(type || 'table');
  return ['table','checklist','form','quiz','google_sheet','google_form'].indexOf(type) >= 0 ? type : 'table';
}

function normalizeWorkspaceExpiresAt_(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  var d = new Date(value);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
  d.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
  return d;
}

function isWorkspaceExpired_(expiresAt) {
  if (!expiresAt) return false;
  var d = expiresAt instanceof Date ? expiresAt : new Date(expiresAt);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return false;
  return new Date().getTime() > d.getTime();
}

function formatWorkspaceDate_(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  var d = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
  return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy');
}

function getWorkspaceRowsData_() {
  var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  return { sheet: sheet, data: data };
}

function findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId) {
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i=1; i<data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0]) === String(workspaceId)) return { row: i+1, values: data[i] };
  }
  return null;
}

function workspaceToObject_(row) {
  var cols = [];
  var rows = [];
  try { cols = JSON.parse(row[6] || '[]'); } catch(e) { cols = []; }
  try { rows = JSON.parse(row[7] || '[]'); } catch(e) { rows = []; }
  var scope = row[14] || 'personal';
  var expiresAt = row[15] || '';
  var workspaceType = row[16] || 'table';
  return {
    id: row[0], title: row[1], description: row[2], ownerPhone: String(row[3] || '').replace(/'/g,''),
    ownerName: row[4], department: row[5], columns: cols, rows: rows, token: row[8], accessMode: row[9] || 'view',
    requireLoginToEdit: row[10] === true || String(row[10]).toUpperCase()==='TRUE',
    active: row[11] === true || String(row[11]).toUpperCase()==='TRUE',
    createdAt: row[12], updatedAt: row[13], scope: scope, expiresAt: expiresAt, workspaceType: workspaceType,
    googleSheetUrl: row[17] || '', googleFormUrl: row[18] || '', responseSheetUrl: row[19] || '', externalId: row[20] || ''
  };
}

function createSharedWorkspace(data, actorPhone) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    if (!actor.phone) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้สร้างพื้นที่ทำงาน' };

    var type = normalizeWorkspaceType_(data.workspaceType);
    var scope = normalizeWorkspaceScope_(data.scope);
    var columns = normalizeWorkspaceColumns_(data.columns, type);
    var rows;
    if (type === 'form') {
      rows = [];
    } else if (data.rows && data.rows.length) {
      rows = normalizeWorkspaceRows_(data.rows, columns);
    } else if (type === 'checklist') {
      rows = normalizeWorkspaceRows_([[1,'เตรียมข้อมูล/เอกสาร', actor.name || '', '', 'ยังไม่เริ่ม', '']], columns);
    } else {
      rows = normalizeWorkspaceRows_([[1,'',data.title || '', data.description || '', '', '', actor.name || '', '']], columns);
    }

    var id = 'WS-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8);
    var token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g,'').substring(0, 24);
    var mode = data.accessMode === 'edit' ? 'edit' : 'view';
    var requireLogin = data.requireLoginToEdit === true || data.requireLoginToEdit === 'true';
    var expiresAt = normalizeWorkspaceExpiresAt_(data.expiresAt);
    var now = new Date();

    sheet.appendRow([
      id, data.title || 'พื้นที่ทำงานร่วมกัน', data.description || '', "'" + actor.phone, actor.name, actor.department,
      JSON.stringify(columns), JSON.stringify(rows), token, mode, requireLogin, true, now, now,
      scope, expiresAt || '', type, '', '', '', ''
    ]);
    logAudit_(actor.phone, actor.name, 'CREATE_WORKSPACE', 'Workspace', id, 'สร้างพื้นที่ทำงานร่วมกัน: ' + (data.title || ''), null, { accessMode: mode, requireLoginToEdit: requireLogin, scope: scope, expiresAt: expiresAt, workspaceType: type });
    return { success:true, message:'สร้างพื้นที่ทำงานร่วมกันแล้ว', id:id, token:token, url:getWorkspacePublicUrl_(id, token) };
  } catch(e) { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'createSharedWorkspace', e, data || {}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getMySharedWorkspaces(actorPhone) {
  try {
    var pack = getWorkspaceRowsData_();
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    var list = [];
    for (var i=1; i<pack.data.length; i++) {
      var ws = workspaceToObject_(pack.data[i]);
      if (!ws.active) continue;
      var canSee = ws.ownerPhone === actor.phone || (ws.scope === 'department' && ws.department === actor.department);
      if (!canSee && actor.role !== 'Super Admin') continue;
      list.push({
        id:ws.id, title:ws.title, description:ws.description, ownerName:ws.ownerName, ownerPhone:ws.ownerPhone,
        department:ws.department, accessMode:ws.accessMode, requireLoginToEdit:ws.requireLoginToEdit,
        updatedAt:ws.updatedAt ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(ws.updatedAt), 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '',
        scope:ws.scope || 'personal', workspaceType:ws.workspaceType || 'table', expiresAt:formatWorkspaceDate_(ws.expiresAt),
        expired:isWorkspaceExpired_(ws.expiresAt), canManage: ws.ownerPhone === actor.phone || actor.role === 'Super Admin',
        googleSheetUrl: ws.googleSheetUrl || '', googleFormUrl: ws.googleFormUrl || '', responseSheetUrl: ws.responseSheetUrl || '', externalId: ws.externalId || '',
        url:(ws.workspaceType === 'google_sheet' ? (ws.googleSheetUrl || getWorkspacePublicUrl_(ws.id, ws.token)) : (ws.workspaceType === 'google_form' ? (ws.googleFormUrl || getWorkspacePublicUrl_(ws.id, ws.token)) : getWorkspacePublicUrl_(ws.id, ws.token)))
      });
    }
    return { success:true, data:list.reverse() };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getWorkspacePublicUrl_(id, token) {
  return getPublicAppBaseUrl_() + '/api/share?workspaceId=' + encodeURIComponent(id) + (token ? '&token=' + encodeURIComponent(token) : '');
}

function getSharedWorkspacePublicUrl(workspaceId, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบพื้นที่ทำงาน' };
    var ws = workspaceToObject_(found.values);
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    if (ws.ownerPhone !== actor.phone && actor.role !== 'Super Admin') return { success:false, message:'คุณไม่มีสิทธิ์สร้างลิงก์ของพื้นที่นี้' };
    return { success:true, url:getWorkspacePublicUrl_(ws.id, ws.token) };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function deleteSharedWorkspace(workspaceId, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบพื้นที่ทำงาน' };
    var ws = workspaceToObject_(found.values);
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    if (ws.ownerPhone !== actor.phone && actor.role !== 'Super Admin') return { success:false, message:'ลบได้เฉพาะผู้สร้างพื้นที่ทำงานหรือ Super Admin เท่านั้น' };
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 12).setValue(false);
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 14).setValue(new Date());
    logAudit_(actorPhone, actor.name || '', 'DELETE_WORKSPACE', 'Workspace', workspaceId, 'ปิดพื้นที่ทำงานร่วมกัน', found.values, null);
    return { success:true, message:'ปิดพื้นที่ทำงานแล้ว' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getPublicWorkspace(workspaceId, token) {
  try {
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบพื้นที่ทำงาน' };
    var ws = workspaceToObject_(found.values);
    if (!ws.active) return { success:false, message:'พื้นที่ทำงานนี้ถูกปิดแล้ว' };
    if (String(ws.token) !== String(token || '')) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์ไม่ถูกต้องหรือหมดสิทธิ์เข้าถึง' };
    if (isWorkspaceExpired_(ws.expiresAt)) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์พื้นที่ทำงานนี้หมดอายุแล้ว' };
    return { success:true, data:{
      id:ws.id, title:ws.title, description:ws.description, ownerName:ws.ownerName, department:ws.department,
      columns:ws.columns, rows:ws.rows, accessMode:ws.accessMode, requireLoginToEdit:ws.requireLoginToEdit,
      updatedAt:ws.updatedAt ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(ws.updatedAt), 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '',
      scope:ws.scope || 'personal', expiresAt:formatWorkspaceDate_(ws.expiresAt), workspaceType:ws.workspaceType || 'table',
      googleSheetUrl:ws.googleSheetUrl || '', googleFormUrl:ws.googleFormUrl || '', responseSheetUrl:ws.responseSheetUrl || '', externalId:ws.externalId || '',
      responseMeta:ws.responseMeta || []
    } };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function updatePublicWorkspaceRows(workspaceId, token, rows) {
  try {
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบพื้นที่ทำงาน' };
    var ws = workspaceToObject_(found.values);
    if (String(ws.token) !== String(token || '')) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์ไม่ถูกต้อง' };
    if (isWorkspaceExpired_(ws.expiresAt)) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์พื้นที่ทำงานนี้หมดอายุแล้ว' };
    if (ws.accessMode !== 'edit') return { success:false, message:'พื้นที่นี้เปิดให้ดูอย่างเดียว' };
    if (ws.requireLoginToEdit) return { success:false, message:'พื้นที่นี้กำหนดให้ต้องล็อกอินก่อนแก้ไข' };
    if (ws.workspaceType === 'form') return { success:false, message:'พื้นที่แบบฟอร์มออนไลน์ต้องส่งข้อมูลผ่านปุ่มส่งแบบฟอร์ม' };
    var cleanRows = normalizeWorkspaceRows_(rows, ws.columns);
    var fillMeta = {submittedAt:haosV742NowText_(), submittedBy:'public-edit'};
    var responseMeta = haosV742NormalizeResponseMeta_(ws.responseMeta || [], cleanRows, fillMeta);
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 8).setValue(JSON.stringify(cleanRows));
    sheet.getRange(found.row, haosV742WorkspaceMetaColumn_(sheet)).setValue(JSON.stringify(responseMeta));
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 14).setValue(new Date());
    return { success:true, message:'บันทึกการแก้ไขแล้ว' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function submitPublicWorkspaceForm(workspaceId, token, row) {
  try {
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบแบบฟอร์ม' };
    var ws = workspaceToObject_(found.values);
    if (String(ws.token) !== String(token || '')) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์ไม่ถูกต้อง' };
    if (!ws.active) return { success:false, message:'แบบฟอร์มนี้ถูกปิดแล้ว' };
    if (isWorkspaceExpired_(ws.expiresAt)) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์แบบฟอร์มนี้หมดอายุแล้ว' };
    if (ws.workspaceType !== 'form') return { success:false, message:'พื้นที่นี้ไม่ใช่แบบฟอร์มออนไลน์' };
    var vals = row || [];
    var clean = [];
    for (var i=0; i<ws.columns.length; i++) {
      var col = String(ws.columns[i] || '');
      if (i === 0 && col.toLowerCase().indexOf('timestamp') !== -1) clean.push(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'));
      else clean.push(String(vals[i] == null ? '' : vals[i]).slice(0, 3000));
    }
    var rows = ws.rows || [];
    rows.push(clean);
    rows = normalizeWorkspaceRows_(rows, ws.columns);
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 8).setValue(JSON.stringify(rows));
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 14).setValue(new Date());
    return { success:true, message:'ส่งข้อมูลแบบฟอร์มเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function updateSharedWorkspaceRows(workspaceId, rows, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบพื้นที่ทำงาน' };
    var ws = workspaceToObject_(found.values);
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    if (ws.ownerPhone !== actor.phone && actor.role !== 'Super Admin') return { success:false, message:'แก้ไขในระบบได้เฉพาะผู้สร้างพื้นที่ทำงานหรือ Super Admin เท่านั้น' };
    var cleanRows = normalizeWorkspaceRows_(rows, ws.columns);
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 8).setValue(JSON.stringify(cleanRows));
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 14).setValue(new Date());
    return { success:true, message:'บันทึกข้อมูลพื้นที่ทำงานแล้ว' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}


// ==========================================
// Google Workspace Integration: real Google Sheet / Form workspaces
// ==========================================
function parseWorkspaceFieldForGoogle_(label) {
  var raw = String(label || '').trim();
  var dropdown = raw.match(/\[(?:dropdown|ดรอปดาวน์):\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
  var radio = raw.match(/\[(?:radio|ตัวเลือก):\s*([^\]]+)\]/i);
  var checkbox = /\[(?:checkbox|check|ติ๊กถูก)\]/i.test(raw);
  var image = /\[(?:image|รูปภาพ|แนบรูปภาพ)\]/i.test(raw) || /รูปภาพ|ภาพถ่าย|หลักฐานภาพ/i.test(raw);
  var clean = raw.replace(/\[(?:dropdown|ดรอปดาวน์|radio|ตัวเลือก):[^\]]+\]/ig, '')
    .replace(/\[(?:image|รูปภาพ|แนบรูปภาพ|checkbox|check|ติ๊กถูก)\]/ig, '')
    .trim() || raw;
  function splitOpts(s){ return String(s || '').split(',').map(function(x){ return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); }
  if (dropdown) return { label: clean, type:'dropdown', options: splitOpts(dropdown[1]) };
  if (radio) return { label: clean, type:'radio', options: splitOpts(radio[1]) };
  if (checkbox) return { label: clean, type:'checkbox', options: [] };
  if (image) return { label: clean, type:'image', options: [] };
  if (/รายละเอียด|คำอธิบาย|หมายเหตุ|ข้อเสนอแนะ/i.test(clean)) return { label: clean, type:'paragraph', options: [] };
  return { label: clean, type:'text', options: [] };
}

function applyWorkspaceDriveSharing_(fileId, accessMode) {
  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
  if (accessMode === 'edit') file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.EDIT);
  else file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
}

function appendExternalWorkspaceRow_(data, actor, type, urls, columns, rows) {
  var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
  var id = 'WS-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8);
  var token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g,'').substring(0, 24);
  var mode = data.accessMode === 'edit' ? 'edit' : 'view';
  var requireLogin = data.requireLoginToEdit === true || data.requireLoginToEdit === 'true';
  var scope = normalizeWorkspaceScope_(data.scope);
  var expiresAt = normalizeWorkspaceExpiresAt_(data.expiresAt);
  var now = new Date();
  sheet.appendRow([
    id, data.title || 'พื้นที่ทำงานร่วมกัน', data.description || '', "'" + actor.phone, actor.name, actor.department,
    JSON.stringify(columns || []), JSON.stringify(rows || []), token, mode, requireLogin, true, now, now,
    scope, expiresAt || '', type, urls.sheetUrl || '', urls.formUrl || '', urls.responseSheetUrl || '', urls.externalId || ''
  ]);
  logAudit_(actor.phone, actor.name, 'CREATE_' + String(type).toUpperCase(), 'Workspace', id, 'สร้างพื้นที่ทำงานแบบ Google Workspace: ' + (data.title || ''), null, { urls: urls, scope: scope });
  return { success:true, message:'สร้างพื้นที่ทำงาน Google Workspace แล้ว', id:id, token:token, url:(urls.formUrl || urls.sheetUrl || getWorkspacePublicUrl_(id, token)), googleSheetUrl:urls.sheetUrl || '', googleFormUrl:urls.formUrl || '', responseSheetUrl:urls.responseSheetUrl || '' };
}

function createGoogleSheetWorkspace(data, actorPhone) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    if (!actor.phone) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้สร้างพื้นที่ทำงาน' };
    var title = data.title || 'Google Sheet Workspace';
    var columns = normalizeWorkspaceColumns_(data.columns, 'table');
    var rows = data.rows && data.rows.length ? normalizeWorkspaceRows_(data.rows, columns) : [];
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.create(title);
    var sh = ss.getSheets()[0];
    sh.setName('Workspace');
    if (columns.length) {
      var displayHeaders = columns.map(function(c){ return parseWorkspaceFieldForGoogle_(c).label; });
      sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, displayHeaders.length).setValues([displayHeaders]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#e0f2fe');
      sh.setFrozenRows(1);
      // Basic data validation for dropdown/radio/checkbox columns.
      columns.forEach(function(c, idx){
        var field = parseWorkspaceFieldForGoogle_(c);
        var range = sh.getRange(2, idx+1, 500, 1);
        if ((field.type === 'dropdown' || field.type === 'radio') && field.options.length) {
          var rule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(field.options, true).setAllowInvalid(false).build();
          range.setDataValidation(rule);
        } else if (field.type === 'checkbox') {
          range.insertCheckboxes();
        }
      });
    }
    if (rows.length && columns.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, columns.length).setValues(rows);
    sh.autoResizeColumns(1, Math.max(columns.length, 1));
    var meta = ss.insertSheet('About');
    meta.getRange(1,1,6,2).setValues([
      ['ระบบ', 'Health Assistant OS'],
      ['ชื่อพื้นที่', title],
      ['คำอธิบาย', data.description || ''],
      ['เจ้าของ', actor.name || actor.phone],
      ['กลุ่มงาน', actor.department || ''],
      ['สร้างเมื่อ', new Date()]
    ]);
    applyWorkspaceDriveSharing_(ss.getId(), data.accessMode === 'edit' ? 'edit' : 'view');
    return appendExternalWorkspaceRow_(data, actor, 'google_sheet', { sheetUrl:ss.getUrl(), externalId:ss.getId() }, columns, rows);
  } catch(e) { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'createGoogleSheetWorkspace', e, data || {}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function createGoogleFormWorkspace(data, actorPhone) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    if (!actor.phone) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้สร้างแบบฟอร์ม' };
    var title = data.title || 'Google Form Workspace';
    var columns = normalizeWorkspaceColumns_(data.columns, 'form');
    var form = FormApp.create(title);
    form.setDescription((data.description || '') + '\n\nสร้างจาก Health Assistant OS');
    try { form.setCollectEmail(data.requireLoginToEdit === true || data.requireLoginToEdit === 'true'); } catch(ignore) {}
    columns.forEach(function(c, idx){
      var field = parseWorkspaceFieldForGoogle_(c);
      var label = field.label || ('คำถาม ' + (idx+1));
      if (/timestamp/i.test(label)) return;
      if (field.type === 'radio' && field.options.length) form.addMultipleChoiceItem().setTitle(label).setChoiceValues(field.options).setRequired(false);
      else if (field.type === 'dropdown' && field.options.length) form.addListItem().setTitle(label).setChoiceValues(field.options).setRequired(false);
      else if (field.type === 'checkbox' && field.options.length) form.addCheckboxItem().setTitle(label).setChoiceValues(field.options).setRequired(false);
      else if (field.type === 'checkbox') form.addCheckboxItem().setTitle(label).setChoiceValues(['ใช่']).setRequired(false);
      else if (field.type === 'image') {
        try { form.addFileUploadItem().setTitle(label).setHelpText('แนบไฟล์หรือรูปภาพประกอบ').setRequired(false); }
        catch(err) { form.addParagraphTextItem().setTitle(label + ' (แนบลิงก์ไฟล์)').setRequired(false); }
      } else if (field.type === 'paragraph') form.addParagraphTextItem().setTitle(label).setRequired(false);
      else form.addTextItem().setTitle(label).setRequired(false);
    });
    var resp = SpreadsheetApp.create(title + ' - Responses');
    form.setDestination(FormApp.DestinationType.SPREADSHEET, resp.getId());
    // Publish links. The form response URL is for users; edit URL is stored as formUrl too for owner convenience.
    var formUrl = form.getPublishedUrl();
    var editUrl = form.getEditUrl();
    try { DriveApp.getFileById(resp.getId()).setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW); } catch(ignore) {}
    return appendExternalWorkspaceRow_(data, actor, 'google_form', { formUrl:formUrl, sheetUrl:editUrl, responseSheetUrl:resp.getUrl(), externalId:form.getId() }, columns, []);
  } catch(e) { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'createGoogleFormWorkspace', e, data || {}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}



// ==========================================
// Live Opinion Survey / Word Cloud (Kahoot-like)
// ==========================================
function getLiveSurveySheet_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_LIVE_SURVEYS, [
    'Survey ID','Token','Title','Question','Owner Phone','Owner Name','Department','Scope','Access Mode','Status','Expires At','Created At','Updated At'
  ], '#fae8ff');
}

function getLiveSurveyResponseSheet_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_LIVE_RESPONSES, [
    'Response ID','Survey ID','Opinion','Normalized Opinion','Participant Name','Timestamp','Source'
  ], '#fce7f3');
}

function normalizeOpinion_(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .replace(/[“”]/g, '"')
    .replace(/[‘’]/g, "'")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

function liveSurveyRowToObject_(row) {
  return {
    id: row[0], token: row[1], title: row[2], question: row[3], ownerPhone: String(row[4] || '').replace(/'/g,''),
    ownerName: row[5] || '', department: row[6] || '', scope: row[7] || 'personal', accessMode: row[8] || 'submit',
    status: row[9] || 'Active', expiresAt: row[10] || '', createdAt: row[11] || '', updatedAt: row[12] || ''
  };
}

function findLiveSurveyRow_(sheet, surveyId) {
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0]) === String(surveyId)) return { row: i + 1, values: data[i] };
  }
  return null;
}

function isLiveSurveyExpired_(survey) {
  if (!survey || !survey.expiresAt) return false;
  var d = new Date(survey.expiresAt);
  return !isNaN(d.getTime()) && d.getTime() < Date.now();
}

function getLiveSurveyPublicUrl_(id, token) {
  return buildPublicPageUrl_({ surveyId: id, token: token || '' });
}

function createLiveOpinionSurvey(data, actorPhone) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    if (!actor.phone) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้สร้างแบบสำรวจ' };
    var title = String(data.title || '').trim() || 'สำรวจความคิดเห็นสด';
    var question = String(data.question || '').trim() || 'พิมพ์ความคิดเห็นของคุณ';
    var scope = data.scope === 'department' ? 'department' : 'personal';
    var expiresAt = data.expiresAt ? new Date(data.expiresAt) : '';
    var id = 'SUR-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
    var token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 20);
    var sheet = getLiveSurveySheet_();
    sheet.appendRow([id, token, title, question, "'" + actor.phone, actor.name || '', actor.department || '', scope, 'submit', 'Active', expiresAt || '', new Date(), new Date()]);
    logAudit_(actor.phone, actor.name || '', 'CREATE_LIVE_SURVEY', 'LiveSurvey', id, 'สร้างแบบสำรวจความคิดเห็นสด: ' + title, null, {question:question, scope:scope});
    return { success:true, message:'สร้างลิงก์สำรวจความคิดเห็นสดเรียบร้อยแล้ว', id:id, token:token, url:getLiveSurveyPublicUrl_(id, token) };
  } catch(e) { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'createLiveOpinionSurvey', e, data || {}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getMyLiveOpinionSurveys(actorPhone, department) {
  try {
    var sheet = getLiveSurveySheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var actor = getWorkspaceActor_(actorPhone);
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var s = liveSurveyRowToObject_(data[i]);
      var canSee = s.ownerPhone === String(actorPhone).replace(/'/g,'') || (s.scope === 'department' && String(s.department) === String(department || actor.department || ''));
      if (!canSee) continue;
      var counts = getLiveOpinionSurveyResults(s.id, s.token, 8);
      out.push({
        id:s.id, title:s.title, question:s.question, scope:s.scope, ownerName:s.ownerName, status:s.status,
        expiresAt: s.expiresAt instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(s.expiresAt,'GMT+7','yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') : (s.expiresAt || ''),
        isExpired:isLiveSurveyExpired_(s), publicUrl:getLiveSurveyPublicUrl_(s.id, s.token), token:s.token,
        responseCount: counts.success ? counts.total : 0, top: counts.success ? counts.data.slice(0,3) : []
      });
    }
    out.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.id).localeCompare(String(a.id)); });
    return { success:true, data:out };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[] }; }
}

function getPublicLiveOpinionSurvey(surveyId, token) {
  try {
    var sheet = getLiveSurveySheet_();
    var found = findLiveSurveyRow_(sheet, surveyId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบแบบสำรวจนี้' };
    var s = liveSurveyRowToObject_(found.values);
    if (String(s.token) !== String(token || '')) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์ไม่ถูกต้องหรือหมดสิทธิ์เข้าถึง' };
    if (s.status !== 'Active') return { success:false, message:'แบบสำรวจนี้ถูกปิดแล้ว' };
    if (isLiveSurveyExpired_(s)) return { success:false, message:'ลิงก์แบบสำรวจนี้หมดอายุแล้ว' };
    return { success:true, data:{ id:s.id, token:s.token, title:s.title, question:s.question, status:s.status } };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function submitLiveOpinionResponse(surveyId, token, opinion, participantName) {
  try {
    var pub = getPublicLiveOpinionSurvey(surveyId, token);
    if (!pub.success) return pub;
    opinion = String(opinion || '').trim();
    if (!opinion) return { success:false, message:'กรุณาพิมพ์ความคิดเห็นก่อนส่ง' };
    if (opinion.length > 120) opinion = opinion.substring(0, 120);
    var sheet = getLiveSurveyResponseSheet_();
    sheet.appendRow(['LVR-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0,8), surveyId, opinion, normalizeOpinion_(opinion), String(participantName || '').trim(), new Date(), 'public']);
    return { success:true, message:'ส่งความคิดเห็นเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
  } catch(e) { logError_('', 'submitLiveOpinionResponse', e, {surveyId:surveyId}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getLiveOpinionSurveyResults(surveyId, token, limit) {
  try {
    if (token) {
      var pub = getPublicLiveOpinionSurvey(surveyId, token);
      if (!pub.success) return pub;
    }
    var sheet = getLiveSurveyResponseSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var map = {};
    var label = {};
    var namesMap = {};
    var total = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][1]) !== String(surveyId)) continue;
      var norm = String(data[i][3] || normalizeOpinion_(data[i][2]));
      if (!norm) continue;
      total++;
      map[norm] = (map[norm] || 0) + 1;
      if (!label[norm]) label[norm] = String(data[i][2] || '').trim();

      var participantName = String(data[i][4] || '').trim();
      if (participantName) {
        if (!namesMap[norm]) namesMap[norm] = {};
        namesMap[norm][participantName] = (namesMap[norm][participantName] || 0) + 1;
      }
    }
    var arr = Object.keys(map).map(function(k){
      var names = Object.keys(namesMap[k] || {}).sort(function(a,b){
        return (namesMap[k][b] - namesMap[k][a]) || String(a).localeCompare(String(b), 'th');
      });
      var visibleNames = names.slice(0, 5);
      var hiddenNameCount = Math.max(0, names.length - visibleNames.length);
      return {
        text: label[k] || k,
        count: map[k],
        names: visibleNames,
        namesText: visibleNames.join(', ') + (hiddenNameCount > 0 ? ' +' + hiddenNameCount : ''),
        hiddenNameCount: hiddenNameCount
      };
    });
    arr.sort(function(a,b){ return b.count - a.count || String(a.text).localeCompare(String(b.text),'th'); });
    if (limit) arr = arr.slice(0, Number(limit));
    return { success:true, total:total, data:arr };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[] }; }
}

function closeLiveOpinionSurvey(surveyId, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getLiveSurveySheet_();
    var found = findLiveSurveyRow_(sheet, surveyId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบแบบสำรวจ' };
    var s = liveSurveyRowToObject_(found.values);
    if (s.ownerPhone !== String(actorPhone).replace(/'/g,'') && !isSuperAdmin_(actorPhone)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ปิดแบบสำรวจนี้' };
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 10).setValue('Closed');
    sheet.getRange(found.row, 13).setValue(new Date());
    logAudit_(actorPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone), 'CLOSE_LIVE_SURVEY', 'LiveSurvey', surveyId, 'ปิดแบบสำรวจความคิดเห็นสด', null, null);
    return { success:true, message:'ปิดแบบสำรวจเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function deleteLiveOpinionSurvey(surveyId, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getLiveSurveySheet_();
    var found = findLiveSurveyRow_(sheet, surveyId);
    if (!found) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบแบบสำรวจ' };
    var s = liveSurveyRowToObject_(found.values);
    if (s.ownerPhone !== String(actorPhone).replace(/'/g,'') && !isSuperAdmin_(actorPhone)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ลบแบบสำรวจนี้' };
    sheet.deleteRow(found.row);
    logAudit_(actorPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone), 'DELETE_LIVE_SURVEY', 'LiveSurvey', surveyId, 'ลบแบบสำรวจความคิดเห็นสด', found.values, null);
    return { success:true, message:'ลบแบบสำรวจเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}




function isITServiceStaff_(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return false;
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rowPhone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      if (rowPhone !== target) continue;
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (status === 'Inactive') return false;
      return role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin' || dept.indexOf('สุขภาพดิจิทัล') !== -1;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

function getITServiceStaffPhones_() {
  var list = [];
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return list;
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var phone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (phone && status !== 'Inactive' && (role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin' || dept.indexOf('สุขภาพดิจิทัล') !== -1)) {
        if (list.indexOf(phone) === -1) list.push(phone);
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return list;
}

function getITBookingSheet_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS, [
    'Booking ID', 'Topic', 'Booking Type', 'Join Option', 'Meeting ID', 'Passcode',
    'Resource ID', 'Room Name', 'Other Location', 'Start Time', 'End Time',
    'Owner Phone', 'Owner Name', 'Owner Position', 'Department', 'Status', 'Approver Phone',
    'Admin Room ID', 'Admin Meeting ID', 'Admin Passcode', 'Admin Link', 'Cancel Reason', 'Signature Data',
    'Created At', 'Updated At',
    'Staff Name', 'Staff Position', 'Supervisor Name', 'Supervisor Position',
    'Supervisor Signature Data', 'Admin Signature Data', 'Approval Note',
    'Join Link', 'Google Calendar Event ID', 'Google Calendar Sync', 'Last Calendar Sync'
  ], '#ccfbf1');
}

function itBookingRowToObject_(row) {
  var start = row[9];
  var end = row[10];
  var created = row[21];
  var updated = row[22];
  function fmtDateTime_(v) {
    if (!v) return '';
    var d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(v || '');
    return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');
  }
  return {
    id: row[0], topic: row[1], bookingType: row[2], joinOption: row[3], meetingId: row[4], passcode: row[5],
    resourceId: row[6], roomName: row[7], otherLocation: row[8], startTime: fmtDateTime_(start), endTime: fmtDateTime_(end),
    rawStartTime: start instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(start, 'GMT+7', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm") : String(start || ''),
    rawEndTime: end instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(end, 'GMT+7', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm") : String(end || ''),
    ownerPhone: String(row[11] || '').replace(/'/g, ''), ownerName: row[12], ownerPosition: getUserPositionByPhone_(row[11]), department: row[13], status: row[14] || 'รออนุมัติ', approverPhone: String(row[15] || '').replace(/'/g, ''),
    adminRoomId: row[16], adminPasscode: row[17], adminLink: row[18], cancelReason: row[19], signatureData: row[20],
    createdAt: fmtDateTime_(created), updatedAt: fmtDateTime_(updated),
    staffName: row[23] || '', staffPosition: row[24] || '', supervisorName: row[25] || '', supervisorPosition: row[26] || '',
    supervisorSignatureData: row[27] || '', adminSignatureData: row[28] || '', approvalNote: row[29] || '',
    joinLink: row[30] || row[18] || '', googleCalendarEventId: row[31] || '', googleCalendarSync: row[32] || '', lastCalendarSync: row[33] || ''
  };
}

function getITBookings(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var targetPhone = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var userRole = String(role || 'User');
    var userDept = String(department || '');
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var item = itBookingRowToObject_(data[i]);
      var visible = item.ownerPhone === targetPhone || isITServiceStaff_(targetPhone) || (userRole === 'Head' && item.department === userDept);
      if (visible) out.push(item);
    }
    out.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.createdAt || '').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt || '')); });
    return { success:true, data: out };
  } catch(e) { logError_(phone || '', 'getITBookings', e, null); return { success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[] }; }
}

function saveITBooking(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    if (!data.topic) return { success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุเรื่องการจอง' };
    if (!data.startTime || !data.endTime) return { success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุช่วงวันเวลาให้ครบ' };
    var start = new Date(data.startTime);
    var end = new Date(data.endTime);
    if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) return { success:false, message:'รูปแบบวันเวลาไม่ถูกต้อง' };
    if (end.getTime() <= start.getTime()) return { success:false, message:'เวลาสิ้นสุดต้องมากกว่าเวลาเริ่มต้น' };
    var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
    var id = 'ITB-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
    var status = 'รอเจ้าหน้าที่';
    var row = [
      id, data.topic || '', data.bookingType || 'Host', data.joinOption || '', data.meetingId || '', data.passcode || '',
      data.resourceId || '', data.roomName || '', data.otherLocation || '', start, end,
      "'" + String(data.ownerPhone || '').replace(/'/g, ''), data.ownerName || '', data.department || '', status, '',
      '', '', '', '', data.signatureData || '', new Date(), new Date(),
      '', '', '', '', '', '', ''
    ];
    sheet.appendRow(row);
    var itbRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    try {
      var joinLinkCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Join Link'); if (joinLinkCol) sheet.getRange(itbRow, joinLinkCol).setValue(data.joinLink || data.meetingLink || '');
      var calIdITB = syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_(itBookingRowToObject_(sheet.getRange(itbRow,1,1,sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0]));
      var itbCalCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Google Calendar Event ID'); if (itbCalCol) sheet.getRange(itbRow, itbCalCol).setValue(calIdITB || '');
      var itbCalSyncCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Google Calendar Sync'); if (itbCalSyncCol) sheet.getRange(itbRow, itbCalSyncCol).setValue(calIdITB && calIdITB !== 'Error' ? 'Synced' : 'Error');
      var itbCalLastCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Last Calendar Sync'); if (itbCalLastCol) sheet.getRange(itbRow, itbCalLastCol).setValue(new Date());
    } catch(itbCalErr) { logError_(data.ownerPhone || '', 'saveITBooking calendar sync', itbCalErr, {id:id}); }
    try {
      var notif = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS);
      if (notif) {
        getITServiceStaffPhones_().forEach(function(staffPhone) {
          notif.appendRow(['NTF-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8), "'" + staffPhone, 'มีคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom ใหม่รอเจ้าหน้าที่ดำเนินการ: ' + data.topic, false, new Date(), 'IT Booking', id, 'ปกติ']);
        });
      }
    } catch(ignore) {}
    logAudit_(data.ownerPhone, data.ownerName, 'CREATE_IT_BOOKING', 'ITBooking', id, 'สร้างคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom: ' + data.topic, null, itBookingRowToObject_(row));
    return { success:true, message:'บันทึกคำขอจองเรียบร้อยแล้ว และส่งให้เจ้าหน้าที่กลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัลดำเนินการแล้ว', id:id };
  } catch(e) { logError_(data && data.ownerPhone || '', 'saveITBooking', e, {data:data}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function updateITBookingStatus(bookingId, action, actorPhone, payload) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(bookingId)) {
        var before = itBookingRowToObject_(data[i]);
        var actor = String(actorPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
        var canAct = isITServiceStaff_(actor);
        if (!canAct) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์จัดการคำขอนี้' };
        var status = before.status;
        if (action === 'approve') status = 'อนุมัติ';
        else if (action === 'reject') status = 'ไม่อนุญาต';
        else if (action === 'cancel') status = 'ยกเลิก';
        else return { success:false, message:'Action ไม่ถูกต้อง' };
        sheet.getRange(i+1, 15).setValue(status);
        if (payload.adminRoomId !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 17).setValue(payload.adminRoomId || '');
        if (payload.adminPasscode !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 18).setValue(payload.adminPasscode || '');
        if (payload.adminLink !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 19).setValue(payload.adminLink || '');
        if (payload.reason !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 20).setValue(payload.reason || '');
        if (payload.staffName !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 24).setValue(payload.staffName || '');
        if (payload.staffPosition !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 25).setValue(payload.staffPosition || '');
        if (payload.supervisorName !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 26).setValue(payload.supervisorName || '');
        if (payload.supervisorPosition !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 27).setValue(payload.supervisorPosition || '');
        if (payload.supervisorSignatureData !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 28).setValue(payload.supervisorSignatureData || '');
        if (payload.adminSignatureData !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 29).setValue(payload.adminSignatureData || '');
        if (payload.approvalNote !== undefined) sheet.getRange(i+1, 30).setValue(payload.approvalNote || '');
        sheet.getRange(i+1, 23).setValue(new Date());
        try {
          var rowNow = sheet.getRange(i+1,1,1,sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
          var itemNow = itBookingRowToObject_(rowNow); itemNow.status = status;
          var existingCal = itemNow.googleCalendarEventId || '';
          var calIT = syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_(itemNow, existingCal);
          var cCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Google Calendar Event ID'); if (cCol && calIT && calIT !== 'Error') sheet.getRange(i+1,cCol).setValue(calIT);
          var cSync = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Google Calendar Sync'); if (cSync) sheet.getRange(i+1,cSync).setValue(calIT && calIT !== 'Error' ? 'Synced' : 'Error');
          var cLast = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Last Calendar Sync'); if (cLast) sheet.getRange(i+1,cLast).setValue(new Date());
        } catch(calUpdErr) { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'updateITBookingStatus calendar sync', calUpdErr, {bookingId:bookingId}); }
        try {
          var notif = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS);
          if (notif) notif.appendRow(['NTF-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8), "'" + before.ownerPhone, 'คำขอจองห้อง/Zoom ของคุณ: ' + before.topic + ' — ' + status, false, new Date(), 'IT Booking', bookingId, status === 'ไม่อนุญาต' ? 'ด่วน' : 'ปกติ']);
        } catch(ignore) {}
        logAudit_(actorPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone), 'UPDATE_IT_BOOKING_STATUS', 'ITBooking', bookingId, 'เปลี่ยนสถานะคำขอจองเป็น ' + status, before, payload);
        return { success:true, message:'อัปเดตสถานะเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
      }
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอจอง' };
  } catch(e) { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'updateITBookingStatus', e, {bookingId:bookingId, action:action}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}



// ==========================================
// IT Services v43: Meeting Minutes Integration
// ==========================================
function getMeetingMinutesSheet_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_MEETING_MINUTES, [
    'Minutes ID', 'Title', 'Date', 'Location', 'Chairman', 'Attendees', 'Agenda', 'Conclusion', 'Recorder Phone', 'Recorder Name', 'Department', 'Google Doc URL', 'PDF URL', 'Related Schedule ID', 'Audio File URL', 'Created At', 'Updated At'
  ], '#dbeafe');
}

function getMeetingMinutesFolder_() {
  var folderName = 'HealthAssistant_MeetingMinutes';
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(folderName);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(folderName);
}

function normalizeDateForDoc_(value) {
  if (!value) return '-';
  var d = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(value || '-');
  var months = ['มกราคม','กุมภาพันธ์','มีนาคม','เมษายน','พฤษภาคม','มิถุนายน','กรกฎาคม','สิงหาคม','กันยายน','ตุลาคม','พฤศจิกายน','ธันวาคม'];
  return d.getDate() + ' ' + months[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + (d.getFullYear() + 543);
}

function createMeetingMinutesDocument_(data, id) {
  var folder = getMeetingMinutesFolder_();
  var title = 'รายงานการประชุม_' + (data.title || id || 'Meeting') + '_' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmm');
  var doc = DocumentApp.create(title);
  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  folder.addFile(file);
  try { DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file); } catch(ignore) {}
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.clear();
  body.appendParagraph('รายงานการประชุม').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
  body.appendParagraph(String(data.title || '-')).setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
  body.appendParagraph('วันที่ ' + normalizeDateForDoc_(data.date));
  body.appendParagraph('สถานที่: ' + (data.location || '-'));
  body.appendParagraph('ประธาน: ' + (data.chairman || '-'));
  body.appendParagraph('ผู้จดรายงาน: ' + (data.recorderName || data.recorder || '-'));
  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendParagraph('ผู้เข้าร่วมประชุม').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3);
  body.appendParagraph(data.attendees || '-');
  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendParagraph('วาระ/สาระสำคัญการประชุม').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3);
  body.appendParagraph(data.agenda || '-');
  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendParagraph('สรุปผล / มติ / ข้อสั่งการ').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3);
  body.appendParagraph(data.conclusion || '-');
  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendParagraph('สร้างจาก Health Assistant OS').setFontSize(9).setForegroundColor('#64748b');
  doc.saveAndClose();
  file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
  var pdf = file.getAs(MimeType.PDF).setName(title + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = folder.createFile(pdf);
  pdfFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
  return { docUrl: file.getUrl(), pdfUrl: pdfFile.getUrl(), docId: doc.getId(), pdfId: pdfFile.getId() };
}

function saveMeetingMinutesV2(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    if (!data.title) return { success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุหัวข้อการประชุม' };
    var sheet = getMeetingMinutesSheet_();
    var id = data.minutesId || ('MIN-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase());
    var now = new Date();
    data.recorderPhone = String(data.recorderPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var docInfo = createMeetingMinutesDocument_(data, id);
    var row = [
      id, data.title || '', data.date || '', data.location || '', data.chairman || '', data.attendees || '', data.agenda || '', data.conclusion || '',
      "'" + data.recorderPhone, data.recorderName || '', data.department || '', docInfo.docUrl || '', docInfo.pdfUrl || '', data.relatedScheduleId || '', data.audioFileUrl || '', now, now
    ];
    sheet.appendRow(row);
    logAudit_(data.recorderPhone, data.recorderName, 'CREATE_MEETING_MINUTES', 'MeetingMinutes', id, 'สร้างรายงานการประชุม: ' + data.title, null, { id:id, title:data.title, docUrl:docInfo.docUrl, pdfUrl:docInfo.pdfUrl });
    return { success:true, message:'สร้างรายงานการประชุมเรียบร้อยแล้ว', id:id, docUrl:docInfo.docUrl, pdfUrl:docInfo.pdfUrl };
  } catch(e) { logError_(data && data.recorderPhone || '', 'saveMeetingMinutesV2', e, {data:data}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function updateMeetingMinutesV759(data, actorPhone) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var minutesId = String(data.minutesId || data.id || '').trim();
    if (!minutesId) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรหัสรายงานการประชุม'};
    if (!data.title) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุหัวข้อการประชุม'};
    var actor = String(actorPhone || data.recorderPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var profile = getUserProfileByPhone_(actor) || {};
    var role = String(profile.role || '');
    var canAdmin = role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin' || isSuperAdmin_(actor);
    var sheet = getMeetingMinutesSheet_();
    var values = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (String(values[i][0]) !== minutesId) continue;
      var owner = String(values[i][8] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      if (owner !== actor && !canAdmin) return {success:false, message:'แก้ไขได้เฉพาะเจ้าของรายงานหรือผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
      var before = {
        id:values[i][0], title:values[i][1], date:values[i][2], location:values[i][3], chairman:values[i][4],
        attendees:values[i][5], agenda:values[i][6], conclusion:values[i][7], docUrl:values[i][11], pdfUrl:values[i][12]
      };
      data.recorderPhone = owner || actor;
      data.recorderName = data.recorderName || values[i][9] || profile.fullName || profile.name || '';
      data.department = data.department || values[i][10] || profile.department || '';
      var docInfo = createMeetingMinutesDocument_(data, minutesId);
      var createdAt = values[i][15] || new Date();
      var now = new Date();
      var row = [
        minutesId, data.title || '', data.date || '', data.location || '', data.chairman || '', data.attendees || '',
        data.agenda || '', data.conclusion || '', "'" + data.recorderPhone, data.recorderName || '', data.department || '',
        docInfo.docUrl || '', docInfo.pdfUrl || '', data.relatedScheduleId || values[i][13] || '', data.audioFileUrl || values[i][14] || '',
        createdAt, now
      ];
      sheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
      logAudit_(actor, getUserNameByPhone_(actor), 'UPDATE_MEETING_MINUTES', 'MeetingMinutes', minutesId, 'แก้ไขรายงานการประชุม: ' + data.title, before, {id:minutesId, title:data.title, docUrl:docInfo.docUrl, pdfUrl:docInfo.pdfUrl});
      return {success:true, message:'แก้ไขรายงานการประชุมแล้ว', id:minutesId, docUrl:docInfo.docUrl, pdfUrl:docInfo.pdfUrl};
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายงานการประชุม'};
  } catch(e) {
    logError_(actorPhone || (data && data.recorderPhone) || '', 'updateMeetingMinutesV759', e, {data:data});
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getMeetingMinutesHistoryV2(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var sheet = getMeetingMinutesSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var out = [];
    var targetPhone = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var userRole = String(role || 'User');
    var userDept = String(department || '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rowPhone = String(data[i][8] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      var rowDept = String(data[i][10] || '');
      var visible = rowPhone === targetPhone || userRole === 'Super Admin' || userRole === 'Admin' || (userRole === 'Head' && rowDept === userDept);
      if (!visible) continue;
      var d = data[i][2];
      var dateStr = d instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd') : String(d || '');
      var created = data[i][15];
      out.push({
        id:data[i][0], title:data[i][1], date:dateStr, location:data[i][3], chairman:data[i][4], attendees:data[i][5], agenda:data[i][6], conclusion:data[i][7],
        recorderPhone:rowPhone, recorderName:data[i][9], department:rowDept, docUrl:data[i][11], pdfUrl:data[i][12], relatedScheduleId:data[i][13], audioFileUrl:data[i][14],
        createdAt: created instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(created, 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : String(created || '')
      });
    }
    out.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.createdAt || '').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt || '')); });
    return { success:true, data:out };
  } catch(e) { logError_(phone || '', 'getMeetingMinutesHistoryV2', e, null); return { success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[] }; }
}

function analyzeMeetingMinutesText(text) {
  try {
    if (!text) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบข้อความสำหรับวิเคราะห์' };
    var prompt = 'คุณคือเลขานุการการประชุมของหน่วยงานสาธารณสุขไทย จงวิเคราะห์ข้อความต่อไปนี้และจัดทำข้อมูลสำหรับรายงานการประชุม\n' +
      'ให้ตอบเป็น JSON object เท่านั้น ห้ามมี markdown หรือข้อความอื่นปน\n' +
      'schema: {"title":"หัวข้อการประชุม","date":"YYYY-MM-DD ถ้าพบ","location":"สถานที่","chairman":"ประธาน","attendees":"รายชื่อผู้เข้าร่วม แยกบรรทัด","agenda":"สรุปวาระ/สาระสำคัญเป็นข้อๆ","conclusion":"มติ/ข้อสั่งการ/งานที่ต้องติดตามเป็นข้อๆ"}\n\n' +
      'ข้อความ:\n' + String(text).substring(0, 18000);
    var ai = fetchGeminiAI(prompt, null, null);
    if (!ai.success) return { success:false, message:ai.message };
    var parsed = parseGeminiJson_(ai.text);
    return { success:true, data:parsed, raw:ai.text, modelUsed:ai.model };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:'AI วิเคราะห์ข้อความประชุมไม่สำเร็จ: ' + e.toString() }; }
}

function analyzeMeetingAudio(base64Data, fileName, mimeType) {
  try {
    if (!base64Data) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบไฟล์เสียงสำหรับวิเคราะห์' };
    var mt = String(mimeType || '').toLowerCase();
    if (mt.indexOf('audio/') !== 0) return { success:false, message:'รองรับเฉพาะไฟล์เสียงเท่านั้น' };
    var prompt = 'คุณคือเลขานุการการประชุมของหน่วยงานสาธารณสุขไทย จงฟังไฟล์เสียงการประชุมนี้ แล้วสรุปเพื่อกรอกแบบฟอร์มรายงานการประชุม\n' +
      'ให้ตอบเป็น JSON object เท่านั้น ห้ามมี markdown หรือข้อความอื่นปน\n' +
      'schema: {"title":"หัวข้อการประชุม","date":"YYYY-MM-DD ถ้าพบ","location":"สถานที่","chairman":"ประธาน","attendees":"รายชื่อผู้เข้าร่วม แยกบรรทัด","agenda":"สรุปวาระ/สาระสำคัญเป็นข้อๆ","conclusion":"มติ/ข้อสั่งการ/งานที่ต้องติดตามเป็นข้อๆ"}\n' +
      'หากฟังไม่ชัด ให้สรุปเท่าที่ได้และระบุส่วนที่ไม่แน่ใจไว้ใน conclusion';
    var ai = fetchGeminiAI(prompt, base64Data, mimeType);
    if (!ai.success) return { success:false, message:ai.message };
    var parsed = parseGeminiJson_(ai.text);
    return { success:true, data:parsed, raw:ai.text, modelUsed:ai.model };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:'AI วิเคราะห์เสียงประชุมไม่สำเร็จ: ' + e.toString() }; }
}

function deleteMeetingMinutesV2(minutesId, phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getMeetingMinutesSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var actor = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i=1; i<data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(minutesId)) {
        var owner = String(data[i][8] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
        var p = getUserProfileByPhone_(actor) || {};
        var r = String(p.role || '');
        if (owner !== actor && r !== 'Super Admin' && r !== 'Admin' && !isSuperAdmin_(actor)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ลบรายงานนี้' };
        var before = { id:data[i][0], title:data[i][1], docUrl:data[i][11], pdfUrl:data[i][12] };
        sheet.deleteRow(i+1);
        logAudit_(actor, getUserNameByPhone_(actor), 'DELETE_MEETING_MINUTES', 'MeetingMinutes', minutesId, 'ลบรายงานการประชุม: ' + before.title, before, null);
        return { success:true, message:'ลบรายงานการประชุมแล้ว' };
      }
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายงานการประชุม' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getBackupLogSheetV63_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_BACKUP_LOGS, ['Backup ID','File URL','Created By','Timestamp','Type','Note'], '#e0f2fe');
}

  return getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_PERMISSION_MATRIX, ['Feature Key','Feature Name','User','Head','Admin','Super Admin','Updated At'], '#f5f3ff');
}
function defaultPermissionMatrixV64_() {
  return [
    {key:'dailyReport', name:'บันทึกรายงานประจำวัน', User:true, Head:true, Admin:true, 'Super Admin':true},
    {key:'teamReports', name:'ดูรายงานคนในทีม', User:false, Head:true, Admin:true, 'Super Admin':true},

function seedPermissionMatrixV64_() {
  var sh = getPermissionMatrixSheetV64_();
  if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) return;
  var rows = defaultPermissionMatrixV64_().map(function(x){ return [x.key,x.name,!!x.User,!!x.Head,!!x.Admin,!!x['Super Admin'],new Date()]; });
  sh.getRange(2,1,rows.length,7).setValues(rows);
}
