function syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_(item, existingEventId) {
  try {
    var start = new Date(item.rawStartTime || item.startTime); var end = new Date(item.rawEndTime || item.endTime);
    if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) return 'Error';
    var title = '[จอง Zoom/ห้อง] ' + (item.topic || '-');
    var loc = item.roomName || item.otherLocation || item.adminRoomId || item.joinLink || item.adminLink || '';
    var desc = ['ระบบจองห้องประชุม / Zoom', 'สถานะ: ' + (item.status || '-'), 'ประเภท: ' + (item.bookingType || '-'), 'ผู้ขอ: ' + (item.ownerName || '-'), 'กลุ่มงาน: ' + (item.department || '-'), 'Meeting ID: ' + (item.meetingId || item.adminMeetingId || item.adminRoomId || '-'), 'Passcode: ' + (item.passcode || item.adminPasscode || '-'), 'Link: ' + (item.joinLink || item.adminLink || '-')].join('\n');
    var cal = CalendarApp.getDefaultCalendar(); var ev = null; if (existingEventId) { try { ev = cal.getEventById(existingEventId); } catch(ignore){} }
    if (ev) { ev.setTitle(title); ev.setTime(start,end); ev.setLocation(loc); ev.setDescription(desc); return ev.getId(); }
    return cal.createEvent(title, start, end, {location:loc, description:desc}).getId();
  } catch(e) { try { logError_('', 'syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_', e, item); } catch(ignore){} return 'Error'; }
}

// ========================= v70.10: IT Services permission hardening =========================
function haosV710IsDigitalHealthDept_(dept) {
  dept = String(dept || '');
  return dept.indexOf('สุขภาพดิจิทัล') !== -1 || /digital\s*health/i.test(dept);
}

function haosV710IsITManagerRole_(role) {
  role = String(role || '');
  return role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin' || /super\s*admin|admin/i.test(role);
}

function haosV710ITNoManageResponse_() {
  return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะสมาชิกกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล หรือผู้ดูแลระบบเท่านั้นที่มีสิทธิ์จัดการ/แก้ไขรายการนี้'};
}

function isITServiceStaff_(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return false;
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rowPhone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      if (rowPhone !== target) continue;
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (status === 'Inactive') return false;
      return haosV710IsITManagerRole_(role) || haosV710IsDigitalHealthDept_(dept);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

function getITServiceStaffPhones_() {
  var list = [];
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return list;
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var phone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (phone && status !== 'Inactive' && (haosV710IsITManagerRole_(role) || haosV710IsDigitalHealthDept_(dept))) {
        if (list.indexOf(phone) === -1) list.push(phone);
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return list;
}

function haosV710ActorCanManageIT_(actor) {
  actor = actor || {};
  if (haosV710IsITManagerRole_(actor.role) || haosV710IsDigitalHealthDept_(actor.department)) return true;
  return actor.phone ? isITServiceStaff_(actor.phone) : false;
}

  try {

    return { success:false, message:e.toString(), assets:[], software:[], licenses:[], maps:[], tickets:[], alerts:[] };
  }
}

function saveITAssetV70(asset, actor) {
  try {
    asset = asset || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!asset.assetName) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อทรัพย์สิน'};
    if (asset.assetId && !haosV710ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV710ITNoManageResponse_();
    var sh = getITAssetSheetV70_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var found = asset.assetId ? haosV70FindRowById_(sh, asset.assetId, 1) : -1;
    if (asset.assetId && found <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบทรัพย์สินที่ต้องการแก้ไข'};
    var old = found > 1 ? itAssetRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, 29).getValues()[0]) : {};
    var id = asset.assetId || haosV70Id_('AST');
    var purchaseDate = haosV7115CorrectAssetDateByFiscal_(
      haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.purchaseDate || old.purchaseDate || ''),
      asset.fiscalYear || old.fiscalYear || '',
      haosV7116AssetDateHint_(asset)
    );
    var warrantyStart = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.warrantyStart || old.warrantyStart || '');
    var warrantyEnd = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.warrantyEnd || old.warrantyEnd || '');
    var lastCheckedAt = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.lastCheckedAt || old.lastCheckedAt || '');
    var condition = haosV7124DisplayCondition_(asset.condition || old.condition || '', asset.status || old.status || '');
    if (!condition) condition = 'ดี';
    var remark = haosV7124CleanRemark_(asset.remark || old.remark || '');
    var row = [
      id, asset.assetCode || '', asset.assetNumber || '', asset.assetName || '', asset.category || '', asset.brand || '', asset.model || '',
      asset.serialNumber || '', asset.spec || '', purchaseDate || '', asset.fiscalYear || old.fiscalYear || '', asset.budgetSource || '', asset.price || '',
      asset.vendor || '', warrantyStart || '', warrantyEnd || '', asset.ownerDepartment || actor.department || '', asset.currentUserName || '',
      "'" + haosV70Phone_(asset.currentUserPhone), asset.location || '', asset.status || 'ใช้งาน', condition, lastCheckedAt || '',
      remark || '', asset.photoUrl || '', old.createdAt || asset.createdAt || now, now, old.createdBy || asset.createdBy || actor.name || actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.phone || ''
    ];
    if (found > 1) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', found > 1 ? 'UPDATE_IT_ASSET' : 'CREATE_IT_ASSET', 'ITAsset', id, asset.assetName || '', null, asset);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกทรัพย์สิน IT แล้ว', assetId:id};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, keys) {
  raw = raw || {};
  keys = Array.isArray(keys) ? keys : [];
  for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
    var key = keys[i];
    if (raw[key] !== undefined && raw[key] !== null && String(raw[key]).trim() !== '') return raw[key];
  }
  return '';
}

function haosV7111ImportKey_(value) {
  var text = String(value || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
  if (!text) return '';
  if (/^[-–—]+$/.test(text)) return '';
  if (/^(n\/a|na|null|none|undefined)$/i.test(text)) return '';
  if (/^(ต่ำกว่าเกณฑ์|ต่ํากว่าเกณฑ์|ไม่มี|ไม่ระบุ|ไม่ทราบ|blank)$/i.test(text)) return '';
  return text;
}

function haosV7124Pad2_(value) {
  value = Number(value || 0);
  return (value < 10 ? '0' : '') + value;
}

function haosV7124NormalizeYear_(year) {
  var y = Number(year);
  if (isNaN(y)) return 0;
  if (y >= 0 && y < 100) y += 2500;
  if (y >= 2400) y -= 543;
  return y;
}

function haosV7124Ymd_(year, month, day) {
  var y = haosV7124NormalizeYear_(year);
  var m = Number(month);
  var d = Number(day);
  if (!y || m < 1 || m > 12 || d < 1 || d > 31) return '';
  return String(y) + '-' + haosV7124Pad2_(m) + '-' + haosV7124Pad2_(d);
}

function haosV7124ExcelSerialDate_(value) {
  var serial = Number(value);
  if (isNaN(serial) || serial <= 20000) return '';
  var date = new Date(Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30) + Math.floor(serial) * 86400000);
  return haosV7124Ymd_(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth() + 1, date.getUTCDate());
}

function haosV7124ImportText_(value) {
  return String(value === null || value === undefined ? '' : value).replace(/'/g, '').trim();
}

function haosV7124IsCheckMark_(value) {
  return /^[✓✔✅]+$/.test(String(value || '').trim());
}

function haosV7124MarkerText_(value, label) {
  var text = haosV7124ImportText_(value);
  if (!text) return '';
  if (haosV7124IsCheckMark_(text)) return label;
  return text;
}

function haosV7124CleanRemark_(value) {
  var text = haosV7124ImportText_(value);
  if (!text) return '';
  if (haosV7124IsCheckMark_(text)) return '';
  if (/นำเข้าจากชีตโปรแกรมคอมพิวเตอร์/i.test(text)) return '';
  return text;
}

function haosV7124DisplayCondition_(condition, status) {
  var text = haosV7124ImportText_(condition);
  if (!text) return '';
  if (!haosV7124IsCheckMark_(text)) return text;
  var statusText = String(status || '');
  if (/ชำรุด|ชํารุด|เสีย|ซ่อม|repair|broken/i.test(statusText)) return 'ชำรุด';
  return '';
}

function haosV7124IsSoftwareAsset_(asset) {
  asset = asset || {};
  var text = [
    asset.assetName, asset.category, asset.smartCategory, asset.manualCategory,
    asset.autoSmartCategory, asset.spec, asset.remark, asset.sourceSheet, asset.unit
  ].join(' ').toLowerCase();
  return /software|license|program|โปรแกรม|ซอฟต์|ซอฟ|ลิขสิทธิ์/.test(text);
}

function haosV7124LicenseCountFallback_(asset, linkedCount) {
  var count = Number(linkedCount || 0);
  if (count > 0) return count;
  return haosV7124IsSoftwareAsset_(asset) ? 1 : 0;
}

function haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    var serialDate = haosV7124ExcelSerialDate_(value);
    if (serialDate) return serialDate;
  }
  if (Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === '[object Date]' && !isNaN(value.getTime())) {
    return haosV7124Ymd_(value.getFullYear(), value.getMonth() + 1, value.getDate());
  }
  var text = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!text) return '';
  var m = text.match(/^(\d{4})[-\/](\d{1,2})[-\/](\d{1,2})/);
  if (m) {
    return haosV7124Ymd_(m[1], m[2], m[3]) || text;
  }
  m = text.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-\/](\d{1,2})[-\/](\d{2,4})/);
  if (m) {
    return haosV7124Ymd_(m[3], m[2], m[1]) || text;
  }
  var parsed = new Date(text);
  if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
    return haosV7124Ymd_(parsed.getFullYear(), parsed.getMonth() + 1, parsed.getDate());
  }
  return text;
}

function haosV7111NormalizeAssetImportRow_(raw, actor) {
  raw = raw || {};
  var damaged = haosV7124MarkerText_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['damaged', 'ชำรุด', 'รายการเสียหายใช้อยู่หรือไม่ได้ใช้ ชำรุด', 'รายการเสียหายใช้อยู่หรือไม่ได้ใช้']), 'ชำรุด');
  var deteriorated = haosV7124MarkerText_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['deteriorated', 'เสื่อมสภาพ', 'รายการเสียหายใช้อยู่หรือไม่ได้ใช้ เสื่อมสภาพ']), 'เสื่อมสภาพ');
  var lost = haosV7124MarkerText_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['lost', 'สูญไป', 'สูญหาย', 'รายการเสียหายใช้อยู่หรือไม่ได้ใช้ สูญไป']), 'สูญหาย');
  var unused = haosV7124MarkerText_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['unused', 'ไม่ใช้', 'ไม่ได้ใช้', 'รายการเสียหายใช้อยู่หรือไม่ได้ใช้ ไม่ใช้']), 'ไม่ได้ใช้');
  var damagedText = [damaged, deteriorated, lost, unused].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
  var ownerDept = haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['ownerDepartment', 'department', 'ผู้ใช้งาน', 'กลุ่มงาน']);
  var detail = haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['spec', 'detail', 'รายละเอียด']);
  var assetName = haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['assetName', 'รายการครุภัณฑ์', 'ชื่อทรัพย์สิน', 'ชื่อครุภัณฑ์']);
  var status = haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['status', 'สถานะ']);
  var condition = haosV7124DisplayCondition_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['condition', 'สภาพ']), status);
  if (!status) status = damagedText ? 'ชำรุด/รอตรวจสอบ' : 'ใช้งาน';
  if (!condition) condition = damagedText ? damagedText : 'ดี';
  var asset = {
    assetId: haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['assetId', 'Asset ID']),
    assetCode: haosV7111ImportKey_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['assetCode', 'รหัสครุภัณฑ์', 'รหัสทรัพย์สิน'])),
    assetNumber: haosV7124ImportText_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['assetNumber', 'gfmisNumber', 'หมายเลขทรัพย์ในระบบ GFMIS'])),
    assetName: String(assetName || '').trim(),
    category: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['category', 'ประเภทครุภัณฑ์', 'หมวด']) || '').trim(),
    brand: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['brand', 'ยี่ห้อ']) || '').trim(),
    model: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['model', 'รุ่น']) || '').trim(),
    serialNumber: haosV7111ImportKey_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['serialNumber', 'serial', 'Serial Number', 'S/N'])),
    spec: String(detail || '').trim(),
    purchaseDate: haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['purchaseDate', 'วัน/เดือน/ปี', 'วันที่ได้มา'])),
    fiscalYear: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['fiscalYear', 'ปีงบประมาณ']) || '').trim(),
    budgetSource: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['budgetSource', 'แหล่งงบประมาณ']) || '').trim(),
    price: haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['price', 'มูลค่าการได้มา', 'ราคา']),
    vendor: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['vendor', 'ผู้ขาย']) || '').trim(),
    warrantyStart: haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['warrantyStart', 'เริ่มประกัน'])),
    warrantyEnd: haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['warrantyEnd', 'หมดประกัน'])),
    ownerDepartment: String(ownerDept || actor.department || '').trim(),
    currentUserName: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['currentUserName', 'ผู้ครอบครอง', 'ผู้ใช้ปัจจุบัน']) || '').trim(),
    currentUserPhone: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['currentUserPhone', 'เบอร์ผู้ใช้']) || '').trim(),
    location: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['location', 'สถานที่']) || '').trim(),
    status: status,
    condition: condition,
    lastCheckedAt: haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['lastCheckedAt', 'วันที่ตรวจล่าสุด'])),
    remark: haosV7124CleanRemark_(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['remark', 'หมายเหตุ'])),
    photoUrl: String(haosV7111ImportValue_(raw, ['photoUrl', 'รูปภาพ']) || '').trim()
  };
  asset.purchaseDate = haosV7115CorrectAssetDateByFiscal_(asset.purchaseDate, asset.fiscalYear, haosV7116AssetDateHint_(asset));
  return asset;
}

function importITAssetsV7111(rows, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    rows = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
    if (!rows.length) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายการสำหรับนำเข้า'};
    if (rows.length > 800) rows = rows.slice(0, 800);

    var sh = getITAssetSheetV70_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    sh.getRange('A:C').setNumberFormat('@');
    sh.getRange('H:H').setNumberFormat('@');
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var byId = {}, byCode = {}, byNumber = {}, bySerial = {};
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rowNo = i + 1;
      var assetId = String(data[i][0] || '').trim();
      var code = haosV7111ImportKey_(data[i][1]);
      var num = haosV7111ImportKey_(data[i][2]);
      var serial = haosV7111ImportKey_(data[i][7]);
      if (assetId) byId[assetId] = rowNo;
      if (code) byCode[code] = rowNo;
      if (num) byNumber[num] = rowNo;
      if (serial) bySerial[serial] = rowNo;
    }

    var inserted = 0, updated = 0, skipped = 0;
    var matchedById = 0, matchedByCode = 0, matchedByNumber = 0, matchedBySerial = 0;
    rows.forEach(function(raw) {
      var asset = haosV7111NormalizeAssetImportRow_(raw, actor);
      var assetNumberKey = haosV7111ImportKey_(asset.assetNumber);
      if (!asset.assetName && !asset.assetCode && !asset.assetNumber && !asset.serialNumber) {
        skipped++;
        return;
      }
      var rowNo = 0;
      if (asset.assetId && byId[asset.assetId]) {
        rowNo = byId[asset.assetId];
        matchedById++;
      } else if (asset.assetCode) {
        rowNo = byCode[asset.assetCode] || 0;
        if (rowNo) matchedByCode++;
      } else if (assetNumberKey && byNumber[assetNumberKey]) {
        rowNo = byNumber[assetNumberKey];
        matchedByNumber++;
      } else if (asset.serialNumber && bySerial[asset.serialNumber]) {
        rowNo = bySerial[asset.serialNumber];
        matchedBySerial++;
      }
      var old = rowNo > 1 ? itAssetRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(rowNo, 1, 1, 29).getValues()[0]) : {};
      var id = old.assetId || asset.assetId || haosV70Id_('AST');
      var out = [
        id,
        asset.assetCode ? "'" + asset.assetCode : '',
        asset.assetNumber ? "'" + asset.assetNumber : '',
        asset.assetName || old.assetName || '',
        asset.category || old.category || '',
        asset.brand || old.brand || '',
        asset.model || old.model || '',
        asset.serialNumber ? "'" + asset.serialNumber : (old.serialNumber || ''),
        asset.spec || old.spec || '',
        asset.purchaseDate || old.purchaseDate || '',
        asset.fiscalYear || old.fiscalYear || '',
        asset.budgetSource || old.budgetSource || '',
        asset.price || old.price || '',
        asset.vendor || old.vendor || '',
        asset.warrantyStart || old.warrantyStart || '',
        asset.warrantyEnd || old.warrantyEnd || '',
        asset.ownerDepartment || old.ownerDepartment || actor.department || '',
        asset.currentUserName || old.currentUserName || '',
        asset.currentUserPhone ? "'" + haosV70Phone_(asset.currentUserPhone) : (old.currentUserPhone || ''),
        asset.location || old.location || '',
        asset.status || old.status || 'ใช้งาน',
        asset.condition || old.condition || 'ดี',
        asset.lastCheckedAt || old.lastCheckedAt || '',
        asset.remark || old.remark || '',
        asset.photoUrl || old.photoUrl || '',
        old.createdAt || now,
        now,
        old.createdBy || actor.name || actor.phone || '',
        actor.name || actor.phone || ''
      ];
      if (rowNo > 1) {
        sh.getRange(rowNo, 1, 1, out.length).setValues([out]);
        if (asset.assetCode) byCode[asset.assetCode] = rowNo;
        if (assetNumberKey) byNumber[assetNumberKey] = rowNo;
        if (asset.serialNumber) bySerial[asset.serialNumber] = rowNo;
        byId[id] = rowNo;
        updated++;
      } else {
        sh.appendRow(out);
        inserted++;
        var newRow = sh.getLastRow();
        if (asset.assetCode) byCode[asset.assetCode] = newRow;
        if (assetNumberKey) byNumber[assetNumberKey] = newRow;
        if (asset.serialNumber) bySerial[asset.serialNumber] = newRow;
        byId[id] = newRow;
      }
    });
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', 'IMPORT_IT_ASSETS', 'ITAssets', 'BULK', 'นำเข้าทะเบียนทรัพย์สิน IT', null, {inserted:inserted, updated:updated, skipped:skipped, total:rows.length, matchedById:matchedById, matchedByCode:matchedByCode, matchedByNumber:matchedByNumber, matchedBySerial:matchedBySerial});
    return {success:true, inserted:inserted, updated:updated, skipped:skipped, total:rows.length, matchedById:matchedById, matchedByCode:matchedByCode, matchedByNumber:matchedByNumber, matchedBySerial:matchedBySerial, version:'v70.124-it-asset-master-data-normalize'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function saveITSoftwareLicenseV70(payload, actor) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV710ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV710ITNoManageResponse_();
    if (!payload.softwareName) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อซอฟต์แวร์'};
    var swSh = getITSoftwareSheetV70_();
    var licSh = getITLicenseSheetV70_();
    var mapSh = getITAssetSoftwareMapSheetV70_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var softwareId = payload.softwareId || haosV70Id_('SW');
    if (!payload.softwareId) {
      swSh.appendRow([softwareId, payload.softwareName || '', payload.softwareVendor || payload.vendor || '', payload.softwareCategory || '', payload.licenseType || 'Subscription', payload.version || '', payload.description || '', payload.defaultRenewalDays || 60, now, now]);
    }
    var licenseId = payload.licenseId || haosV70Id_('LIC');
    if (!payload.licenseId) {
      licSh.appendRow([licenseId, softwareId, payload.licenseName || payload.softwareName || '', payload.licenseKeyMasked || '', payload.seatTotal || 1, payload.seatUsed || 0, payload.purchaseDate || '', payload.startDate || '', payload.expiryDate || '', payload.renewalAlertDays || 60, payload.licenseStatus || 'Active', payload.vendor || payload.softwareVendor || '', payload.contractNumber || '', payload.poNumber || '', payload.price || '', payload.attachmentUrl || '', payload.remark || '', now, now]);
    }
    var mapId = '';
    if (payload.assetId) {
      mapId = haosV70Id_('MAP');
      mapSh.appendRow([mapId, payload.assetId || '', softwareId, licenseId, payload.assignedToUserName || '', "'" + haosV70Phone_(payload.assignedToUserPhone), payload.installDate || '', payload.deviceName || '', payload.activationStatus || 'Activated', '', payload.note || '', now, now]);
    }
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', 'SAVE_IT_LICENSE', 'ITLicense', licenseId, payload.softwareName || '', null, payload);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกซอฟต์แวร์/License แล้ว', softwareId:softwareId, licenseId:licenseId, mapId:mapId};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function saveITRepairTicketV70(ticket, actor) {
  try {
    ticket = ticket || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!ticket.problemTitle) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุหัวข้อปัญหา'};
    if (ticket.ticketId && !haosV710ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV710ITNoManageResponse_();
    var sh = getITRepairTicketSheetV70_();
    var assets = getAllITAssetsV70_();
    var asset = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) if (assets[i].assetId === ticket.assetId) asset = assets[i];
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var found = ticket.ticketId ? haosV70FindRowById_(sh, ticket.ticketId, 1) : -1;
    if (ticket.ticketId && found <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อมที่ต้องการแก้ไข'};
    var old = found > 1 ? itRepairTicketRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, 26).getValues()[0]) : {};
    var id = ticket.ticketId || haosV70Id_('RPT');
    var no = ticket.ticketNo || old.ticketNo || ('IT-' + Utilities.formatDate(now, 'GMT+7', 'yyyyMMdd') + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0,4).toUpperCase());
    var status = ticket.ticketId ? (ticket.status || old.status || 'New') : 'New';
    var row = [
      id, no, ticket.assetId || old.assetId || '', ticket.assetCode || (asset ? asset.assetCode : old.assetCode || ''), ticket.assetName || (asset ? asset.assetName : old.assetName || ''),
      ticket.requesterName || old.requesterName || actor.name || '', "'" + haosV70Phone_(ticket.requesterPhone || old.requesterPhone || actor.phone), ticket.requesterDepartment || old.requesterDepartment || actor.department || '',
      ticket.location || (asset ? asset.location : old.location || ''), ticket.problemCategory || old.problemCategory || 'Hardware', ticket.problemTitle || old.problemTitle || '', ticket.problemDetail || old.problemDetail || '',
      ticket.priority || old.priority || 'Normal', ticket.impact || old.impact || '', status, ticket.assignedTo || old.assignedTo || '', ticket.reportedAt || old.reportedAt || now,
      old.acceptedAt || '', old.resolvedAt || '', old.closedAt || '', ticket.slaDueAt || old.slaDueAt || '', ticket.resolutionSummary || old.resolutionSummary || '', ticket.repairCost || old.repairCost || '', ticket.attachmentUrl || old.attachmentUrl || '', old.createdAt || ticket.createdAt || now, now
    ];
    if (found > 1) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    if (!ticket.ticketId) {
      try {
        createNotificationsV732_(getITServiceStaffPhones_(), 'มีใบแจ้งซ่อม IT ใหม่: ' + no + ' - ' + ticket.problemTitle, 'IT Repair', id, ticket.priority || 'Normal', {module:'itRepair', action:'openITRepair', createdBy:ticket.requesterPhone || actor.phone});
      } catch(ignore) {}
    }
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', found > 1 ? 'UPDATE_IT_REPAIR' : 'CREATE_IT_REPAIR', 'ITRepairTicket', id, no + ' ' + ticket.problemTitle, null, ticket);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกใบแจ้งซ่อมแล้ว', ticketId:id, ticketNo:no};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function updateITRepairTicketStatusV70(ticketId, status, note, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV710ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV710ITNoManageResponse_();
    var sh = getITRepairTicketSheetV70_();
    var row = haosV70FindRowById_(sh, ticketId, 1);
    if (row <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อม'};
    var before = itRepairTicketRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, 26).getValues()[0]);
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    sh.getRange(row, 15).setValue(status || 'In Progress');
    sh.getRange(row, 26).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Assigned') sh.getRange(row, 18).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Resolved') sh.getRange(row, 19).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Closed') sh.getRange(row, 20).setValue(now);
    if (note) sh.getRange(row, 22).setValue(note);
    getITRepairUpdateSheetV70_().appendRow([haosV70Id_('UPD'), ticketId, status || '', note || '', actor.name || actor.phone || '', now, '', false]);
    try {
      createNotificationV732_(before.requesterPhone, 'สถานะใบแจ้งซ่อม IT ' + (before.ticketNo || ticketId) + ': ' + (status || 'In Progress') + (note ? ' | ' + note : ''), 'IT Repair Status', ticketId, before.priority || 'Normal', {module:'itRepair', action:'openITRepair', createdBy:actor.phone});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตสถานะใบแจ้งซ่อมแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function runITLicenseExpiryAlertV70(actorPhone) {
  try {
    if (!isITServiceStaff_(actorPhone)) return haosV710ITNoManageResponse_();
    var data = getITAssetModuleDataV70(actorPhone || '', '', '');
    if (!data.success) return data;
    var alerts = data.alerts || [];
    getITServiceStaffPhones_().forEach(function(staffPhone) {
      alerts.slice(0, 20).forEach(function(a) {
        createNotificationV732_(staffPhone, 'License ใกล้หมดอายุ: ' + (a.softwareName || a.licenseName || a.licenseId) + ' เหลือ ' + a.daysLeft + ' วัน', 'IT License', a.licenseId, a.level, {module:'itAsset', action:'openITAsset', createdBy:actorPhone});
      });
    });
    return {success:true, message:'ตรวจและสร้างการแจ้งเตือน License แล้ว', count:alerts.length};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV70() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.10-it-permissions', functions:['getITAssetModuleDataV70','saveITAssetV70','importITAssetsV7111','saveITSoftwareLicenseV70','saveITRepairTicketV70','updateITRepairTicketStatusV70','runITLicenseExpiryAlertV70']};
}



function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheck() {
  var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
  var required = [
    'getErrorLogs',
    'getWorkTags',
    'getDashboardAnalytics',
    'getNotificationCenter'
  ];
  var missing = required.filter(function(name){ return !allowed[name]; });
  return {
    success: missing.length === 0,
    version: 'v43-meeting-minutes-integration',
    required: required,
    missing: missing,
    message: missing.length ? ('Missing: ' + missing.join(', ')) : 'Bridge whitelist is ready.'
  };
}

function handleVercelBridge_(payload) {
  var expected = getVercelApiSecret_();
  if (!expected || payload.secret !== expected) return { __bridgeError:true, success:false, message:'Unauthorized Vercel bridge request' };
  var fnName = payload.fn;
  var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
  if (!allowed[fnName]) return { __bridgeError:true, success:false, message:'Function not allowed: ' + fnName };
  var fn = (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis[fnName] : this[fnName]);
  if (typeof fn !== 'function') { try { fn = eval(fnName); } catch (ignore) {} }
  if (typeof fn !== 'function') return { __bridgeError:true, success:false, message:'Function not found: ' + fnName };
  try { return fn.apply(null, payload.args || []); } catch (err) { logError_((payload.args && payload.args[0]) || '', fnName, err, { args: payload.args || [] }); return { __bridgeError:true, success:false, message:err.toString() }; }
}

function doPost(e) {
  try {
    var contentType = String(e && e.postData && e.postData.type || '').toLowerCase();
    if (e && e.postData && contentType.indexOf("application/json") !== -1) {
      var payload = JSON.parse(e.postData.contents || '{}');
      if (payload && payload._vercelBridge) return jsonOutput_(handleVercelBridge_(payload));
      var update = payload;
      if (update.message && update.message.text) {
        var chatId = update.message.chat.id;
        var text = update.message.text.trim();
        if (text === "/start") sendTelegram("👋 พิมพ์ /mywork เพื่อดูงานวันนี้ครับ", chatId);
        else if (text === "/mywork") {
          var data = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME).getDataRange().getValues();
          var works = [];
          for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
            if (data[i][7].toString() === chatId.toString() && data[i][9] === "Pending") works.push("🗓 " + data[i][1] + " (" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(data[i][2]), "GMT+7", "HH:mm") + ")");
          }
          sendTelegram(works.length > 0 ? "💼 งานของคุณ:\n" + works.join("\n") : "✅ ไม่มีงานรอแจ้งเตือน", chatId);
        } else sendTelegram("🤖: " + chatWithAI(text), chatId);
      }
    }
  } catch (error) { Logger.log(error); }
  return ContentService.createTextOutput("OK");
}


function manageAutomation(status) {
  var value = (status === 'on' || status === 'ON') ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
  setSetting_('SYSTEM_STATUS', value, 'สถานะการแจ้งเตือน/ระบบอัตโนมัติ');
  logAudit_('', 'Admin', 'UPDATE_SYSTEM_STATUS', 'Settings', 'SYSTEM_STATUS', 'เปลี่ยนสถานะระบบเป็น ' + value, null, {status: value});
  return { success: true, message: value === 'ON' ? 'เปิดระบบแจ้งเตือน/บอทแล้ว' : 'ปิดระบบแจ้งเตือน/บอทแล้ว' };
}


function mainWorkflow() { 
  checkAndNotify(); 
}

function checkAndNotify() {
  if (getSetting_("SYSTEM_STATUS", "ON") === "OFF") return;
  var ss = getDB();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME);
  var userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  
  if (!sheet || !userSheet) return;
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var usersData = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var now = new Date();
  
  var emailNotifMap = {};
  for(var u = 1; u < usersData.length; u++) {
     emailNotifMap[usersData[u][0].toString().replace(/'/g, '')] = usersData[u][9] || "On";
  }
  
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][9] === "อยู่ระหว่างการดำเนินการ" && data[i][12] === "Approved") {
      var startTime = new Date(data[i][2]);
      if (now >= new Date(startTime.getTime() - (data[i][8] * 60000)) && now < startTime) {
        var msg = "🔔 [แจ้งเตือนงาน]\nเรื่อง: " + data[i][1] + "\nเวลา: " + Utilities.formatDate(startTime, "GMT+7", "HH:mm น.");
        
        if (data[i][15] && data[i][15] !== "") {
          msg += "\n📝 รายละเอียด: " + data[i][15];
        }
        if (data[i][16] && data[i][16] !== "") {
          msg += "\n📍 สถานที่: " + data[i][16];
        }
        if (data[i][4] && data[i][4] !== "") {
          msg += "\n🔗 ลิงก์เข้าร่วม: " + data[i][4];
        }
        if (data[i][7]) {
          sendTelegram(msg, data[i][7]);
        }
        
        var ownerPhone = data[i][10].toString().replace(/'/g, '');
        if (data[i][6] && emailNotifMap[ownerPhone] !== "Off") {
          MailApp.sendEmail(data[i][6], "แจ้งเตือนงาน", msg);
        }
        
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, 10).setValue("Notified");
      }
    }
  }
}

function sendTelegram(msg, chatId) {
  if (!msg || msg === "" || !chatId || chatId === "") return;
  var options = {
    "method": "post",
    "contentType": "application/json",
    "payload": JSON.stringify({"chat_id": String(chatId), "text": String(msg)}),
    "muteHttpExceptions": true
  };
  var token = getTelegramBotToken_();
  if (!token) return;
  UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://api.telegram.org/bot" + token + "/sendMessage", options);
}

  getLiveSurveySheet_();
  getLiveSurveyResponseSheet_();
  getITBookingSheet_();
  getMeetingMinutesSheet_();

  return { success: true, message: 'Workflow tables are ready' };
}

function saveRecurringRule(rule) {
  try {
    workflowSetup();
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_RECURRING);
    var ruleId = 'REC-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8);
    var start = rule.startDate ? new Date(rule.startDate) : new Date();
    var nextRun = calculateNextRun_(start, rule.frequency || 'weekly', Number(rule.interval || 1), rule.byDay || '', true);
    sheet.appendRow([
      ruleId,
      "'" + String(rule.ownerPhone || ''),
      rule.ownerName || '',
      rule.eventName || '',
      rule.details || '',
      rule.location || '',
      rule.meetingLink || '',
      rule.resourceId || '',
      rule.leadTime || 15,
      rule.workStatus || 'อยู่ระหว่างการดำเนินการ',
      rule.frequency || 'weekly',
      Number(rule.interval || 1),
      rule.byDay || '',
      start,
      rule.endDate || '',
      nextRun,
      true,
      '',
      new Date()
    ]);
    logAudit_(rule.ownerPhone || '', rule.ownerName || '', 'CREATE_RECURRING_RULE', 'RecurringRule', ruleId, 'สร้างงานประจำ: ' + (rule.eventName || ''), null, rule);
    return { success: true, message: 'สร้างกฎงานประจำเรียบร้อย', ruleId: ruleId };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

function getMyRecurringRules(ownerPhone) {
  try {
    workflowSetup();
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_RECURRING);
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(ownerPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var result = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][1]).replace(/'/g, '') === target) {
        result.push({
          id: data[i][0],
          eventName: data[i][3],
          frequency: data[i][10],
          interval: data[i][11] || 1,
          byDay: data[i][12] || '',
          startDate: data[i][13] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][13], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy') : data[i][13],
          endDate: data[i][14] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][14], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy') : data[i][14],
          nextRun: data[i][15] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][15], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : data[i][15],
          active: data[i][16] === true || String(data[i][16]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'
        });
      }
    }
    return { success: true, data: result.reverse() };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

function toggleRecurringRule(ruleId, active, ownerPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_RECURRING);
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(ownerPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === ruleId && String(data[i][1]).replace(/'/g, '') === target) {
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, 17).setValue(!!active);
        logAudit_(ownerPhone, '', active ? 'ENABLE_RECURRING_RULE' : 'DISABLE_RECURRING_RULE', 'RecurringRule', ruleId, (active ? 'เปิด' : 'ปิด') + 'งานประจำ', null, {active: !!active});
        return { success: true, message: active ? 'เปิดใช้งานงานประจำแล้ว' : 'ปิดใช้งานงานประจำแล้ว' };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบกฎงานประจำ หรือคุณไม่มีสิทธิ์จัดการ' };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

function deleteRecurringRule(ruleId, ownerPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_RECURRING);
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(ownerPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === ruleId && String(data[i][1]).replace(/'/g, '') === target) {
        var name = data[i][3];
        sheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        logAudit_(ownerPhone, '', 'DELETE_RECURRING_RULE', 'RecurringRule', ruleId, 'ลบงานประจำ: ' + name, null, null);
        return { success: true, message: 'ลบงานประจำเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบกฎงานประจำ หรือคุณไม่มีสิทธิ์ลบ' };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

function generateDueRecurringSchedules() {
  try {
    workflowSetup();
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_RECURRING);
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var now = new Date();
    var created = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var active = data[i][16] === true || String(data[i][16]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
      if (!active) continue;
      var nextRun = data[i][15] ? new Date(data[i][15]) : null;
      if (!nextRun || isNaN(nextRun.getTime()) || nextRun > now) continue;
      var endDate = data[i][14] ? new Date(data[i][14]) : null;
      if (endDate && !isNaN(endDate.getTime()) && now > endDate) {
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, 17).setValue(false);
        continue;
      }
      createScheduleFromRecurring_(data[i]);
      created++;
      var frequency = data[i][10] || 'weekly';
      var interval = Number(data[i][11] || 1);
      var byDay = data[i][12] || '';
      var newNext = calculateNextRun_(nextRun, frequency, interval, byDay, false);
      sheet.getRange(i + 1, 16).setValue(newNext);
      sheet.getRange(i + 1, 18).setValue(new Date());
    }
    return { success: true, message: 'สร้างงานประจำที่ถึงกำหนดแล้ว ' + created + ' รายการ', created: created };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

function createScheduleFromRecurring_(row) {
  var start = row[15] ? new Date(row[15]) : new Date();
  var data = {
    ownerPhone: String(row[1]).replace(/'/g, ''),
    ownerName: row[2] || '',
    eventName: row[3] || '',
    details: row[4] || '',
    location: row[5] || '',
    meetingLink: row[6] || '',
    resourceId: row[7] || '',
    leadTime: row[8] || 15,
    workStatus: row[9] || 'อยู่ระหว่างการดำเนินการ',
    startTime: Utilities.formatDate(start, 'GMT+7', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"),
    userEmail: '',
    chatId: '',
    assignees: [],
    fileUrl: '',
    approverPhone: '',
    visibility: 'Private'
  };
  return saveScheduleV2(data);
}

function calculateNextRun_(baseDate, frequency, interval, byDay, firstRun) {
  var d = new Date(baseDate);
  interval = Math.max(1, Number(interval || 1));
  if (!firstRun) {
    if (frequency === 'daily') d.setDate(d.getDate() + interval);
    else if (frequency === 'workday') {
      do { d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1); } while (d.getDay() === 0 || d.getDay() === 6);
    }
    else if (frequency === 'monthly') d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + interval);
    else d.setDate(d.getDate() + (7 * interval));
  }
  if (frequency === 'weekly' && byDay) {
    var map = {SU:0, MO:1, TU:2, WE:3, TH:4, FR:5, SA:6};
    var target = map[String(byDay).toUpperCase()];
    if (target !== undefined) {
      var guard = 0;
      while (d.getDay() !== target && guard < 8) { d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1); guard++; }
    }
  }
  return d;
}

// Override: richer approval status with reason + history + owner notification.
function updateApprovalStatus(id, status, approverPhone, reason) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME);
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var history = getOrCreateSheet_('ApprovalHistory', ['History ID', 'Schedule ID', 'Action', 'Actor Phone', 'Reason', 'Timestamp'], '#fef3c7');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][0] === id) {
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, 13).setValue(status);
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, 14).setValue("'" + approverPhone);
        var ownerPhone = String(data[i][10]).replace(/'/g, '');
        var eventName = data[i][1];
        var msgMap = { Approved: '✅ อนุมัติแล้ว', Rejected: '❌ ไม่อนุมัติ', RevisionRequested: '📝 ขอให้แก้ไขข้อมูล' };
        var statusText = msgMap[status] || status;
        history.appendRow(['APR-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8), id, status, "'" + approverPhone, reason || '', new Date()]);
        createNotificationV732_(ownerPhone, 'ผลการพิจารณางาน: ' + eventName + ' - ' + statusText + (reason ? ' | หมายเหตุ: ' + reason : ''), 'Schedule Approval Result', id, status === 'Rejected' ? 'ด่วน' : 'ปกติ', {module:'schedule', action:'openSchedule', createdBy:approverPhone});
        var chatId = data[i][7];
        if (chatId) sendTelegram('📢 ผลการพิจารณา: ' + eventName + '\nสถานะ: ' + statusText + (reason ? '\nหมายเหตุ: ' + reason : ''), chatId);
        logAudit_(approverPhone, '', 'UPDATE_APPROVAL', 'Schedule', id, 'อัปเดตสถานะอนุมัติเป็น ' + statusText, null, {status: status, reason: reason || ''});
        return { success: true, message: 'อัปเดตสถานะเรียบร้อย' };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบข้อมูล' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

function getITBookingHeadersV47_() {
  return [
    'Booking ID', 'Topic', 'Booking Type', 'Join Option', 'Meeting ID', 'Passcode', 'Meeting Link',
    'Resource ID', 'Room Name', 'Other Location', 'Start Time', 'End Time',
    'Owner Phone', 'Owner Name', 'Owner Position', 'Department', 'Status', 'Approver Phone',
    'Admin Room ID', 'Admin Meeting ID', 'Admin Passcode', 'Admin Link', 'Cancel Reason', 'Signature Data',
    'Created At', 'Updated At', 'Staff Name', 'Staff Position', 'Supervisor Name', 'Supervisor Position',
    'Supervisor Signature Data', 'Admin Signature Data', 'Approval Note',
    'Approver Name', 'Approver Position', 'Approver Signature Data'
  ];
}

function getITBookingSheet_() {
  var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS);
  if (!sheet) sheet = getDB().insertSheet(SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS);
  ensureHeaders_(sheet, getITBookingHeadersV47_(), '#ccfbf1');
  return sheet;
}

function itBookingHeaderMapV47_(sheet) {
  var headers = sheet.getRange(1,1,1,sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
  var map = {};
  headers.forEach(function(h, i){ map[String(h)] = i + 1; });
  return map;
}
function itGetByHeader_(row, map, name) { var idx = map[name]; return idx ? row[idx-1] : ''; }
function itSetByHeader_(sheet, rowIndex, map, name, value) { var idx = map[name]; if (idx) sheet.getRange(rowIndex, idx).setValue(value); }
function itPlainTextV794_(value) { return String(value == null ? '' : value).replace(/^'+/, ''); }
function itSetTextByHeaderV794_(sheet, rowIndex, map, name, value) {
  var idx = map[name];
  if (!idx) return;
  var range = sheet.getRange(rowIndex, idx);
  range.setNumberFormat('@');
  range.setValue(itPlainTextV794_(value));
}

function itBookingRowToObject_(row) {
  var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
  var map = itBookingHeaderMapV47_(sheet);
  function fmtDateTime_(v) {
    if (!v) return '';
    var d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(v || '');
    return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');
  }
  var start = itGetByHeader_(row, map, 'Start Time');
  var end = itGetByHeader_(row, map, 'End Time');
  var ownerPhone = String(itGetByHeader_(row, map, 'Owner Phone') || '').replace(/'/g, '');
  return {
    id: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Booking ID'), topic: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Topic'), bookingType: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Booking Type'), joinOption: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Join Option'),
    meetingId: itPlainTextV794_(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Meeting ID')), passcode: itPlainTextV794_(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Passcode')), meetingLink: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Meeting Link'),
    resourceId: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Resource ID'), roomName: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Room Name'), otherLocation: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Other Location'),
    startTime: fmtDateTime_(start), endTime: fmtDateTime_(end), rawStartTime: start instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(start, 'GMT+7', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm") : String(start || ''), rawEndTime: end instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(end, 'GMT+7', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm") : String(end || ''),
    ownerPhone: ownerPhone, ownerName: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Owner Name'), ownerPosition: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Owner Position') || getUserPositionByPhone_(ownerPhone), department: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Department'), status: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Status') || 'รอเจ้าหน้าที่', approverPhone: String(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Approver Phone') || '').replace(/'/g, ''),
    adminRoomId: itPlainTextV794_(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Admin Room ID')), adminMeetingId: itPlainTextV794_(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Admin Meeting ID')), adminPasscode: itPlainTextV794_(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Admin Passcode')), adminLink: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Admin Link'), cancelReason: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Cancel Reason'), signatureData: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Signature Data'),
    createdAt: fmtDateTime_(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Created At')), updatedAt: fmtDateTime_(itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Updated At')),
    staffName: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Staff Name'), staffPosition: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Staff Position'), supervisorName: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Supervisor Name'), supervisorPosition: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Supervisor Position'),
    supervisorSignatureData: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Supervisor Signature Data'), adminSignatureData: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Admin Signature Data'), approvalNote: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Approval Note'),
    approverName: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Approver Name'), approverPosition: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Approver Position'), approverSignatureData: itGetByHeader_(row,map,'Approver Signature Data')
  };
}

function saveITBooking(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    if (!data.topic) return { success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุเรื่องการจอง' };
    if (!data.startTime || !data.endTime) return { success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุช่วงวันเวลาให้ครบ' };
    var start = new Date(data.startTime);
    var end = new Date(data.endTime);
    if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) return { success:false, message:'รูปแบบวันเวลาไม่ถูกต้อง' };
    if (end.getTime() <= start.getTime()) return { success:false, message:'เวลาสิ้นสุดต้องมากกว่าเวลาเริ่มต้น' };
    var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
    var headers = sheet.getRange(1,1,1,sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String);
    var id = 'ITB-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
    var rowObj = {
      'Booking ID': id, 'Topic': data.topic || '', 'Booking Type': data.bookingType || 'Host', 'Join Option': data.joinOption || '',
      'Meeting ID': itPlainTextV794_(data.meetingId || ''), 'Passcode': itPlainTextV794_(data.passcode || ''), 'Meeting Link': data.meetingLink || '',
      'Resource ID': data.resourceId || '', 'Room Name': data.roomName || '', 'Other Location': data.otherLocation || '',
      'Start Time': start, 'End Time': end, 'Owner Phone': "'" + String(data.ownerPhone || '').replace(/'/g, ''), 'Owner Name': data.ownerName || '', 'Owner Position': data.ownerPosition || data.position || getUserPositionByPhone_(data.ownerPhone || '') || '', 'Department': data.department || '',
      'Status': 'รอเจ้าหน้าที่', 'Approver Phone': '', 'Admin Room ID': '', 'Admin Meeting ID': '', 'Admin Passcode': '', 'Admin Link': data.meetingLink || '', 'Cancel Reason': '',
      'Signature Data': data.signatureData || '', 'Created At': new Date(), 'Updated At': new Date(),
      'Staff Name': '', 'Staff Position': '', 'Supervisor Name': '', 'Supervisor Position': '', 'Supervisor Signature Data': '', 'Admin Signature Data': '', 'Approval Note': '',
      'Approver Name': '', 'Approver Position': '', 'Approver Signature Data': ''
    };
    sheet.appendRow(headers.map(function(h){ return rowObj[h] !== undefined ? rowObj[h] : ''; }));
    var savedRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    var savedMap = itBookingHeaderMapV47_(sheet);
    itSetTextByHeaderV794_(sheet, savedRow, savedMap, 'Meeting ID', data.meetingId || '');
    itSetTextByHeaderV794_(sheet, savedRow, savedMap, 'Passcode', data.passcode || '');
    try {
      createNotificationsV732_(getITServiceStaffPhones_(), 'มีคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom ใหม่รอเจ้าหน้าที่ดำเนินการ: ' + data.topic, 'IT Booking', id, 'ปกติ', {module:'itBooking', action:'openItBooking', createdBy:data.ownerPhone});
      if (data.ownerPhone) createNotificationV732_(data.ownerPhone, 'ส่งคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom แล้ว: ' + data.topic, 'IT Booking Submitted', id, 'ปกติ', {module:'itBooking', action:'openItBooking', createdBy:data.ownerPhone});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    logAudit_(data.ownerPhone, data.ownerName, 'CREATE_IT_BOOKING', 'ITBooking', id, 'สร้างคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom: ' + data.topic, null, data);
    return { success:true, message:'บันทึกคำขอจองเรียบร้อยแล้ว และส่งให้เจ้าหน้าที่กลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัลดำเนินการแล้ว', id:id };
  } catch(e) { logError_(data && data.ownerPhone || '', 'saveITBooking', e, {data:data}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function updateITBookingStatus(bookingId, action, actorPhone, payload) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var map = itBookingHeaderMapV47_(sheet);
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
      if (String(itGetByHeader_(data[i], map, 'Booking ID')) === String(bookingId)) {
        var before = itBookingRowToObject_(data[i]);
        var actor = String(actorPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
        if (!isITServiceStaff_(actor)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์จัดการคำขอนี้' };
        var status = before.status;
        if (action === 'approve') status = 'อนุมัติ'; else if (action === 'reject') status = 'ไม่อนุญาต'; else if (action === 'cancel') status = 'ยกเลิก'; else return { success:false, message:'Action ไม่ถูกต้อง' };
        var r = i + 1;
        itSetByHeader_(sheet, r, map, 'Status', status);
        itSetByHeader_(sheet, r, map, 'Updated At', new Date());
        var fields = { adminRoomId:'Admin Room ID', adminMeetingId:'Admin Meeting ID', adminPasscode:'Admin Passcode', adminLink:'Admin Link', reason:'Cancel Reason', approvalNote:'Approval Note', staffName:'Staff Name', staffPosition:'Staff Position', supervisorName:'Supervisor Name', supervisorPosition:'Supervisor Position', supervisorSignatureData:'Supervisor Signature Data', adminSignatureData:'Admin Signature Data', approverName:'Approver Name', approverPosition:'Approver Position', approverSignatureData:'Approver Signature Data' };
        Object.keys(fields).forEach(function(k){
          if (payload[k] === undefined) return;
          if (k === 'adminRoomId' || k === 'adminMeetingId' || k === 'adminPasscode') itSetTextByHeaderV794_(sheet, r, map, fields[k], payload[k] || '');
          else itSetByHeader_(sheet, r, map, fields[k], payload[k] || '');
        });
        try { createNotificationV732_(before.ownerPhone, 'คำขอจองห้อง/Zoom ของคุณ: ' + before.topic + ' — ' + status, 'IT Booking Status', bookingId, status === 'ไม่อนุญาต' ? 'ด่วน' : 'ปกติ', {module:'itBooking', action:'openItBooking', createdBy:actor}); } catch(ignore) {}
        logAudit_(actorPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone), 'UPDATE_IT_BOOKING_STATUS', 'ITBooking', bookingId, 'เปลี่ยนสถานะคำขอจองเป็น ' + status, before, payload);
        return { success:true, message:'อัปเดตสถานะเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
      }
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอจอง' };
  } catch(e) { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'updateITBookingStatus', e, {bookingId:bookingId, action:action}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function deleteITBooking(bookingId, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var map = itBookingHeaderMapV47_(sheet);
    var actor = String(actorPhone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
      if (String(itGetByHeader_(data[i], map, 'Booking ID')) === String(bookingId)) {
        var before = itBookingRowToObject_(data[i]);
        if (before.ownerPhone !== actor && !isITServiceStaff_(actor)) return { success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ลบรายการนี้' };
        sheet.deleteRow(i+1);
        try { if (before.ownerPhone && before.ownerPhone !== actor) createNotificationV732_(before.ownerPhone, 'คำขอจองห้อง/Zoom ถูกลบ: ' + before.topic, 'IT Booking Deleted', bookingId, 'ด่วน', {module:'itBooking', action:'openItBooking', createdBy:actor}); } catch(ignore) {}
        logAudit_(actor, getUserNameByPhone_(actor), 'DELETE_IT_BOOKING', 'ITBooking', bookingId, 'ลบคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom: ' + before.topic, before, null);
        return { success:true, message:'ลบคำขอจองเรียบร้อยแล้ว' };
      }
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอจอง' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getMySignature(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบชีต Users' };
    ensureHeaders_(sheet, ['Signature Data'], '#d4edda');
    var col = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Signature Data');
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) if (String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'') === target) return { success:true, signatureData: data[i][col-1] || '' };
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function saveMySignature(phone, signatureData) {
  try {
    if (!signatureData) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบข้อมูลลายเซ็น' };
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบชีต Users' };
    ensureHeaders_(sheet, ['Signature Data'], '#d4edda');
    var col = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Signature Data');
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'') === target) {
        sheet.getRange(i+1, col).setValue(signatureData);
        logAudit_(target, getUserNameByPhone_(target), 'SAVE_SIGNATURE', 'User', target, 'บันทึกลายเซ็นอิเล็กทรอนิกส์', null, null);
        return { success:true, message:'บันทึกลายเซ็นแล้ว' };
      }
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getDigitalHealthHeadInfo() {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return { success:true, data:{ fullName:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', position:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', signatureData:'' } };
    ensureHeaders_(sheet, ['Signature Data'], '#d4edda');
    var sigCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Signature Data');
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (status !== 'Inactive' && dept.indexOf('สุขภาพดิจิทัล') !== -1 && (role === 'Head' || role === 'Admin' || role === 'Super Admin')) {
        return { success:true, data:{ phone:String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,''), fullName:data[i][2] || 'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', position:data[i][3] || 'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', signatureData:sigCol>0 ? (data[i][sigCol-1] || '') : '' } };
      }
    }
    return { success:true, data:{ fullName:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', position:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', signatureData:'' } };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function seedPermissionMatrixV64_() {
  var sh = getPermissionMatrixSheetV64_();
  if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) return;
  var rows = defaultPermissionMatrixV64_().map(function(x){ return [x.key,x.name,!!x.User,!!x.Head,!!x.Admin,!!x['Super Admin'],new Date()]; });
  sh.getRange(2,1,rows.length,7).setValues(rows);
}
function getPermissionMatrixV64(adminPhone) {
  try { seedPermissionMatrixV64_(); var sh = getPermissionMatrixSheetV64_(); var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var out=[];
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) out.push({key:data[i][0], name:data[i][1], User:data[i][2]===true || data[i][2]==='TRUE', Head:data[i][3]===true || data[i][3]==='TRUE', Admin:data[i][4]===true || data[i][4]==='TRUE', 'Super Admin':data[i][5]===true || data[i][5]==='TRUE'});
    return {success:true, data:out};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString(), data:defaultPermissionMatrixV64_()}; }
}

    var sh = getPermissionMatrixSheetV64_(); sh.clearContents(); sh.getRange(1,1,1,7).setValues([['Feature Key','Feature Name','User','Head','Admin','Super Admin','Updated At']]);
    matrix = Array.isArray(matrix) ? matrix : defaultPermissionMatrixV64_();
    var rows = matrix.map(function(x){ return [x.key,x.name,!!x.User,!!x.Head,!!x.Admin,!!x['Super Admin'],new Date()]; });
    if (rows.length) sh.getRange(2,1,rows.length,7).setValues(rows);
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'SAVE_PERMISSION_MATRIX', 'Settings', 'PermissionMatrix', 'อัปเดต Permission Matrix', null, matrix);
    try { createNotificationV732_(adminPhone, 'บันทึก Permission Matrix แล้ว', 'System Tools', 'PermissionMatrix', 'ปกติ', {module:'systemTools', action:'openSystemTools', createdBy:adminPhone}); } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true,message:'บันทึก Permission Matrix แล้ว'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}

function createSystemBackupV63(phone, note) {
  try { var role = (getUserProfileByPhone_(phone)||{}).role || ''; if (role !== 'Super Admin' && role !== 'Admin') return {success:false,message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์สร้าง Backup'};
    var ss = getDB(); var names = [SHEET_NAME,SHEET_USERS,SHEET_RESOURCES,SHEET_REPORTS,SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS,SHEET_SETTINGS,SHEET_AUDIT,SHEET_ATTACHMENTS,SHEET_RECURRING,SHEET_WORK_STATUSES,SHEET_WORK_TAGS,SHEET_WORKSPACES,SHEET_LIVE_SURVEYS,SHEET_LIVE_RESPONSES,SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS,SHEET_MEETING_MINUTES,SHEET_PERMISSION_MATRIX];
    var payload = {version:'v63-backup', createdAt:new Date(), createdBy:phone, sheets:{}};
    names.forEach(function(n){ var sh = ss.getSheetByName(n); if (sh) payload.sheets[n] = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); });
    var folderIt = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('HealthAssistant_Backups'); var folder = folderIt.hasNext()?folderIt.next():DriveApp.createFolder('HealthAssistant_Backups');
    var id = 'BKP-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0,8).toUpperCase();
    var blob = Utilities.newBlob(JSON.stringify(payload,null,2), 'application/json', id + '.json');
    var file = folder.createFile(blob); file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    getBackupLogSheetV63_().appendRow([id, file.getUrl(), "'"+String(phone||''), new Date(), 'JSON', note || 'Manual backup']);
    logAudit_(phone, getUserNameByPhone_(phone), 'CREATE_BACKUP', 'Backup', id, 'สร้าง Backup ระบบ', null, {url:file.getUrl()});
    try { createNotificationV732_(phone, 'สร้าง Backup ระบบสำเร็จ: ' + id, 'System Backup', id, 'ปกติ', {module:'systemTools', action:'openSystemTools', createdBy:phone}); } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, message:'สร้าง Backup สำเร็จ', id:id, url:file.getUrl()};
  } catch(e) { logError_(phone || '', 'createSystemBackupV63', e, null); return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}

function restoreSystemBackupV63(phone, fileIdOrUrl, confirmText) {
  try { var role = (getUserProfileByPhone_(phone)||{}).role || ''; if (role !== 'Super Admin') return {success:false,message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin'}; if (confirmText !== 'RESTORE') return {success:false,message:'กรุณายืนยันด้วยคำว่า RESTORE'};
    var id = String(fileIdOrUrl||'').match(/[-\w]{25,}/); if (!id) return {success:false,message:'ไม่พบ File ID ของ Backup'};
    var json = DriveApp.getFileById(id[0]).getBlob().getDataAsString(); var payload = JSON.parse(json); if (!payload.sheets) return {success:false,message:'ไฟล์ Backup ไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    var ss = getDB(); Object.keys(payload.sheets).forEach(function(name){ var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name) || ss.insertSheet(name); sh.clearContents(); var vals = payload.sheets[name]; if (vals && vals.length) sh.getRange(1,1,vals.length,vals[0].length).setValues(vals); });
    logAudit_(phone, getUserNameByPhone_(phone), 'RESTORE_BACKUP', 'Backup', id[0], 'Restore backup', null, {file:fileIdOrUrl});
    try { createNotificationV732_(phone, 'Restore Backup สำเร็จ: ' + id[0], 'System Backup', id[0], 'ด่วน', {module:'systemTools', action:'openSystemTools', createdBy:phone}); } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true,message:'Restore สำเร็จ'};
  } catch(e){ logError_(phone||'', 'restoreSystemBackupV63', e, {fileIdOrUrl:fileIdOrUrl}); return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}

function deleteGoogleCalendarEventV66_(eventId) {
  try { if (!eventId || eventId === 'Error') return true; var ev = CalendarApp.getDefaultCalendar().getEventById(String(eventId)); if (ev) ev.deleteEvent(); return true; } catch(e) { return false; }
}
function syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_(item, existingEventId) {
  try {
    var start = new Date(item.rawStartTime || item.startTime); var end = new Date(item.rawEndTime || item.endTime);
    if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) return 'Error';
    var title = '[จอง Zoom/ห้อง] ' + (item.topic || '-');
    var loc = item.roomName || item.otherLocation || item.adminRoomId || item.joinLink || item.adminLink || '';
    var desc = ['ระบบจองห้องประชุม / Zoom', 'สถานะ: ' + (item.status || '-'), 'ประเภท: ' + (item.bookingType || '-'), 'ผู้ขอ: ' + (item.ownerName || '-'), 'กลุ่มงาน: ' + (item.department || '-'), 'Meeting ID: ' + (item.meetingId || item.adminMeetingId || item.adminRoomId || '-'), 'Passcode: ' + (item.passcode || item.adminPasscode || '-'), 'Link: ' + (item.joinLink || item.adminLink || '-')].join('\n');
    var cal = CalendarApp.getDefaultCalendar(); var ev = null; if (existingEventId) { try { ev = cal.getEventById(existingEventId); } catch(ignore){} }
    if (ev) { ev.setTitle(title); ev.setTime(start,end); ev.setLocation(loc); ev.setDescription(desc); return ev.getId(); }
    return cal.createEvent(title, start, end, {location:loc, description:desc}).getId();
  } catch(e) { try { logError_('', 'syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_', e, item); } catch(ignore){} return 'Error'; }
}
function resyncAllGoogleCalendarV66(phone) {
  try { ensureCalendarIntegrationV66_(); var role = (getUserProfileByPhone_(phone)||{}).role || ''; if (role !== 'Super Admin' && role !== 'Admin') return {success:false,message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์'};
    var count = 0; var ss=getDB(); var sh=ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME); if (sh) { var data=sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var calCol=getColumnIndex_(sh,'Google Calendar Event ID'), syncCol=getColumnIndex_(sh,'Google Calendar Sync'), lastCol=getColumnIndex_(sh,'Last Calendar Sync'); for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++){ var obj={eventName:data[i][1],startTime:data[i][2],endTime:data[i][3],meetingLink:data[i][4],leadTime:data[i][8],notifyLeadTime:data[i][8],workStatus:data[i][9],details:data[i][15],location:data[i][16]}; var id=syncToGoogleCalendar(obj, calCol?data[i][calCol-1]:''); if(calCol&&id&&id!=='Error') sh.getRange(i+1,calCol).setValue(id); if(syncCol) sh.getRange(i+1,syncCol).setValue(id&&id!=='Error'?'Synced':'Error'); if(lastCol) sh.getRange(i+1,lastCol).setValue(new Date()); count++; }}
    var it=getITBookingSheet_(); var itData=it.getDataRange().getValues(); var c=getColumnIndex_(it,'Google Calendar Event ID'), cs=getColumnIndex_(it,'Google Calendar Sync'), cl=getColumnIndex_(it,'Last Calendar Sync'); for (var r=1;r<itData.length;r++){ var item=itBookingRowToObject_(itData[r]); var id2=syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_(item, c?itData[r][c-1]:''); if(c&&id2&&id2!=='Error') it.getRange(r+1,c).setValue(id2); if(cs) it.getRange(r+1,cs).setValue(id2&&id2!=='Error'?'Synced':'Error'); if(cl) it.getRange(r+1,cl).setValue(new Date()); count++; }
    try { createNotificationV732_(phone, 'ซิงก์ Google Calendar สำเร็จ ' + count + ' รายการ', 'System Calendar', 'GoogleCalendarV66', 'ปกติ', {module:'systemTools', action:'openSystemTools', createdBy:phone}); } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true,message:'ซิงก์ Google Calendar สำเร็จ ' + count + ' รายการ'};
  } catch(e){ return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}
