    getMeetingMinutesHistoryV2:true, saveMeetingMinutesV2:true, analyzeMeetingMinutesText:true, analyzeMeetingAudio:true, deleteMeetingMinutesV2:true,
    getITBookings:true, saveITBooking:true, updateITBookingStatus:true, deleteITBooking:true,
    getMySignature:true, saveMySignature:true, getMySignatures:true, saveMySignatures:true,
    getDigitalHealthHeadInfo:true, getTeamReportStatusYesterdayV68:true, getHeadOfDepartmentV68:true, bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68:true
  };
}


// v55 compatibility helper: System Health / Setup can call this safely.
// The real sheet/header upgrade logic lives in getITBookingSheet_().
function ensureITBookingSheet_() {
  return getITBookingSheet_();
}

function getSystemHealth() {
  try {
    var ss = getDB();
    var checks = [];
    var requiredSheets = [
      SHEET_NAME, SHEET_USERS, SHEET_RESOURCES, SHEET_REPORTS, SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS, SHEET_SETTINGS, SHEET_AUDIT,
      SHEET_WORK_STATUSES, SHEET_WORK_TAGS, SHEET_ERROR_LOGS, SHEET_ATTACHMENTS, SHEET_RECURRING,
      SHEET_SHARED_WORKSPACES, SHEET_LIVE_SURVEYS, SHEET_LIVE_RESPONSES, SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS, SHEET_MEETING_MINUTES
    ];
    requiredSheets.forEach(function(name) {
      var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
      checks.push({ name: 'ชีต ' + name, ok: !!sh, detail: sh ? Math.max(0, sh.getLastRow() - 1) + ' records' : 'ไม่พบชีต' });
    });
    var users = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (users) {
      try {
        ensureHeaders_(users, ['Signature Data', 'Signature List JSON'], '#d4edda');
        checks.push({ name: 'Users: Signature Data / Signature List JSON', ok: true, detail: 'พร้อมสำหรับลายเซ็นหลายชุด' });
      } catch (e) { checks.push({ name:'Users: Signature Columns', ok:false, detail:e.toString() }); }
    }
    var it = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS);
    if (it) {
      try {
        ensureITBookingSheet_();
        checks.push({ name: 'ITBookings: Digital Paper Fields', ok: true, detail: 'รองรับฟอร์มดิจิทัล/ผู้อนุญาต/ลายเซ็น' });
      } catch (e2) { checks.push({ name:'ITBookings: Digital Paper Fields', ok:false, detail:e2.toString() }); }
    }
    checks.push({ name: 'Gemini API Key', ok: !!getGeminiApiKey_(), detail: getGeminiApiKey_() ? 'พร้อมใช้งาน' : 'ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า' });
    checks.push({ name: 'Telegram Bot Token', ok: !!getTelegramBotToken_(), detail: getTelegramBotToken_() ? 'พร้อมใช้งาน' : 'ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า' });
    checks.push({ name: 'Web App URL', ok: !!getWebAppUrl(), detail: getWebAppUrl() || 'ยังไม่ได้ deploy' });
    var allOk = checks.every(function(c){ return c.ok; });
    return { success: true, allOk: allOk, checks: checks, systemStatus: getSetting_('SYSTEM_STATUS', 'ON'), version:'v55' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

function getMySignatures(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบชีต Users', signatures:[] };
    ensureHeaders_(sheet, ['Signature Data', 'Signature List JSON'], '#d4edda');
    var sigCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Signature Data');
    var listCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Signature List JSON');
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'') === target) {
        var raw = data[i][listCol-1] || '';
        var arr = [];
        if (raw) {
          try { arr = JSON.parse(raw); } catch(ignore) { arr = []; }
        }
        var single = data[i][sigCol-1] || '';
        if (!arr.length && single) arr = [{ name:'ลายเซ็นหลัก', data:single, isDefault:true }];
        arr = (arr || []).filter(function(x){ return x && x.data; }).slice(0,10).map(function(x,idx){ return { name:x.name || ('ลายเซ็น ' + (idx+1)), data:x.data, isDefault:!!x.isDefault }; });
        if (arr.length && !arr.some(function(x){return x.isDefault;})) arr[0].isDefault = true;
        return { success:true, signatures:arr, signatureData:(arr.find(function(x){return x.isDefault;}) || arr[0] || {}).data || '' };
      }
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้', signatures:[] };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString(), signatures:[] }; }
}

function saveMySignatures(phone, signatures) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบชีต Users' };
    ensureHeaders_(sheet, ['Signature Data', 'Signature List JSON'], '#d4edda');
    var sigCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Signature Data');
    var listCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Signature List JSON');
    var arr = Array.isArray(signatures) ? signatures : [];
    arr = arr.filter(function(x){ return x && x.data; }).slice(0,10).map(function(x,idx){ return { name:x.name || ('ลายเซ็น ' + (idx+1)), data:x.data, isDefault:!!x.isDefault }; });
    if (arr.length && !arr.some(function(x){return x.isDefault;})) arr[0].isDefault = true;
    var def = (arr.find(function(x){return x.isDefault;}) || arr[0] || {}).data || '';
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'') === target) {
        sheet.getRange(i+1, listCol).setValue(JSON.stringify(arr));
        sheet.getRange(i+1, sigCol).setValue(def);
        logAudit_(target, getUserNameByPhone_(target), 'SAVE_SIGNATURES', 'User', target, 'บันทึกลายเซ็นหลายชุด (' + arr.length + ' รายการ)', null, null);
        return { success:true, message:'บันทึกลายเซ็นแล้ว', signatures:arr };
      }
    }
    return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function getMySignature(phone) {
  var res = getMySignatures(phone);
  return { success:res.success, message:res.message || '', signatureData:res.signatureData || '' };
}

function saveMySignature(phone, signatureData) {
  if (!signatureData) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบข้อมูลลายเซ็น' };
  var res = getMySignatures(phone);
  var arr = res.success ? (res.signatures || []) : [];
  if (!arr.length) arr = [{ name:'ลายเซ็นหลัก', data:signatureData, isDefault:true }];
  else { arr[0].data = signatureData; arr[0].isDefault = true; }
  return saveMySignatures(phone, arr);
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheck() {
  var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
  var required = ['getErrorLogs','getWorkTags','getDashboardAnalytics','getNotificationCenter','getMySignatures','saveMySignatures','getITBookings','deleteITBooking'];
  var missing = required.filter(function(name){ return !allowed[name]; });
  return { success: missing.length === 0, version: 'v49-itservices-polish', required: required, missing: missing, message: missing.length ? ('Missing: ' + missing.join(', ')) : 'Bridge whitelist is ready.' };
}

// ========================= v50: Stability audit metadata =========================
function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheck() {
  var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
  var required = [
    'getErrorLogs','getWorkTags','getDashboardAnalytics','getNotificationCenter',
    'getMySignatures','saveMySignatures','getITBookings','deleteITBooking',
    'uploadFileToDrive','getSystemHealth'
  ];
  var missing = required.filter(function(name){ return !allowed[name]; });
  return {
    success: missing.length === 0,
    version: 'v50-stability-audit-hotfix',
    required: required,
    missing: missing,
    message: missing.length ? ('Missing: ' + missing.join(', ')) : 'Bridge whitelist is ready for v50.'
  };
}


// v59 UI overhaul: frontend visual refresh only.

// ========================= v60-v62: Help Center + Executive Dashboard + Formal Export =========================
function haosThaiDateShortV62_(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  var d = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(value || '');
  var m = ['ม.ค.','ก.พ.','มี.ค.','เม.ย.','พ.ค.','มิ.ย.','ก.ค.','ส.ค.','ก.ย.','ต.ค.','พ.ย.','ธ.ค.'];
  return d.getDate() + ' ' + m[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + (d.getFullYear()+543);
}
function haosDateKeyV62_(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  var d = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(value || '').slice(0,10);
  return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
}
function haosInDateRangeV62_(value, startDate, endDate) {
  if (!startDate && !endDate) return true;
  var key = haosDateKeyV62_(value);
  if (!key) return true;
  if (startDate && key < startDate) return false;
  if (endDate && key > endDate) return false;
  return true;
}
function haosCanSeeAllV62_(role) {
  return role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin';
}
function haosExportFolderV62_() {
  var name = 'HealthAssistant_FormalExports';
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(name);
}
function haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(name) {
  var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 1) return [];
  return sh.getDataRange().getValues();
}
function getExecutiveDashboardV61(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var todayKey = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    var now = new Date();
    var monthKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM');
    var cleanPhone = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    var canAll = haosCanSeeAllV62_(role);
    var isHead = role === 'Head';
    var reportValues = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_REPORTS);
    var usersValues = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_USERS);
    var scheduleValues = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_NAME);
    var bookingValues = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS);
    var minutesValues = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_MEETING_MINUTES);
    var surveyValues = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_LIVE_SURVEYS);

    var todayReports = 0, weekReports = 0;
    var reports7 = [];
    for (var i=6; i>=0; i--) {
      var dd = new Date(now.getTime() - i*24*60*60*1000);
      reports7.push({ key: Utilities.formatDate(dd,'GMT+7','yyyy-MM-dd'), label: haosThaiDateShortV62_(dd), count: 0 });
    }
    var teamSubmitted = {};
    for (var r=1; r<reportValues.length; r++) {
      var row = reportValues[r];
      var rowPhone = String(row[3] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var rowDept = String(row[5] || '');
      var visible = canAll || rowPhone === cleanPhone || (isHead && rowDept === department);
      if (!visible) continue;
      var dk = haosDateKeyV62_(row[1]);
      if (dk === todayKey) { todayReports++; teamSubmitted[rowPhone] = true; }
      var p = reports7.filter(function(x){return x.key === dk;})[0];
      if (p) { p.count++; weekReports++; }
    }
    var teamTotal = 0;
    if (usersValues.length > 1) {
      for (var u=1; u<usersValues.length; u++) {
        var uDept = String(usersValues[u][4] || '');
        var active = String(usersValues[u][10] || 'Active') !== 'Inactive';
        if (!active) continue;
        if (canAll || (isHead && uDept === department) || String(usersValues[u][0]||'').replace(/'/g,'') === cleanPhone) teamTotal++;
      }
    }
    var pendingSchedules = 0;
    for (var s=1; s<scheduleValues.length; s++) {
      var owner = String(scheduleValues[s][10] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var schedVisible = canAll || owner === cleanPhone || (isHead && String(scheduleValues[s][20] || '') === department);
      if (!schedVisible) continue;
      var ap = String(scheduleValues[s][12] || scheduleValues[s][9] || '');
      if (ap === 'Pending' || ap.indexOf('รอ') >= 0 || ap.indexOf('ดำเนิน') >= 0) pendingSchedules++;
    }
    var todayBookings = 0;
    for (var b=1; b<bookingValues.length; b++) {
      var bOwner = String(bookingValues[b][11] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var bDept = String(bookingValues[b][13] || '');
      var bVisible = canAll || bOwner === cleanPhone || (isHead && bDept === department) || (typeof isITServiceStaff_ === 'function' && isITServiceStaff_(cleanPhone));
      if (!bVisible) continue;
      if (haosDateKeyV62_(bookingValues[b][9]) === todayKey) todayBookings++;
    }
    var monthMinutes = 0;
    for (var m=1; m<minutesValues.length; m++) {
      var recPhone = String(minutesValues[m][8] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var recDept = String(minutesValues[m][10] || '');
      var minVisible = canAll || recPhone === cleanPhone || (isHead && recDept === department);
      if (!minVisible) continue;
      if (haosDateKeyV62_(minutesValues[m][2]).slice(0,7) === monthKey) monthMinutes++;
    }
    var activeSurveys = 0;
    for (var sv=1; sv<surveyValues.length; sv++) {
      var status = String(surveyValues[sv][8] || surveyValues[sv][7] || '');
      if (status !== 'Closed' && status !== 'ปิด') activeSurveys++;
    }
    var insights = [];
    insights.push('วันนี้มีการบันทึกรายงาน ' + todayReports + ' รายการ และช่วง 7 วันล่าสุดมี ' + weekReports + ' รายการ');
    insights.push('สถานะการส่งรายงานของทีมวันนี้: ' + Object.keys(teamSubmitted).length + '/' + teamTotal + ' คน');
    if (pendingSchedules > 0) insights.push('มีงาน/คำขอที่ควรติดตาม ' + pendingSchedules + ' รายการ');
    if (todayBookings > 0) insights.push('วันนี้มีคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom ' + todayBookings + ' รายการ');
    return { success:true, data:{
      generatedAtThai: haosThaiDateShortV62_(new Date()) + ' เวลา ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(),'GMT+7','HH.mm') + ' น.',
      scopeText: canAll ? 'ภาพรวมทั้งระบบ' : (isHead ? 'ภาพรวมกลุ่มงาน ' + department : 'ภาพรวมของฉัน'),
      todayReports: todayReports,
      teamSubmittedText: Object.keys(teamSubmitted).length + '/' + teamTotal,
      pendingSchedules: pendingSchedules,
      todayBookings: todayBookings,
      monthMinutes: monthMinutes,
      activeSurveys: activeSurveys,
      reports7Days: reports7,
      insights: insights
    }};
  } catch(e) { logError_(phone || '', 'getExecutiveDashboardV61', e, {role:role, department:department}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function haosRowsForFormalExportV62_(type, phone, role, department, startDate, endDate) {
  var cleanPhone = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g,'');
  var canAll = haosCanSeeAllV62_(role);
  var isHead = role === 'Head';
  var rows = [], headers = [];
  if (type === 'dailyReports' || type === 'teamReports') {
    headers = ['วันที่', 'เวลา', 'ชื่อ', 'กลุ่มงาน', 'สถานะ', 'รายละเอียด', 'หมายเหตุ', 'ไฟล์'];
    var data = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_REPORTS);
    for (var i=1; i<data.length; i++) {
      var row = data[i];
      var rowPhone = String(row[3] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var rowDept = String(row[5] || '');
      var visible = canAll || rowPhone === cleanPhone || (isHead && rowDept === department);
      if (type === 'dailyReports') visible = visible && (canAll || isHead || rowPhone === cleanPhone);
      if (!visible || !haosInDateRangeV62_(row[1], startDate, endDate)) continue;
      rows.push([haosThaiDateShortV62_(row[1]), row[2] || '', row[4] || '', rowDept, row[6] || '', row[7] || '', row[8] || '', row[9] || '']);
    }
  } else if (type === 'schedules') {
    headers = ['ชื่อเรื่อง', 'เริ่ม', 'สิ้นสุด', 'สถานที่', 'สถานะ', 'เจ้าของ', 'รายละเอียด', 'ไฟล์'];
    var sch = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_NAME);
    for (var s=1; s<sch.length; s++) {
      var owner = String(sch[s][10] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var visible2 = canAll || owner === cleanPhone;
      if (!visible2 || !haosInDateRangeV62_(sch[s][2], startDate, endDate)) continue;
      rows.push([sch[s][1] || '', haosThaiDateShortV62_(sch[s][2]), haosThaiDateShortV62_(sch[s][3]), sch[s][16] || '', sch[s][9] || sch[s][12] || '', owner, sch[s][15] || '', sch[s][14] || '']);
    }
  } else if (type === 'itBookings') {
    headers = ['เรื่อง', 'ประเภท', 'ห้อง/สถานที่', 'เริ่ม', 'สิ้นสุด', 'ผู้ขอ', 'กลุ่มงาน', 'สถานะ', 'Meeting ID/Link'];
    var bks = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS);
    for (var b=1; b<bks.length; b++) {
      var bOwner = String(bks[b][11] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var bDept = String(bks[b][13] || '');
      var visible3 = canAll || bOwner === cleanPhone || (isHead && bDept === department) || (typeof isITServiceStaff_ === 'function' && isITServiceStaff_(cleanPhone));
      if (!visible3 || !haosInDateRangeV62_(bks[b][9], startDate, endDate)) continue;
      rows.push([bks[b][1] || '', bks[b][2] || '', bks[b][8] || bks[b][7] || '', haosThaiDateShortV62_(bks[b][9]), haosThaiDateShortV62_(bks[b][10]), bks[b][12] || '', bDept, bks[b][14] || '', [bks[b][4], bks[b][5], bks[b][6], bks[b][18]].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')]);
    }
  } else if (type === 'meetingMinutes') {
    headers = ['วันที่', 'หัวข้อ', 'สถานที่', 'ประธาน', 'ผู้จด', 'กลุ่มงาน', 'Doc/PDF'];
    var mins = haosSafeSheetValuesV62_(SHEET_MEETING_MINUTES);
    for (var mm=1; mm<mins.length; mm++) {
      var mPhone = String(mins[mm][8] || '').replace(/'/g,'');
      var mDept = String(mins[mm][10] || '');
      var visible4 = canAll || mPhone === cleanPhone || (isHead && mDept === department);
      if (!visible4 || !haosInDateRangeV62_(mins[mm][2], startDate, endDate)) continue;
      rows.push([haosThaiDateShortV62_(mins[mm][2]), mins[mm][1] || '', mins[mm][3] || '', mins[mm][4] || '', mins[mm][9] || '', mDept, [mins[mm][11], mins[mm][12]].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')]);
    }
  } else {
    headers = ['ข้อมูล']; rows = [['ไม่รู้จักประเภทรายงาน']];
  }
  return { headers:headers, rows:rows };
}

function exportFormalPdfExcelV62(options, phone, role, department) {
  try {
    options = options || {};
    var type = options.type || 'dailyReports';
    var title = options.title || ({dailyReports:'รายงานการปฏิบัติงาน', teamReports:'รายละเอียดรายงานของคนในทีม', schedules:'รายงานตารางงานและนัดหมาย', itBookings:'รายงานการจองห้องประชุม / Zoom', meetingMinutes:'รายงานการประชุม'}[type] || 'รายงานจาก Health Assistant OS');
    var startDate = options.startDate || '';
    var endDate = options.endDate || '';
    var pack = haosRowsForFormalExportV62_(type, phone, role, department, startDate, endDate);
    if (!pack.rows.length) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบข้อมูลในช่วงวันที่/สิทธิ์ที่เลือก' };
    var tz = 'GMT+7';
    var stamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz, 'yyyyMMdd_HHmm');
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.create(title + '_' + stamp);
    var sh = ss.getSheets()[0];
    sh.setName('Report');
    sh.getRange(1,1).setValue(title).setFontSize(16).setFontWeight('bold');
    sh.getRange(2,1).setValue('ออกจากระบบ Health Assistant OS เมื่อ ' + haosThaiDateShortV62_(new Date()) + ' เวลา ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(),tz,'HH.mm') + ' น.');
    var rangeText = (startDate || endDate) ? ('ช่วงข้อมูล: ' + (startDate || '-') + ' ถึง ' + (endDate || '-')) : 'ช่วงข้อมูล: ทั้งหมดตามสิทธิ์';
    sh.getRange(3,1).setValue(rangeText);
    sh.getRange(5,1,1,pack.headers.length).setValues([pack.headers]).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#dbeafe');
    sh.getRange(6,1,pack.rows.length,pack.headers.length).setValues(pack.rows);
    sh.setFrozenRows(5);
    sh.autoResizeColumns(1, Math.min(pack.headers.length, 12));
    sh.getRange(1,1,sh.getLastRow(),pack.headers.length).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sh.getRange(1,1,1,pack.headers.length).mergeAcross();
    sh.getRange(2,1,1,pack.headers.length).mergeAcross();
    sh.getRange(3,1,1,pack.headers.length).mergeAcross();
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
    var folder = haosExportFolderV62_();
    folder.addFile(file);
    try { DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file); } catch(ignore) {}
    file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    var pdfFile = folder.createFile(file.getAs(MimeType.PDF).setName(title + '_' + stamp + '.pdf'));
    var excelFile = folder.createFile(file.getAs(MimeType.MICROSOFT_EXCEL).setName(title + '_' + stamp + '.xlsx'));
    pdfFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    excelFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    logAudit_(phone, getUserNameByPhone_(phone), 'EXPORT_FORMAL_REPORT', 'Report', type, 'ส่งออกรายงานแบบทางการ ' + title, null, {rows:pack.rows.length, pdf:pdfFile.getUrl(), excel:excelFile.getUrl()});
    return { success:true, message:'สร้างไฟล์สำเร็จ ' + pack.rows.length + ' รายการ', pdfUrl:pdfFile.getUrl(), excelUrl:excelFile.getUrl(), sheetUrl:file.getUrl(), rowCount:pack.rows.length };
  } catch(e) { logError_(phone || '', 'exportFormalPdfExcelV62', e, {options:options, role:role, department:department}); return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}

function seedPermissionMatrixV64_() {
  var sh = getPermissionMatrixSheetV64_();
  if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) return;
  var rows = defaultPermissionMatrixV64_().map(function(x){ return [x.key,x.name,!!x.User,!!x.Head,!!x.Admin,!!x['Super Admin'],new Date()]; });
  sh.getRange(2,1,rows.length,7).setValues(rows);
}
function getPermissionMatrixV64(adminPhone) {
  try { seedPermissionMatrixV64_(); var sh = getPermissionMatrixSheetV64_(); var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var out=[];
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) out.push({key:data[i][0], name:data[i][1], User:data[i][2]===true || data[i][2]==='TRUE', Head:data[i][3]===true || data[i][3]==='TRUE', Admin:data[i][4]===true || data[i][4]==='TRUE', 'Super Admin':data[i][5]===true || data[i][5]==='TRUE'});
    return {success:true, data:out};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString(), data:defaultPermissionMatrixV64_()}; }
}
function savePermissionMatrixV64(adminPhone, matrix) {
  try { var role = (getUserProfileByPhone_(adminPhone)||{}).role || ''; if (role !== 'Super Admin' && role !== 'Admin') return {success:false,message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์บันทึก Permission Matrix'};
    var sh = getPermissionMatrixSheetV64_(); sh.clearContents(); sh.getRange(1,1,1,7).setValues([['Feature Key','Feature Name','User','Head','Admin','Super Admin','Updated At']]);
    matrix = Array.isArray(matrix) ? matrix : defaultPermissionMatrixV64_();
    var rows = matrix.map(function(x){ return [x.key,x.name,!!x.User,!!x.Head,!!x.Admin,!!x['Super Admin'],new Date()]; });
    if (rows.length) sh.getRange(2,1,rows.length,7).setValues(rows);
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'SAVE_PERMISSION_MATRIX', 'Settings', 'PermissionMatrix', 'อัปเดต Permission Matrix', null, matrix);
    return {success:true,message:'บันทึก Permission Matrix แล้ว'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}

    names.forEach(function(n){ var sh = ss.getSheetByName(n); if (sh) payload.sheets[n] = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); });
    var folderIt = DriveApp.getFoldersByName('HealthAssistant_Backups'); var folder = folderIt.hasNext()?folderIt.next():DriveApp.createFolder('HealthAssistant_Backups');
    var id = 'BKP-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0,8).toUpperCase();

    var file = folder.createFile(blob); file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    getBackupLogSheetV63_().appendRow([id, file.getUrl(), "'"+String(phone||''), new Date(), 'JSON', note || 'Manual backup']);
    logAudit_(phone, getUserNameByPhone_(phone), 'CREATE_BACKUP', 'Backup', id, 'สร้าง Backup ระบบ', null, {url:file.getUrl()});

function restoreSystemBackupV63(phone, fileIdOrUrl, confirmText) {

    var json = DriveApp.getFileById(id[0]).getBlob().getDataAsString(); var payload = JSON.parse(json); if (!payload.sheets) return {success:false,message:'ไฟล์ Backup ไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    var ss = getDB(); Object.keys(payload.sheets).forEach(function(name){ var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name) || ss.insertSheet(name); sh.clearContents(); var vals = payload.sheets[name]; if (vals && vals.length) sh.getRange(1,1,vals.length,vals[0].length).setValues(vals); });
    logAudit_(phone, getUserNameByPhone_(phone), 'RESTORE_BACKUP', 'Backup', id[0], 'Restore backup', null, {file:fileIdOrUrl});
    return {success:true,message:'Restore สำเร็จ'};
  } catch(e){ logError_(phone||'', 'restoreSystemBackupV63', e, {fileIdOrUrl:fileIdOrUrl}); return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}

function deleteGoogleCalendarEventV66_(eventId) {
  try { if (!eventId || eventId === 'Error') return true; var ev = CalendarApp.getDefaultCalendar().getEventById(String(eventId)); if (ev) ev.deleteEvent(); return true; } catch(e) { return false; }
}
function syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_(item, existingEventId) {
  try {
    var start = new Date(item.rawStartTime || item.startTime); var end = new Date(item.rawEndTime || item.endTime);
    if (isNaN(start.getTime()) || isNaN(end.getTime())) return 'Error';
    var title = '[จอง Zoom/ห้อง] ' + (item.topic || '-');
    var loc = item.roomName || item.otherLocation || item.adminRoomId || item.joinLink || item.adminLink || '';
    var desc = ['ระบบจองห้องประชุม / Zoom', 'สถานะ: ' + (item.status || '-'), 'ประเภท: ' + (item.bookingType || '-'), 'ผู้ขอ: ' + (item.ownerName || '-'), 'กลุ่มงาน: ' + (item.department || '-'), 'Meeting ID: ' + (item.meetingId || item.adminMeetingId || item.adminRoomId || '-'), 'Passcode: ' + (item.passcode || item.adminPasscode || '-'), 'Link: ' + (item.joinLink || item.adminLink || '-')].join('\n');
    var cal = CalendarApp.getDefaultCalendar(); var ev = null; if (existingEventId) { try { ev = cal.getEventById(existingEventId); } catch(ignore){} }
    if (ev) { ev.setTitle(title); ev.setTime(start,end); ev.setLocation(loc); ev.setDescription(desc); return ev.getId(); }
    return cal.createEvent(title, start, end, {location:loc, description:desc}).getId();
  } catch(e) { try { logError_('', 'syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_', e, item); } catch(ignore){} return 'Error'; }
}
function resyncAllGoogleCalendarV66(phone) {
  try { ensureCalendarIntegrationV66_(); var role = (getUserProfileByPhone_(phone)||{}).role || ''; if (role !== 'Super Admin' && role !== 'Admin') return {success:false,message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์'};
    var count = 0; var ss=getDB(); var sh=ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME); if (sh) { var data=sh.getDataRange().getValues(); var calCol=getColumnIndex_(sh,'Google Calendar Event ID'), syncCol=getColumnIndex_(sh,'Google Calendar Sync'), lastCol=getColumnIndex_(sh,'Last Calendar Sync'); for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++){ var obj={eventName:data[i][1],startTime:data[i][2],endTime:data[i][3],meetingLink:data[i][4],leadTime:data[i][8],notifyLeadTime:data[i][8],workStatus:data[i][9],details:data[i][15],location:data[i][16]}; var id=syncToGoogleCalendar(obj, calCol?data[i][calCol-1]:''); if(calCol&&id&&id!=='Error') sh.getRange(i+1,calCol).setValue(id); if(syncCol) sh.getRange(i+1,syncCol).setValue(id&&id!=='Error'?'Synced':'Error'); if(lastCol) sh.getRange(i+1,lastCol).setValue(new Date()); count++; }}
    var it=getITBookingSheet_(); var itData=it.getDataRange().getValues(); var c=getColumnIndex_(it,'Google Calendar Event ID'), cs=getColumnIndex_(it,'Google Calendar Sync'), cl=getColumnIndex_(it,'Last Calendar Sync'); for (var r=1;r<itData.length;r++){ var item=itBookingRowToObject_(itData[r]); var id2=syncITBookingToGoogleCalendarV66_(item, c?itData[r][c-1]:''); if(c&&id2&&id2!=='Error') it.getRange(r+1,c).setValue(id2); if(cs) it.getRange(r+1,cs).setValue(id2&&id2!=='Error'?'Synced':'Error'); if(cl) it.getRange(r+1,cl).setValue(new Date()); count++; }
    return {success:true,message:'ซิงก์ Google Calendar สำเร็จ ' + count + ' รายการ'};

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheck() {
  var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
  var required = ['getExecutiveDashboardV61','exportFormalPdfExcelV62','getMySignatures','saveMySignatures','getITBookings','deleteITBooking','getSystemHealth','createSystemBackupV63','getPermissionMatrixV64','getBrandingConfigV65','resyncAllGoogleCalendarV66','getTeamReportStatusYesterdayV68','getHeadOfDepartmentV68','bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68'];
  var missing = required.filter(function(name){ return !allowed[name]; });
  return { success: missing.length === 0, version: 'v68-admin-report-filter-polish' , required: required, missing: missing, message: missing.length ? ('Missing: ' + missing.join(', ')) : 'Bridge whitelist is ready for v63-v68.' };
}

function getSystemHealth() {
  try {
    var ss = getDB();
    var checks = [];
    var requiredSheets = [SHEET_NAME, SHEET_USERS, SHEET_RESOURCES, SHEET_REPORTS, SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS, SHEET_SETTINGS, SHEET_AUDIT, SHEET_WORK_STATUSES, SHEET_WORK_TAGS, SHEET_ERROR_LOGS, SHEET_ATTACHMENTS, SHEET_RECURRING, SHEET_WORKSPACES, SHEET_LIVE_SURVEYS, SHEET_LIVE_RESPONSES, SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS, SHEET_MEETING_MINUTES, SHEET_BACKUP_LOGS, SHEET_PERMISSION_MATRIX];
    requiredSheets.forEach(function(name){ var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); checks.push({ name:'ชีต ' + name, ok:!!sh, detail:sh ? Math.max(0, sh.getLastRow()-1) + ' records' : 'ไม่พบชีต' }); });
    var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
    ['getExecutiveDashboardV61','exportFormalPdfExcelV62','getMySignatures','saveMySignatures','createSystemBackupV63','getPermissionMatrixV64','getBrandingConfigV65','resyncAllGoogleCalendarV66'].forEach(function(fn){ checks.push({ name:'Bridge: ' + fn, ok:!!allowed[fn], detail:allowed[fn] ? 'พร้อมใช้งาน' : 'ยังไม่อยู่ใน whitelist' }); });
    try { var users = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS); if (users) { ensureHeaders_(users, ['Signature Data','Signature List JSON'], '#d4edda'); checks.push({ name:'Users: Signature Columns', ok:true, detail:'พร้อมใช้หลายลายเซ็น' }); } } catch(e1){ checks.push({ name:'Users: Signature Columns', ok:false, detail:e1.toString() }); }
    try { if (typeof ensureITBookingSheet_ === 'function') { ensureITBookingSheet_(); checks.push({ name:'ITBookings: Digital Paper Fields', ok:true, detail:'พร้อมใช้งาน' }); } } catch(e2){ checks.push({ name:'ITBookings: Digital Paper Fields', ok:false, detail:e2.toString() }); }
    checks.push({ name:'Formal Export Folder', ok:!!haosExportFolderV62_(), detail:'HealthAssistant_FormalExports' });
    checks.push({ name:'Gemini API Key', ok:!!getGeminiApiKey_(), detail:getGeminiApiKey_() ? 'พร้อมใช้งาน' : 'ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า' });
    checks.push({ name:'Telegram Bot Token', ok:!!getTelegramBotToken_(), detail:getTelegramBotToken_() ? 'พร้อมใช้งาน' : 'ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า' });
    checks.push({ name:'Web App URL', ok:!!getWebAppUrl(), detail:getWebAppUrl() || 'ยังไม่ได้ deploy' });
    return { success:true, allOk:checks.every(function(c){return c.ok;}), checks:checks, systemStatus:getSetting_('SYSTEM_STATUS','ON'), version:'v68' };
  } catch(e) { return { success:false, message:e.toString() }; }
}


// =========================================================
// HAOS v68 BACKEND HOTFIX PATCH
// วิธีใช้: วางท้าย Code.gs เวอร์ชันล่าสุด แล้ว Deploy เป็น New version
// ใช้สำหรับปรับรายงานทีมเป็น “เมื่อวาน”, approval helper และ System Health marker
// =========================================================
function haosV68DateKey_(d) {
  try { return Utilities.formatDate(d instanceof Date ? d : new Date(d), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd'); } catch(e) { return ''; }
}
function haosV68YesterdayKey_() {
  var d = new Date();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
}
function getTeamReportStatusYesterdayV68(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var ss = getDB();
    var usersSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS || 'Users');
    var reportsSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_REPORTS || 'DailyReports');
    var yKey = haosV68YesterdayKey_();
    var users = usersSheet ? usersSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    var reports = reportsSheet ? reportsSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    var canAll = role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin';
    var team = [];
    for (var i=1;i<users.length;i++) {
      var uPhone = String(users[i][0]||'').replace(/'/g,'');
      var name = users[i][2] || users[i][1] || uPhone;
      var dept = users[i][4] || '';
      var active = String(users[i][10] || users[i][9] || 'Active') !== 'Inactive';
      if (!active) continue;
      if (canAll || dept === department || uPhone === String(phone||'').replace(/'/g,'')) team.push({phone:uPhone,name:name,department:dept,submitted:false});
    }
    var submitted = {};
    for (var r=1;r<reports.length;r++) {
      if (haosV68DateKey_(reports[r][1]) !== yKey) continue;
      submitted[String(reports[r][3]||'').replace(/'/g,'')] = true;
    }
    team.forEach(function(t){ t.submitted = !!submitted[t.phone]; });
    return {success:true, dateKey:yKey, mode:'yesterday', data:team, submittedCount:team.filter(function(x){return x.submitted;}).length, total:team.length};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[]}; }
}
function getHeadOfDepartmentV68(department) {
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS || 'Users');
    if (!sh) return {success:false,message:'ไม่พบชีต Users'};
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
      var role = String(data[i][7] || data[i][8] || '');
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      if (dept === department && (role === 'Head' || role === 'หัวหน้า')) {
        return {success:true, phone:String(data[i][0]||'').replace(/'/g,''), name:data[i][2]||'', department:dept};
      }
    }
    return {success:false,message:'ไม่พบหัวหน้ากลุ่มงาน'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}
function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68() {
  return {success:true, version:'v68', message:'v68 hotfix patch loaded', functions:['getTeamReportStatusYesterdayV68','getHeadOfDepartmentV68']};
}



// =========================================================
// HAOS v68 FULL MERGE: override getTeamReports to use yesterday for team status.
// This intentionally keeps the detailed report table as all reports, but changes the status cards from today to yesterday.
// =========================================================
function getTeamReports(userDepartment, userRole) {
  try {
    var ss = getDB();
    var sheetUsers = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    var sheetReports = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_REPORTS);
    if (!sheetReports || !sheetUsers) return { success: true, stats: [], reports: [], isHead: false, mode: 'yesterday' };
    var usersData = sheetUsers.getDataRange().getValues();
    var reportsData = sheetReports.getDataRange().getValues();
    var yKey = haosV68YesterdayKey_ ? haosV68YesterdayKey_() : Utilities.formatDate(new Date(new Date().getTime() - 24*60*60*1000), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    var teamMembers = [];
    var fullReports = [];
    var cleanUserDept = String(userDepartment || '').replace(/[0-9\s]/g, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < usersData.length; i++) {
      var dept = String(usersData[i][4] || '');
      if (dept.indexOf(cleanUserDept) !== -1 || dept === userDepartment || userRole === 'Super Admin' || userRole === 'Admin') {
        var active = String(usersData[i][10] || usersData[i][9] || 'Active') !== 'Inactive';
        if (!active) continue;
        teamMembers.push({
          phone: String(usersData[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, ''),
          name: usersData[i][2] || '',
          department: dept,
          submittedToday: false,
          submittedYesterday: false
        });
      }
    }
    for (var j = 1; j < reportsData.length; j++) {
      var repDept = String(reportsData[j][5] || '');
      var deptMatch = repDept.indexOf(cleanUserDept) !== -1 || repDept === userDepartment || userRole === 'Super Admin' || userRole === 'Admin';
      if (!deptMatch) continue;
      var d = reportsData[j][1];
      var repDateStr = (d instanceof Date) ? Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd') : String(d || '').slice(0,10);
      if (userRole === 'Head' || userRole === 'Super Admin' || userRole === 'Admin') {
        fullReports.push({
          id: reportsData[j][0],
          name: reportsData[j][4],
          date: repDateStr,
          time: reportsData[j][2],
          leaveType: reportsData[j][6],
          details: reportsData[j][7],
          remarks: reportsData[j][8],
          fileUrl: reportsData[j][9]
        });
      }
      if (repDateStr === yKey) {
        var repPhone = String(reportsData[j][3] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
        for (var k = 0; k < teamMembers.length; k++) {
          if (teamMembers[k].phone === repPhone) {
            teamMembers[k].submittedToday = true; // keep frontend compatibility
            teamMembers[k].submittedYesterday = true;
            teamMembers[k].leaveType = reportsData[j][6];
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return {
      success: true,
      stats: teamMembers,
      reports: fullReports.reverse(),
      isHead: (userRole === 'Head' || userRole === 'Super Admin' || userRole === 'Admin'),
      mode: 'yesterday',
      dateKey: yKey
    };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}



// =========================================================
// HAOS v68.2 HOTFIX: strict department boundary for team report status
// แก้สมาชิกต่างกลุ่มงานหลุดเข้ามาใน "สถานะของกลุ่มงานเมื่อวาน"
// Root cause เดิมใช้ indexOf(cleanDept) และ Admin/Super Admin ถูกดึงทุกกลุ่มงาน
// =========================================================
function haosV682NormalizeDept_(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '')
    .replace(/[๐-๙]/g, function(ch){ return String('๐๑๒๓๔๕๖๗๘๙'.indexOf(ch)); })
    .replace(/^\s*[0-9]+\s*[.)\-:]*\s*/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

function haosV682SameDept_(a, b) {
  var na = haosV682NormalizeDept_(a);
  var nb = haosV682NormalizeDept_(b);
  return !!na && !!nb && na === nb;
}

function getTeamReports(userDepartment, userRole) {
  try {
    var ss = getDB();
    var sheetUsers = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    var sheetReports = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_REPORTS);
    if (!sheetReports || !sheetUsers) return { success: true, stats: [], reports: [], isHead: false, mode: 'yesterday', version: 'v68.2' };

    var usersData = sheetUsers.getDataRange().getValues();
    var reportsData = sheetReports.getDataRange().getValues();
    var yKey = (typeof haosV68YesterdayKey_ === 'function')
      ? haosV68YesterdayKey_()
      : Utilities.formatDate(new Date(new Date().getTime() - 24*60*60*1000), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd');

    var teamMembers = [];
    var fullReports = [];
    var targetDept = String(userDepartment || '');
    var targetDeptNorm = haosV682NormalizeDept_(targetDept);
    if (!targetDeptNorm) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบข้อมูลกลุ่มงานของผู้ใช้ จึงไม่สามารถโหลดสถานะกลุ่มงานได้', stats: [], reports: [], isHead: false, mode: 'yesterday', version: 'v68.2' };

    var memberPhoneMap = {};
    for (var i = 1; i < usersData.length; i++) {
      var rowDept = String(usersData[i][4] || '');
      if (!haosV682SameDept_(rowDept, targetDept)) continue;
      var activeRaw = String(usersData[i][10] || usersData[i][9] || 'Active');
      var active = !/^(inactive|ปิดใช้งาน|ระงับ|disabled)$/i.test(activeRaw.trim());
      if (!active) continue;
      var phone = String(usersData[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      if (!phone || memberPhoneMap[phone]) continue;
      memberPhoneMap[phone] = true;
      teamMembers.push({
        phone: phone,
        name: usersData[i][2] || '',
        department: rowDept,
        submittedToday: false,
        submittedYesterday: false
      });
    }

    for (var j = 1; j < reportsData.length; j++) {
      var repDept = String(reportsData[j][5] || '');
      var repPhone = String(reportsData[j][3] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      // เช็คทั้ง department และ phone ต้องเป็นสมาชิกทีมจริง ปิดช่องข้อมูลรายงานที่บันทึก dept เพี้ยน/คล้ายกัน
      if (!haosV682SameDept_(repDept, targetDept) && !memberPhoneMap[repPhone]) continue;
      if (repPhone && !memberPhoneMap[repPhone]) continue;

      var d = reportsData[j][1];
      var repDateStr = (d instanceof Date) ? Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd') : String(d || '').slice(0,10);
      if (userRole === 'Head' || userRole === 'Super Admin' || userRole === 'Admin' || userRole === 'หัวหน้า') {
        fullReports.push({
          id: reportsData[j][0],
          name: reportsData[j][4],
          date: repDateStr,
          time: reportsData[j][2],
          leaveType: reportsData[j][6],
          details: reportsData[j][7],
          remarks: reportsData[j][8],
          fileUrl: reportsData[j][9]
        });
      }
      if (repDateStr === yKey && repPhone) {
        for (var k = 0; k < teamMembers.length; k++) {
          if (teamMembers[k].phone === repPhone) {
            teamMembers[k].submittedToday = true; // frontend compatibility
            teamMembers[k].submittedYesterday = true;
            teamMembers[k].leaveType = reportsData[j][6];
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      stats: teamMembers,
      reports: fullReports.reverse(),
      isHead: (userRole === 'Head' || userRole === 'Super Admin' || userRole === 'Admin' || userRole === 'หัวหน้า'),
      mode: 'yesterday',
      dateKey: yKey,
      department: targetDept,
      departmentKey: targetDeptNorm,
      version: 'v68.2'
    };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success: false, message: e.toString(), version: 'v68.2' };
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68() {
  return {success:true, version:'v68.2', message:'v68.2 schedule/team hotfix loaded', functions:['getTeamReports','getTeamReportStatusYesterdayV68','getHeadOfDepartmentV68']};
}


// ==========================================================
// HAOS v68.4 helper patch: strict department user/head utilities
// Frontend still uses getAllUsers() and getHeadOfDepartmentV68() for backward compatibility.
// This override makes head lookup stricter and whitespace-safe.
// ==========================================================
function haosNormalizeDeptV684_(v) {
  return String(v || '').replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
}

function getHeadOfDepartmentV68(department) {
  try {
    var target = haosNormalizeDeptV684_(department);
    if (!target) return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', department:'', message:'ยังไม่ได้ระบุกลุ่มงาน'};
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', department:'', message:'ยังไม่มีข้อมูลผู้ใช้'};
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var phone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim();
      var name = data[i][2] || '';
      var dept = data[i][4] || '';
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '').trim();
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active').trim();
      if (!phone || status === 'Inactive') continue;
      if (haosNormalizeDeptV684_(dept) === target && (role === 'Head' || role === 'หัวหน้า' || role === 'Department Head')) {
        return {success:true, phone:phone, name:name, department:dept};
      }
    }
    return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', department:department || '', message:'ยังไม่ได้กำหนดหัวหน้ากลุ่ม'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68() {
  return {success:true, version:'v68.4', message:'v68.4 report/schedule UX stability patch loaded', functions:['getTeamReports','getTeamReportStatusYesterdayV68','getHeadOfDepartmentV68','bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68']};
}

// ==========================================================
// HAOS v68.5 helper patch: robust department head lookup
// - Supports role names: Head, Department Head, หัวหน้า, หัวหน้ากลุ่ม, หัวหน้ากลุ่มงาน
// - Fallback: position contains หัวหน้า/head in same department
// - Keeps approval optional; frontend sends empty approverPhone when toggle is OFF
// ==========================================================
function haosNormalizeDeptV685_(v) {
  return String(v || '')
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF]/g, '')
    .replace(/[๐-๙]/g, function(ch){ return String('๐๑๒๓๔๕๖๗๘๙'.indexOf(ch)); })
    .replace(/^\s*[0-9]+\s*[.)\-:]*\s*/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}
function haosIsHeadRoleOrPositionV685_(role, position) {
  var r = String(role || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  var p = String(position || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  return r === 'head' || r === 'department head' || r === 'หัวหน้า' || r === 'หัวหน้ากลุ่ม' || r === 'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงาน' ||
         r.indexOf('head') !== -1 || r.indexOf('หัวหน้า') !== -1 ||
         p.indexOf('head') !== -1 || p.indexOf('หัวหน้า') !== -1;
}
function getHeadOfDepartmentV68(department) {
  try {
    var target = haosNormalizeDeptV685_(department);
    if (!target) return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', position:'', department:'', message:'ยังไม่ได้ระบุกลุ่มงาน'};
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', position:'', department:'', message:'ยังไม่มีข้อมูลผู้ใช้'};
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var fallback = null;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var phone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim();
      var name = data[i][2] || '';
      var position = data[i][3] || '';
      var dept = data[i][4] || '';
      var role = data[i][7] || '';
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active').trim();
      if (!phone || /^(inactive|disabled|ปิดใช้งาน|ระงับ)$/i.test(status)) continue;
      if (haosNormalizeDeptV685_(dept) !== target) continue;
      if (haosIsHeadRoleOrPositionV685_(role, position)) {
        return {success:true, phone:phone, name:name, position:position, role:role, department:dept, version:'v68.5'};
      }
      if (!fallback && String(role || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('admin') === -1) {
        fallback = {phone:phone, name:name, position:position, role:role, department:dept};
      }
    }
    // Do not auto-pick fallback as approver; just return a clear message.
    return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', position:'', department:department || '', message:'ยังไม่ได้กำหนดหัวหน้ากลุ่ม', fallback:fallback, version:'v68.5'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, phone:'', name:'', position:'', department:department || '', message:e.toString(), version:'v68.5'};
  }
}
function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68() {
  return {success:true, version:'v68.5', message:'v68.5 report/schedule real fix loaded', functions:['getTeamReports','getHeadOfDepartmentV68','bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV68']};
}


// ==========================================================
// HAOS v68.6 backend helpers
// - Personal Telegram Chat ID per user
// - Public IT Booking save for public.html booking form
// - Bridge whitelist override includes new helpers
// ==========================================================
function saveMyTelegramChatIdV686(phone, chatId) {
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sh) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบชีต Users'};
    ensureHeaders_(sh, ['Telegram Chat ID'], '#d4edda');
    var col = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Telegram Chat ID');
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim() === target) {
        sh.getRange(i + 1, col).setValue(String(chatId || '').trim());
        logAudit_(target, data[i][2] || '', 'UPDATE_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID', 'User', target, 'อัปเดต Telegram Chat ID ส่วนตัว', null, {chatId: chatId ? 'set' : 'empty'});
        return {success:true, message:'บันทึก Telegram Chat ID แล้ว'};
      }
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function testMyTelegramChatIdV686(chatId) {
  try {
    if (!chatId) return {success:false, message:'ยังไม่ได้ระบุ Chat ID'};
    sendTelegram('✅ ทดสอบการแจ้งเตือนส่วนตัวจาก Health Assistant OS สำเร็จ', String(chatId).trim());
    return {success:true, message:'ส่งข้อความทดสอบแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function savePublicITBookingV686(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    if (!data.topic || !data.startTime || !data.endTime || !data.ownerName || !data.department || !data.position) {
      return {success:false, message:'กรุณากรอกเรื่อง ชื่อผู้ขอ ตำแหน่ง กลุ่มงาน และช่วงเวลาให้ครบ'};
    }
    data.ownerPhone = String(data.ownerPhone || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim();
    data.ownerName = data.ownerName || '';
    data.department = data.department || '';
    data.status = data.status || 'รอเจ้าหน้าที่';
    var result = saveITBooking(data);
    if (result && result.success) {
      result.publicGuest = true;
      result.suggestRegister = true;
      result.message = (result.message || 'บันทึกคำขอแล้ว') + ' หากต้องการติดตามสถานะและใช้ระบบครั้งต่อไปได้สะดวก สามารถนำข้อมูลนี้ไปสมัครสมาชิกได้';
    }
    return result;
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

// Override profile update to preserve existing behavior and optional personal Telegram.
var updateUserProfileV686_old_ = (typeof updateUserProfile === 'function') ? updateUserProfile : null;
function updateUserProfile(data) {
  var res = updateUserProfileV686_old_ ? updateUserProfileV686_old_(data) : {success:true, message:'อัปเดตข้อมูลส่วนตัวเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  try {
    if (data && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(data, 'telegramChatId')) saveMyTelegramChatIdV686(data.phone, data.telegramChatId);
  } catch(ignore) {}
  return res;
}

function savePublicITBookingV687(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    data.topic = String(data.topic || '').trim();
    data.ownerName = String(data.ownerName || '').trim();
    data.position = String(data.position || '').trim();
    data.department = String(data.department || '').trim();
    data.startTime = data.startTime || '';
    data.endTime = data.endTime || '';
    if (!data.topic || !data.ownerName || !data.position || !data.department || !data.startTime || !data.endTime) {
      return {success:false, message:'กรุณากรอกเรื่อง ชื่อผู้ขอ ตำแหน่ง กลุ่มงาน และช่วงเวลาให้ครบ'};
    }
    data.ownerPhone = String(data.ownerPhone || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim();
    data.bookingType = data.bookingType || 'Host';
    data.status = data.status || 'รอเจ้าหน้าที่';
    data.ownerPosition = data.ownerPosition || data.position || '';
    data.otherLocation = data.otherLocation || data.location || '';
    var result = saveITBooking(data);
    if (result && result.success) {
      result.publicGuest = true;
      result.suggestRegister = true;
      result.version = 'v68.7';
      result.message = (result.message || 'บันทึกคำขอจองเรียบร้อยแล้ว') + ' ระบบแนะนำให้สมัครสมาชิกเพื่อใช้ติดตามสถานะในครั้งถัดไป';
    }
    return result;
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString(), version:'v68.7'}; }
}

// ==========================================================
// HAOS v70: IT Asset Registry + Software License + Repair Tickets MVP
// ==========================================================
function haosV70Now_() { return new Date(); }
function haosV70Id_(prefix) { return prefix + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(); }
function haosV70Phone_(v) { return String(v || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim(); }
function haosV70Date_(v) {
  return haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(v);
}
function haosV7116AssetDateHint_(asset) {
  if (Array.isArray(asset)) return asset.join(' ');
  asset = asset || {};
  return [
    asset.assetCode, asset.assetNumber, asset.assetName, asset.category,
    asset.smartCategory, asset.spec, asset.fiscalYear
  ].filter(function(v){ return v !== null && v !== undefined && String(v).trim() !== ''; }).join(' ');
}

function haosV7115InferAssetBeYear_(fiscalYear, assetNumber) {
  var text = String(fiscalYear || '').trim();
  var m = text.match(/(\d{4})/);
  if (m) {
    var y4 = Number(m[1]);
    if (y4 >= 2400) return y4;
    if (y4 >= 2000 && y4 < 2400) return y4 + 543;
  }
  m = text.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{2})(?:\D|$)/);
  if (m) return 2500 + Number(m[1]);
  var hint = String(assetNumber || '');
  m = hint.match(/\/\s*(\d{2})(?:\D|$)/);
  if (m) return 2500 + Number(m[1]);
  m = hint.match(/(?:ปี|พ\.ศ\.|พศ|งบ|งปม)\s*(\d{2})(?:\D|$)/i);
  if (m) return 2500 + Number(m[1]);
  return 0;
}
function haosV7115CorrectAssetDateByFiscal_(dateValue, fiscalYear, assetNumber) {
  var normalized = haosV70Date_(dateValue);
  if (!String(normalized || '').match(/^\d{4}-\d{1,2}-\d{1,2}$/)) {
    var rawText = String(dateValue || normalized || '').trim();
    var thaiMonths = {
      'มกราคม':1,'ม.ค.':1,'มค':1,'กุมภาพันธ์':2,'ก.พ.':2,'กพ':2,'มีนาคม':3,'มี.ค.':3,'มีค':3,
      'เมษายน':4,'เม.ย.':4,'เมย':4,'พฤษภาคม':5,'พ.ค.':5,'พค':5,'มิถุนายน':6,'มิ.ย.':6,'มิย':6,
      'กรกฎาคม':7,'ก.ค.':7,'กค':7,'สิงหาคม':8,'ส.ค.':8,'สค':8,'กันยายน':9,'ก.ย.':9,'กย':9,
      'ตุลาคม':10,'ต.ค.':10,'ตค':10,'พฤศจิกายน':11,'พ.ย.':11,'พย':11,'ธันวาคม':12,'ธ.ค.':12,'ธค':12
    };
    var tm = rawText.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*(?:•|\s)\s*([ก-๙.]+)\s*(?:•|\s)\s*(\d{4})/);
    if (tm && thaiMonths[tm[2]]) {
      var ty = Number(tm[3]);
      if (ty >= 2400) ty -= 543;
      normalized = haosV7124Ymd_(ty, thaiMonths[tm[2]], Number(tm[1]));
    }
  }
  var m = String(normalized || '').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})$/);
  if (!m) return normalized;
  var ceYear = Number(m[1]);
  var beYear = ceYear < 2400 ? ceYear + 543 : ceYear;
  var inferredBeYear = haosV7115InferAssetBeYear_(fiscalYear, assetNumber);
  if (inferredBeYear >= 2500 && beYear >= 2400 && Math.abs(inferredBeYear - beYear) >= 20) {
    return haosV7124Ymd_(inferredBeYear, Number(m[2]), Number(m[3]));
  }
  return normalized;
}
function haosV70DateTime_(v) {
  if (!v) return '';
  var d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(v || '');
  return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');
}
function haosV70Num_(v) {
  var n = Number(v || 0);
  return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n;
}
function haosV70FindRowById_(sheet, id, idCol) {
  var values = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
    if (String(values[i][idCol - 1] || '') === String(id || '')) return i + 1;
  }
  return -1;
}

function itAssetRowToObjectV70_(r) {
  var purchaseDate = haosV70Date_(r[9]);
  purchaseDate = haosV7115CorrectAssetDateByFiscal_(purchaseDate, r[10], haosV7116AssetDateHint_([r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], r[8], r[10]]));
  var condition = haosV7124DisplayCondition_(r[21], r[20]);
  if (!condition) condition = 'ดี';
  return {
    assetId:r[0]||'', assetCode:r[1]||'', assetNumber:r[2]||'', assetName:r[3]||'', category:r[4]||'',
    brand:r[5]||'', model:r[6]||'', serialNumber:r[7]||'', spec:r[8]||'', purchaseDate:purchaseDate,
    fiscalYear:r[10]||'', budgetSource:r[11]||'', price:r[12]||'', vendor:r[13]||'', warrantyStart:haosV70Date_(r[14]),
    warrantyEnd:haosV70Date_(r[15]), ownerDepartment:r[16]||'', currentUserName:r[17]||'',
    currentUserPhone:haosV70Phone_(r[18]), location:r[19]||'', status:r[20]||'ใช้งาน', condition:condition,
    lastCheckedAt:haosV70Date_(r[22]), remark:haosV7124CleanRemark_(r[23]), photoUrl:r[24]||'', createdAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[25]),
    updatedAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[26]), createdBy:r[27]||'', updatedBy:r[28]||''
  };
}
function itSoftwareRowToObjectV70_(r) {
  return { softwareId:r[0]||'', softwareName:r[1]||'', vendor:r[2]||'', category:r[3]||'', licenseType:r[4]||'', version:r[5]||'', description:r[6]||'', defaultRenewalDays:r[7]||60, createdAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[8]), updatedAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[9]) };
}
function itLicenseRowToObjectV70_(r) {
  return {
    licenseId:r[0]||'', softwareId:r[1]||'', licenseName:r[2]||'', licenseKeyMasked:r[3]||'', seatTotal:haosV70Num_(r[4]),
    seatUsed:haosV70Num_(r[5]), purchaseDate:haosV70Date_(r[6]), startDate:haosV70Date_(r[7]), expiryDate:haosV70Date_(r[8]),
    renewalAlertDays:haosV70Num_(r[9]||60), licenseStatus:r[10]||'Active', vendor:r[11]||'', contractNumber:r[12]||'',
    poNumber:r[13]||'', price:r[14]||'', attachmentUrl:r[15]||'', remark:r[16]||'', createdAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[17]), updatedAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[18])
  };
}
function itAssetSoftwareMapRowToObjectV70_(r) {
  return {
    mapId:r[0]||'', assetId:r[1]||'', softwareId:r[2]||'', licenseId:r[3]||'', assignedToUserName:r[4]||'',
    assignedToUserPhone:haosV70Phone_(r[5]), installDate:haosV70Date_(r[6]), deviceName:r[7]||'', activationStatus:r[8]||'Activated',
    removedDate:haosV70Date_(r[9]), note:r[10]||'', createdAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[11]), updatedAt:haosV70DateTime_(r[12])
  };
}

    ticketId:r[0]||'', ticketNo:r[1]||'', assetId:r[2]||'', assetCode:r[3]||'', assetName:r[4]||'', requesterName:r[5]||'',
    requesterPhone:haosV70Phone_(r[6]), requesterDepartment:r[7]||'', location:r[8]||'', problemCategory:r[9]||'Other',

function getAllITRepairTicketsV70_() {
  var sh = getITRepairTicketSheetV70_(), data = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) if (data[i][0]) out.push(itRepairTicketRowToObjectV70_(data[i]));
  out.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.createdAt || b.reportedAt).localeCompare(String(a.createdAt || a.reportedAt)); });
  return out;
}

function buildITLicenseAlertsV70_(licenses, software) {
  var swMap = {};
  (software || []).forEach(function(s){ swMap[s.softwareId] = s; });
  var today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  var alerts = [];
  (licenses || []).forEach(function(l) {
    if (!l.expiryDate) return;
    var exp = new Date(l.expiryDate);
    if (isNaN(exp.getTime())) return;
    exp.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    var daysLeft = Math.ceil((exp.getTime() - today.getTime()) / 86400000);
    var threshold = Number(l.renewalAlertDays || 60);
    if (daysLeft <= threshold) {
      alerts.push({
        licenseId:l.licenseId, softwareId:l.softwareId, softwareName:(swMap[l.softwareId] || {}).softwareName || '',
        licenseName:l.licenseName || '', expiryDate:l.expiryDate, daysLeft:daysLeft,
        level: daysLeft < 0 ? 'expired' : (daysLeft <= 7 ? 'critical' : (daysLeft <= 30 ? 'warning' : 'notice'))
      });
    }
  });
  alerts.sort(function(a,b){ return a.daysLeft - b.daysLeft; });
  return alerts;
}

function buildITAssetDashboardV70_(assets, licenses, tickets, alerts) {
  var byStatus = {}, byCategory = {};
  (assets || []).forEach(function(a){
    byStatus[a.status || 'ไม่ระบุ'] = (byStatus[a.status || 'ไม่ระบุ'] || 0) + 1;
    byCategory[a.category || 'ไม่ระบุ'] = (byCategory[a.category || 'ไม่ระบุ'] || 0) + 1;
  });
  var openStatuses = {'New':true,'Assigned':true,'In Progress':true,'Waiting Parts':true,'Waiting Vendor':true};
  var openTickets = (tickets || []).filter(function(t){ return openStatuses[t.status] || ['ใหม่','รับงานแล้ว','กำลังดำเนินการ','รออะไหล่','รอผู้ขาย'].indexOf(t.status) !== -1; }).length;
  return {
    totalAssets:(assets || []).length,
    activeAssets:(assets || []).filter(function(a){ return String(a.status || '').indexOf('ใช้งาน') !== -1 || a.status === 'Active'; }).length,
    repairAssets:(assets || []).filter(function(a){ return String(a.status || '').indexOf('ซ่อม') !== -1 || a.status === 'Repair'; }).length,
    totalLicenses:(licenses || []).length,
    expiringLicenses:(alerts || []).filter(function(a){ return a.daysLeft >= 0 && a.daysLeft <= 60; }).length,
    expiredLicenses:(alerts || []).filter(function(a){ return a.daysLeft < 0; }).length,
    openTickets:openTickets,
    byStatus:byStatus,
    byCategory:byCategory
  };
}

function getITAssetModuleDataV70(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var assets = getAllITAssetsV70_();
    var software = getAllITSoftwareV70_();
    var licenses = getAllITLicensesV70_();
    var maps = getAllITAssetSoftwareMapsV70_();
    var tickets = getAllITRepairTicketsV70_();
    var alerts = buildITLicenseAlertsV70_(licenses, software);
    return { success:true, assets:assets, software:software, licenses:licenses, maps:maps, tickets:tickets, alerts:alerts, dashboard:buildITAssetDashboardV70_(assets, licenses, tickets, alerts), version:'v70' };
  } catch(e) {
    logError_(phone || '', 'getITAssetModuleDataV70', e, null);
    return { success:false, message:e.toString(), assets:[], software:[], licenses:[], maps:[], tickets:[], alerts:[] };
  }
}

function saveITAssetV70(asset, actor) {
  try {
    asset = asset || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!asset.assetName) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อทรัพย์สิน'};
    var sh = getITAssetSheetV70_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var id = asset.assetId || haosV70Id_('AST');
    var row = [
      id, asset.assetCode || '', asset.assetNumber || '', asset.assetName || '', asset.category || '', asset.brand || '', asset.model || '',
      asset.serialNumber || '', asset.spec || '', asset.purchaseDate || '', asset.fiscalYear || '', asset.budgetSource || '', asset.price || '',
      asset.vendor || '', asset.warrantyStart || '', asset.warrantyEnd || '', asset.ownerDepartment || '', asset.currentUserName || '',
      "'" + haosV70Phone_(asset.currentUserPhone), asset.location || '', asset.status || 'ใช้งาน', asset.condition || 'ดี', asset.lastCheckedAt || '',
      asset.remark || '', asset.photoUrl || '', asset.createdAt || now, now, asset.createdBy || actor.name || actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.phone || ''
    ];
    var found = asset.assetId ? haosV70FindRowById_(sh, asset.assetId, 1) : -1;
    if (found > 1) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', found > 1 ? 'UPDATE_IT_ASSET' : 'CREATE_IT_ASSET', 'ITAsset', id, asset.assetName || '', null, asset);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกทรัพย์สิน IT แล้ว', assetId:id};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function saveITSoftwareLicenseV70(payload, actor) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!payload.softwareName) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อซอฟต์แวร์'};
    var swSh = getITSoftwareSheetV70_();
    var licSh = getITLicenseSheetV70_();
    var mapSh = getITAssetSoftwareMapSheetV70_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var softwareId = payload.softwareId || haosV70Id_('SW');
    if (!payload.softwareId) {
      swSh.appendRow([softwareId, payload.softwareName || '', payload.softwareVendor || payload.vendor || '', payload.softwareCategory || '', payload.licenseType || 'Subscription', payload.version || '', payload.description || '', payload.defaultRenewalDays || 60, now, now]);
    }
    var licenseId = payload.licenseId || haosV70Id_('LIC');
    if (!payload.licenseId) {
      licSh.appendRow([licenseId, softwareId, payload.licenseName || payload.softwareName || '', payload.licenseKeyMasked || '', payload.seatTotal || 1, payload.seatUsed || 0, payload.purchaseDate || '', payload.startDate || '', payload.expiryDate || '', payload.renewalAlertDays || 60, payload.licenseStatus || 'Active', payload.vendor || payload.softwareVendor || '', payload.contractNumber || '', payload.poNumber || '', payload.price || '', payload.attachmentUrl || '', payload.remark || '', now, now]);
    }
    var mapId = '';
    if (payload.assetId) {
      mapId = haosV70Id_('MAP');
      mapSh.appendRow([mapId, payload.assetId || '', softwareId, licenseId, payload.assignedToUserName || '', "'" + haosV70Phone_(payload.assignedToUserPhone), payload.installDate || '', payload.deviceName || '', payload.activationStatus || 'Activated', '', payload.note || '', now, now]);
    }
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', 'SAVE_IT_LICENSE', 'ITLicense', licenseId, payload.softwareName || '', null, payload);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกซอฟต์แวร์/License แล้ว', softwareId:softwareId, licenseId:licenseId, mapId:mapId};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function saveITRepairTicketV70(ticket, actor) {
  try {
    ticket = ticket || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!ticket.problemTitle) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุหัวข้อปัญหา'};
    var sh = getITRepairTicketSheetV70_();
    var assets = getAllITAssetsV70_();
    var asset = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) if (assets[i].assetId === ticket.assetId) asset = assets[i];
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var id = ticket.ticketId || haosV70Id_('RPT');
    var no = ticket.ticketNo || ('IT-' + Utilities.formatDate(now, 'GMT+7', 'yyyyMMdd') + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0,4).toUpperCase());
    var row = [
      id, no, ticket.assetId || '', ticket.assetCode || (asset ? asset.assetCode : ''), ticket.assetName || (asset ? asset.assetName : ''),
      ticket.requesterName || actor.name || '', "'" + haosV70Phone_(ticket.requesterPhone || actor.phone), ticket.requesterDepartment || actor.department || '',
      ticket.location || (asset ? asset.location : ''), ticket.problemCategory || 'Hardware', ticket.problemTitle || '', ticket.problemDetail || '',
      ticket.priority || 'Normal', ticket.impact || '', ticket.status || 'New', ticket.assignedTo || '', ticket.reportedAt || now,
      '', '', '', ticket.slaDueAt || '', '', ticket.repairCost || '', ticket.attachmentUrl || '', ticket.createdAt || now, now
    ];
    var found = ticket.ticketId ? haosV70FindRowById_(sh, ticket.ticketId, 1) : -1;
    if (found > 1) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    if (!ticket.ticketId) {
      try {
        createNotificationsV732_(getITServiceStaffPhones_(), 'มีใบแจ้งซ่อม IT ใหม่: ' + no + ' - ' + ticket.problemTitle, 'IT Repair', id, ticket.priority || 'Normal', {module:'itRepair', action:'openITRepair', createdBy:ticket.requesterPhone || actor.phone});
      } catch(ignore) {}
    }
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', found > 1 ? 'UPDATE_IT_REPAIR' : 'CREATE_IT_REPAIR', 'ITRepairTicket', id, no + ' ' + ticket.problemTitle, null, ticket);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกใบแจ้งซ่อมแล้ว', ticketId:id, ticketNo:no};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function updateITRepairTicketStatusV70(ticketId, status, note, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var sh = getITRepairTicketSheetV70_();
    var row = haosV70FindRowById_(sh, ticketId, 1);
    if (row <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อม'};
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var before = itRepairTicketRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, 26).getValues()[0]);
    sh.getRange(row, 15).setValue(status || 'In Progress');
    sh.getRange(row, 26).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Assigned') sh.getRange(row, 18).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Resolved') sh.getRange(row, 19).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Closed') sh.getRange(row, 20).setValue(now);
    if (note) sh.getRange(row, 22).setValue(note);
    getITRepairUpdateSheetV70_().appendRow([haosV70Id_('UPD'), ticketId, status || '', note || '', actor.name || actor.phone || '', now, '', false]);
    try {
      createNotificationV732_(before.requesterPhone, 'สถานะใบแจ้งซ่อม IT ' + (before.ticketNo || ticketId) + ': ' + (status || 'In Progress') + (note ? ' | ' + note : ''), 'IT Repair Status', ticketId, before.priority || 'Normal', {module:'itRepair', action:'openITRepair', createdBy:actor.phone});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตสถานะใบแจ้งซ่อมแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function runITLicenseExpiryAlertV70(actorPhone) {
  try {
    var data = getITAssetModuleDataV70(actorPhone || '', '', '');
    if (!data.success) return data;
    var alerts = data.alerts || [];
    try {
      getITServiceStaffPhones_().forEach(function(staffPhone) {
        alerts.slice(0, 20).forEach(function(a) {
          createNotificationV732_(staffPhone, 'License ใกล้หมดอายุ: ' + (a.softwareName || a.licenseName || a.licenseId) + ' เหลือ ' + a.daysLeft + ' วัน', 'IT License', a.licenseId, a.level, {module:'itAsset', action:'openITAsset', createdBy:actorPhone});
        });
      });
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, message:'ตรวจและสร้างการแจ้งเตือน License แล้ว', count:alerts.length};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function getPublicITServicesDashboardV703(kind) {
  try {
    kind = String(kind || 'itservices');
    var data = getITAssetModuleDataV70('', '', '');
    if (!data || !data.success) return data || {success:false, message:'โหลดข้อมูล IT Services ไม่สำเร็จ'};
    var assets = data.assets || [];
    var licenses = data.licenses || [];
    var software = data.software || [];
    var tickets = data.tickets || [];
    var alerts = data.alerts || [];
    function countBy(list, key) {
      var out = {};
      (list || []).forEach(function(item) {
        var value = String(item[key] || 'ไม่ระบุ');
        out[value] = (out[value] || 0) + 1;
      });
      return out;
    }
    function safeDate(v) {
      if (!v) return '';
      try {
        if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === '[object Date]') {
          return Utilities.formatDate(v, 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');
        }
      } catch(ignore) {}
      return String(v);
    }
    var assetLicenseCount = {};
    (data.maps || []).forEach(function(m) {
      if (String(m.activationStatus || '') !== 'Removed') assetLicenseCount[m.assetId] = (assetLicenseCount[m.assetId] || 0) + 1;
    });
    var safeAssets = assets.slice(0, 18).map(function(a) {
      return {
        assetName: a.assetName || '',
        category: a.category || '',
        ownerDepartment: a.ownerDepartment || '',
        location: a.location || '',
        status: a.status || '',
        condition: a.condition || '',
        warrantyEnd: safeDate(a.warrantyEnd),
        licenseCount: assetLicenseCount[a.assetId] || 0
      };
    });
    var safeAlerts = alerts.slice(0, 12).map(function(a) {
      return {
        softwareName: a.softwareName || '',
        licenseName: a.licenseName || '',
        expiryDate: safeDate(a.expiryDate),
        daysLeft: a.daysLeft,
        level: a.level || ''
      };
    });
    var safeTickets = tickets.slice(0, 15).map(function(t) {
      return {
        ticketNo: t.ticketNo || '',
        assetName: t.assetName || '',
        requesterDepartment: t.requesterDepartment || '',
        problemCategory: t.problemCategory || '',
        priority: t.priority || '',
        status: t.status || '',
        reportedAt: safeDate(t.reportedAt || t.createdAt)
      };
    });
    return {
      success: true,
      kind: kind,
      generatedAt: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
      dashboard: data.dashboard || {},
      counts: {
        assetStatus: countBy(assets, 'status'),
        assetCategory: countBy(assets, 'category'),
        licenseStatus: countBy(licenses, 'licenseStatus'),
        ticketStatus: countBy(tickets, 'status'),
        ticketPriority: countBy(tickets, 'priority')
      },
      assets: safeAssets,
      softwareCount: software.length,
      alerts: safeAlerts,
      tickets: safeTickets,
      publicNotice: 'ข้อมูลสาธารณะนี้แสดงเฉพาะภาพรวมและข้อมูลที่ไม่อ่อนไหว ต้องเข้าสู่ระบบก่อนใช้งานหรือจัดการข้อมูล'
    };
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}
