  return {success:true, version:'v70.3-public-it-services', functions:['getPublicITServicesDashboardV703']};
}

// ========================= v70.112-v70.113: Public IT Asset detailed read-only dashboard + category report =========================
function haosV7112PublicAssetLogin_(auth) {
  auth = auth || {};
  var phone = '';
  try {
    phone = typeof haosV729CleanPhone_ === 'function' ? haosV729CleanPhone_(auth.phone) : haosV70Phone_(auth.phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
  } catch(ignore) {
    phone = haosV70Phone_(auth.phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
  }
  var pin = String(auth.pin || '').replace(/^'/, '').trim();
  if (!phone || !pin) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุเบอร์โทรและ PIN'};
  var found = null;
  try {
    found = typeof haosV729FindUser_ === 'function' ? haosV729FindUser_(phone) : null;
  } catch(ignore2) {}
  if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบบัญชีผู้ใช้งานนี้'};
  if (String(found.values[1] || '').replace(/^'/, '') !== pin) {
    return {success:false, message:'เบอร์โทรหรือ PIN ไม่ถูกต้อง'};
  }
  var status = String(found.values[10] || 'Active');
  if (status === 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'บัญชียังรออนุมัติจากผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
  if (status === 'Rejected') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ไม่ได้รับอนุมัติ'};
  if (status === 'Inactive') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ถูกปิดใช้งาน'};

  var base = {
    phone: phone,
    fullName: found.values[2] || '',
    position: found.values[3] || '',
    department: found.values[4] || '',
    email: found.values[5] || '',
    role: found.values[7] || 'User',
    accountRole: found.values[7] || 'User',
    accountStatus: status
  };
  try {
    if (typeof haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_ === 'function' && typeof haosV729ChooseActiveProfileFromList_ === 'function' && typeof haosV729ApplyProfileToUserData_ === 'function') {
      var profiles = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(phone).filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
      var activeProfile = haosV729ChooseActiveProfileFromList_(found, profiles);
      base = haosV729ApplyProfileToUserData_(base, activeProfile, profiles);
    }
  } catch(ignore3) {}
  return {success:true, user:base};
}

function haosV7112CountBy_(list, key) {
  var out = {};
  (list || []).forEach(function(item) {
    var value = String(item[key] || 'ไม่ระบุ');
    out[value] = (out[value] || 0) + 1;
  });
  return out;
}

function haosV7112Unique_(list, key) {
  var seen = {}, out = [];
  (list || []).forEach(function(item) {
    var value = String(item[key] || '').trim();
    if (value && !seen[value]) {
      seen[value] = true;
      out.push(value);
    }
  });
  out.sort();
  return out;
}

function haosV7112BuildAssetLicenseDetails_(software, licenses, maps) {
  var swById = {}, licById = {}, byAsset = {};
  (software || []).forEach(function(s){ swById[s.softwareId] = s; });
  (licenses || []).forEach(function(l){ licById[l.licenseId] = l; });
  (maps || []).forEach(function(m) {
    if (!m.assetId || String(m.activationStatus || '') === 'Removed') return;
    var sw = swById[m.softwareId] || {};
    var lic = licById[m.licenseId] || {};
    if (!byAsset[m.assetId]) byAsset[m.assetId] = [];
    byAsset[m.assetId].push({
      mapId: m.mapId || '',
      softwareId: m.softwareId || '',
      softwareName: sw.softwareName || '',
      softwareVendor: sw.vendor || '',
      softwareCategory: sw.category || '',
      version: sw.version || '',
      licenseId: m.licenseId || '',
      licenseName: lic.licenseName || '',
      licenseType: sw.licenseType || '',
      licenseStatus: lic.licenseStatus || '',
      licenseKeyMasked: lic.licenseKeyMasked || '',
      seatTotal: lic.seatTotal || 0,
      seatUsed: lic.seatUsed || 0,
      expiryDate: lic.expiryDate || '',
      assignedToUserName: m.assignedToUserName || '',
      assignedToUserPhone: m.assignedToUserPhone || '',
      installDate: m.installDate || '',
      deviceName: m.deviceName || '',
      activationStatus: m.activationStatus || '',
      note: m.note || ''
    });
  });
  return byAsset;
}

function haosV7113SmartAssetCategory_(asset) {
  asset = asset || {};
  if (asset.manualCategory) return String(asset.manualCategory || '').trim();
  var text = [
    asset.assetName,
    asset.category,
    asset.smartCategory,
    asset.manualCategory,
    asset.autoSmartCategory,
    asset.brand,
    asset.model,
    asset.spec,
    asset.remark,
    asset.sourceSheet
  ].join(' ').toLowerCase();
  if (/software|license|program|โปรแกรม|ซอฟต์|ซอฟ|ลิขสิทธิ์/.test(text)) return 'Software / License';
  if (/server|storage|nas|san|backup storage|เครื่องแม่ข่าย|จัดเก็บข้อมูล/.test(text)) return 'Server / Storage';
  if (/tablet|ipad|แท็บเล็ต|แท๊บเล็ต/.test(text)) return 'Tablet / iPad';
  if (/notebook|laptop|โน้ตบุ๊ก|โน๊ตบุ๊ก|คอมพิวเตอร์ชนิดพกพา|พกพา/.test(text)) return 'Notebook';
  if (/printer|print|เครื่องพิมพ์|ปริ้น|เลเซอร์|inkjet|dot matrix/.test(text)) return 'เครื่องพิมพ์ / Printer';
  if (/ups|เครื่องสำรองไฟ|สำรองไฟ|ไฟฟ้าสำรอง|battery backup/.test(text)) return 'UPS / ไฟฟ้าสำรอง';
  if (/switch|router|access point|wireless|wifi|wi-fi|network|hub|lan|สวิตช์|เราเตอร์|เครือข่าย|กระจายสัญญาณ/.test(text)) return 'อุปกรณ์เครือข่าย';
  if (/scanner|scan|สแกนเนอร์|เครื่องสแกน|สแกน/.test(text)) return 'Scanner';
  if (/monitor|display|จอภาพ|จอคอม|จอรับสัญญาณภาพ/.test(text)) return 'จอภาพ / Monitor';
  if (/projector|โปรเจคเตอร์|โปรเจ็กเตอร์|เครื่องฉาย/.test(text)) return 'Projector';
  if (/desktop|pc|computer|คอมพิวเตอร์|all in one|aio|ตั้งโต๊ะ/.test(text)) return 'คอมพิวเตอร์ตั้งโต๊ะ / PC';
  return 'อื่นๆ / ต้องจัดหมวดเพิ่ม';
}

function haosV7113SumAssetValue_(assets) {
  return (assets || []).reduce(function(sum, a) {
    var raw = String((a && a.price) || '').replace(/,/g, '').trim();
    var value = Number(raw);
    return sum + (isNaN(value) ? 0 : value);
  }, 0);
}

function haosV7113TopCountList_(countMap, limit) {
  return Object.keys(countMap || {}).map(function(key) {
    return {name:key, count:countMap[key] || 0};
  }).sort(function(a, b) {
    return (b.count || 0) - (a.count || 0) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name));
  }).slice(0, limit || 8);
}

function haosV7113BuildExecutiveSummary_(assets, licenses, alerts, tickets) {
  var categoryCounts = haosV7112CountBy_(assets || [], 'smartCategory');
  var rawCategoryCounts = haosV7112CountBy_(assets || [], 'category');
  var statusCounts = haosV7112CountBy_(assets || [], 'status');
  var conditionCounts = haosV7112CountBy_(assets || [], 'condition');
  var departmentCounts = haosV7112CountBy_(assets || [], 'ownerDepartment');
  var needsAttention = (assets || []).filter(function(a) {
    var s = [a.status, a.condition].join(' ').toLowerCase();
    return /ชำรุด|เสีย|เสื่อม|ไม่ใช้|ซ่อม|inactive|repair|broken/.test(s);
  }).length;
  return {
    totalAssets:(assets || []).length,
    totalLicenses:(licenses || []).length,
    totalValue:haosV7113SumAssetValue_(assets || []),
    needsAttention:needsAttention,
    alertCount:(alerts || []).length,
    ticketCount:(tickets || []).length,
    topCategories:haosV7113TopCountList_(categoryCounts, 10),
    rawCategories:haosV7113TopCountList_(rawCategoryCounts, 10),
    topDepartments:haosV7113TopCountList_(departmentCounts, 8),
    status:statusCounts,
    condition:conditionCounts
  };
}

function getPublicITAssetDetailedDashboardV7112(auth) {
  try {
    var login = haosV7112PublicAssetLogin_(auth || {});
    if (!login.success) return login;
    var user = login.user || {};
    var data = getITAssetModuleDataV70(user.phone || '', user.role || '', user.department || '');
    if (!data || !data.success) return data || {success:false, message:'โหลดข้อมูลทะเบียนทรัพย์สินไม่สำเร็จ'};

    var assets = data.assets || [];
    var software = data.software || [];
    var licenses = data.licenses || [];
    var maps = data.maps || [];
    var alerts = data.alerts || [];
    var tickets = data.tickets || [];
    var limit = Math.max(50, Math.min(Number((auth || {}).limit || 1200), 2000));
    var licenseByAsset = haosV7112BuildAssetLicenseDetails_(software, licenses, maps);
    var detailedAssets = assets.slice(0, limit).map(function(a) {
      var linkedLicenses = licenseByAsset[a.assetId] || [];
      var smartCategory = haosV7113SmartAssetCategory_(a);
      var displayAsset = {};
      for (var k in a) displayAsset[k] = a[k];
      displayAsset.smartCategory = smartCategory;
      return {
        assetId:a.assetId || '',
        assetCode:a.assetCode || '',
        assetNumber:a.assetNumber || '',
        assetName:a.assetName || '',
        category:a.category || '',
        smartCategory:smartCategory,
        brand:a.brand || '',
        model:a.model || '',
        serialNumber:a.serialNumber || '',
        spec:a.spec || '',
        purchaseDate:haosV7115CorrectAssetDateByFiscal_(a.purchaseDate, a.fiscalYear, haosV7116AssetDateHint_(a)) || '',
        fiscalYear:a.fiscalYear || '',
        budgetSource:a.budgetSource || '',
        price:a.price || '',
        vendor:a.vendor || '',
        warrantyStart:a.warrantyStart || '',
        warrantyEnd:a.warrantyEnd || '',
        ownerDepartment:a.ownerDepartment || '',
        currentUserName:a.currentUserName || '',
        currentUserPhone:a.currentUserPhone || '',
        location:a.location || '',
        status:a.status || '',
        condition:haosV7124DisplayCondition_(a.condition || '', a.status || '') || 'ดี',
        lastCheckedAt:a.lastCheckedAt || '',
        remark:haosV7124CleanRemark_(a.remark || ''),
        photoUrl:a.photoUrl || '',
        updatedAt:a.updatedAt || '',
        licenseCount:haosV7124LicenseCountFallback_(displayAsset, linkedLicenses.length),
        licenses:linkedLicenses
      };
    });
    var canManage = false;
    try {
      canManage = typeof haosV711ActorCanManageIT_ === 'function' ? haosV711ActorCanManageIT_({phone:user.phone, role:user.role, department:user.department}) : false;
    } catch(ignore) {}
    try {
      logAudit_(user.phone || '', user.fullName || '', 'VIEW_PUBLIC_IT_ASSET_DETAIL', 'ITAssets', 'PUBLIC_READONLY', 'Public detailed IT Asset dashboard read-only login', null, {assetCount:detailedAssets.length, canManage:canManage});
    } catch(ignore2) {}
    return {
      success:true,
      version:'v70.124-itasset-master-data-normalize',
      readOnly:!canManage,
      editable:canManage,
      changeRequestEnabled:true,
      canManageIT:canManage,
      truncated:assets.length > detailedAssets.length,
      generatedAt:Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
      user:{phone:user.phone || '', fullName:user.fullName || '', role:user.role || '', department:user.department || '', position:user.position || '', email:user.email || ''},
      dashboard:data.dashboard || buildITAssetDashboardV70_(assets, licenses, tickets, alerts),
      counts:{
        assetStatus:haosV7112CountBy_(assets, 'status'),
        assetCategory:haosV7112CountBy_(assets, 'category'),
        assetSmartCategory:haosV7112CountBy_(detailedAssets, 'smartCategory'),
        assetDepartment:haosV7112CountBy_(assets, 'ownerDepartment'),
        assetLocation:haosV7112CountBy_(assets, 'location'),
        licenseStatus:haosV7112CountBy_(licenses, 'licenseStatus')
      },
      executiveSummary:haosV7113BuildExecutiveSummary_(detailedAssets, licenses, alerts, tickets),
      filters:{
        categories:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'category'),
        smartCategories:haosV7112Unique_(detailedAssets, 'smartCategory'),
        statuses:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'status'),
        departments:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'ownerDepartment'),
        locations:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'location')
      },
      assets:detailedAssets,
      software:software,
      licenses:licenses,
      alerts:alerts,
      tickets:tickets.slice(0, 80)
    };
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

// ========================= v70.11: final IT Services permission overrides =========================
function haosV711IsDigitalHealthDept_(dept) {
  dept = String(dept || '');
  return dept.indexOf('สุขภาพดิจิทัล') !== -1 || /digital\s*health/i.test(dept);
}

function haosV711IsITManagerRole_(role) {
  role = String(role || '');
  return role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin' || /super\s*admin|admin/i.test(role);
}

function haosV711ITNoManageResponse_() {
  return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะสมาชิกกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล หรือผู้ดูแลระบบเท่านั้นที่มีสิทธิ์จัดการ/แก้ไขรายการนี้'};
}

function isITServiceStaff_(phone) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return false;
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rowPhone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      if (rowPhone !== target) continue;
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (status === 'Inactive') return false;
      return haosV711IsITManagerRole_(role) || haosV711IsDigitalHealthDept_(dept);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

function getITServiceStaffPhones_() {
  var list = [];
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return list;
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var phone = String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g, '');
      var dept = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (phone && status !== 'Inactive' && (haosV711IsITManagerRole_(role) || haosV711IsDigitalHealthDept_(dept))) {
        if (list.indexOf(phone) === -1) list.push(phone);
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return list;
}

function haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor) {
  actor = actor || {};
  if (haosV711IsITManagerRole_(actor.role) || haosV711IsDigitalHealthDept_(actor.department)) return true;
  return actor.phone ? isITServiceStaff_(actor.phone) : false;
}

function getITAssetModuleDataV70(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var assets = getAllITAssetsV70_();
    assets = assets.map(function(a) {
      a = a || {};
      if (!a.smartCategory) a.smartCategory = haosV7113SmartAssetCategory_(a);
      return a;
    });
    var software = getAllITSoftwareV70_();
    var licenses = getAllITLicensesV70_();
    var maps = getAllITAssetSoftwareMapsV70_();
    var tickets = getAllITRepairTicketsV70_();
    var alerts = buildITLicenseAlertsV70_(licenses, software);
    var canManage = haosV711ActorCanManageIT_({phone:phone || '', role:role || '', department:department || ''});
    var executiveSummary = haosV7113BuildExecutiveSummary_(assets, licenses, alerts, tickets);
    return {
      success:true,
      assets:assets,
      software:software,
      licenses:licenses,
      maps:maps,
      tickets:tickets,
      alerts:alerts,
      dashboard:buildITAssetDashboardV70_(assets, licenses, tickets, alerts),
      executiveSummary:executiveSummary,
      filters:{
        smartCategories:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'smartCategory'),
        categories:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'category'),
        statuses:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'status'),
        departments:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'ownerDepartment'),
        locations:haosV7112Unique_(assets, 'location')
      },
      permissions:{canAddAsset:true, canCreateRepair:true, canManageIT:canManage},
      version:'v70.116-itasset-date-main-public-alignment'
    };
  } catch(e) {
    logError_(phone || '', 'getITAssetModuleDataV70', e, null);
    return { success:false, message:e.toString(), assets:[], software:[], licenses:[], maps:[], tickets:[], alerts:[] };
  }
}

function saveITAssetV70(asset, actor) {
  try {
    asset = asset || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!asset.assetName) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อทรัพย์สิน'};
    if (asset.assetId && !haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var sh = getITAssetSheetV70_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var found = asset.assetId ? haosV70FindRowById_(sh, asset.assetId, 1) : -1;
    if (asset.assetId && found <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบทรัพย์สินที่ต้องการแก้ไข'};
    var old = found > 1 ? itAssetRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, 29).getValues()[0]) : {};
    var id = asset.assetId || haosV70Id_('AST');
    var purchaseDate = haosV7115CorrectAssetDateByFiscal_(
      haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.purchaseDate || old.purchaseDate || ''),
      asset.fiscalYear || old.fiscalYear || '',
      haosV7116AssetDateHint_(asset)
    );
    var warrantyStart = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.warrantyStart || old.warrantyStart || '');
    var warrantyEnd = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.warrantyEnd || old.warrantyEnd || '');
    var lastCheckedAt = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(asset.lastCheckedAt || old.lastCheckedAt || '');
    var condition = haosV7124DisplayCondition_(asset.condition || old.condition || '', asset.status || old.status || '');
    if (!condition) condition = 'ดี';
    var remark = haosV7124CleanRemark_(asset.remark || old.remark || '');
    var row = [
      id, asset.assetCode || '', asset.assetNumber || '', asset.assetName || '', asset.category || '', asset.brand || '', asset.model || '',
      asset.serialNumber || '', asset.spec || '', purchaseDate || '', asset.fiscalYear || old.fiscalYear || '', asset.budgetSource || '', asset.price || '',
      asset.vendor || '', warrantyStart || '', warrantyEnd || '', asset.ownerDepartment || actor.department || '', asset.currentUserName || '',
      "'" + haosV70Phone_(asset.currentUserPhone), asset.location || '', asset.status || 'ใช้งาน', condition, lastCheckedAt || '',
      remark || '', asset.photoUrl || '', old.createdAt || asset.createdAt || now, now, old.createdBy || asset.createdBy || actor.name || actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.phone || ''
    ];
    if (found > 1) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', found > 1 ? 'UPDATE_IT_ASSET' : 'CREATE_IT_ASSET', 'ITAsset', id, asset.assetName || '', null, asset);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกทรัพย์สิน IT แล้ว', assetId:id};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function saveITSoftwareLicenseV70(payload, actor) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    if (!payload.softwareName) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อซอฟต์แวร์'};
    var swSh = getITSoftwareSheetV70_();
    var licSh = getITLicenseSheetV70_();
    var mapSh = getITAssetSoftwareMapSheetV70_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var softwareId = payload.softwareId || haosV70Id_('SW');
    if (!payload.softwareId) {
      swSh.appendRow([softwareId, payload.softwareName || '', payload.softwareVendor || payload.vendor || '', payload.softwareCategory || '', payload.licenseType || 'Subscription', payload.version || '', payload.description || '', payload.defaultRenewalDays || 60, now, now]);
    }
    var licenseId = payload.licenseId || haosV70Id_('LIC');
    if (!payload.licenseId) {
      licSh.appendRow([licenseId, softwareId, payload.licenseName || payload.softwareName || '', payload.licenseKeyMasked || '', payload.seatTotal || 1, payload.seatUsed || 0, payload.purchaseDate || '', payload.startDate || '', payload.expiryDate || '', payload.renewalAlertDays || 60, payload.licenseStatus || 'Active', payload.vendor || payload.softwareVendor || '', payload.contractNumber || '', payload.poNumber || '', payload.price || '', payload.attachmentUrl || '', payload.remark || '', now, now]);
    }
    var mapId = '';
    if (payload.assetId) {
      mapId = haosV70Id_('MAP');
      mapSh.appendRow([mapId, payload.assetId || '', softwareId, licenseId, payload.assignedToUserName || '', "'" + haosV70Phone_(payload.assignedToUserPhone), payload.installDate || '', payload.deviceName || '', payload.activationStatus || 'Activated', '', payload.note || '', now, now]);
    }
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', 'SAVE_IT_LICENSE', 'ITLicense', licenseId, payload.softwareName || '', null, payload);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกซอฟต์แวร์/License แล้ว', softwareId:softwareId, licenseId:licenseId, mapId:mapId};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function saveITRepairTicketV70(ticket, actor) {
  try {
    ticket = ticket || {}; actor = actor || {};
    if (!ticket.problemTitle) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุหัวข้อปัญหา'};
    if (ticket.ticketId && !haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var sh = getITRepairTicketSheetV70_();
    var assets = getAllITAssetsV70_();
    var asset = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) if (assets[i].assetId === ticket.assetId) asset = assets[i];
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var found = ticket.ticketId ? haosV70FindRowById_(sh, ticket.ticketId, 1) : -1;
    if (ticket.ticketId && found <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อมที่ต้องการแก้ไข'};
    var old = found > 1 ? itRepairTicketRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, 26).getValues()[0]) : {};
    var id = ticket.ticketId || haosV70Id_('RPT');
    var no = ticket.ticketNo || old.ticketNo || ('IT-' + Utilities.formatDate(now, 'GMT+7', 'yyyyMMdd') + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0,4).toUpperCase());
    var status = ticket.ticketId ? (ticket.status || old.status || 'New') : 'New';
    var row = [
      id, no, ticket.assetId || old.assetId || '', ticket.assetCode || (asset ? asset.assetCode : old.assetCode || ''), ticket.assetName || (asset ? asset.assetName : old.assetName || ''),
      ticket.requesterName || old.requesterName || actor.name || '', "'" + haosV70Phone_(ticket.requesterPhone || old.requesterPhone || actor.phone), ticket.requesterDepartment || old.requesterDepartment || actor.department || '',
      ticket.location || (asset ? asset.location : old.location || ''), ticket.problemCategory || old.problemCategory || 'Hardware', ticket.problemTitle || old.problemTitle || '', ticket.problemDetail || old.problemDetail || '',
      ticket.priority || old.priority || 'Normal', ticket.impact || old.impact || '', status, ticket.assignedTo || old.assignedTo || '', ticket.reportedAt || old.reportedAt || now,
      old.acceptedAt || '', old.resolvedAt || '', old.closedAt || '', ticket.slaDueAt || old.slaDueAt || '', ticket.resolutionSummary || old.resolutionSummary || '', ticket.repairCost || old.repairCost || '', ticket.attachmentUrl || old.attachmentUrl || '', old.createdAt || ticket.createdAt || now, now
    ];
    if (found > 1) sh.getRange(found, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    if (!ticket.ticketId) {
      try {
        getITServiceStaffPhones_().forEach(function(staffPhone) {
          var notif = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS);
          if (notif) notif.appendRow(['NTF-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8), "'" + staffPhone, 'มีใบแจ้งซ่อม IT ใหม่: ' + no + ' - ' + ticket.problemTitle, false, new Date(), 'IT Repair', id, ticket.priority || 'Normal']);
        });
      } catch(ignore) {}
    }
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || '', found > 1 ? 'UPDATE_IT_REPAIR' : 'CREATE_IT_REPAIR', 'ITRepairTicket', id, no + ' ' + ticket.problemTitle, null, ticket);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกใบแจ้งซ่อมแล้ว', ticketId:id, ticketNo:no};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function updateITRepairTicketStatusV70(ticketId, status, note, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var sh = getITRepairTicketSheetV70_();
    var row = haosV70FindRowById_(sh, ticketId, 1);
    if (row <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อม'};
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    sh.getRange(row, 15).setValue(status || 'In Progress');
    sh.getRange(row, 26).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Assigned') sh.getRange(row, 18).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Resolved') sh.getRange(row, 19).setValue(now);
    if (status === 'Closed') sh.getRange(row, 20).setValue(now);
    if (note) sh.getRange(row, 22).setValue(note);
    getITRepairUpdateSheetV70_().appendRow([haosV70Id_('UPD'), ticketId, status || '', note || '', actor.name || actor.phone || '', now, '', false]);
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตสถานะใบแจ้งซ่อมแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function runITLicenseExpiryAlertV70(actorPhone) {
  try {
    if (!isITServiceStaff_(actorPhone)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var data = getITAssetModuleDataV70(actorPhone || '', '', '');
    if (!data.success) return data;
    var alerts = data.alerts || [];
    var notif = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS);
    if (notif) {
      getITServiceStaffPhones_().forEach(function(staffPhone) {
        alerts.slice(0, 20).forEach(function(a) {
          notif.appendRow(['NTF-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8), "'" + staffPhone, 'License ใกล้หมดอายุ: ' + (a.softwareName || a.licenseName || a.licenseId) + ' เหลือ ' + a.daysLeft + ' วัน', false, new Date(), 'IT License', a.licenseId, a.level]);
        });
      });
    }
    return {success:true, message:'ตรวจและสร้างการแจ้งเตือน License แล้ว', count:alerts.length};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false, message:e.toString()}; }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV70() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.111-it-asset-import', functions:['getITAssetModuleDataV70','saveITAssetV70','importITAssetsV7111','saveITSoftwareLicenseV70','saveITRepairTicketV70','updateITRepairTicketStatusV70','runITLicenseExpiryAlertV70']};
}

// ========================= v70.12: central help/support contacts =========================
function haosV712IsCentralSupport_(role, department) {
  role = String(role || '');
  department = String(department || '');
  return role === 'Super Admin' ||
    role === 'Admin' ||
    /super\s*admin|admin/i.test(role) ||
    department.indexOf('สุขภาพดิจิทัล') !== -1 ||
    /digital\s*health/i.test(department);
}

function getCentralSupportContactsV712(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheet) return {success:true, contacts:[], version:'v70.12-support-contacts'};
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var contacts = [];
    var seen = {};
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var row = data[i] || [];
      var rowPhone = String(row[0] || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
      var fullName = String(row[2] || '').trim();
      var position = String(row[3] || '').trim();
      var dept = String(row[4] || '').trim();
      var email = String(row[5] || '').trim();
      var rowRole = String(row[7] || '').trim();
      var status = String(row[10] || 'Active').trim();
      if (!rowPhone && !email) continue;
      if (/inactive/i.test(status)) continue;
      if (!haosV712IsCentralSupport_(rowRole, dept)) continue;
      var key = rowPhone || email;
      if (seen[key]) continue;
      seen[key] = true;
      contacts.push({
        phone: rowPhone,
        fullName: fullName || rowPhone || email,
        position: position,
        department: dept,
        email: email,
        role: rowRole || (haosV711IsDigitalHealthDept_(dept) ? 'Digital Health Admin' : 'Admin')
      });
    }
    contacts.sort(function(a, b) {
      var ar = /super/i.test(a.role || '') ? 0 : (/admin/i.test(a.role || '') ? 1 : 2);
      var br = /super/i.test(b.role || '') ? 0 : (/admin/i.test(b.role || '') ? 1 : 2);
      if (ar !== br) return ar - br;
      return String(a.fullName || '').localeCompare(String(b.fullName || ''));
    });
    return {success:true, contacts:contacts.slice(0, 20), version:'v70.12-support-contacts'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), contacts:[]};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV712() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.12-help-info-schedule', functions:['getCentralSupportContactsV712']};
}

// ========================= v70.13: configurable help contacts + live help chat =========================
function haosV713SupportSheet_() {
  var ss = getDB();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('HelpSupportContacts');
  if (!sh) sh = ss.insertSheet('HelpSupportContacts');
  ensureHeaders_(sh, ['Phone', 'Enabled', 'Chat Enabled', 'Sort Order', 'Updated At', 'Updated By'], '#d9ead3');
  sh.getRange('A:A').setNumberFormat('@');
  return sh;
}

function haosV713ChatSessionSheet_() {
  var ss = getDB();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('HelpChatSessions');
  if (!sh) sh = ss.insertSheet('HelpChatSessions');
  ensureHeaders_(sh, ['Session ID', 'User Phone', 'User Name', 'Department', 'Status', 'Assigned To', 'Created At', 'Last Message At', 'Closed At'], '#d9eaf7');
  sh.getRange('A:B').setNumberFormat('@');
  return sh;
}

function haosV713ChatMessageSheet_() {
  var ss = getDB();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('HelpChatMessages');
  if (!sh) sh = ss.insertSheet('HelpChatMessages');
  ensureHeaders_(sh, ['Message ID', 'Session ID', 'Sender Phone', 'Sender Name', 'Sender Role', 'Message', 'Timestamp', 'Is Admin'], '#fff2cc');
  sh.getRange('A:C').setNumberFormat('@');
  return sh;
}

function haosV713CleanPhone_(phone) {
  return String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
}

function haosV713Id_(prefix) {
  return String(prefix || 'ID') + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
}

function haosV713UserByPhone_(phone) {
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sh) return null;
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = haosV713CleanPhone_(phone);
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rowPhone = haosV713CleanPhone_(data[i][0]);
      if (rowPhone !== target) continue;
      return {
        phone: rowPhone,
        fullName: String(data[i][2] || ''),
        position: String(data[i][3] || ''),
        department: String(data[i][4] || ''),
        email: String(data[i][5] || ''),
        role: String(data[i][7] || 'User'),
        status: String(data[i][10] || 'Active')
      };
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return null;
}

function haosV713AllActiveUsers_() {
  var users = [];
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sh) return users;
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var status = String(data[i][10] || 'Active');
      if (/inactive/i.test(status)) continue;
      var phone = haosV713CleanPhone_(data[i][0]);
      if (!phone) continue;
      users.push({
        phone: phone,
        fullName: String(data[i][2] || phone),
        position: String(data[i][3] || ''),
        department: String(data[i][4] || ''),
        email: String(data[i][5] || ''),
        role: String(data[i][7] || 'User')
      });
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return users;
}

function haosV713SupportConfigMap_() {
  var map = {};
  var hasRows = false;
  try {
    var sh = haosV713SupportSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var phone = haosV713CleanPhone_(data[i][0]);
      if (!phone) continue;
      hasRows = true;
      map[phone] = {
        enabled: String(data[i][1] || '').toLowerCase() !== 'false',
        chatEnabled: String(data[i][2] || '').toLowerCase() !== 'false',
        sortOrder: Number(data[i][3] || 999)
      };
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  map.__hasRows = hasRows;
  return map;
}

function haosV713FallbackSupportUser_(u) {
  if (!u) return false;
  return haosV712IsCentralSupport_(u.role, u.department);
}

function haosV713ConfiguredSupportContacts_() {
  var cfg = haosV713SupportConfigMap_();
  var users = haosV713AllActiveUsers_();
  var contacts = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < users.length; i++) {
    var u = users[i];
    var c = cfg[u.phone];
    var enabled = cfg.__hasRows ? !!(c && c.enabled) : haosV713FallbackSupportUser_(u);
    if (!enabled) continue;
    contacts.push({
      phone: u.phone,
      fullName: u.fullName,
      position: u.position,
      department: u.department,
      email: u.email,
      role: u.role,
      chatEnabled: c ? c.chatEnabled !== false : true,
      sortOrder: c ? c.sortOrder : (haosV711IsITManagerRole_(u.role) ? 1 : 50)
    });
  }
  contacts.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.sortOrder !== b.sortOrder) return a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder;
    return String(a.fullName || '').localeCompare(String(b.fullName || ''));
  });
  return contacts.slice(0, 30);
}

function getCentralSupportContactsV712(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    return {success:true, contacts:haosV713ConfiguredSupportContacts_(), version:'v70.13-configurable-support'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), contacts:[]};
  }
}

function getHelpSupportContactSettingsV713(adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (!isSuperAdmin_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var cfg = haosV713SupportConfigMap_();
    var users = haosV713AllActiveUsers_().map(function(u) {
      var c = cfg[u.phone];
      var fallback = haosV713FallbackSupportUser_(u);
      return {
        phone: u.phone,
        fullName: u.fullName,
        position: u.position,
        department: u.department,
        email: u.email,
        role: u.role,
        selected: cfg.__hasRows ? !!(c && c.enabled) : fallback,
        chatEnabled: c ? c.chatEnabled !== false : fallback,
        sortOrder: c ? c.sortOrder : (fallback ? 10 : 999)
      };
    });
    return {success:true, users:users, hasCustomSettings:cfg.__hasRows, version:'v70.13-support-settings'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), users:[]};
  }
}

function saveHelpSupportContactSettingsV713(adminPhone, contacts) {
  try {
    if (!isSuperAdmin_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    contacts = Array.isArray(contacts) ? contacts : [];
    var sh = haosV713SupportSheet_();
    if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, 6).clearContent();
    var now = new Date();
    var rows = [];
    contacts.forEach(function(c, idx) {
      var phone = haosV713CleanPhone_(c.phone);
      if (!phone) return;
      rows.push(["'" + phone, true, c.chatEnabled !== false, Number(c.sortOrder || (idx + 1)), now, "'" + haosV713CleanPhone_(adminPhone)]);
    });
    if (rows.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, 6).setValues(rows);
    logAudit_(adminPhone || '', getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'SAVE_HELP_SUPPORT_CONTACTS', 'HelpSupportContacts', 'CONFIG', 'ตั้งค่าผู้ติดต่อ Help Center', null, {count:rows.length});
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกผู้ติดต่อ Help Center แล้ว', count:rows.length};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV713CanUseSupportInbox_(phone) {
  var clean = haosV713CleanPhone_(phone);
  if (isSuperAdmin_(clean) || isITServiceStaff_(clean)) return true;
  var contacts = haosV713ConfiguredSupportContacts_();
  for (var i = 0; i < contacts.length; i++) {
    if (haosV713CleanPhone_(contacts[i].phone) === clean && contacts[i].chatEnabled !== false) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function createHelpChatSessionV713(actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var phone = haosV713CleanPhone_(actor.phone);
    var sh = haosV713ChatSessionSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (haosV713CleanPhone_(data[i][1]) === phone && String(data[i][4] || 'Open') !== 'Closed') {
        return {success:true, sessionId:String(data[i][0] || ''), reused:true};
      }
    }
    var id = haosV713Id_('HCHAT');
    var now = new Date();
    sh.appendRow([id, "'" + phone, actor.name || actor.fullName || getUserNameByPhone_(phone), actor.department || '', 'Open', '', now, now, '']);
    return {success:true, sessionId:id, reused:false};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV713FindSessionRow_(sessionId) {
  var sh = haosV713ChatSessionSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0] || '') === String(sessionId || '')) return {sheet:sh, row:i + 1, values:data[i]};
  }
  return null;
}

function sendHelpChatMessageV713(sessionId, actor, message) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    message = String(message || '').trim();
    if (!message) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาพิมพ์ข้อความ'};
    var session = haosV713FindSessionRow_(sessionId);
    if (!session) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบห้องแชท'};
    var actorPhone = haosV713CleanPhone_(actor.phone);
    var ownerPhone = haosV713CleanPhone_(session.values[1]);
    var isAdmin = haosV713CanUseSupportInbox_(actorPhone);
    if (actorPhone !== ownerPhone && !isAdmin) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ส่งข้อความในห้องแชทนี้'};
    var now = new Date();
    var msgId = haosV713Id_('HMSG');
    haosV713ChatMessageSheet_().appendRow([msgId, sessionId, "'" + actorPhone, actor.name || actor.fullName || getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone), actor.role || '', message, now, isAdmin]);
    session.sheet.getRange(session.row, 5).setValue('Open');
    session.sheet.getRange(session.row, 8).setValue(now);
    if (isAdmin && !session.values[5]) session.sheet.getRange(session.row, 6).setValue("'" + actorPhone);
    if (!isAdmin) {
      try {
        createNotificationsV732_(
          haosV713ConfiguredSupportContacts_().filter(function(c){return c.chatEnabled !== false;}).slice(0, 10).map(function(c){return c.phone;}),
          'มีข้อความ Help Chat ใหม่จาก ' + (actor.name || actor.fullName || actorPhone) + ': ' + message.substring(0, 80),
          'Help Chat',
          sessionId,
          'Normal',
          {module:'helpChat', action:'openHelpChat', createdBy:actorPhone}
        );
      } catch(ignore) {}
    }
    return {success:true, messageId:msgId};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getHelpChatMessagesV713(sessionId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var session = haosV713FindSessionRow_(sessionId);
    if (!session) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบห้องแชท', messages:[]};
    var actorPhone = haosV713CleanPhone_(actor.phone);
    var ownerPhone = haosV713CleanPhone_(session.values[1]);
    if (actorPhone !== ownerPhone && !haosV713CanUseSupportInbox_(actorPhone)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ดูห้องแชทนี้', messages:[]};
    var msgSh = haosV713ChatMessageSheet_();
    var data = msgSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][1] || '') !== String(sessionId || '')) continue;
      out.push({
        id:String(data[i][0] || ''),
        sessionId:String(data[i][1] || ''),
        senderPhone:haosV713CleanPhone_(data[i][2]),
        senderName:String(data[i][3] || ''),
        senderRole:String(data[i][4] || ''),
        message:String(data[i][5] || ''),
        timestamp:data[i][6],
        isAdmin:String(data[i][7]).toLowerCase() === 'true' || data[i][7] === true
      });
    }
    return {success:true, session:{id:sessionId, userPhone:ownerPhone, userName:String(session.values[2] || ''), department:String(session.values[3] || ''), status:String(session.values[4] || 'Open')}, messages:out};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), messages:[]};
  }
}

function getHelpChatInboxV713(adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV713CanUseSupportInbox_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ดู Help Chat Inbox', sessions:[]};
    var sh = haosV713ChatSessionSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var sessions = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      sessions.push({
        id:String(data[i][0] || ''),
        userPhone:haosV713CleanPhone_(data[i][1]),
        userName:String(data[i][2] || ''),
        department:String(data[i][3] || ''),
        status:String(data[i][4] || 'Open'),
        assignedTo:haosV713CleanPhone_(data[i][5]),
        createdAt:data[i][6],
        lastMessageAt:data[i][7],
        closedAt:data[i][8] || ''
      });
    }
    sessions.sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(b.lastMessageAt || b.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(a.lastMessageAt || a.createdAt).getTime(); });
    return {success:true, sessions:sessions.slice(0, 100)};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), sessions:[]};
  }
}

function closeHelpChatSessionV713(sessionId, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var session = haosV713FindSessionRow_(sessionId);
    if (!session) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบห้องแชท'};
    var clean = haosV713CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    var owner = haosV713CleanPhone_(session.values[1]);
    if (clean !== owner && !haosV713CanUseSupportInbox_(clean)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ปิดห้องแชทนี้'};
    session.sheet.getRange(session.row, 5).setValue('Closed');
    session.sheet.getRange(session.row, 9).setValue(new Date());
    return {success:true, message:'ปิดห้องแชทแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function deleteHelpChatSessionV713(sessionId, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var session = haosV713FindSessionRow_(sessionId);
    if (!session) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบห้องแชท'};
    var clean = haosV713CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    var owner = haosV713CleanPhone_(session.values[1]);
    if (clean !== owner && !haosV713CanUseSupportInbox_(clean)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ลบห้องแชทนี้'};

    var msgSh = haosV713ChatMessageSheet_();
    var data = msgSh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var deletedMessages = 0;
    for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (String(data[i][1] || '') === String(sessionId || '')) {
        msgSh.deleteRow(i + 1);
        deletedMessages++;
      }
    }
    session.sheet.deleteRow(session.row);
    logAudit_(clean || '', getUserNameByPhone_(clean), 'DELETE_HELP_CHAT', 'HelpChatSessions', sessionId, 'ลบห้องแชท Help Center', null, {messages:deletedMessages});
    return {success:true, deletedMessages:deletedMessages, message:'ลบห้องแชทแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV713() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.111-support-chat-delete-attachments', functions:['getHelpSupportContactSettingsV713','saveHelpSupportContactSettingsV713','createHelpChatSessionV713','sendHelpChatMessageV713','getHelpChatMessagesV713','getHelpChatInboxV713','closeHelpChatSessionV713','deleteHelpChatSessionV713']};
}

function haosV714Sheet_(name, headers, color) {
  return getOrCreateSheet_(name, headers, color || '#e0f2fe');
}

  return haosV714Sheet_('EMeetingMaster', [
    'Meeting ID','Title','Meeting Type','Department','Committee','Start Time','End Time','Location',
    'Meeting Link','External Meeting ID','Passcode','Chair','Organizer Phone','Organizer Name','Status',
    'RSVP Token','Live Token','Current Agenda ID','Current Document URL','Created At','Updated At','Visibility','Notes'
  ], '#dbeafe');

  ], '#ede9fe');

    'Participant ID','Meeting ID','Name','Position','Department','Phone','Email','Role','RSVP Status',
    'Delegate Name','Delegate Position','RSVP Note','Responded At','Created At','Updated At'
  ], '#dcfce7');

  ], '#fef3c7');
}

  return haosV714Sheet_('EMeetingLiveState', [
    'Meeting ID','Current Agenda ID','Current Document URL','Current Document Name','Sync Message','Updated At','Updated By'
  ], '#ccfbf1');

function haosV714ActionSheet_() {
  return haosV714Sheet_('EMeetingActionItems', [
    'Action ID','Meeting ID','Agenda ID','Title','Owner','Owner Phone','Due Date','Status','Created At','Source'
  ], '#fee2e2');
}

function haosV714Clean_(v) {
  return String(v || '').replace(/'/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}

function haosV714Id_(prefix) {
  return String(prefix || 'EMT') + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
}

function haosV714Token_() {
  return Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 18);
}

function haosV714Fmt_(v, pattern) {
  if (!v) return '';
  try {
    var d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
    if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', pattern || 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm');
  } catch(ignore) {}
  return String(v || '');
}

function haosV714RawLocal_(v) {
  if (!v) return '';
  try {
    var d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
    if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm");
  } catch(ignore) {}
  return String(v || '');
}

function haosV714FindRowById_(sheet, id, col) {
  col = col || 1;
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][col - 1] || '') === String(id || '')) return {row:i + 1, values:data[i]};
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV714MeetingRow_(row) {
  return {
    meetingId:String(row[0] || ''),
    title:String(row[1] || ''),
    meetingType:String(row[2] || ''),
    department:String(row[3] || ''),
    committee:String(row[4] || ''),
    startTime:haosV714Fmt_(row[5]),
    endTime:haosV714Fmt_(row[6]),
    rawStartTime:haosV714RawLocal_(row[5]),
    rawEndTime:haosV714RawLocal_(row[6]),
    location:String(row[7] || ''),
    meetingLink:String(row[8] || ''),
    externalMeetingId:String(row[9] || ''),
    passcode:String(row[10] || ''),
    chair:String(row[11] || ''),
    organizerPhone:haosV714Clean_(row[12]),
    organizerName:String(row[13] || ''),
    status:String(row[14] || 'ร่าง'),
    rsvpToken:String(row[15] || ''),
    liveToken:String(row[16] || ''),
    currentAgendaId:String(row[17] || ''),
    currentDocumentUrl:String(row[18] || ''),
    createdAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[19], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
    updatedAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[20], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
    visibility:String(row[21] || 'ทั้งระบบ'),
    notes:String(row[22] || '')
  };
}

function haosV714AgendaRow_(row) {
  return {
    agendaId:String(row[0] || ''),
    meetingId:String(row[1] || ''),
    no:Number(row[2] || 0),
    agendaType:String(row[3] || ''),
    title:String(row[4] || ''),
    presenter:String(row[5] || ''),
    details:String(row[6] || ''),
    documentUrl:String(row[7] || ''),
    documentName:String(row[8] || ''),
    decision:String(row[9] || ''),
    actionOwner:String(row[10] || ''),
    dueDate:haosV714Fmt_(row[11], 'yyyy-MM-dd'),
    status:String(row[12] || 'เตรียมวาระ'),
    createdAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[13], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
    updatedAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[14], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')
  };
}

function haosV714ParticipantRow_(row, safe) {
  var out = {
    participantId:String(row[0] || ''),
    meetingId:String(row[1] || ''),
    name:String(row[2] || ''),
    position:String(row[3] || ''),
    department:String(row[4] || ''),
    role:String(row[7] || 'ผู้เข้าร่วม'),
    rsvpStatus:String(row[8] || 'ยังไม่ตอบรับ'),
    delegateName:String(row[9] || ''),
    delegatePosition:String(row[10] || ''),
    rsvpNote:String(row[11] || ''),
    respondedAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[12], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')
  };
  if (!safe) {
    out.phone = haosV714Clean_(row[5]);
    out.email = String(row[6] || '');
  }
  return out;
}

function haosV714LiveRow_(row) {
  return {
    meetingId:String(row[0] || ''),
    currentAgendaId:String(row[1] || ''),
    currentDocumentUrl:String(row[2] || ''),
    currentDocumentName:String(row[3] || ''),
    syncMessage:String(row[4] || ''),
    updatedAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[5], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
    updatedBy:String(row[6] || '')
  };
}

function haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId) {
  var found = haosV714FindRowById_(haosV714MeetingSheet_(), meetingId, 1);
  return found ? haosV714MeetingRow_(found.values) : null;
}

function haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor) {
  actor = actor || {};
  var phone = haosV714Clean_(actor.phone || actor.userPhone || '');
  if (!phone) return false;
  if (meeting && haosV714Clean_(meeting.organizerPhone) === phone) return true;
  if (isSuperAdmin_(phone) || isITServiceStaff_(phone)) return true;
  return false;
}

function haosV714IsVisible_(meeting, phone, role, department) {
  if (!meeting) return false;
  phone = haosV714Clean_(phone);
  role = String(role || '');
  department = String(department || '');
  if (meeting.visibility !== 'เฉพาะกลุ่มงาน') return true;
  if (haosV714Clean_(meeting.organizerPhone) === phone) return true;
  if (role === 'Super Admin' || role === 'Admin' || isITServiceStaff_(phone)) return true;
  return String(meeting.department || '') === department;
}

function haosV714RowsByMeeting_(sheet, meetingId, mapper, safe) {
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][1] || '') === String(meetingId || '')) out.push(mapper(data[i], safe));
  }
  return out;
}

function haosV714LiveByMeeting_(meetingId) {
  var sh = haosV714LiveSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0] || '') === String(meetingId || '')) return {sheet:sh, row:i + 1, item:haosV714LiveRow_(data[i])};
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV714DeleteRowsByMeeting_(sheet, meetingId) {
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (String(data[i][1] || '') === String(meetingId || '')) sheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
  }
}

function haosV714ParsePeople_(value, meetingId) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
  var text = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!text) return [];
  return text.split(/\n+/).map(function(line) {
    var p = line.split('|').map(function(x){ return haosV714Clean_(x); });
    return {meetingId:meetingId, name:p[0] || '', position:p[1] || '', department:p[2] || '', phone:p[3] || '', email:p[4] || '', role:p[5] || 'ผู้เข้าร่วม'};
  }).filter(function(x){ return x.name; });
}

function haosV714ParseAgendas_(value, meetingId) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
  var text = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!text) return [];
  return text.split(/\n+/).map(function(line, idx) {
    var p = line.split('|').map(function(x){ return haosV714Clean_(x); });
    return {meetingId:meetingId, no:idx + 1, agendaType:p[0] || 'เรื่องเพื่อทราบ', title:p[1] || p[0] || '', presenter:p[2] || '', documentUrl:p[3] || '', documentName:p[4] || '', details:p[5] || ''};
  }).filter(function(x){ return x.title; });
}

function getEMeetingModuleDataV714(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var sh = haosV714MeetingSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var meetings = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var item = haosV714MeetingRow_(data[i]);
      if (!haosV714IsVisible_(item, phone, role, department)) continue;
      meetings.push(item);
    }
    var agendaData = haosV714AgendaSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var partData = haosV714ParticipantSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var agendaCounts = {}, participantCounts = {}, acceptedCounts = {};
    for (var a = 1; a < agendaData.length; a++) agendaCounts[String(agendaData[a][1] || '')] = (agendaCounts[String(agendaData[a][1] || '')] || 0) + 1;
    for (var p = 1; p < partData.length; p++) {
      var mid = String(partData[p][1] || '');
      participantCounts[mid] = (participantCounts[mid] || 0) + 1;
      if (String(partData[p][8] || '') === 'เข้าร่วมประชุม') acceptedCounts[mid] = (acceptedCounts[mid] || 0) + 1;
    }
    meetings.forEach(function(m) {
      m.agendaCount = agendaCounts[m.meetingId] || 0;
      m.participantCount = participantCounts[m.meetingId] || 0;
      m.acceptedCount = acceptedCounts[m.meetingId] || 0;
    });
    meetings.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.rawStartTime || b.createdAt || '').localeCompare(String(a.rawStartTime || a.createdAt || '')); });
    return {success:true, meetings:meetings, dashboard:{
      totalMeetings:meetings.length,
      draft:meetings.filter(function(m){return m.status === 'ร่าง';}).length,
      planned:meetings.filter(function(m){return m.status === 'เตรียมประชุม' || m.status === 'เปิดตอบรับ';}).length,
      completed:meetings.filter(function(m){return m.status === 'สรุปมติแล้ว' || m.status === 'ปิดประชุม';}).length
    }};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), meetings:[]};
  }
}

function getEMeetingDetailV714(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    var phone = haosV714Clean_(actor.phone || '');
    if (!haosV714IsVisible_(meeting, phone, actor.role, actor.department)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ดูการประชุมนี้'};
    var agendas = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714AgendaSheet_(), meetingId, haosV714AgendaRow_);
    agendas.sort(function(a,b){return Number(a.no || 0) - Number(b.no || 0);});
    var participants = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714ParticipantSheet_(), meetingId, haosV714ParticipantRow_, false);
    var live = haosV714LiveByMeeting_(meetingId);
    meeting.canEdit = haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor);
    return {success:true, meeting:meeting, agendas:agendas, participants:participants, liveState:live ? live.item : null};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function saveEMeetingV714(meeting, actor) {
  try {
    meeting = meeting || {};
    actor = actor || {};
    if (!meeting.title) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อการประชุม'};
    var actorPhone = haosV714Clean_(actor.phone || meeting.organizerPhone || '');
    var now = new Date();
    var sh = haosV714MeetingSheet_();
    var id = meeting.meetingId || haosV714Id_('EMT');
    var found = haosV714FindRowById_(sh, id, 1);
    var existing = found ? haosV714MeetingRow_(found.values) : null;
    if (existing && !haosV714CanEdit_(existing, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์แก้ไขการประชุมนี้'};
    var start = meeting.startTime ? new Date(meeting.startTime) : '';
    var end = meeting.endTime ? new Date(meeting.endTime) : '';
    var rsvpToken = existing ? existing.rsvpToken : haosV714Token_();
    var liveToken = existing ? existing.liveToken : haosV714Token_();
    var organizerName = meeting.organizerName || actor.name || actor.fullName || getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone) || '';
    var row = [
      id, meeting.title || '', meeting.meetingType || 'ประชุมทั่วไป', meeting.department || actor.department || '',
      meeting.committee || '', start || '', end || '', meeting.location || '', meeting.meetingLink || '',
      meeting.externalMeetingId || '', meeting.passcode || '', meeting.chair || '', "'" + actorPhone, organizerName,
      meeting.status || (existing ? existing.status : 'ร่าง'), rsvpToken, liveToken,
      existing ? existing.currentAgendaId : '', existing ? existing.currentDocumentUrl : '',
      existing ? found.values[19] : now, now, meeting.visibility || 'ทั้งระบบ', meeting.notes || ''
    ];
    if (found) sh.getRange(found.row, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else sh.appendRow(row);
    if (meeting.participantsText || Array.isArray(meeting.participants)) {
      saveEMeetingParticipantsV714(id, haosV714ParsePeople_(meeting.participants || meeting.participantsText, id), actor, true);
    }
    if (meeting.agendaText || Array.isArray(meeting.agendaItems)) {
      saveEMeetingAgendaV714(id, haosV714ParseAgendas_(meeting.agendaItems || meeting.agendaText, id), actor, true);
    }
    logAudit_(actorPhone, organizerName, found ? 'UPDATE_EMEETING' : 'CREATE_EMEETING', 'EMeetingMaster', id, meeting.title || '', existing, meeting);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกการประชุมแล้ว', meetingId:id, rsvpToken:rsvpToken, liveToken:liveToken};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function saveEMeetingAgendaV714(meetingId, agendas, actor, replace) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์จัดการวาระ'};
    agendas = Array.isArray(agendas) ? agendas : [agendas || {}];
    var sh = haosV714AgendaSheet_();
    if (replace) haosV714DeleteRowsByMeeting_(sh, meetingId);
    var now = new Date();
    var rows = [];
    agendas.forEach(function(a, idx) {
      if (!a || !a.title) return;
      var agendaId = a.agendaId || haosV714Id_('AGD');
      rows.push([agendaId, meetingId, Number(a.no || idx + 1), a.agendaType || 'เรื่องเพื่อทราบ', a.title || '', a.presenter || '', a.details || '', a.documentUrl || '', a.documentName || '', a.decision || '', a.actionOwner || '', a.dueDate || '', a.status || 'เตรียมวาระ', now, now]);
      if (a.documentUrl) haosV714DocumentSheet_().appendRow([haosV714Id_('DOC'), meetingId, agendaId, a.documentName || a.title || 'เอกสารประกอบวาระ', a.documentUrl || '', 'Agenda', 1, actor.name || actor.phone || '', now, true]);
    });
    if (rows.length) sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, rows[0].length).setValues(rows);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกวาระแล้ว', count:rows.length};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function saveEMeetingMinuteDecisionV714(meetingId, agendaId, decision, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    decision = decision || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์บันทึกมติ'};
    var sh = haosV714AgendaSheet_();
    var found = haosV714FindRowById_(sh, agendaId, 1);
    if (!found || String(found.values[1] || '') !== String(meetingId || '')) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบวาระ'};
    sh.getRange(found.row, 10).setValue(decision.decision || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 11).setValue(decision.actionOwner || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 12).setValue(decision.dueDate || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 13).setValue(decision.status || 'สรุปมติแล้ว');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 15).setValue(new Date());

      haosV714ActionSheet_().appendRow([haosV714Id_('ACT'), meetingId, agendaId, decision.actionTitle || found.values[4] || '', decision.actionOwner || '', decision.ownerPhone || '', decision.dueDate || '', 'รอดำเนินการ', new Date(), 'มติการประชุม']);
    }
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกมติวาระแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function setEMeetingLiveStateV714(meetingId, agendaId, documentUrl, documentName, message, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};

    else haosV714LiveSheet_().appendRow(row);
    var mFound = haosV714FindRowById_(haosV714MeetingSheet_(), meetingId, 1);
    if (mFound) {
      mFound.sheet = haosV714MeetingSheet_();

      mFound.sheet.getRange(mFound.row, 19).setValue(documentUrl || '');
      mFound.sheet.getRange(mFound.row, 21).setValue(now);
    }
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตเอกสารที่กำลังเปิดร่วมกันแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
