}

function createEMeetingMinutesV714(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var detail = getEMeetingDetailV714(meetingId, actor);
    if (!detail.success) return detail;
    if (!detail.meeting.canEdit) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์สร้างรายงานการประชุม'};
    var m = detail.meeting;
    var attendees = (detail.participants || []).map(function(p) {
      return p.name + (p.position ? ' - ' + p.position : '') + (p.rsvpStatus ? ' (' + p.rsvpStatus + ')' : '');
    }).join('\n');
    var agendaText = (detail.agendas || []).map(function(a) {
      return (a.no || '') + '. ' + a.title + (a.details ? '\n' + a.details : '');
    }).join('\n\n');
    var conclusion = (detail.agendas || []).map(function(a) {
      return (a.no || '') + '. ' + a.title + '\nมติ: ' + (a.decision || '-') + (a.actionOwner ? '\nผู้รับผิดชอบ: ' + a.actionOwner : '') + (a.dueDate ? '\nกำหนด: ' + a.dueDate : '');
    }).join('\n\n');
    var result = saveMeetingMinutesV2({
      title:m.title,
      date:m.rawStartTime || m.startTime,
      location:m.location || m.meetingLink || '',
      chairman:m.chair || '',
      attendees:attendees,
      agenda:agendaText,
      conclusion:conclusion,
      recorderPhone:actor.phone || m.organizerPhone || '',
      recorderName:actor.name || actor.fullName || m.organizerName || '',
      department:m.department || '',
      relatedScheduleId:meetingId
    });
    if (result && result.success) {
      var found = haosV714FindRowById_(haosV714MeetingSheet_(), meetingId, 1);
      if (found) {
        haosV714MeetingSheet_().getRange(found.row, 15).setValue('สรุปมติแล้ว');
        haosV714MeetingSheet_().getRange(found.row, 21).setValue(new Date());
      }
    }
    return result;
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function searchEMeetingArchiveV714(query, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    query = haosV714Clean_(query).toLowerCase();
    var data = getEMeetingModuleDataV714(actor.phone || '', actor.role || '', actor.department || '');
    if (!data.success) return data;
    var out = [];
    data.meetings.forEach(function(m) {
      var agendas = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714AgendaSheet_(), m.meetingId, haosV714AgendaRow_);
      var hay = [m.title, m.department, m.committee, m.notes].concat(agendas.map(function(a){return [a.title,a.details,a.decision,a.documentName].join(' ');})).join(' ').toLowerCase();
      if (!query || hay.indexOf(query) !== -1) out.push({meeting:m, agendas:agendas.slice(0, 12)});
    });
    return {success:true, results:out.slice(0, 50)};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), results:[]};
  }
}

function getPublicEMeetingV714(meetingId, token, mode) {
  try {
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    mode = String(mode || 'rsvp');
    token = String(token || '');
    var ok = (mode === 'rsvp' && token === meeting.rsvpToken) || (mode !== 'rsvp' && token === meeting.liveToken) || token === meeting.rsvpToken || token === meeting.liveToken;
    if (!ok) return {success:false, message:'ลิงก์ไม่ถูกต้องหรือหมดอายุ'};
    var agendas = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714AgendaSheet_(), meetingId, haosV714AgendaRow_);
    agendas.sort(function(a,b){return Number(a.no || 0) - Number(b.no || 0);});
    var participants = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714ParticipantSheet_(), meetingId, haosV714ParticipantRow_, true);
    var live = haosV714LiveByMeeting_(meetingId);
    meeting.rsvpToken = '';
    meeting.liveToken = '';
    meeting.organizerPhone = '';
    return {success:true, meeting:meeting, agendas:agendas, participants:participants, liveState:live ? live.item : null, generatedAt:haosV714Fmt_(new Date(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function submitEMeetingRsvpV714(meetingId, token, response) {
  try {
    response = response || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (String(token || '') !== String(meeting.rsvpToken || '')) return {success:false, message:'ลิงก์ตอบรับไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    var sh = haosV714ParticipantSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var row = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][1] || '') !== String(meetingId || '')) continue;
      if (String(data[i][0] || '') === String(response.participantId || '') || String(data[i][2] || '') === String(response.name || '')) {
        row = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!row) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายชื่อผู้เข้าประชุม'};
    sh.getRange(row, 9).setValue(response.rsvpStatus || 'เข้าร่วมประชุม');
    sh.getRange(row, 10).setValue(response.delegateName || '');
    sh.getRange(row, 11).setValue(response.delegatePosition || '');
    sh.getRange(row, 12).setValue(response.rsvpNote || '');
    sh.getRange(row, 13).setValue(new Date());
    sh.getRange(row, 15).setValue(new Date());
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกการตอบรับแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV714() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.14-emeeting-manage', functions:['getEMeetingModuleDataV714','getEMeetingDetailV714','saveEMeetingV714','saveEMeetingAgendaV714','saveEMeetingParticipantsV714','saveEMeetingMinuteDecisionV714','setEMeetingLiveStateV714','createEMeetingMinutesV714','searchEMeetingArchiveV714','getPublicEMeetingV714','submitEMeetingRsvpV714']};
}

// ========================= v70.15: e-Meeting production dashboard + documents =========================
function haosV715DocumentRow_(row) {
  return {
    documentId:String(row[0] || ''),
    meetingId:String(row[1] || ''),
    agendaId:String(row[2] || ''),
    documentName:String(row[3] || ''),
    documentUrl:String(row[4] || ''),
    documentType:String(row[5] || ''),
    version:String(row[6] || '1'),
    uploadedBy:String(row[7] || ''),
    uploadedAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[8], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
    isShared:row[9] === true || String(row[9] || '').toLowerCase() === 'true'
  };
}

function haosV715ActionRow_(row) {
  return {
    actionId:String(row[0] || ''),
    meetingId:String(row[1] || ''),
    agendaId:String(row[2] || ''),
    title:String(row[3] || ''),
    owner:String(row[4] || ''),
    ownerPhone:haosV714Clean_(row[5]),
    dueDate:haosV714Fmt_(row[6], 'yyyy-MM-dd'),
    status:String(row[7] || 'รอดำเนินการ'),
    createdAt:haosV714Fmt_(row[8], 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'),
    source:String(row[9] || '')
  };
}

function haosV715RowsByMeeting_(sheet, meetingId, mapper) {
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][1] || '') === String(meetingId || '')) out.push(mapper(data[i]));
  }
  return out;
}

function haosV715DocumentsByMeeting_(meetingId) {
  var docs = haosV715RowsByMeeting_(haosV714DocumentSheet_(), meetingId, haosV715DocumentRow_);
  docs.sort(function(a, b) { return String(b.uploadedAt || '').localeCompare(String(a.uploadedAt || '')); });
  return docs;
}

function haosV715ActionsByMeeting_(meetingId) {
  var actions = haosV715RowsByMeeting_(haosV714ActionSheet_(), meetingId, haosV715ActionRow_);
  actions.sort(function(a, b) { return String(a.dueDate || '9999-12-31').localeCompare(String(b.dueDate || '9999-12-31')); });
  return actions;
}

function haosV715DaysUntil_(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  try {
    var d = new Date(value);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
    var today = new Date();
    var start = new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth(), today.getDate()).getTime();
    var day = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()).getTime();
    return Math.round((day - start) / 86400000);
  } catch(e) {
    return '';
  }
}

function haosV715PublicUrl_(moduleName, meeting) {
  return buildPublicPageUrl_({
    module:moduleName || '',
    meetingId:meeting.meetingId || '',
    token:moduleName === 'emeeting-rsvp' ? meeting.rsvpToken || '' : meeting.liveToken || ''
  });
}

function getEMeetingModuleDataV715(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var base = getEMeetingModuleDataV714(phone, role, department);
    if (!base || !base.success) return base;
    var docsData = haosV714DocumentSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var actionsData = haosV714ActionSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var docsByMeeting = {}, openActionsByMeeting = {}, dueActions = 0;
    for (var d = 1; d < docsData.length; d++) {
      var dm = String(docsData[d][1] || '');
      docsByMeeting[dm] = (docsByMeeting[dm] || 0) + 1;
    }
    for (var a = 1; a < actionsData.length; a++) {
      var am = String(actionsData[a][1] || '');
      var st = String(actionsData[a][7] || 'รอดำเนินการ');
      if (st !== 'เสร็จสิ้น' && st !== 'ปิดงาน') {
        openActionsByMeeting[am] = (openActionsByMeeting[am] || 0) + 1;
        var days = haosV715DaysUntil_(actionsData[a][6]);
        if (days !== '' && days <= 7) dueActions++;
      }
    }
    var pendingRsvp = 0, upcoming7 = 0, inMeeting = 0, documentsTotal = 0, openActions = 0;
    var canManageAny = isSuperAdmin_(phone) || isITServiceStaff_(phone);
    (base.meetings || []).forEach(function(m) {
      m.documentCount = docsByMeeting[m.meetingId] || 0;
      m.openActionCount = openActionsByMeeting[m.meetingId] || 0;
      m.daysToStart = haosV715DaysUntil_(m.rawStartTime || m.startTime);
      m.canEdit = haosV714CanEdit_(m, {phone:phone, role:role, department:department});
      m.rsvpUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-rsvp', m);
      m.liveUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-live', m);
      documentsTotal += m.documentCount;
      openActions += m.openActionCount;
      pendingRsvp += Math.max(0, Number(m.participantCount || 0) - Number(m.acceptedCount || 0));
      if (m.daysToStart !== '' && m.daysToStart >= 0 && m.daysToStart <= 7) upcoming7++;
      if (m.status === 'กำลังประชุม') inMeeting++;
    });
    base.dashboard = base.dashboard || {};
    base.dashboard.pendingRsvp = pendingRsvp;
    base.dashboard.upcoming7 = upcoming7;
    base.dashboard.inMeeting = inMeeting;
    base.dashboard.documentsTotal = documentsTotal;
    base.dashboard.openActions = openActions;
    base.dashboard.dueActions = dueActions;
    base.permissions = {canCreate:true, canManageAny:canManageAny};
    base.version = 'v70.15-emeeting-dashboard';
    return base;
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), meetings:[]};
  }
}

function getEMeetingDetailV715(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    var detail = getEMeetingDetailV714(meetingId, actor);
    if (!detail || !detail.success) return detail;
    var docs = haosV715DocumentsByMeeting_(meetingId);
    var actions = haosV715ActionsByMeeting_(meetingId);
    var docsByAgenda = {};
    docs.forEach(function(doc) {
      var key = doc.agendaId || '_meeting';
      if (!docsByAgenda[key]) docsByAgenda[key] = [];
      docsByAgenda[key].push(doc);
    });
    (detail.agendas || []).forEach(function(a) {
      a.documents = docsByAgenda[a.agendaId] || [];
      if (a.documentUrl && !a.documents.some(function(d){ return d.documentUrl === a.documentUrl; })) {
        a.documents.unshift({
          documentId:'',
          meetingId:meetingId,
          agendaId:a.agendaId,
          documentName:a.documentName || a.title || 'เอกสารประกอบวาระ',
          documentUrl:a.documentUrl,
          documentType:'Agenda',
          version:'1',
          uploadedBy:'',
          uploadedAt:'',
          isShared:true
        });
      }
    });
    detail.documents = docs;
    detail.actions = actions;
    detail.version = 'v70.15-emeeting-detail';
    return detail;
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function saveEMeetingDocumentV715(meetingId, agendaId, documents, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์เพิ่มเอกสารประชุม'};
    documents = Array.isArray(documents) ? documents : [documents || {}];
    var sh = haosV714DocumentSheet_();
    var now = new Date();
    var rows = [];
    documents.forEach(function(doc, idx) {
      doc = doc || {};
      if (!doc.documentUrl) return;
      rows.push([
        doc.documentId || haosV714Id_('DOC'),
        meetingId,
        agendaId || doc.agendaId || '',
        doc.documentName || doc.fileName || ('เอกสารประชุม ' + (idx + 1)),
        doc.documentUrl || '',
        doc.documentType || doc.mimeType || 'File',
        doc.version || '1',
        actor.name || actor.fullName || actor.phone || '',
        now,
        doc.isShared === false ? false : true
      ]);
    });
    if (!rows.length) return {success:false, message:'ยังไม่มีเอกสารที่ต้องบันทึก'};
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, rows[0].length).setValues(rows);
    if (agendaId && rows[0][4]) {
      var ag = haosV714FindRowById_(haosV714AgendaSheet_(), agendaId, 1);
      if (ag && String(ag.values[1] || '') === String(meetingId || '') && !ag.values[7]) {
        haosV714AgendaSheet_().getRange(ag.row, 8).setValue(rows[0][4]);
        haosV714AgendaSheet_().getRange(ag.row, 9).setValue(rows[0][3]);
        haosV714AgendaSheet_().getRange(ag.row, 15).setValue(now);
      }
    }
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.fullName || '', 'ADD_EMEETING_DOCUMENT', 'EMeetingDocuments', meetingId, 'เพิ่มเอกสารประชุม ' + rows.length + ' รายการ', null, {meetingId:meetingId, agendaId:agendaId, count:rows.length});
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกเอกสารประชุมแล้ว', count:rows.length};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function deleteEMeetingDocumentV715(documentId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var sh = haosV714DocumentSheet_();
    var found = haosV714FindRowById_(sh, documentId, 1);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบเอกสาร'};
    var doc = haosV715DocumentRow_(found.values);
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(doc.meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ลบเอกสารประชุม'};
    sh.deleteRow(found.row);
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.fullName || '', 'DELETE_EMEETING_DOCUMENT', 'EMeetingDocuments', documentId, doc.documentName || '', doc, null);
    return {success:true, message:'ลบเอกสารประชุมแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function updateEMeetingStatusV715(meetingId, status, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์เปลี่ยนสถานะการประชุม'};
    var found = haosV714FindRowById_(haosV714MeetingSheet_(), meetingId, 1);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    haosV714MeetingSheet_().getRange(found.row, 15).setValue(status || meeting.status || 'เตรียมประชุม');
    haosV714MeetingSheet_().getRange(found.row, 21).setValue(new Date());
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตสถานะการประชุมแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function sendEMeetingRsvpReminderV715(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var detail = getEMeetingDetailV715(meetingId, actor);
    if (!detail || !detail.success) return detail;
    if (!detail.meeting.canEdit) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์แจ้งเตือนตอบรับการประชุม'};
    var meeting = detail.meeting;
    var url = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-rsvp', meeting);
    var count = 0;
    (detail.participants || []).forEach(function(p) {
      var phone = haosV714Clean_(p.phone);
      if (!phone) return;
      if (p.rsvpStatus && p.rsvpStatus !== 'ยังไม่ตอบรับ') return;
      createNotificationV732_(phone, 'แจ้งเตือนตอบรับเข้าร่วมประชุม: ' + meeting.title + ' | ' + url, 'e-Meeting RSVP', meetingId, 'ปกติ', {module:'eMeeting', action:'openEMeeting', createdBy:actor.phone});
      count++;
    });
    return {success:true, message:'ส่งแจ้งเตือนตอบรับการประชุมแล้ว ' + count + ' รายชื่อ', count:count};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function generateEMeetingInviteTextV715(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    var detail = getEMeetingDetailV715(meetingId, actor || {});
    if (!detail || !detail.success) return detail;
    var m = detail.meeting;
    var rsvpUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-rsvp', m);
    var liveUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-live', m);
    var lines = [
      'ขอเชิญเข้าร่วมประชุม: ' + (m.title || ''),
      'วันเวลา: ' + (m.startTime || '-') + (m.endTime ? ' ถึง ' + m.endTime : ''),
      'สถานที่/ช่องทาง: ' + (m.location || m.meetingLink || '-'),
      m.chair ? 'ประธาน: ' + m.chair : '',
      '',
      'ลิงก์ตอบรับเข้าร่วมประชุม: ' + rsvpUrl,
      m.meetingLink ? 'ลิงก์ประชุมออนไลน์: ' + m.meetingLink : '',
      'ลิงก์อ่านเอกสารประชุมร่วมกัน: ' + liveUrl
    ].filter(function(x){ return x !== ''; });
    return {success:true, text:lines.join('\n'), rsvpUrl:rsvpUrl, liveUrl:liveUrl};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getPublicEMeetingV715(meetingId, token, mode) {
  try {
    var base = getPublicEMeetingV714(meetingId, token, mode);
    if (!base || !base.success) return base;
    var docs = haosV715DocumentsByMeeting_(meetingId).filter(function(d){ return d.isShared !== false; });
    var docsByAgenda = {};
    docs.forEach(function(doc) {
      var key = doc.agendaId || '_meeting';
      if (!docsByAgenda[key]) docsByAgenda[key] = [];
      docsByAgenda[key].push(doc);
    });
    (base.agendas || []).forEach(function(a) {
      a.documents = docsByAgenda[a.agendaId] || [];
      if (a.documentUrl && !a.documents.some(function(d){ return d.documentUrl === a.documentUrl; })) {
        a.documents.unshift({documentName:a.documentName || a.title || 'เอกสารประกอบวาระ', documentUrl:a.documentUrl, documentType:'Agenda'});
      }
      if (mode === 'rsvp') a.decision = '';
    });
    base.documents = docs;
    base.version = 'v70.15-public-emeeting';
    return base;
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV715() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.15-emeeting-production', functions:[
    'getEMeetingModuleDataV715','getEMeetingDetailV715','saveEMeetingDocumentV715',
    'deleteEMeetingDocumentV715','updateEMeetingStatusV715','sendEMeetingRsvpReminderV715',
    'generateEMeetingInviteTextV715','getPublicEMeetingV715'
  ]};
}

// ========================= v70.16: e-Meeting phase 4 completion =========================
function haosV716ReminderLogSheet_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_('EMeetingReminderLog', [
    'Log ID','Meeting ID','Participant ID','Target Phone','Reminder Type','Reminder Date','Created At','Message'
  ], '#e0e7ff');
}

function haosV716PackageFolder_() {
  var name = 'HealthAssistant_EMeetingPackages';
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(name);
}

function haosV716ParticipantMap_() {
  var data = haosV714ParticipantSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
  var byMeeting = {}, byPhoneMeeting = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var p = haosV714ParticipantRow_(data[i], false);
    if (!p.meetingId || !p.participantId) continue;
    if (!byMeeting[p.meetingId]) byMeeting[p.meetingId] = [];
    byMeeting[p.meetingId].push(p);
    var phone = haosV714Clean_(p.phone);
    if (phone) byPhoneMeeting[phone + '|' + p.meetingId] = p;
  }
  return {byMeeting:byMeeting, byPhoneMeeting:byPhoneMeeting};
}

function haosV716IsParticipant_(meetingId, phone) {
  phone = haosV714Clean_(phone);
  if (!phone) return false;
  var data = haosV714ParticipantSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][1] || '') !== String(meetingId || '')) continue;
    if (haosV714Clean_(data[i][5]) === phone) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function haosV716CanSee_(meeting, actor) {
  actor = actor || {};
  return haosV714IsVisible_(meeting, actor.phone, actor.role, actor.department) ||
    haosV716IsParticipant_(meeting.meetingId, actor.phone);
}

function haosV716ReminderKeyExists_(meetingId, participantId, type, dayKey) {
  var sh = haosV716ReminderLogSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][1] || '') === String(meetingId || '') &&
        String(data[i][2] || '') === String(participantId || '') &&
        String(data[i][4] || '') === String(type || '') &&
        String(data[i][5] || '') === String(dayKey || '')) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function haosV716AppendNotification_(phone, message, type, entityId, priority) {
  phone = haosV714Clean_(phone);
  if (!phone) return false;
  var notif = getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS, ['Notif ID','Target Phone','Message','Is Read','Timestamp','Type','Entity ID','Priority'], '#f8d7da');
  notif.appendRow(['NTF-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8), "'" + phone, message, false, new Date(), type || 'e-Meeting', entityId || '', priority || 'ปกติ']);
  return true;
}

function getEMeetingModuleDataV716(phone, role, department) {
  try {
    var actor = {phone:phone || '', role:role || '', department:department || ''};
    var sh = haosV714MeetingSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var pmap = haosV716ParticipantMap_();
    var meetings = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var m = haosV714MeetingRow_(data[i]);
      if (!haosV716CanSee_(m, actor)) continue;
      meetings.push(m);
    }
    var agendaData = haosV714AgendaSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var docsData = haosV714DocumentSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var actionsData = haosV714ActionSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var agendaCounts = {}, docsByMeeting = {}, openActionsByMeeting = {}, dueActions = 0;
    for (var a = 1; a < agendaData.length; a++) agendaCounts[String(agendaData[a][1] || '')] = (agendaCounts[String(agendaData[a][1] || '')] || 0) + 1;
    for (var d = 1; d < docsData.length; d++) docsByMeeting[String(docsData[d][1] || '')] = (docsByMeeting[String(docsData[d][1] || '')] || 0) + 1;
    for (var x = 1; x < actionsData.length; x++) {
      var am = String(actionsData[x][1] || '');
      var st = String(actionsData[x][7] || 'รอดำเนินการ');
      if (st !== 'เสร็จสิ้น' && st !== 'ปิดงาน') {
        openActionsByMeeting[am] = (openActionsByMeeting[am] || 0) + 1;
        var days = haosV715DaysUntil_(actionsData[x][6]);
        if (days !== '' && days <= 7) dueActions++;
      }
    }
    var pendingRsvp = 0, upcoming7 = 0, inMeeting = 0, documentsTotal = 0, openActions = 0, invitedCount = 0;
    meetings.forEach(function(m) {
      var people = pmap.byMeeting[m.meetingId] || [];
      var accepted = people.filter(function(p){ return p.rsvpStatus === 'เข้าร่วมประชุม'; }).length;
      var pending = people.filter(function(p){ return !p.rsvpStatus || p.rsvpStatus === 'ยังไม่ตอบรับ'; }).length;
      m.agendaCount = agendaCounts[m.meetingId] || 0;
      m.participantCount = people.length;
      m.acceptedCount = accepted;
      m.pendingRsvpCount = pending;
      m.documentCount = docsByMeeting[m.meetingId] || 0;
      m.openActionCount = openActionsByMeeting[m.meetingId] || 0;
      m.daysToStart = haosV715DaysUntil_(m.rawStartTime || m.startTime);
      m.canEdit = haosV714CanEdit_(m, actor);
      m.isInvited = !!pmap.byPhoneMeeting[haosV714Clean_(phone) + '|' + m.meetingId];
      m.rsvpUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-rsvp', m);
      m.liveUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-live', m);
      documentsTotal += m.documentCount;
      openActions += m.openActionCount;
      pendingRsvp += pending;
      invitedCount += m.isInvited ? 1 : 0;
      if (m.daysToStart !== '' && m.daysToStart >= 0 && m.daysToStart <= 7) upcoming7++;
      if (m.status === 'กำลังประชุม') inMeeting++;
    });
    meetings.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.rawStartTime || b.createdAt || '').localeCompare(String(a.rawStartTime || a.createdAt || '')); });
    return {success:true, meetings:meetings, dashboard:{
      totalMeetings:meetings.length,
      draft:meetings.filter(function(m){return m.status === 'ร่าง';}).length,
      planned:meetings.filter(function(m){return m.status === 'เตรียมประชุม' || m.status === 'เปิดตอบรับ';}).length,
      completed:meetings.filter(function(m){return m.status === 'สรุปมติแล้ว' || m.status === 'ปิดประชุม';}).length,
      pendingRsvp:pendingRsvp,
      upcoming7:upcoming7,
      inMeeting:inMeeting,
      documentsTotal:documentsTotal,
      openActions:openActions,
      dueActions:dueActions,
      invitedCount:invitedCount
    }, permissions:{canCreate:true, canManageAny:isSuperAdmin_(phone) || isITServiceStaff_(phone)}, version:'v70.16-emeeting-final'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), meetings:[]};
  }
}

function getEMeetingDetailV716(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV716CanSee_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ดูการประชุมนี้'};
    var agendas = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714AgendaSheet_(), meetingId, haosV714AgendaRow_);
    agendas.sort(function(a,b){return Number(a.no || 0) - Number(b.no || 0);});
    var participants = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714ParticipantSheet_(), meetingId, haosV714ParticipantRow_, false);
    var live = haosV714LiveByMeeting_(meetingId);
    meeting.canEdit = haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor);
    meeting.isInvited = haosV716IsParticipant_(meetingId, actor.phone);
    var detail = {success:true, meeting:meeting, agendas:agendas, participants:participants, liveState:live ? live.item : null};
    var docs = haosV715DocumentsByMeeting_(meetingId);
    var actions = haosV715ActionsByMeeting_(meetingId);
    var docsByAgenda = {};
    docs.forEach(function(doc) {
      var key = doc.agendaId || '_meeting';
      if (!docsByAgenda[key]) docsByAgenda[key] = [];
      docsByAgenda[key].push(doc);
    });
    detail.agendas.forEach(function(a) {
      a.documents = docsByAgenda[a.agendaId] || [];
      if (a.documentUrl && !a.documents.some(function(d){ return d.documentUrl === a.documentUrl; })) {
        a.documents.unshift({documentId:'', meetingId:meetingId, agendaId:a.agendaId, documentName:a.documentName || a.title || 'เอกสารประกอบวาระ', documentUrl:a.documentUrl, documentType:'Agenda', version:'1', uploadedBy:'', uploadedAt:'', isShared:true});
      }
    });
    detail.documents = docs;
    detail.actions = actions;
    detail.version = 'v70.16-emeeting-detail';
    return detail;
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function runEMeetingReminderV716(daysAhead, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    daysAhead = Number(daysAhead || 1);
    var actorPhone = haosV714Clean_(actor.phone || actor);
    var isGlobal = !actorPhone || isSuperAdmin_(actorPhone) || isITServiceStaff_(actorPhone);
    var meetings = getEMeetingModuleDataV716(actorPhone, actor.role || '', actor.department || '');
    if (!meetings.success) return meetings;
    var todayKey = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyy-MM-dd');
    var count = 0, skipped = 0;
    meetings.meetings.forEach(function(m) {
      if (!isGlobal && haosV714Clean_(m.organizerPhone) !== actorPhone) return;
      if (m.status === 'ปิดประชุม' || m.status === 'ยกเลิก') return;
      var days = haosV715DaysUntil_(m.rawStartTime || m.startTime);
      if (days === '' || days < 0 || days > daysAhead) return;
      var detail = getEMeetingDetailV716(m.meetingId, {phone:actorPhone || m.organizerPhone, role:actor.role || 'Super Admin', department:actor.department || m.department});
      if (!detail.success) return;
      var rsvpUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-rsvp', detail.meeting);
      var liveUrl = haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-live', detail.meeting);
      (detail.participants || []).forEach(function(p) {
        var phone = haosV714Clean_(p.phone);
        if (!phone) { skipped++; return; }
        var type = (!p.rsvpStatus || p.rsvpStatus === 'ยังไม่ตอบรับ') ? 'RSVP' : 'MEETING';
        if (haosV716ReminderKeyExists_(m.meetingId, p.participantId, type, todayKey)) { skipped++; return; }
        var msg = type === 'RSVP'
          ? 'แจ้งเตือนตอบรับประชุม: ' + m.title + ' วันที่ ' + (m.startTime || '-') + ' | ลิงก์ตอบรับ: ' + rsvpUrl
          : 'แจ้งเตือนประชุม: ' + m.title + ' วันที่ ' + (m.startTime || '-') + ' | เอกสาร: ' + liveUrl;
        haosV716AppendNotification_(phone, msg, type === 'RSVP' ? 'e-Meeting ตอบรับการประชุม' : 'e-Meeting Reminder', m.meetingId, days <= 1 ? 'ด่วน' : 'ปกติ');
        haosV716ReminderLogSheet_().appendRow(['EMR-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8), m.meetingId, p.participantId || '', "'" + phone, type, todayKey, new Date(), msg]);
        count++;
      });
    });
    return {success:true, message:'รันแจ้งเตือน e-Meeting แล้ว', sent:count, skipped:skipped, daysAhead:daysAhead};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function exportEMeetingPackageV716(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var detail = getEMeetingDetailV716(meetingId, actor);
    if (!detail.success) return detail;
    if (!detail.meeting.canEdit) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์สร้างชุดเอกสารประชุม'};
    var m = detail.meeting;
    var folder = haosV716PackageFolder_();
    var title = 'ชุดเอกสารประชุม_' + (m.title || meetingId) + '_' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'GMT+7', 'yyyyMMdd_HHmm');
    var doc = DocumentApp.create(title);
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
    folder.addFile(file);
    try { DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file); } catch(ignore) {}
    var body = doc.getBody();
    body.clear();
    body.appendParagraph('ชุดเอกสารการประชุม').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph(m.title || '-').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph('วันเวลา: ' + (m.startTime || '-') + (m.endTime ? ' ถึง ' + m.endTime : ''));
    body.appendParagraph('สถานที่/ช่องทาง: ' + (m.location || m.meetingLink || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('ประธาน: ' + (m.chair || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('ผู้จัด: ' + (m.organizerName || '-') + ' / ' + (m.department || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('สถานะ: ' + (m.status || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('ลิงก์สำคัญ').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3);
    body.appendParagraph('ลิงก์ตอบรับการประชุม: ' + haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-rsvp', m));
    body.appendParagraph('ลิงก์อ่านเอกสารร่วมกัน: ' + haosV715PublicUrl_('emeeting-live', m));
    if (m.meetingLink) body.appendParagraph('ลิงก์ประชุมออนไลน์: ' + m.meetingLink);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('วาระ เอกสาร และมติ').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3);
    (detail.agendas || []).forEach(function(a) {
      body.appendParagraph((a.no || '') + '. ' + (a.title || '-')).setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING4);
      body.appendParagraph('ประเภท: ' + (a.agendaType || '-') + ' | ผู้นำเสนอ: ' + (a.presenter || '-'));
      if (a.details) body.appendParagraph('รายละเอียด: ' + a.details);
      var docs = a.documents || [];
      if (docs.length) {
        body.appendParagraph('เอกสารประกอบ:');
        docs.forEach(function(d) { body.appendListItem((d.documentName || 'เอกสาร') + ' - ' + (d.documentUrl || '-')); });
      }
      body.appendParagraph('มติ: ' + (a.decision || '-'));
      if (a.actionOwner || a.dueDate) body.appendParagraph('ผู้รับผิดชอบ/กำหนด: ' + (a.actionOwner || '-') + ' / ' + (a.dueDate || '-'));
      body.appendParagraph('');
    });
    body.appendParagraph('รายชื่อและสถานะตอบรับ').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3);
    (detail.participants || []).forEach(function(p) {
      body.appendListItem((p.name || '-') + ' | ' + (p.position || '-') + ' | ' + (p.department || '-') + ' | ' + (p.rsvpStatus || '-') + (p.delegateName ? ' | ผู้แทน: ' + p.delegateName : ''));
    });
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('งานติดตามจากมติ').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3);
    if ((detail.actions || []).length) {
      detail.actions.forEach(function(a) { body.appendListItem((a.title || '-') + ' | ' + (a.owner || '-') + ' | ' + (a.dueDate || '-') + ' | ' + (a.status || '-')); });
    } else {
      body.appendParagraph('-');
    }
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('สร้างจาก Health Assistant OS e-Meeting').setFontSize(9).setForegroundColor('#64748b');
    doc.saveAndClose();
    file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    var pdfFile = folder.createFile(file.getAs(MimeType.PDF).setName(title + '.pdf'));
    pdfFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.fullName || '', 'EXPORT_EMEETING_PACKAGE', 'EMeetingMaster', meetingId, 'สร้างชุดเอกสารประชุม: ' + (m.title || ''), null, {docUrl:file.getUrl(), pdfUrl:pdfFile.getUrl()});
    return {success:true, message:'สร้างชุดเอกสารประชุมแล้ว', docUrl:file.getUrl(), pdfUrl:pdfFile.getUrl(), docId:doc.getId(), pdfId:pdfFile.getId()};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getPublicEMeetingLiveStateV716(meetingId, token) {
  try {
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (String(token || '') !== String(meeting.liveToken || '') && String(token || '') !== String(meeting.rsvpToken || '')) return {success:false, message:'ลิงก์ไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    var live = haosV714LiveByMeeting_(meetingId);
    var agendas = haosV714RowsByMeeting_(haosV714AgendaSheet_(), meetingId, haosV714AgendaRow_);
    agendas.sort(function(a,b){return Number(a.no || 0) - Number(b.no || 0);});
    var current = null;
    var liveItem = live ? live.item : null;
    for (var i = 0; i < agendas.length; i++) if (agendas[i].agendaId === (liveItem && liveItem.currentAgendaId)) current = agendas[i];
    if (!current) current = agendas[0] || {};
    var docs = haosV715DocumentsByMeeting_(meetingId).filter(function(d){ return d.isShared !== false && (!current.agendaId || d.agendaId === current.agendaId || !d.agendaId); });
    var docUrl = (liveItem && liveItem.currentDocumentUrl) || current.documentUrl || (docs[0] && docs[0].documentUrl) || '';
    var docName = (liveItem && liveItem.currentDocumentName) || current.documentName || (docs[0] && docs[0].documentName) || current.title || '';
    var updatedAt = (liveItem && liveItem.updatedAt) || haosV714Fmt_(new Date(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss');
    return {success:true, meetingId:meetingId, currentAgendaId:current.agendaId || '', currentAgendaTitle:current.title || '', documentUrl:docUrl, documentName:docName, syncMessage:(liveItem && liveItem.syncMessage) || current.title || '', updatedAt:updatedAt, fingerprint:[current.agendaId || '', docUrl, docName, updatedAt].join('|')};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function updateEMeetingActionStatusV716(actionId, status, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var sh = haosV714ActionSheet_();
    var found = haosV714FindRowById_(sh, actionId, 1);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบงานติดตาม'};
    var action = haosV715ActionRow_(found.values);
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(action.meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์แก้ไขงานติดตาม'};
    sh.getRange(found.row, 8).setValue(status || 'รอดำเนินการ');
    try {
      if (action.ownerPhone) createNotificationV732_(action.ownerPhone, 'อัปเดตงานติดตามจากมติ: ' + (action.title || '-') + ' — ' + (status || 'รอดำเนินการ'), 'e-Meeting Action', actionId, 'ปกติ', {module:'eMeeting', action:'openEMeeting', createdBy:actor.phone});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.fullName || '', 'UPDATE_EMEETING_ACTION', 'EMeetingActionItems', actionId, 'อัปเดตงานติดตาม: ' + (action.title || ''), action, {status:status});
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตงานติดตามแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV716() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.16-emeeting-phase4-final', functions:[
    'getEMeetingModuleDataV716','getEMeetingDetailV716','runEMeetingReminderV716',
    'exportEMeetingPackageV716','getPublicEMeetingLiveStateV716','updateEMeetingActionStatusV716'
  ]};
}

// ========================= v70.18: account approval, invite links, email notification preferences =========================
function haosV718CleanPhone_(phone) {
  return String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
}

function haosV718UserSheet_() {
  var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  if (!sh) {
    Setup();
    sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  }
  ensureHeaders_(sh, [
    'Phone (Username)', 'PIN (Password)', 'Full Name', 'Position', 'Department', 'Email',
    'PDPA Status', 'Role', 'Timestamp', 'Email Notify', 'Account Status', 'Last Login',
    'Approved At', 'Approved By', 'Invite Token', 'Email Notify Preferences JSON'
  ], '#d4edda');
  sh.getRange('A:B').setNumberFormat('@');
  return sh;
}

function haosV718InviteSheet_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_('InviteLinks', [
    'Token', 'Created At', 'Created By Phone', 'Created By Name', 'Expires At',
    'Max Uses', 'Used Count', 'Active', 'Note', 'Last Used At'
  ], '#e0f2fe');
}

function haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone) {
  var sh = haosV718UserSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = haosV718CleanPhone_(phone);
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][0]) === target) return {sheet:sh, row:i + 1, values:data[i]};
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV718UserObjFromRow_(row, sheet) {
  sheet = sheet || haosV718UserSheet_();
  var prefCol = getColumnIndex_(sheet, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
  return {
    phone:haosV718CleanPhone_(row[0]),
    pin:String(row[1] || '').replace(/^'/, ''),
    fullName:row[2] || '',
    position:row[3] || '',
    department:row[4] || '',
    email:row[5] || '',
    role:row[7] || 'User',
    emailNotify:row[9] || 'On',
    accountStatus:row[10] || 'Active',
    emailNotifyPreferences:prefCol > 0 ? row[prefCol - 1] || '' : ''
  };
}

function haosV718IsAdminRole_(role) {
  role = String(role || '');
  return role === 'Admin' || role === 'Super Admin' || /super\s*admin|^admin$/i.test(role);
}

function haosV718CanAdminUsers_(phone) {
  var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
  if (!found) return false;
  var u = haosV718UserObjFromRow_(found.values, found.sheet);
  return haosV718IsAdminRole_(u.role) && u.accountStatus !== 'Inactive' && u.accountStatus !== 'Pending' && u.accountStatus !== 'Rejected';
}

function haosV718DefaultEmailPrefs_(emailNotify) {
  if (emailNotify === 'Off') return {mode:'none', categories:[]};
  return {mode:'all', categories:['schedule','approval','dailyReport','itBooking','itAsset','itRepair','eMeeting','helpChat','system']};
}

function haosV718ParseEmailPrefs_(raw, emailNotify) {
  if (!raw) return haosV718DefaultEmailPrefs_(emailNotify);
  try {
    var parsed = JSON.parse(String(raw || '{}'));
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') return haosV718DefaultEmailPrefs_(emailNotify);
    if (!parsed.mode) parsed.mode = emailNotify === 'Off' ? 'none' : 'all';
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed.categories)) parsed.categories = [];
    return parsed;
  } catch(e) {
    return haosV718DefaultEmailPrefs_(emailNotify);
  }
}

function haosV718NotificationCategory_(type) {
  type = String(type || '').toLowerCase();
  if (type.indexOf('approval') !== -1 || type.indexOf('อนุมัติ') !== -1) return 'approval';
  if (type.indexOf('schedule') !== -1 || type.indexOf('งาน') !== -1 || type.indexOf('today') !== -1) return 'schedule';
  if (type.indexOf('report') !== -1 || type.indexOf('รายงาน') !== -1) return 'dailyReport';
  if (type.indexOf('booking') !== -1 || type.indexOf('zoom') !== -1 || type.indexOf('จอง') !== -1) return 'itBooking';
  if (type.indexOf('license') !== -1 || type.indexOf('asset') !== -1 || type.indexOf('ทรัพย์สิน') !== -1) return 'itAsset';
  if (type.indexOf('repair') !== -1 || type.indexOf('helpdesk') !== -1 || type.indexOf('แจ้งซ่อม') !== -1) return 'itRepair';
  if (type.indexOf('meeting') !== -1 || type.indexOf('rsvp') !== -1) return 'eMeeting';
  if (type.indexOf('chat') !== -1 || type.indexOf('help') !== -1) return 'helpChat';
  return 'system';
}

function haosV718ShouldSendEmail_(user, type) {
  if (!user || !user.email) return false;
  var prefs = haosV718ParseEmailPrefs_(user.emailNotifyPreferences, user.emailNotify);
  if (prefs.mode === 'none' || user.emailNotify === 'Off') return false;
  if (prefs.mode === 'all') return true;
  var cat = haosV718NotificationCategory_(type);
  return prefs.categories.indexOf(cat) !== -1;
}

function haosV718SendEmailNotification_(phone, message, type, entityId, priority) {
  try {
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
    if (!found) return {sent:false, reason:'user-not-found'};
    var u = haosV718UserObjFromRow_(found.values, found.sheet);
    if (!haosV718ShouldSendEmail_(u, type)) return {sent:false, reason:'preference-off'};
    var subject = '[Health Assistant OS] ' + (type || 'การแจ้งเตือน');
    var body = [
      'เรียน ' + (u.fullName || 'ผู้ใช้งาน'),
      '',
      message || '',
      '',
      'ประเภท: ' + (type || 'ทั่วไป'),
      'ความสำคัญ: ' + (priority || 'ปกติ'),
      entityId ? ('รหัสอ้างอิง: ' + entityId) : '',
      '',
      'คุณสามารถเปลี่ยนการรับอีเมลได้ที่ ตั้งค่าโปรไฟล์ส่วนตัว > การแจ้งเตือนทางอีเมล'
    ].filter(function(x){ return x !== ''; }).join('\n');
    MailApp.sendEmail(u.email, subject, body);
    return {sent:true};
  } catch(e) {
    try { logError_(phone || '', 'haosV718SendEmailNotification_', e, {type:type, entityId:entityId}); } catch(ignore) {}
    return {sent:false, reason:e.toString()};
  }
}

function createNotificationV718_(phone, message, type, entityId, priority) {
  var sh = getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS, ['Notif ID','Target Phone','Message','Is Read','Timestamp','Type','Entity ID','Priority','Email Sent At','Email Status'], '#f8d7da');
  ensureHeaders_(sh, ['Notif ID','Target Phone','Message','Is Read','Timestamp','Type','Entity ID','Priority','Email Sent At','Email Status'], '#f8d7da');
  var email = haosV718SendEmailNotification_(phone, message, type, entityId, priority);
  var now = new Date();
  sh.appendRow([
    'NTF-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8),
    "'" + haosV718CleanPhone_(phone),
    message || '',
    false,
    now,
    type || 'ทั่วไป',
    entityId || '',
    priority || 'ปกติ',
    email.sent ? now : '',
    email.sent ? 'Sent' : (email.reason || 'Skipped')
  ]);
}

function loginAccount(phone, pin) {
  try {
    var sh = haosV718UserSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var lastLoginCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Last Login');
    var prefCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
    var target = haosV718CleanPhone_(phone);
    var pinText = String(pin || '').replace(/^'/, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][0]) === target && String(data[i][1] || '').replace(/^'/, '') === pinText) {
        var accountStatus = data[i][10] || 'Active';
        if (accountStatus === 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้อยู่ระหว่างรอ Admin / Super Admin อนุมัติ กรุณารอการอนุมัติก่อนเข้าใช้งาน'};
        if (accountStatus === 'Rejected') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ไม่ได้รับการอนุมัติ กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
        if (accountStatus === 'Inactive') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ถูกปิดใช้งาน กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
        if (lastLoginCol > 0) sh.getRange(i + 1, lastLoginCol).setValue(new Date());
        logAudit_(target, data[i][2], 'LOGIN', 'User', target, 'เข้าสู่ระบบสำเร็จ', null, null);
        return {success:true, userData:{
          phone:haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][0]), fullName:data[i][2], position:data[i][3], department:data[i][4],
          email:data[i][5], role:data[i][7] || 'User', emailNotify:data[i][9] || 'On', accountStatus:accountStatus,
          emailNotifyPreferences:prefCol > 0 ? data[i][prefCol - 1] || '' : ''
        }};
      }
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ข้อมูลไม่ถูกต้อง'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV718CheckInvite_(token) {
  token = String(token || '').trim();
  if (!token) return {valid:false};
  var sh = haosV718InviteSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var now = new Date();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0] || '') !== token) continue;
    var active = data[i][7] === true || String(data[i][7]).toLowerCase() === 'true';
    var exp = data[i][4] ? new Date(data[i][4]) : null;
    var maxUses = Number(data[i][5] || 0);
    var used = Number(data[i][6] || 0);
    if (!active) return {valid:false, message:'ลิงก์เชิญนี้ถูกปิดใช้งานแล้ว'};
    if (exp && !isNaN(exp.getTime()) && exp.getTime() < now.getTime()) return {valid:false, message:'ลิงก์เชิญนี้หมดอายุแล้ว'};
    if (maxUses > 0 && used >= maxUses) return {valid:false, message:'ลิงก์เชิญนี้ถูกใช้ครบจำนวนแล้ว'};
    return {valid:true, row:i + 1, sheet:sh, values:data[i]};
  }
  return {valid:false, message:'ไม่พบลิงก์เชิญนี้'};
}

function registerAccount(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    if (String(data.phone || '').length !== 10) return {success:false, message:'กรุณากรอกเบอร์โทรศัพท์ 10 หลัก'};
    var sh = haosV718UserSheet_();
    var usersData = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var phone = haosV718CleanPhone_(data.phone);
    for (var i = 1; i < usersData.length; i++) {
      if (haosV718CleanPhone_(usersData[i][0]) === phone) return {success:false, message:'เบอร์นี้ลงทะเบียนไว้แล้ว'};
    }
    var inviteToken = String(data.inviteToken || '').trim();
    var invite = inviteToken ? haosV718CheckInvite_(inviteToken) : {valid:false};
    if (inviteToken && !invite.valid) return {success:false, message:invite.message || 'ลิงก์เชิญไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    var generatedPIN = Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
    var status = invite.valid ? 'Active' : 'Pending';
    var now = new Date();
    var row = [
      "'" + phone, "'" + generatedPIN, data.fullName || '', data.position || '', data.department || '', data.email || '',
      'Accepted', 'User', now, 'On', status, '', invite.valid ? now : '', invite.valid ? haosV718CleanPhone_(invite.values[2]) : '',
      inviteToken || '', JSON.stringify(haosV718DefaultEmailPrefs_('On'))
    ];
    sh.appendRow(row);
    if (invite.valid) {
      invite.sheet.getRange(invite.row, 7).setValue(Number(invite.values[6] || 0) + 1);
      invite.sheet.getRange(invite.row, 10).setValue(now);
    }
    logAudit_(phone, data.fullName || '', invite.valid ? 'REGISTER_INVITE' : 'REGISTER_PENDING', 'User', phone, invite.valid ? 'สมัครผ่าน invite link และเปิดใช้งานทันที' : 'ลงทะเบียนผู้ใช้ใหม่ รออนุมัติ', null, data);
    if (invite.valid) {
      return {success:true, approved:true, message:'ลงทะเบียนผ่านลิงก์เชิญสำเร็จ! รหัส PIN ของคุณคือ: ' + generatedPIN, pin:generatedPIN};
    }
    try {
      var admins = getAdminUsersData();
      if (admins && admins.success) {
        admins.data.filter(function(u){return haosV718IsAdminRole_(u.role) && u.accountStatus !== 'Inactive';}).forEach(function(a) {
          createNotificationV718_(a.phone, 'มีสมาชิกใหม่รออนุมัติ: ' + (data.fullName || phone), 'User Approval', phone, 'ปกติ');
        });
      }
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, pending:true, message:'ลงทะเบียนสำเร็จและอยู่ระหว่างรอ Admin / Super Admin อนุมัติ หลังอนุมัติแล้วใช้ PIN นี้เข้าสู่ระบบ: ' + generatedPIN, pin:generatedPIN};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function updateUserProfile(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(data.phone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบบัญชีผู้ใช้งานนี้ในระบบ'};
    var sh = found.sheet;
    sh.getRange(found.row, 4).setValue(data.position || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 5).setValue(data.department || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 6).setValue(data.email || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 10).setValue(data.emailNotify || 'On');
    var prefCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
    if (prefCol > 0 && data.emailNotifyPreferences) {
      sh.getRange(found.row, prefCol).setValue(typeof data.emailNotifyPreferences === 'string' ? data.emailNotifyPreferences : JSON.stringify(data.emailNotifyPreferences));
    }
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตข้อมูลส่วนตัวเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getAdminUsersData() {
  try {
    var sh = haosV718UserSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var prefCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
    var approvedAtCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Approved At');
    var approvedByCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Approved By');
    var result = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var rawPin = String(data[i][1] || '').replace(/^'/, '');
      result.push({
        phone:haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][0]), pin:rawPin, name:data[i][2], position:data[i][3], department:data[i][4],
        email:data[i][5], role:data[i][7] || 'User', emailNotify:data[i][9] || 'On', accountStatus:data[i][10] || 'Active',
        lastLogin:data[i][11] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][11], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : (data[i][11] || '-'),
        approvedAt:approvedAtCol > 0 && data[i][approvedAtCol - 1] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][approvedAtCol - 1], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '',
        approvedBy:approvedByCol > 0 ? haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][approvedByCol - 1]) : '',
        emailNotifyPreferences:prefCol > 0 ? data[i][prefCol - 1] || '' : ''
      });
    }
    return {success:true, data:result};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function approveUserRegistrationV718(targetPhone, adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(targetPhone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน'};
    var sh = found.sheet;
    var oldStatus = found.values[10] || 'Active';
    sh.getRange(found.row, 11).setValue('Active');
    var approvedAtCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Approved At');
    var approvedByCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Approved By');
    if (approvedAtCol > 0) sh.getRange(found.row, approvedAtCol).setValue(new Date());
    if (approvedByCol > 0) sh.getRange(found.row, approvedByCol).setValue("'" + haosV718CleanPhone_(adminPhone));
    var u = haosV718UserObjFromRow_(sh.getRange(found.row, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0], sh);
    try { MailApp.sendEmail(u.email, '[Health Assistant OS] บัญชีของคุณได้รับการอนุมัติแล้ว', 'บัญชี Health Assistant OS ของคุณได้รับการอนุมัติแล้ว\n\nเบอร์โทรศัพท์: ' + u.phone + '\nPIN: ' + u.pin + '\n\nเข้าสู่ระบบได้ทันที'); } catch(ignore) {}
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'APPROVE_USER', 'User', targetPhone, 'อนุมัติสมาชิกใหม่', {status:oldStatus}, {status:'Active'});
    return {success:true, message:'อนุมัติผู้ใช้งานเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function rejectUserRegistrationV718(targetPhone, adminPhone, reason) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(targetPhone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน'};
    if (haosV718CleanPhone_(targetPhone) === '0868246621') return {success:false, message:'ไม่อนุญาตให้ปฏิเสธบัญชี Master Admin'};
    var sh = found.sheet;
    var oldStatus = found.values[10] || 'Active';
    sh.getRange(found.row, 11).setValue('Rejected');
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'REJECT_USER', 'User', targetPhone, 'ปฏิเสธสมาชิกใหม่: ' + (reason || ''), {status:oldStatus}, {status:'Rejected', reason:reason || ''});
    return {success:true, message:'ปฏิเสธคำขอสมัครเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function createUserInviteLinkV718(adminPhone, options) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    options = options || {};
    var admin = getUserProfileByPhone_(adminPhone) || {};
    var days = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(options.days || 30), 365));
    var maxUses = Math.max(0, Number(options.maxUses || 0));
    var token = 'INV-' + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 18).toUpperCase();
    var expires = new Date();
    expires.setDate(expires.getDate() + days);
    var sh = haosV718InviteSheet_();
    sh.appendRow([token, new Date(), "'" + haosV718CleanPhone_(adminPhone), admin.fullName || admin.name || '', expires, maxUses, 0, true, options.note || '', '']);
    var url = getPublicAppBaseUrl_() + '/?invite=' + encodeURIComponent(token);
    logAudit_(adminPhone, admin.fullName || '', 'CREATE_INVITE_LINK', 'InviteLink', token, 'สร้างลิงก์เชิญสมัครสมาชิก', null, {expiresAt:expires, maxUses:maxUses});
    return {success:true, token:token, url:url, expiresAt:Utilities.formatDate(expires, 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm'), maxUses:maxUses};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getUserInviteLinksV718(adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var sh = haosV718InviteSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var now = new Date();
    var out = [];
    for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      var exp = data[i][4] ? new Date(data[i][4]) : null;
      var active = data[i][7] === true || String(data[i][7]).toLowerCase() === 'true';
      out.push({
        token:data[i][0], createdAt:data[i][1] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][1], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : data[i][1],
        createdByPhone:haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][2]), createdByName:data[i][3] || '',
        expiresAt:exp && !isNaN(exp.getTime()) ? Utilities.formatDate(exp, 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '',
        expired:exp && !isNaN(exp.getTime()) ? exp.getTime() < now.getTime() : false,
        maxUses:Number(data[i][5] || 0), usedCount:Number(data[i][6] || 0), active:active,
        note:data[i][8] || '', url:getPublicAppBaseUrl_() + '/?invite=' + encodeURIComponent(data[i][0])
      });
    }
    return {success:true, data:out};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function revokeUserInviteLinkV718(token, adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var sh = haosV718InviteSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]) === String(token)) {
        sh.getRange(i + 1, 8).setValue(false);
        logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'REVOKE_INVITE_LINK', 'InviteLink', token, 'ปิดลิงก์เชิญสมัครสมาชิก', null, null);
        return {success:true, message:'ปิดลิงก์เชิญแล้ว'};
      }
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบลิงก์เชิญ'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getEmailNotificationSettingsV718(phone) {
  try {
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน'};
    var u = haosV718UserObjFromRow_(found.values, found.sheet);
    return {success:true, email:u.email || '', emailNotify:u.emailNotify || 'On', preferences:haosV718ParseEmailPrefs_(u.emailNotifyPreferences, u.emailNotify)};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function saveEmailNotificationSettingsV718(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(data.phone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน'};
    var sh = found.sheet;
    var emailNotify = data.emailNotify || (data.preferences && data.preferences.mode === 'none' ? 'Off' : 'On');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 10).setValue(emailNotify);
    if (data.email !== undefined) sh.getRange(found.row, 6).setValue(data.email || '');
    var prefCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
    if (prefCol > 0) sh.getRange(found.row, prefCol).setValue(JSON.stringify(data.preferences || haosV718DefaultEmailPrefs_(emailNotify)));
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกการแจ้งเตือนทางอีเมลแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function runEmailNotificationDigestV718(targetPhone) {
  try {
    if (targetPhone && typeof targetPhone === 'object') targetPhone = '';
    var sh = getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS, ['Notif ID','Target Phone','Message','Is Read','Timestamp','Type','Entity ID','Priority','Email Sent At','Email Status'], '#f8d7da');
    ensureHeaders_(sh, ['Notif ID','Target Phone','Message','Is Read','Timestamp','Type','Entity ID','Priority','Email Sent At','Email Status'], '#f8d7da');
    var sentCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Sent At');
    var statusCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Status');
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var target = haosV718CleanPhone_(targetPhone);
    var sent = 0, skipped = 0;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][sentCol - 1]) continue;
      var phone = haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][1]);
      if (target && phone !== target) continue;
      var isRead = data[i][3] === true || String(data[i][3]).toLowerCase() === 'true';
      if (isRead) continue;
      var res = haosV718SendEmailNotification_(phone, data[i][2], data[i][5] || 'ทั่วไป', data[i][6] || '', data[i][7] || 'ปกติ');
      if (res.sent) {
        sh.getRange(i + 1, sentCol).setValue(new Date());
        sh.getRange(i + 1, statusCol).setValue('Sent');
        sent++;
      } else {
        sh.getRange(i + 1, statusCol).setValue(res.reason || 'Skipped');
        skipped++;
      }
    }
    return {success:true, sent:sent, skipped:skipped, message:'ส่งอีเมลแจ้งเตือนแล้ว ' + sent + ' รายการ'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function setupEmailNotificationDigestTriggerV718(adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t) {
      if (t.getHandlerFunction && t.getHandlerFunction() === 'runEmailNotificationDigestV718') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t);
    });
    ScriptApp.newTrigger('runEmailNotificationDigestV718').timeBased().everyMinutes(15).create();
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'SETUP_EMAIL_NOTIFICATION_TRIGGER', 'System', 'EmailDigest', 'ตั้ง trigger ส่งอีเมลแจ้งเตือนทุก 15 นาที', null, null);
    return {success:true, message:'ตั้งค่า trigger ส่งอีเมลแจ้งเตือนทุก 15 นาทีแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

}

// ========================= v70.19: Super Admin direct user profile editing =========================
function haosV719CanSuperEditUsers_(phone) {
  var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
  if (!found) return false;
  var u = haosV718UserObjFromRow_(found.values, found.sheet);
  return u.role === 'Super Admin' && u.accountStatus !== 'Inactive' && u.accountStatus !== 'Pending' && u.accountStatus !== 'Rejected';
}

function superAdminUpdateUserProfileV719(targetPhone, payload, actorPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV719CanSuperEditUsers_(actorPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    payload = payload || {};
    var target = haosV718CleanPhone_(targetPhone);
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(target);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน'};
    var sh = found.sheet;
    var before = haosV718UserObjFromRow_(found.values, sh);
    var fullName = String(payload.fullName || '').trim();
    var position = String(payload.position || '').trim();
    var department = String(payload.department || '').trim();
    var email = String(payload.email || '').trim();
    var role = String(payload.role || before.role || 'User').trim();
    var accountStatus = String(payload.accountStatus || before.accountStatus || 'Active').trim();
    var validRoles = ['User','Head','Admin','Super Admin'];
    var validStatuses = ['Active','Pending','Inactive','Rejected'];
    if (!fullName) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อ-นามสกุล'};
    if (validRoles.indexOf(role) === -1) return {success:false, message:'ระดับสิทธิ์ไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    if (validStatuses.indexOf(accountStatus) === -1) return {success:false, message:'สถานะบัญชีไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    if (target === '0868246621') {
      role = 'Super Admin';
      accountStatus = 'Active';
    }
    sh.getRange(found.row, 3).setValue(fullName);
    sh.getRange(found.row, 4).setValue(position);
    sh.getRange(found.row, 5).setValue(department);
    sh.getRange(found.row, 6).setValue(email);
    sh.getRange(found.row, 8).setValue(role);
    sh.getRange(found.row, 11).setValue(accountStatus);
    var after = haosV718UserObjFromRow_(sh.getRange(found.row, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0], sh);
    logAudit_(actorPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone), 'SUPER_ADMIN_UPDATE_USER_PROFILE', 'User', target, 'Super Admin แก้ไขข้อมูลผู้ใช้งาน: ' + fullName, before, after);
    return {success:true, message:'แก้ไขข้อมูลผู้ใช้งานเรียบร้อยแล้ว', user:after};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV719() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.19-super-admin-user-edit', functions:['superAdminUpdateUserProfileV719']};
}

// ========================= v70.21: e-Meeting public UX + delegate organization =========================
function haosV721ParticipantHeaders_() {
  return [
    'Participant ID','Meeting ID','Name','Position','Department','Phone','Email','Role','RSVP Status',
    'Delegate Name','Delegate Position','Delegate Department','RSVP Note','Responded At','Created At','Updated At'
  ];
}

function haosV721HeaderMap_(sheet) {
  var values = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
  var map = {};
  values.forEach(function(h, idx) {
    h = String(h || '').trim();
    if (h) map[h] = idx;
  });
  return map;
}

function haosV721Read_(row, map, name, fallbackIdx) {
  var idx = map[name];
  if (idx !== undefined) return row[idx] || '';
  return fallbackIdx >= 0 ? row[fallbackIdx] || '' : '';
}

function haosV721Set_(row, map, name, value) {
  var idx = map[name];
  if (idx !== undefined) row[idx] = value;
}

function haosV714ParticipantSheet_() {
  return haosV714Sheet_('EMeetingParticipants', haosV721ParticipantHeaders_(), '#dcfce7');
}

function haosV714ParticipantRow_(row, safe) {
  var sh = haosV714ParticipantSheet_();
  var map = haosV721HeaderMap_(sh);
  var out = {
    participantId:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Participant ID', 0) || ''),
    meetingId:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Meeting ID', 1) || ''),
    name:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Name', 2) || ''),
    position:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Position', 3) || ''),
    department:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Department', 4) || ''),
    role:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Role', 7) || 'ผู้เข้าร่วม'),
    rsvpStatus:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'RSVP Status', 8) || 'ยังไม่ตอบรับ'),
    delegateName:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Delegate Name', 9) || ''),
    delegatePosition:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Delegate Position', 10) || ''),
    delegateDepartment:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Delegate Department', -1) || ''),
    rsvpNote:String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'RSVP Note', 11) || ''),
    respondedAt:haosV714Fmt_(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Responded At', 12), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')
  };
  if (!safe) {
    out.phone = haosV714Clean_(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Phone', 5));
    out.email = String(haosV721Read_(row, map, 'Email', 6) || '');
  }
  return out;
}

function saveEMeetingParticipantsV714(meetingId, participants, actor, replace) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (!haosV714CanEdit_(meeting, actor)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์จัดการรายชื่อผู้เข้าประชุม'};
    participants = Array.isArray(participants) ? participants : [participants || {}];
    var sh = haosV714ParticipantSheet_();
    var map = haosV721HeaderMap_(sh);
    if (replace) haosV714DeleteRowsByMeeting_(sh, meetingId);
    var now = new Date();
    var rows = [];
    participants.forEach(function(p) {
      if (!p || !p.name) return;
      var row = new Array(sh.getLastColumn()).fill('');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Participant ID', p.participantId || haosV714Id_('PTC'));
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Meeting ID', meetingId);
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Name', p.name || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Position', p.position || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Department', p.department || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Phone', "'" + haosV714Clean_(p.phone));
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Email', p.email || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Role', p.role || 'ผู้เข้าร่วม');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'RSVP Status', p.rsvpStatus || 'ยังไม่ตอบรับ');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Delegate Name', p.delegateName || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Delegate Position', p.delegatePosition || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Delegate Department', p.delegateDepartment || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'RSVP Note', p.rsvpNote || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Responded At', p.respondedAt || '');
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Created At', now);
      haosV721Set_(row, map, 'Updated At', now);
      rows.push(row);
    });
    if (rows.length) sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, sh.getLastColumn()).setValues(rows);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกรายชื่อผู้เข้าประชุมแล้ว', count:rows.length};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function submitEMeetingRsvpV714(meetingId, token, response) {
  try {
    response = response || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบการประชุม'};
    if (String(token || '') !== String(meeting.rsvpToken || '')) return {success:false, message:'ลิงก์ตอบรับไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    var sh = haosV714ParticipantSheet_();
    var map = haosV721HeaderMap_(sh);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var row = 0;
    var idIdx = map['Participant ID'] !== undefined ? map['Participant ID'] : 0;
    var meetingIdx = map['Meeting ID'] !== undefined ? map['Meeting ID'] : 1;
    var nameIdx = map['Name'] !== undefined ? map['Name'] : 2;
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][meetingIdx] || '') !== String(meetingId || '')) continue;
      if (String(data[i][idIdx] || '') === String(response.participantId || '') || String(data[i][nameIdx] || '') === String(response.name || '')) {
        row = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!row) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายชื่อผู้เข้าประชุม'};
    var now = new Date();
    if (map['RSVP Status'] !== undefined) sh.getRange(row, map['RSVP Status'] + 1).setValue(response.rsvpStatus || 'เข้าร่วมประชุม');
    if (map['Delegate Name'] !== undefined) sh.getRange(row, map['Delegate Name'] + 1).setValue(response.delegateName || '');
    if (map['Delegate Position'] !== undefined) sh.getRange(row, map['Delegate Position'] + 1).setValue(response.delegatePosition || '');
    if (map['Delegate Department'] !== undefined) sh.getRange(row, map['Delegate Department'] + 1).setValue(response.delegateDepartment || '');
    if (map['RSVP Note'] !== undefined) sh.getRange(row, map['RSVP Note'] + 1).setValue(response.rsvpNote || '');
    if (map['Responded At'] !== undefined) sh.getRange(row, map['Responded At'] + 1).setValue(now);
    if (map['Updated At'] !== undefined) sh.getRange(row, map['Updated At'] + 1).setValue(now);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกการตอบรับแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getUserDirectoryForPickerV721() {
  try {
    var sh = typeof haosV718UserSheet_ === 'function' ? haosV718UserSheet_() : getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sh) return {success:true, data:[]};
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var statusCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Account Status');
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var status = String(statusCol > 0 ? (data[i][statusCol - 1] || 'Active') : (data[i][10] || 'Active')).trim() || 'Active';
      if (/^(Inactive|Pending|Rejected)$/i.test(status)) continue;
      var name = String(data[i][2] || '').trim();
      if (!name) continue;
      out.push({
        phone:haosV714Clean_(data[i][0]),
        name:name,
        fullName:name,
        position:String(data[i][3] || '').trim(),
        department:String(data[i][4] || '').trim(),
        email:String(data[i][5] || '').trim(),
        role:String(data[i][7] || 'User').trim(),
        accountStatus:status
      });
    }
    out.sort(function(a,b){ return String(a.department || '').localeCompare(String(b.department || ''), 'th') || String(a.name || '').localeCompare(String(b.name || ''), 'th'); });
    return {success:true, data:out, version:'v70.21-user-directory'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[]};
  }
}

}

// ========================= v70.22: active user directory, e-Meeting delete, IT repair memo =========================
function haosV722CleanPhone_(value) {
  return String(value || '').replace(/'/g, '').replace(/\D/g, '');
}

function haosV722UserStatusAllowed_(status) {
  status = String(status || 'Active').trim() || 'Active';
  return !/^(Inactive|Pending|Rejected)$/i.test(status);
}

function getAllUsers() {
  try {
    var sheetUsers = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (!sheetUsers) return { success: true, data: [], version: 'v70.22-active-users' };
    var usersData = sheetUsers.getDataRange().getValues();
    var usersList = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < usersData.length; i++) {
      var status = String(usersData[i][10] || 'Active').trim() || 'Active';
      if (!haosV722UserStatusAllowed_(status)) continue;
      var name = String(usersData[i][2] || '').trim();
      if (!name) continue;
      usersList.push({
        phone: haosV722CleanPhone_(usersData[i][0]),
        name: name,
        fullName: name,
        position: String(usersData[i][3] || '').trim(),
        department: String(usersData[i][4] || '').trim(),
        email: String(usersData[i][5] || '').trim(),
        role: String(usersData[i][7] || 'User').trim(),
        accountStatus: status
      });
    }
    usersList.sort(function(a,b) {
      return String(a.department || '').localeCompare(String(b.department || ''), 'th') ||
        String(a.name || '').localeCompare(String(b.name || ''), 'th');
    });
    return { success: true, data: usersList, version: 'v70.22-active-users' };
  } catch (error) {
    return { success: false, message: error.toString(), data: [] };
  }
}

function haosV722DeleteRowsByIdColumn_(sheet, id, col) {
  if (!sheet) return 0;
  col = col || 1;
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var count = 0;
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (String(data[i][col - 1] || '') === String(id || '')) {
      sheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      count++;
    }
  }
  return count;
}

function deleteEMeetingV722(meetingId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var meeting = haosV714MeetingById_(meetingId);
    if (!meeting) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายการ e-Meeting ที่ต้องการลบ'};
    var actorPhone = haosV714Clean_(actor.phone || actor.userPhone || '');
    if (!actorPhone || haosV714Clean_(meeting.organizerPhone) !== actorPhone) {
      return {success:false, message:'ลบได้เฉพาะผู้สร้างรายการ e-Meeting นี้เท่านั้น'};
    }
    var before = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(meeting));
    haosV714DeleteRowsByMeeting_(haosV714AgendaSheet_(), meetingId);
    haosV714DeleteRowsByMeeting_(haosV714ParticipantSheet_(), meetingId);
    haosV714DeleteRowsByMeeting_(haosV714DocumentSheet_(), meetingId);
    haosV714DeleteRowsByMeeting_(haosV714ActionSheet_(), meetingId);
    haosV722DeleteRowsByIdColumn_(haosV714LiveSheet_(), meetingId, 1);
    var found = haosV714FindRowById_(haosV714MeetingSheet_(), meetingId, 1);
    if (found) haosV714MeetingSheet_().deleteRow(found.row);
    logAudit_(actorPhone, actor.name || actor.fullName || '', 'DELETE_EMEETING', 'EMeetingMaster', meetingId, meeting.title || '', before, null);
    return {success:true, message:'ลบ e-Meeting และข้อมูลประกอบเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV722RepairStatusLabel_(status) {
  var s = String(status || '').trim();
  var map = {
    'New':'ใหม่',
    'Assigned':'รับงานแล้ว',
    'In Progress':'กำลังดำเนินการ',
    'Waiting Parts':'รออะไหล่',
    'Waiting Vendor':'รอช่าง/ผู้จำหน่าย',
    'Resolved':'แก้ไขแล้ว',
    'Closed':'ปิดงาน',
    'Cancelled':'ยกเลิก'
  };
  return map[s] || s || 'ใหม่';
}

function haosV722PriorityLabel_(priority) {
  var s = String(priority || '').trim();
  var map = {'Low':'ต่ำ','Normal':'ปกติ','High':'สูง','Critical':'วิกฤต'};
  return map[s] || s || 'ปกติ';
}

function haosV722ThaiDate_(value) {
  try {
    var d = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value || new Date());
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) d = new Date();
    var months = ['มกราคม','กุมภาพันธ์','มีนาคม','เมษายน','พฤษภาคม','มิถุนายน','กรกฎาคม','สิงหาคม','กันยายน','ตุลาคม','พฤศจิกายน','ธันวาคม'];
    return Utilities.formatDate(d, 'GMT+7', 'd') + ' ' + months[d.getMonth()] + ' ' + (d.getFullYear() + 543);
  } catch(e) {
    return '';
  }
}

function haosV722RepairTicketById_(ticketId) {
  var tickets = getAllITRepairTicketsV70_();
  for (var i = 0; i < tickets.length; i++) {
    if (String(tickets[i].ticketId || '') === String(ticketId || '')) return tickets[i];
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV722RepairAssetById_(assetId) {
  if (!assetId) return null;
  var assets = getAllITAssetsV70_();
  for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) {
    if (String(assets[i].assetId || '') === String(assetId || '')) return assets[i];
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV722SplitUrls_(value) {
  var raw = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!raw) return [];
  var seen = {};
  return raw.split(/[\n,]+/).map(function(x){ return String(x || '').trim(); }).filter(function(url) {
    if (!url || seen[url]) return false;
    seen[url] = true;
    return true;
  });
}

function haosV722RepairMemoModel_(ticket, actor) {
  var asset = haosV722RepairAssetById_(ticket.assetId) || {};
  var dept = ticket.requesterDepartment || asset.ownerDepartment || (actor && actor.department) || 'กลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล';
  var assetName = ticket.assetName || asset.assetName || 'อุปกรณ์ IT';
  var assetCode = ticket.assetCode || asset.assetCode || asset.assetNumber || '';
  var reason = ticket.problemDetail || ticket.problemTitle || 'พบปัญหาการใช้งานและต้องการให้ตรวจสอบ/ซ่อมแซม';
  var subject = 'ขอแจ้งซ่อม' + assetName;
  var body = 'ด้วย' + dept + ' มีความประสงค์ขอแจ้งซ่อม' + assetName +
    (assetCode ? ' หมายเลขครุภัณฑ์/รหัสทรัพย์สิน ' + assetCode : '') +
    (ticket.location ? ' สถานที่ใช้งาน ' + ticket.location : '') +
    ' เนื่องจาก' + reason;
  return {
    memoNo: 'นย 0033.015/',
    agency: 'สำนักงานสาธารณสุขจังหวัดนครนายก กลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล โทร 0 3738 6390 ต่อ 108',
    dateText: haosV722ThaiDate_(ticket.reportedAt || ticket.createdAt || new Date()),
    subject: subject,
    to: 'นายแพทย์สาธารณสุขจังหวัดนครนายก',
    body: body,
    consideration: 'จึงเรียนมาเพื่อโปรดพิจารณา',
    signerName: 'นายสิรเศรษฐ นันทธีโร',
    signerPosition: 'นักวิเคราะห์นโยบายและแผนชำนาญการพิเศษ',
    signerRole: 'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล',
    attachmentTitle: 'รายละเอียดแนบท้ายบันทึกข้อความที่ นย 0033.015/ ลงวันที่ ' + haosV722ThaiDate_(new Date()),
    attachmentSubject: 'เรื่อง ขออนุมัติแจ้งซ่อม' + assetName,
    ticket: ticket,
    asset: asset,
    statusLabel: haosV722RepairStatusLabel_(ticket.status),
    priorityLabel: haosV722PriorityLabel_(ticket.priority),
    attachments: haosV722SplitUrls_(ticket.attachmentUrl),
    sourceTemplate: {
      subject: 'ขอแจ้งซ่อมเครื่องปรับอากาศ ยี่ห้อ KINDAI',
      sampleAssetNo: '4102-001-0008/09',
      sampleReason: 'มีเสียงดังผิดปกติ ในขณะที่เครื่องปรับอากาศกำลังทำความเย็น'
    }
  };
}

function getITRepairDigitalMemoV722(ticketId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var ticket = haosV722RepairTicketById_(ticketId);
    if (!ticket) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อม'};
    return {success:true, memo:haosV722RepairMemoModel_(ticket, actor), version:'v70.22-it-repair-memo'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV722RepairMemoFolder_() {
  var name = 'HealthAssistant_ITRepairMemos';
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  return folders.hasNext() ? folders.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(name);
}

function createITRepairMemoDocumentV722(ticketId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var res = getITRepairDigitalMemoV722(ticketId, actor);
    if (!res || !res.success) return res;
    var m = res.memo;
    var title = 'บันทึกข้อความแจ้งซ่อม_' + (m.ticket.ticketNo || m.ticket.ticketId || ticketId);
    var doc = DocumentApp.create(title);
    var body = doc.getBody();
    body.clear();
    body.appendParagraph('บันทึกข้อความ').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph('ส่วนราชการ  ' + m.agency);
    body.appendParagraph('ที่  ' + m.memoNo + '        วันที่  ' + m.dateText);
    var subjectPara = body.appendParagraph('เรื่อง  ' + m.subject);
    subjectPara.editAsText().setBold(true);
    body.appendParagraph('เรียน  ' + m.to);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('        ' + m.body);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('        ' + m.consideration);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('(' + m.signerName + ')').setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph(m.signerPosition).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph(m.signerRole).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendPageBreak();
