    var attachTitlePara = body.appendParagraph(m.attachmentTitle);
    attachTitlePara.editAsText().setBold(true);
    var attachSubjectPara = body.appendParagraph(m.attachmentSubject);
    attachSubjectPara.editAsText().setBold(true);
    body.appendParagraph('เลขที่ใบแจ้งซ่อม: ' + (m.ticket.ticketNo || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('ทรัพย์สิน/อุปกรณ์: ' + (m.ticket.assetName || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('รหัสทรัพย์สิน/ครุภัณฑ์: ' + (m.ticket.assetCode || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('สถานที่: ' + (m.ticket.location || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('ผู้แจ้ง: ' + (m.ticket.requesterName || '-') + ' / ' + (m.ticket.requesterDepartment || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('หมวดปัญหา: ' + (m.ticket.problemCategory || '-') + '   ความเร่งด่วน: ' + m.priorityLabel);
    body.appendParagraph('สถานะล่าสุด: ' + m.statusLabel);
    body.appendParagraph('รายละเอียดปัญหา:');
    body.appendParagraph(m.ticket.problemDetail || m.ticket.problemTitle || '-');
    if (m.attachments && m.attachments.length) {
      body.appendParagraph('ไฟล์แนบ/รูปประกอบ:');
      m.attachments.forEach(function(url){ body.appendListItem(url); });
    }
    doc.saveAndClose();
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
    var folder = haosV722RepairMemoFolder_();
    try { folder.addFile(file); DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file); } catch(ignore) {}
    var pdf = folder.createFile(file.getBlob().getAs(MimeType.PDF).setName(title + '.pdf'));
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.fullName || '', 'CREATE_IT_REPAIR_MEMO', 'ITRepairTicket', ticketId, title, null, {docUrl:file.getUrl(), pdfUrl:pdf.getUrl()});
    return {success:true, docUrl:file.getUrl(), pdfUrl:pdf.getUrl(), message:'สร้างเอกสารบันทึกข้อความแจ้งซ่อมเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    var msg = e && e.toString ? e.toString() : String(e);
    if (/DocumentApp\.create|auth\/documents|permission to call DocumentApp|authorization/i.test(msg)) {
      return {
        success:false,
        code:'DOCUMENT_SCOPE_REQUIRED',
        message:'โปรเจกต์ Apps Script ยังไม่ได้รับสิทธิ์สร้าง Google Docs กรุณาอัปเดตไฟล์ appsscript.json ให้มี scope https://www.googleapis.com/auth/documents แล้ว Run ฟังก์ชัน authorizeDocumentExportsV722 หนึ่งครั้งจาก Apps Script editor เพื่อกดอนุญาตสิทธิ์ จากนั้น Deploy เวอร์ชันใหม่'
      };
    }
    return {success:false, message:msg};
  }
}

function authorizeDocumentExportsV722() {
  var doc = DocumentApp.create('HAOS Document Export Authorization Check');
  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  file.setTrashed(true);
  return {success:true, message:'อนุญาตสิทธิ์ DocumentApp / DriveApp สำหรับสร้างเอกสารเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV722() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.22-active-users-emeeting-delete-itrepair-memo', functions:['getAllUsers','deleteEMeetingV722','getITRepairDigitalMemoV722','createITRepairMemoDocumentV722','authorizeDocumentExportsV722']};
}

// ========================= v70.23: IT Helpdesk official document workflow =========================
function haosV723RepairDocumentHeaders_() {
  return [
    'Document ID','Ticket ID','Ticket No','Document No','Document Status','Current Step',
    'Requester Head Status','Requester Head By','Requester Head At','Requester Head Note',
    'Central Registry No','Central Registry Received At','Central Registry Receiver','Central Registry Status','Central Registry Note',
    'Digital Registry No','Digital Registry Received At','Digital Registry Receiver','Digital Registry Status','Digital Registry Note',
    'IT Inspection Status','IT Inspector','IT Inspected At','IT Cause','IT Repair Plan','IT Parts','IT Estimated Cost','IT Recommendation',
    'Digital Head Status','Digital Head By','Digital Head At','Digital Head Note',
    'Procurement Registry No','Procurement Received At','Procurement Receiver','Procurement Status','Procurement Note',
    'Approval Status','Approver','Approved At','Approved Budget','Vendor','Procurement Ref','Repair Result','Actual Cost','Accepted By','Closed At',
    'Doc URL','PDF URL','Locked At','Created By','Created At','Updated At'
  ];
}

function getITRepairDocumentSheetV723_() {
  return getOrCreateSheet_('ITRepairDocuments', haosV723RepairDocumentHeaders_(), '#fef3c7');
}

function haosV723RepairDocumentRowToObject_(row, headers) {
  headers = headers || haosV723RepairDocumentHeaders_();
  var raw = {};
  headers.forEach(function(h, i){ raw[h] = row[i] || ''; });
  function dt(k){ return haosV70DateTime_(raw[k]); }
  return {
    documentId: raw['Document ID'] || '',
    ticketId: raw['Ticket ID'] || '',
    ticketNo: raw['Ticket No'] || '',
    documentNo: raw['Document No'] || '',
    documentStatus: raw['Document Status'] || 'ร่างเอกสาร',
    currentStep: raw['Current Step'] || 'draft',
    requesterHead: {status:raw['Requester Head Status']||'', by:raw['Requester Head By']||'', at:dt('Requester Head At'), note:raw['Requester Head Note']||''},
    centralRegistry: {no:raw['Central Registry No']||'', receivedAt:dt('Central Registry Received At'), receiver:raw['Central Registry Receiver']||'', status:raw['Central Registry Status']||'', note:raw['Central Registry Note']||''},
    digitalRegistry: {no:raw['Digital Registry No']||'', receivedAt:dt('Digital Registry Received At'), receiver:raw['Digital Registry Receiver']||'', status:raw['Digital Registry Status']||'', note:raw['Digital Registry Note']||''},
    itInspection: {status:raw['IT Inspection Status']||'', inspector:raw['IT Inspector']||'', inspectedAt:dt('IT Inspected At'), cause:raw['IT Cause']||'', repairPlan:raw['IT Repair Plan']||'', parts:raw['IT Parts']||'', estimatedCost:raw['IT Estimated Cost']||'', recommendation:raw['IT Recommendation']||''},
    digitalHead: {status:raw['Digital Head Status']||'', by:raw['Digital Head By']||'', at:dt('Digital Head At'), note:raw['Digital Head Note']||''},
    procurement: {no:raw['Procurement Registry No']||'', receivedAt:dt('Procurement Received At'), receiver:raw['Procurement Receiver']||'', status:raw['Procurement Status']||'', note:raw['Procurement Note']||''},
    closeout: {approvalStatus:raw['Approval Status']||'', approver:raw['Approver']||'', approvedAt:dt('Approved At'), approvedBudget:raw['Approved Budget']||'', vendor:raw['Vendor']||'', procurementRef:raw['Procurement Ref']||'', repairResult:raw['Repair Result']||'', actualCost:raw['Actual Cost']||'', acceptedBy:raw['Accepted By']||'', closedAt:dt('Closed At')},
    docUrl: raw['Doc URL'] || '',
    pdfUrl: raw['PDF URL'] || '',
    lockedAt: dt('Locked At'),
    createdBy: raw['Created By'] || '',
    createdAt: dt('Created At'),
    updatedAt: dt('Updated At')
  };
}

function haosV723FindRepairDocument_(ticketId, createIfMissing, actor) {
  var sh = getITRepairDocumentSheetV723_();
  var headers = haosV723RepairDocumentHeaders_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][1] || '') === String(ticketId || '')) {
      return {sheet:sh, row:i + 1, headers:headers, doc:haosV723RepairDocumentRowToObject_(data[i], headers)};
    }
  }
  if (!createIfMissing) return {sheet:sh, row:-1, headers:headers, doc:null};
  var ticket = haosV722RepairTicketById_(ticketId) || {};
  var now = haosV70Now_();
  var row = headers.map(function(){ return ''; });
  function set(name, value) {
    var idx = headers.indexOf(name);
    if (idx >= 0) row[idx] = value;
  }
  set('Document ID', haosV70Id_('RPD'));
  set('Ticket ID', ticket.ticketId || ticketId || '');
  set('Ticket No', ticket.ticketNo || '');
  set('Document No', 'นย 0033.015/');
  set('Document Status', 'ร่างเอกสาร');
  set('Current Step', 'draft');
  var requesterHeadDefault = haosV723RequesterHeadDefault_(ticket, actor);
  set('Requester Head By', requesterHeadDefault.name || '');
  set('Requester Head Note', requesterHeadDefault.note || '');
  if (requesterHeadDefault.autoApproved) {
    set('Requester Head Status', requesterHeadDefault.status || 'เห็นชอบอัตโนมัติ');
    set('Requester Head At', now);
    set('Document Status', 'รอสารบัญกลางสำนักงานรับเรื่อง');
    set('Current Step', 'centralRegistry');
  }
  set('Created By', (actor && (actor.name || actor.fullName || actor.phone)) || '');
  set('Created At', now);
  set('Updated At', now);
  sh.appendRow(row);
  return {sheet:sh, row:sh.getLastRow(), headers:headers, doc:haosV723RepairDocumentRowToObject_(row, headers)};
}

function haosV723WorkflowState_(done, negative) {
  if (negative) return 'blocked';
  return done ? 'done' : 'pending';
}

function haosV723IsNegativeStatus_(status) {
  status = String(status || '');
  return status.indexOf('ไม่เห็นชอบ') !== -1 || status.indexOf('ส่งกลับ') !== -1 || status.indexOf('ยกเลิก') !== -1;
}

function haosV723IsActiveUserStatus_(status) {
  status = String(status || 'Active').trim();
  return !/^(inactive|disabled|pending|rejected|ปิดใช้งาน|ระงับ|รออนุมัติ|ไม่อนุมัติ)$/i.test(status);
}

function haosV723AdminFallbackApprover_(actor) {
  actor = actor || {};
  var actorName = actor.name || actor.fullName || actor.phone || '';
  if (/admin|super/i.test(String(actor.role || '')) && actorName) {
    return {name:actorName, position:'Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบ', phone:actor.phone || '', source:'actor-admin'};
  }
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (sh && sh.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        var role = String(data[i][7] || '');
        var status = data[i][10] || 'Active';
        if (!haosV723IsActiveUserStatus_(status)) continue;
        if (/super\s*admin|admin|ผู้ดูแลระบบ/i.test(role)) {
          return {
            phone:String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim(),
            name:data[i][2] || 'Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบ',
            position:data[i][3] || role || 'Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบ',
            source:'system-admin'
          };
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(ignore) {}
  return {name:actorName || 'Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบ', position:'Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบ', phone:actor.phone || '', source:'system-default'};
}

function haosV726RepairSignerDefaults_(ticket, actor) {
  ticket = ticket || {};
  actor = actor || {};
  var actorName = actor.name || actor.fullName || actor.phone || '';
  var actorDept = actor.department || '';
  var defaults = {
    centralRegistry: {role:'สารบัญกลางสำนักงาน', name:'', position:'เจ้าหน้าที่สารบัญกลางสำนักงาน', note:'รับเรื่องและลงเลขรับกลางของสำนักงาน'},
    digitalRegistry: {role:'ธุรการกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', name:'', position:'ธุรการกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', note:'รับเรื่อง ลงเลขรับกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล และส่งต่อ IT'},
    itRepairReviewer: {role:'เจ้าหน้าที่ IT ผู้พิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไข', name:'', position:'เจ้าหน้าที่ IT', note:'พิจารณาอาการ สาเหตุ แนวทางซ่อม ราคาประเมิน และข้อเสนอแนะ'},
    digitalHead: {role:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', name:'', position:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', note:'รับรองผลพิจารณาการซ่อมก่อนส่งพัสดุ/ผู้บริหาร'},
    procurementRegistry: {role:'ธุรการ ส่งงานพัสดุ / บันทึกรับเรื่องพัสดุ', name:'', position:'เจ้าหน้าที่พัสดุ', note:'รับเรื่องพัสดุ เลขรับ และสถานะการดำเนินการ'},
    secretariat: {role:'ธุรการ งานเลขานุการ', name:'', position:'เจ้าหน้าที่งานเลขานุการ', note:'เตรียมนำเสนอผู้บริหารลงนามอนุมัติ'},
    finalApprover: {role:'นายแพทย์สาธารณสุขจังหวัดนครนายก ผู้ลงนามอนุมัติเอกสารสูงสุด', name:'', position:'นายแพทย์สาธารณสุขจังหวัดนครนายก', note:'ผู้อนุมัติเอกสารสูงสุดของกระบวนการ'}
  };
  var users = [];
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (sh && sh.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        var status = data[i][10] || 'Active';
        if (!haosV723IsActiveUserStatus_(status)) continue;
        users.push({
          phone:String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim(),
          name:data[i][2] || '',
          position:data[i][3] || '',
          department:data[i][4] || '',
          role:data[i][7] || ''
        });
      }
    }
  } catch(ignore) {}
  function has(v, re){ return re.test(String(v || '')); }
  function pick(test) {
    for (var i = 0; i < users.length; i++) {
      if (test(users[i])) return users[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function applyUser(key, user) {
    if (!user) return;
    defaults[key].phone = user.phone || '';
    defaults[key].name = user.name || defaults[key].name || '';
    defaults[key].position = user.position || defaults[key].position || '';
    defaults[key].department = user.department || '';
    defaults[key].source = 'users';
  }
  applyUser('centralRegistry', pick(function(u){ return has(u.department + ' ' + u.position + ' ' + u.role, /สารบัญกลาง|สารบรรณ|ธุรการ|บริหารทั่วไป|อำนวยการ/i); }));
  applyUser('digitalRegistry', pick(function(u){ return has(u.department, /สุขภาพดิจิทัล|ดิจิทัล|IT|ไอที/i) && has(u.position + ' ' + u.role, /ธุรการ|สารบัญ|สารบรรณ|admin|ผู้ดูแลระบบ/i); }));
  applyUser('itRepairReviewer', pick(function(u){ return has(u.department, /สุขภาพดิจิทัล|ดิจิทัล|IT|ไอที/i) && has(u.name + ' ' + u.position + ' ' + u.role, /IT|ไอที|คอมพิวเตอร์|เทคโนโลยี|ผู้ดูแลระบบ|ซ่อม|ช่าง/i); }));
  applyUser('digitalHead', pick(function(u){ return has(u.department, /สุขภาพดิจิทัล|ดิจิทัล|IT|ไอที/i) && haosIsHeadRoleOrPositionV685_(u.role, u.position); }));
  applyUser('procurementRegistry', pick(function(u){ return has(u.department + ' ' + u.position + ' ' + u.role, /พัสดุ|จัดซื้อ|จัดจ้าง/i); }));
  applyUser('secretariat', pick(function(u){ return has(u.department + ' ' + u.position + ' ' + u.role, /เลขานุการ|ผู้บริหาร|บริหารทั่วไป|อำนวยการ/i); }));
  applyUser('finalApprover', pick(function(u){ return has(u.name + ' ' + u.position + ' ' + u.role, /นายแพทย์สาธารณสุขจังหวัด|สาธารณสุขจังหวัดนครนายก|นพ\.?สสจ|ผู้บริหารสูงสุด/i); }));
  if (!defaults.itRepairReviewer.name && actorName && (/สุขภาพดิจิทัล|ดิจิทัล|IT|ไอที/i.test(String(actorDept || '')) || /admin|super/i.test(String(actor.role || '')))) {
    defaults.itRepairReviewer.name = actorName;
    defaults.itRepairReviewer.position = actor.position || defaults.itRepairReviewer.position;
    defaults.itRepairReviewer.department = actorDept || defaults.itRepairReviewer.department || '';
    defaults.itRepairReviewer.source = 'actor';
  }
  return defaults;
}

function haosV723RequesterHeadDefault_(ticket, actor) {
  ticket = ticket || {};
  var dept = ticket.requesterDepartment || '';
  try {
    var target = haosNormalizeDeptV685_(dept);
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    if (target && sh && sh.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        var rowDept = data[i][4] || '';
        var role = data[i][7] || '';
        var position = data[i][3] || '';
        var status = data[i][10] || 'Active';
        if (!haosV723IsActiveUserStatus_(status)) continue;
        if (haosNormalizeDeptV685_(rowDept) !== target) continue;
        if (haosIsHeadRoleOrPositionV685_(role, position)) {
          return {
            found:true,
            autoApproved:false,
            phone:String(data[i][0] || '').replace(/'/g,'').trim(),
            name:data[i][2] || '',
            position:position || 'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงาน',
            department:rowDept || dept,
            source:'department-head',
            note:'ดึงข้อมูลหัวหน้ากลุ่มงานจากระบบอัตโนมัติ'
          };
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(ignore) {}
  var admin = haosV723AdminFallbackApprover_(actor);
  return {
    found:false,
    autoApproved:true,
    phone:admin.phone || '',
    name:admin.name || 'Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบ',
    position:admin.position || 'Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบ',
    department:dept,
    source:admin.source || 'admin-fallback',
    status:'เห็นชอบอัตโนมัติ',
    note:'ไม่พบหัวหน้ากลุ่มงานของผู้แจ้งในระบบ จึงระบุเป็นอนุมัติโดย Admin / ผู้ดูแลระบบอัตโนมัติ เพื่อป้องกันช่องลงนามว่าง'
  };
}

function haosV723ApplyRequesterHeadFallback_(doc, ticket, actor) {
  doc = doc || {};
  doc.requesterHead = doc.requesterHead || {};
  var def = haosV723RequesterHeadDefault_(ticket, actor);
  doc.requesterHeadDefault = def;
  if (!doc.requesterHead.by) doc.requesterHead.by = def.name || '';
  if (!doc.requesterHead.position) doc.requesterHead.position = def.position || '';
  if (!doc.requesterHead.note && def.note) doc.requesterHead.note = def.note;
  if (def.autoApproved && !doc.requesterHead.status) {
    doc.requesterHead.status = def.status || 'เห็นชอบอัตโนมัติ';
    doc.requesterHead.at = doc.requesterHead.at || '';
    doc.documentStatus = doc.documentStatus === 'ร่างเอกสาร' ? 'รอสารบัญกลางสำนักงานรับเรื่อง' : doc.documentStatus;
    doc.currentStep = doc.currentStep === 'draft' ? 'centralRegistry' : doc.currentStep;
  }
  return doc;
}

function haosV723RepairWorkflowSteps_(ticket, doc) {
  doc = doc || {};
  var rh = doc.requesterHead || {};
  var cr = doc.centralRegistry || {};
  var dr = doc.digitalRegistry || {};
  var it = doc.itInspection || {};
  var dh = doc.digitalHead || {};
  var pr = doc.procurement || {};
  var cl = doc.closeout || {};
  var steps = [
    {key:'ticket', no:1, title:'สร้างใบแจ้งซ่อม', owner:'ผู้แจ้ง', status:'สร้างแล้ว', by:ticket.requesterName||'', at:ticket.reportedAt||ticket.createdAt||'', note:ticket.problemTitle||'', state:'done'},
    {key:'draft', no:2, title:'สร้างร่างบันทึกข้อความ', owner:'ระบบ', status:'พร้อมใช้งาน', by:doc.createdBy||'ระบบ', at:doc.createdAt||ticket.createdAt||'', note:'ระบบดึงข้อมูลจากใบแจ้งซ่อมและทะเบียนทรัพย์สิน', state:'done'},
    {key:'requesterHead', no:3, title:'เสนอหัวหน้ากลุ่มงานของผู้แจ้ง', owner:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานผู้แจ้ง', status:rh.status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:rh.by||'', at:rh.at||'', note:rh.note||'', state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!rh.status, haosV723IsNegativeStatus_(rh.status))},
    {key:'centralRegistry', no:4, title:'สารบัญกลางสำนักงานรับเรื่อง', owner:'ธุรการ/สารบัญกลาง', status:cr.status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:cr.receiver||'', at:cr.receivedAt||'', note:(cr.no?'เลขรับ '+cr.no:'')+(cr.note?(' - '+cr.note):''), state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!(cr.status||cr.no), false)},
    {key:'digitalRegistry', no:5, title:'ธุรการกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัลรับเรื่อง', owner:'ธุรการกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', status:dr.status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:dr.receiver||'', at:dr.receivedAt||'', note:(dr.no?'เลขรับ '+dr.no:'')+(dr.note?(' - '+dr.note):''), state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!(dr.status||dr.no), false)},
    {key:'forwardIt', no:6, title:'ส่งต่อให้เจ้าหน้าที่ IT ผู้พิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไข', owner:'กลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', status:(dr.status||dr.no)?'ส่งต่อแล้ว':'รอดำเนินการ', by:dr.receiver||'', at:dr.receivedAt||'', note:'ส่งต่อจากธุรการกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!(dr.status||dr.no), false)},
    {key:'itInspection', no:7, title:'เจ้าหน้าที่ IT ผู้พิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไข', owner:'เจ้าหน้าที่ IT ผู้พิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไข', status:it.status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:it.inspector||'', at:it.inspectedAt||'', note:it.recommendation||it.repairPlan||it.cause||'', state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!(it.status||it.inspector||it.repairPlan), false)},
    {key:'digitalHead', no:8, title:'เสนอหัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', owner:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', status:dh.status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:dh.by||'', at:dh.at||'', note:dh.note||'', state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!dh.status, haosV723IsNegativeStatus_(dh.status))},
    {key:'procurement', no:9, title:'ส่งงานพัสดุ / บันทึกรับเรื่องพัสดุ', owner:'งานพัสดุ', status:pr.status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:pr.receiver||'', at:pr.receivedAt||'', note:(pr.no?'เลขรับ '+pr.no:'')+(pr.note?(' - '+pr.note):''), state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!(pr.status||pr.no), false)},
    {key:'closeout', no:10, title:'อนุมัติ / ดำเนินการซ่อม / รับคืนและปิดงาน', owner:'พัสดุ/ผู้อนุมัติ/IT', status:cl.approvalStatus||'รอดำเนินการ', by:cl.approver||cl.acceptedBy||'', at:cl.closedAt||cl.approvedAt||'', note:cl.repairResult||cl.procurementRef||'', state:haosV723WorkflowState_(!!(cl.approvalStatus||cl.repairResult||cl.closedAt), haosV723IsNegativeStatus_(cl.approvalStatus))}
  ];
  var firstPending = true;
  steps.forEach(function(s){
    if (s.state === 'pending' && firstPending) { s.state = 'active'; firstPending = false; }
  });
  return steps;
}

function haosV723RepairMemoModel_(ticket, actor) {
  var m = haosV722RepairMemoModel_(ticket, actor);
  var found = haosV723FindRepairDocument_(ticket.ticketId, true, actor);
  var doc = found.doc || {};
  doc = haosV723ApplyRequesterHeadFallback_(doc, ticket, actor);
  var signerRoles = haosV726RepairSignerDefaults_(ticket, actor);
  m.document = doc;
  m.requesterHeadDefault = doc.requesterHeadDefault || {};
  m.signerRoles = signerRoles;
  m.memoNo = doc.documentNo || m.memoNo;
  m.documentStatus = doc.documentStatus || 'ร่างเอกสาร';
  m.currentStep = doc.currentStep || 'draft';
  m.workflow = {steps:haosV723RepairWorkflowSteps_(ticket, doc)};
  var itSigner = signerRoles.itRepairReviewer || {};
  var secretariatSigner = signerRoles.secretariat || {};
  var finalSigner = signerRoles.finalApprover || {};
  m.registryStamps = [
    {title:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานผู้แจ้ง', value:(doc.requesterHead||{}).status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:(doc.requesterHead||{}).by||'', at:(doc.requesterHead||{}).at||'', note:(doc.requesterHead||{}).note||''},
    {title:'สารบัญกลางสำนักงาน', value:(doc.centralRegistry||{}).no||((doc.centralRegistry||{}).status||'รอดำเนินการ'), by:(doc.centralRegistry||{}).receiver||'', at:(doc.centralRegistry||{}).receivedAt||'', note:(doc.centralRegistry||{}).note||''},
    {title:'ธุรการกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', value:(doc.digitalRegistry||{}).no||((doc.digitalRegistry||{}).status||'รอดำเนินการ'), by:(doc.digitalRegistry||{}).receiver||'', at:(doc.digitalRegistry||{}).receivedAt||'', note:(doc.digitalRegistry||{}).note||''},
    {title:'เจ้าหน้าที่ IT ผู้พิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไข', value:(doc.itInspection||{}).status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:(doc.itInspection||{}).inspector||'', at:(doc.itInspection||{}).inspectedAt||'', note:(doc.itInspection||{}).recommendation||('ผู้รับผิดชอบ: ' + (itSigner.name || itSigner.role || 'เจ้าหน้าที่ IT'))},
    {title:'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล', value:(doc.digitalHead||{}).status||'รอดำเนินการ', by:(doc.digitalHead||{}).by||'', at:(doc.digitalHead||{}).at||'', note:(doc.digitalHead||{}).note||''},
    {title:'งานพัสดุ / บันทึกรับเรื่องพัสดุ', value:(doc.procurement||{}).no||((doc.procurement||{}).status||'รอดำเนินการ'), by:(doc.procurement||{}).receiver||'', at:(doc.procurement||{}).receivedAt||'', note:(doc.procurement||{}).note||''},
    {title:'ธุรการ งานเลขานุการ', value:(doc.closeout||{}).approvalStatus?'นำเสนอผู้บริหารแล้ว':'รอดำเนินการ', by:secretariatSigner.name||'', at:(doc.closeout||{}).approvedAt||'', note:secretariatSigner.note||'เตรียมนำเสนอผู้บริหารลงนามอนุมัติ'},
    {title:'ผู้อนุมัติเอกสารสูงสุด', value:(doc.closeout||{}).approvalStatus||'รอดำเนินการ', by:(doc.closeout||{}).approver||finalSigner.name||'', at:(doc.closeout||{}).approvedAt||'', note:(doc.closeout||{}).repairResult||finalSigner.role||''}
  ];
  m.sourceTemplate = {
    subject:'ขออนุมัติแจ้งซ่อมครุภัณฑ์คอมพิวเตอร์',
    sampleAssetNo:'7440-0101-001/61, 7440-0101-077/65',
    sampleReason:'เครื่องทำงานช้า ค้างบ่อย ตรวจพบอุปกรณ์จัดเก็บข้อมูลและหน่วยความจำเสื่อมสภาพ'
  };
  return m;
}

function getITRepairDigitalMemoV722(ticketId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var ticket = haosV722RepairTicketById_(ticketId);
    if (!ticket) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อม'};
    return {success:true, memo:haosV723RepairMemoModel_(ticket, actor), version:'v70.26-it-repair-reviewer-role'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV723SetRepairDocFields_(ticketId, fields, actor) {
  var found = haosV723FindRepairDocument_(ticketId, true, actor);
  var sh = found.sheet;
  var headers = found.headers;
  var row = found.row;
  Object.keys(fields || {}).forEach(function(name){
    var idx = headers.indexOf(name);
    if (idx >= 0) sh.getRange(row, idx + 1).setValue(fields[name]);
  });
  var updatedIdx = headers.indexOf('Updated At');
  if (updatedIdx >= 0) sh.getRange(row, updatedIdx + 1).setValue(haosV70Now_());
}

function updateITRepairDocumentStepV723(ticketId, stepKey, payload, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    payload = payload || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var ticket = haosV722RepairTicketById_(ticketId);
    if (!ticket) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบใบแจ้งซ่อม'};
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var actorName = actor.name || actor.fullName || actor.phone || '';
    var fields = {};
    var ticketStatus = '';
    if (stepKey === 'requesterHead') {
      var requesterHeadDefault = haosV723RequesterHeadDefault_(ticket, actor);
      fields = {
        'Requester Head Status': payload.status || requesterHeadDefault.status || 'เห็นชอบ',
        'Requester Head By': payload.by || requesterHeadDefault.name || actorName,
        'Requester Head At': payload.at || now,
        'Requester Head Note': payload.note || requesterHeadDefault.note || '',
        'Document Status': payload.status === 'ส่งกลับแก้ไข' ? 'ส่งกลับให้แก้ไข' : (payload.status === 'ไม่เห็นชอบ' ? 'ไม่เห็นชอบโดยหัวหน้ากลุ่มงานผู้แจ้ง' : 'รอสารบัญกลางสำนักงานรับเรื่อง'),
        'Current Step': 'centralRegistry'
      };
    } else if (stepKey === 'centralRegistry') {
      fields = {
        'Central Registry No': payload.no || '',
        'Central Registry Received At': payload.receivedAt || now,
        'Central Registry Receiver': payload.receiver || actorName,
        'Central Registry Status': payload.status || 'รับเรื่องแล้ว',
        'Central Registry Note': payload.note || '',
        'Document Status': 'รอธุรการกลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัลรับเรื่อง',
        'Current Step': 'digitalRegistry'
      };
    } else if (stepKey === 'digitalRegistry') {
      fields = {
        'Digital Registry No': payload.no || '',
        'Digital Registry Received At': payload.receivedAt || now,
        'Digital Registry Receiver': payload.receiver || actorName,
        'Digital Registry Status': payload.status || 'รับเรื่องแล้ว',
        'Digital Registry Note': payload.note || '',
        'Document Status': 'รอเจ้าหน้าที่ IT ผู้พิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไข',
        'Current Step': 'itInspection'
      };
      ticketStatus = 'Assigned';
    } else if (stepKey === 'itInspection') {
      fields = {
        'IT Inspection Status': payload.status || 'พิจารณาแล้ว',
        'IT Inspector': payload.inspector || actorName,
        'IT Inspected At': payload.inspectedAt || now,
        'IT Cause': payload.cause || '',
        'IT Repair Plan': payload.repairPlan || '',
        'IT Parts': payload.parts || '',
        'IT Estimated Cost': payload.estimatedCost || '',
        'IT Recommendation': payload.recommendation || '',
        'Document Status': 'รอหัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัลรับรอง',
        'Current Step': 'digitalHead'
      };
      ticketStatus = 'In Progress';
    } else if (stepKey === 'digitalHead') {
      fields = {
        'Digital Head Status': payload.status || 'รับรอง',
        'Digital Head By': payload.by || actorName,
        'Digital Head At': payload.at || now,
        'Digital Head Note': payload.note || '',
        'Document Status': payload.status === 'ส่งกลับแก้ไข' ? 'ส่งกลับให้ IT แก้ไข' : (payload.status === 'ไม่เห็นชอบ' ? 'ไม่เห็นชอบโดยหัวหน้ากลุ่มงานสุขภาพดิจิทัล' : 'รอส่งงานพัสดุ'),
        'Current Step': 'procurement'
      };
    } else if (stepKey === 'procurement') {
      fields = {
        'Procurement Registry No': payload.no || '',
        'Procurement Received At': payload.receivedAt || now,
        'Procurement Receiver': payload.receiver || actorName,
        'Procurement Status': payload.status || 'รับเรื่องแล้ว',
        'Procurement Note': payload.note || '',
        'Document Status': 'รออนุมัติ/ดำเนินการซ่อม',
        'Current Step': 'closeout'
      };
      if (String(payload.status || '').indexOf('รอ') !== -1) ticketStatus = 'Waiting Vendor';
    } else if (stepKey === 'closeout') {
      fields = {
        'Approval Status': payload.approvalStatus || 'อนุมัติ/ดำเนินการแล้ว',
        'Approver': payload.approver || actorName,
        'Approved At': payload.approvedAt || now,
        'Approved Budget': payload.approvedBudget || '',
        'Vendor': payload.vendor || '',
        'Procurement Ref': payload.procurementRef || '',
        'Repair Result': payload.repairResult || '',
        'Actual Cost': payload.actualCost || '',
        'Accepted By': payload.acceptedBy || '',
        'Closed At': payload.closedAt || '',
        'Document Status': payload.closedAt ? 'ปิดงานเอกสารแล้ว' : 'อยู่ระหว่างดำเนินการซ่อม/ตรวจรับ',
        'Current Step': payload.closedAt ? 'closed' : 'closeout'
      };
      if (payload.closedAt) ticketStatus = 'Closed';
      else if (payload.repairResult) ticketStatus = 'Resolved';
    } else {
      return {success:false, message:'ไม่รู้จักขั้นตอนเอกสาร'};
    }
    haosV723SetRepairDocFields_(ticketId, fields, actor);
    if (ticketStatus) {
      try { updateITRepairTicketStatusV70(ticketId, ticketStatus, 'อัปเดตจาก workflow เอกสารแจ้งซ่อม: ' + (fields['Document Status'] || stepKey), actor); } catch(ignore) {}
    }
    ticket = haosV722RepairTicketById_(ticketId) || ticket;
    var memo = haosV723RepairMemoModel_(ticket, actor);
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actorName, 'UPDATE_IT_REPAIR_DOCUMENT_STEP', 'ITRepairDocument', ticketId, stepKey, null, payload);
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตขั้นตอนเอกสารแล้ว', memo:memo};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV723SaveRepairDocumentLinks_(ticketId, docUrl, pdfUrl, actor) {
  haosV723SetRepairDocFields_(ticketId, {
    'Doc URL': docUrl || '',
    'PDF URL': pdfUrl || ''
  }, actor || {});
}

function createITRepairMemoDocumentV722(ticketId, actor) {
  try {
    actor = actor || {};
    var res = getITRepairDigitalMemoV722(ticketId, actor);
    if (!res || !res.success) return res;
    var m = res.memo;
    var title = 'บันทึกข้อความแจ้งซ่อม_' + (m.ticket.ticketNo || m.ticket.ticketId || ticketId);
    var doc = DocumentApp.create(title);
    var body = doc.getBody();
    body.clear();
    body.appendParagraph('บันทึกข้อความ').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph('ส่วนราชการ  ' + m.agency);
    body.appendParagraph('ที่  ' + m.memoNo + '        วันที่  ' + m.dateText);
    body.appendParagraph('สถานะเอกสาร  ' + (m.documentStatus || 'ร่างเอกสาร'));
    var subjectPara = body.appendParagraph('เรื่อง  ' + m.subject);
    subjectPara.editAsText().setBold(true);
    body.appendParagraph('เรียน  ' + m.to);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('        ' + m.body);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('        ' + m.consideration);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('(' + m.signerName + ')').setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph(m.signerPosition).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph(m.signerRole).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    body.appendParagraph('เส้นทางดำเนินเอกสาร').editAsText().setBold(true);
    (m.workflow && m.workflow.steps || []).forEach(function(s){
      body.appendParagraph(s.no + '. ' + s.title + ' - ' + (s.status || '-') + (s.by ? (' โดย ' + s.by) : '') + (s.at ? (' เมื่อ ' + s.at) : ''));
      if (s.note) body.appendParagraph('   หมายเหตุ: ' + s.note);
    });
    body.appendPageBreak();
    body.appendParagraph('รายละเอียดแนบท้ายบันทึกข้อความ').editAsText().setBold(true);
    body.appendParagraph('เลขที่ใบแจ้งซ่อม: ' + (m.ticket.ticketNo || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('ทรัพย์สิน/อุปกรณ์: ' + (m.ticket.assetName || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('รหัสทรัพย์สิน/ครุภัณฑ์: ' + (m.ticket.assetCode || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('สถานที่: ' + (m.ticket.location || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('ผู้แจ้ง: ' + (m.ticket.requesterName || '-') + ' / ' + (m.ticket.requesterDepartment || '-'));
    body.appendParagraph('หมวดปัญหา: ' + (m.ticket.problemCategory || '-') + '   ความเร่งด่วน: ' + m.priorityLabel);
    body.appendParagraph('สถานะล่าสุด: ' + m.statusLabel);
    body.appendParagraph('รายละเอียดปัญหา:');
    body.appendParagraph(m.ticket.problemDetail || m.ticket.problemTitle || '-');
    var it = (m.document || {}).itInspection || {};
    if (it.inspector || it.repairPlan || it.recommendation) {
      body.appendParagraph('');
      body.appendParagraph('ผลพิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไขโดยเจ้าหน้าที่ IT').editAsText().setBold(true);
      body.appendParagraph('ผู้พิจารณาการซ่อมแก้ไข: ' + (it.inspector || '-'));
      body.appendParagraph('สาเหตุ: ' + (it.cause || '-'));
      body.appendParagraph('แนวทางซ่อม: ' + (it.repairPlan || '-'));
      body.appendParagraph('อะไหล่/อุปกรณ์: ' + (it.parts || '-'));
      body.appendParagraph('ราคาประเมิน: ' + (it.estimatedCost || '-'));
      body.appendParagraph('ข้อเสนอแนะ: ' + (it.recommendation || '-'));
    }
    if (m.attachments && m.attachments.length) {
      body.appendParagraph('');
      body.appendParagraph('ไฟล์แนบ/รูปประกอบ:').editAsText().setBold(true);
      m.attachments.forEach(function(url){ body.appendListItem(url); });
    }
    doc.saveAndClose();
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
    var folder = haosV722RepairMemoFolder_();
    try { folder.addFile(file); DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file); } catch(ignore) {}
    var pdf = folder.createFile(file.getBlob().getAs(MimeType.PDF).setName(title + '.pdf'));
    haosV723SaveRepairDocumentLinks_(ticketId, file.getUrl(), pdf.getUrl(), actor);
    logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.fullName || '', 'CREATE_IT_REPAIR_MEMO_V723', 'ITRepairTicket', ticketId, title, null, {docUrl:file.getUrl(), pdfUrl:pdf.getUrl()});
    return {success:true, docUrl:file.getUrl(), pdfUrl:pdf.getUrl(), message:'สร้างเอกสารบันทึกข้อความแจ้งซ่อมพร้อม workflow เรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    var msg = e && e.toString ? e.toString() : String(e);
    if (/DocumentApp\.create|auth\/documents|permission to call DocumentApp|authorization/i.test(msg)) {
      return {
        success:false,
        code:'DOCUMENT_SCOPE_REQUIRED',
        message:'โปรเจกต์ Apps Script ยังไม่ได้รับสิทธิ์สร้าง Google Docs กรุณาอัปเดตไฟล์ appsscript.json ให้มี scope https://www.googleapis.com/auth/documents แล้ว Run ฟังก์ชัน authorizeDocumentExportsV722 หนึ่งครั้งจาก Apps Script editor เพื่อกดอนุญาตสิทธิ์ จากนั้น Deploy เวอร์ชันใหม่'
      };
    }
    return {success:false, message:msg};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV723() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.26-it-repair-reviewer-role', functions:['getITRepairDigitalMemoV722','updateITRepairDocumentStepV723','createITRepairMemoDocumentV722','authorizeDocumentExportsV722']};
}

// ========================= v70.27: forgot PIN + user-requested PIN approval =========================
function haosV727PinRequestHeaders_() {
  return ['Request ID','Phone','Full Name','Position','Department','Email','Requested PIN','Status','Requested At','Reviewed By','Reviewed At','Review Note','Applied At'];
}

function haosV727PinRequestSheet_() {
  var sh = getOrCreateSheet_('PinChangeRequests', haosV727PinRequestHeaders_(), '#ede9fe');
  sh.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('@');
  sh.getRange('G:G').setNumberFormat('@');
  return sh;
}

function haosV727Norm_(v) {
  return String(v || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
}

function haosV727NewPin_() {
  return Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
}

function haosV727ValidatePin_(pin) {
  pin = String(pin || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (!/^\d{4,6}$/.test(pin)) return {ok:false, pin:pin, message:'PIN ต้องเป็นตัวเลข 4-6 หลัก'};
  return {ok:true, pin:pin};
}

function haosV727ActiveUserOrMessage_(found) {
  if (!found) return {ok:false, message:'ไม่พบข้อมูลผู้ใช้งานในระบบ'};
  var status = String(found.values[10] || 'Active');
  if (status === 'Pending') return {ok:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ยังรออนุมัติ กรุณาติดต่อ Admin / Super Admin'};
  if (status === 'Rejected') return {ok:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ไม่ได้รับการอนุมัติ กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
  if (status === 'Inactive') return {ok:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ถูกปิดใช้งาน กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
  return {ok:true};
}

function requestForgotPinResetV727(payload) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var phone = haosV718CleanPhone_(payload.phone);
    if (!phone) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุเบอร์โทรศัพท์'};
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
    var active = haosV727ActiveUserOrMessage_(found);
    if (!active.ok) return {success:false, message:active.message};
    var row = found.values;
    var checks = [
      {label:'ชื่อ-นามสกุล', input:payload.fullName, actual:row[2]},
      {label:'ตำแหน่ง', input:payload.position, actual:row[3]},
      {label:'กลุ่มงาน', input:payload.department, actual:row[4]},
      {label:'อีเมล', input:payload.email, actual:row[5]}
    ];
    for (var i = 0; i < checks.length; i++) {
      if (haosV727Norm_(checks[i].input) !== haosV727Norm_(checks[i].actual)) {
        logAudit_(phone, row[2] || '', 'FORGOT_PIN_RESET_DENIED', 'User', phone, 'ข้อมูลยืนยันตัวตนไม่ตรง: ' + checks[i].label, null, {field:checks[i].label});
        return {success:false, message:'ข้อมูลยืนยันตัวตนไม่ตรงกับระบบ กรุณาตรวจสอบ ' + checks[i].label + ' หรือ ติดต่อ Admin'};
      }
    }
    var newPin = haosV727NewPin_();
    found.sheet.getRange(found.row, 2).setValue("'" + newPin);
    logAudit_(phone, row[2] || '', 'SELF_RESET_PIN', 'User', phone, 'ผู้ใช้รีเซ็ต PIN ด้วยตนเองผ่านข้อมูลยืนยันตัวตน', null, {pin:'******'});
    try {
      if (row[5]) MailApp.sendEmail(row[5], '[Health Assistant OS] รีเซ็ต PIN สำเร็จ', 'เรียน ' + (row[2] || 'ผู้ใช้งาน') + '\n\nระบบรีเซ็ต PIN ให้เรียบร้อยแล้ว\nเบอร์โทรศัพท์: ' + phone + '\nPIN ใหม่: ' + newPin + '\n\nหากไม่ได้ดำเนินการเอง กรุณาติดต่อ Admin / Super Admin ทันที');
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, message:'รีเซ็ต PIN สำเร็จ', pin:newPin, name:row[2] || '', phone:phone};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function requestMyPinChangeV727(phone, requestedPin) {
  try {
    phone = haosV718CleanPhone_(phone);
    var pinCheck = haosV727ValidatePin_(requestedPin);
    if (!pinCheck.ok) return {success:false, message:pinCheck.message};
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
    var active = haosV727ActiveUserOrMessage_(found);
    if (!active.ok) return {success:false, message:active.message};
    var sh = haosV727PinRequestSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][1]) === phone && String(data[i][7] || '') === 'Pending') {
        return {success:false, message:'คุณมีคำขอเปลี่ยน PIN ที่รออนุมัติอยู่แล้ว กรุณารอ Admin / Super Admin พิจารณาก่อน'};
      }
    }
    var row = found.values;
    var reqId = 'PIN-' + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 10).toUpperCase();
    var now = new Date();
    sh.appendRow([reqId, "'" + phone, row[2] || '', row[3] || '', row[4] || '', row[5] || '', "'" + pinCheck.pin, 'Pending', now, '', '', '', '']);
    logAudit_(phone, row[2] || '', 'REQUEST_PIN_CHANGE', 'PinChangeRequest', reqId, 'ผู้ใช้ขอตั้ง PIN เอง รออนุมัติ', null, {pin:'******'});
    try {
      var admins = getAdminUsersData();
      if (admins && admins.success) {
        admins.data.filter(function(u){return haosV718IsAdminRole_(u.role) && u.accountStatus !== 'Inactive' && u.accountStatus !== 'Pending' && u.accountStatus !== 'Rejected';}).forEach(function(a) {
          createNotificationV718_(a.phone, 'มีคำขอเปลี่ยน PIN รออนุมัติจาก ' + (row[2] || phone), 'PIN Approval', reqId, 'ปกติ');
        });
      }
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, message:'ส่งคำขอเปลี่ยน PIN แล้ว กรุณารอ Admin / Super Admin อนุมัติ', requestId:reqId};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getPinChangeRequestsV727(adminPhone, status) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var sh = haosV727PinRequestSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var targetStatus = String(status || 'Pending');
    var rows = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var st = String(data[i][7] || 'Pending');
      if (targetStatus && targetStatus !== 'All' && st !== targetStatus) continue;
      rows.push({
        requestId:data[i][0] || '',
        phone:haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][1]),
        fullName:data[i][2] || '',
        position:data[i][3] || '',
        department:data[i][4] || '',
        email:data[i][5] || '',
        requestedPin:String(data[i][6] || '').replace(/^'/, ''),
        status:st,
        requestedAt:data[i][8] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][8], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : (data[i][8] || ''),
        reviewedBy:haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][9]),
        reviewedAt:data[i][10] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(data[i][10], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : (data[i][10] || ''),
        reviewNote:data[i][11] || ''
      });
    }
    rows.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.requestedAt || '').localeCompare(String(a.requestedAt || '')); });
    return {success:true, data:rows, version:'v70.27-pin-requests'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function reviewPinChangeRequestV727(requestId, decision, adminPhone, note) {
  try {
    if (!haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    requestId = String(requestId || '').trim();
    decision = String(decision || '').trim();
    var approve = decision === 'Approved' || decision === 'อนุมัติ';
    var reject = decision === 'Rejected' || decision === 'ไม่อนุมัติ';
    if (!approve && !reject) return {success:false, message:'ผลการพิจารณาไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    var sh = haosV727PinRequestSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '') !== requestId) continue;
      var currentStatus = String(data[i][7] || 'Pending');
      if (currentStatus !== 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'คำขอนี้ถูกพิจารณาไปแล้ว'};
      var phone = haosV718CleanPhone_(data[i][1]);
      var pin = String(data[i][6] || '').replace(/^'/, '');
      var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
      if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบบัญชีผู้ขอเปลี่ยน PIN'};
      var now = new Date();
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 8).setValue(approve ? 'Approved' : 'Rejected');
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 10).setValue("'" + haosV718CleanPhone_(adminPhone));
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 11).setValue(now);
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 12).setValue(note || '');
      if (approve) {
        found.sheet.getRange(found.row, 2).setValue("'" + pin);
        sh.getRange(i + 1, 13).setValue(now);
      }
      try {
        createNotificationV718_(phone, approve ? 'คำขอเปลี่ยน PIN ของคุณได้รับอนุมัติแล้ว สามารถใช้ PIN ใหม่เข้าสู่ระบบได้' : 'คำขอเปลี่ยน PIN ของคุณไม่ผ่านการอนุมัติ' + (note ? ': ' + note : ''), 'PIN Approval Result', requestId, 'ปกติ');
      } catch(ignore) {}
      logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), approve ? 'APPROVE_PIN_CHANGE' : 'REJECT_PIN_CHANGE', 'PinChangeRequest', requestId, approve ? 'อนุมัติคำขอเปลี่ยน PIN' : 'ไม่อนุมัติคำขอเปลี่ยน PIN', {status:'Pending'}, {status:approve ? 'Approved' : 'Rejected', pin:'******', note:note || ''});
      return {success:true, message:approve ? 'อนุมัติและเปลี่ยน PIN ให้ผู้ใช้งานเรียบร้อยแล้ว' : 'บันทึกผลไม่อนุมัติเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอเปลี่ยน PIN'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV727() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.27-forgot-pin-user-pin-approval', functions:['requestForgotPinResetV727','requestMyPinChangeV727','getPinChangeRequestsV727','reviewPinChangeRequestV727']};
}

// ========================= v70.29: multi work profiles per user =========================
function haosV729CleanPhone_(phone) {
  try {
    if (typeof haosV718CleanPhone_ === 'function') return haosV718CleanPhone_(phone);
  } catch(ignore) {}
  return String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
}

function haosV729ProfileHeaders_() {
  return [
    'Profile ID', 'User Phone', 'Full Name Snapshot', 'Department', 'Position',
    'Profile Role', 'Is Primary', 'Is Active', 'Can Approve', 'Signature ID',
    'Notify Email', 'Notify Telegram', 'Created At', 'Updated At', 'Updated By', 'Notes'
  ];
}

function haosV729UserSheet_() {
  var sh;
  try {
    sh = typeof haosV718UserSheet_ === 'function' ? haosV718UserSheet_() : getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  } catch(e) {
    sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  }
  if (!sh) {
    Setup();
    sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  }
  ensureHeaders_(sh, ['Active Profile ID'], '#d4edda');
  sh.getRange('A:B').setNumberFormat('@');
  return sh;
}

function haosV729ProfileSheet_() {
  var sh = getOrCreateSheet_('UserProfiles', haosV729ProfileHeaders_(), '#dbeafe');
  sh.getRange('B:B').setNumberFormat('@');
  return sh;
}

function haosV729Bool_(value, defaultValue) {
  if (value === true || String(value).toLowerCase() === 'true' || String(value) === '1' || String(value).toLowerCase() === 'yes' || String(value) === 'ใช่') return true;
  if (value === false || String(value).toLowerCase() === 'false' || String(value) === '0' || String(value).toLowerCase() === 'no' || String(value) === 'ไม่') return false;
  return !!defaultValue;
}

function haosV729UserObj_(row, sheet) {
  try {
    if (typeof haosV718UserObjFromRow_ === 'function') return haosV718UserObjFromRow_(row, sheet || haosV729UserSheet_());
  } catch(ignore) {}
  return {
    phone: haosV729CleanPhone_(row[0]),
    pin: String(row[1] || '').replace(/^'/, ''),
    fullName: row[2] || '',
    position: row[3] || '',
    department: row[4] || '',
    email: row[5] || '',
    role: row[7] || 'User',
    emailNotify: row[9] || 'On',
    accountStatus: row[10] || 'Active',
    emailNotifyPreferences: ''
  };
}

function haosV729FastUserScanCols_(sheet) {
  return Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 12);
}

function haosV729HydrateSmallUserCols_(sheet, rowNumber, rowValues) {
  rowValues = rowValues || [];
  var scanCols = haosV729FastUserScanCols_(sheet);
  var wanted = ['Email Notify Preferences JSON', 'Active Profile ID'];
  wanted.forEach(function(header) {
    var col = getColumnIndex_(sheet, header);
    if (col > scanCols && col > 0) rowValues[col - 1] = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, col).getValue();
  });
  return rowValues;
}

function haosV729FindUser_(phone) {
  var sh = haosV729UserSheet_();
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return null;
  var scanCols = haosV729FastUserScanCols_(sh);
  var data = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, scanCols).getValues();
  var target = haosV729CleanPhone_(phone);
  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (haosV729CleanPhone_(data[i][0]) === target) {
      var rowNumber = i + 2;
      return {sheet:sh, row:rowNumber, values:haosV729HydrateSmallUserCols_(sh, rowNumber, data[i].slice())};
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV729NormalizeProfileRole_(role) {
  role = String(role || '').trim();
  if (/^(head|หัวหน้า|department_head)$/i.test(role)) return 'Head';
  if (/^(coordinator|ผู้ประสานงาน)$/i.test(role)) return 'Coordinator';
  if (/^(acting head|acting_head|รักษาการหัวหน้า)$/i.test(role)) return 'Acting Head';
  if (/^(admin|super admin)$/i.test(role)) return role;
  return 'Member';
}

function haosV729EffectiveRole_(accountRole, profileRole, canApprove) {
  accountRole = String(accountRole || 'User');
  profileRole = haosV729NormalizeProfileRole_(profileRole);
  if (/^Super Admin$/i.test(accountRole)) return 'Super Admin';
  if (/^Admin$/i.test(accountRole)) return 'Admin';
  if (profileRole === 'Head' || profileRole === 'Acting Head' || canApprove === true) return 'Head';
  return 'User';
}

function haosV729ProfileFromRow_(row, user) {
  var canApprove = haosV729Bool_(row[8], false);
  var profileRole = haosV729NormalizeProfileRole_(row[5] || 'Member');
  var accountRole = user && user.role ? user.role : '';
  return {
    profileId: row[0] || '',
    phone: haosV729CleanPhone_(row[1]),
    fullName: row[2] || (user && user.fullName) || '',
    department: row[3] || '',
    position: row[4] || '',
    profileRole: profileRole,
    isPrimary: haosV729Bool_(row[6], false),
    isActive: haosV729Bool_(row[7], true),
    canApprove: canApprove,
    signatureId: row[9] || '',
    notifyEmail: row[10] || '',
    notifyTelegram: row[11] || '',
    createdAt: row[12] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(row[12], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : (row[12] || ''),
    updatedAt: row[13] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(row[13], 'GMT+7', 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : (row[13] || ''),
    updatedBy: haosV729CleanPhone_(row[14]),
    notes: row[15] || '',
    accountRole: accountRole || 'User',
    effectiveRole: haosV729EffectiveRole_(accountRole, profileRole, canApprove)
  };
}

function haosV729ProfileKey_(profile) {
  function norm(value) {
    return String(value || '')
      .replace(/^\d+\s*/, '')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
  }
  profile = profile || {};
  return [
    haosV729CleanPhone_(profile.phone || profile.userPhone || ''),
    norm(profile.department),
    norm(profile.position),
    norm(haosV729NormalizeProfileRole_(profile.profileRole || 'Member')),
    profile.canApprove === true ? 'approve' : 'no-approve'
  ].join('|');
}

function haosV729DeduplicateProfilesForUser_(phone) {
  var found = haosV729FindUser_(phone);
  if (!found) return [];
  var user = haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet);
  var activeCol = getColumnIndex_(found.sheet, 'Active Profile ID');
  var activeProfileId = activeCol > 0 ? String(found.values[activeCol - 1] || '').trim() : '';
  var sh = haosV729ProfileSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = haosV729CleanPhone_(phone);
  var profiles = [];
  var groups = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (haosV729CleanPhone_(data[i][1]) !== target) continue;
    var profile = haosV729ProfileFromRow_(data[i], user);
    profile.row = i + 1;
    profiles.push(profile);
    if (!profile.isActive) continue;
    var key = haosV729ProfileKey_(profile);
    if (!groups[key]) groups[key] = [];
    groups[key].push(profile);
  }

  var changed = false;
  Object.keys(groups).forEach(function(key) {
    var items = groups[key];
    if (items.length <= 1) return;
    var keep = null;
    for (var j = 0; j < items.length; j++) {
      if (String(items[j].profileId) === activeProfileId) {
        keep = items[j];
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!keep) {
      for (var k = 0; k < items.length; k++) {
        if (items[k].isPrimary) {
          keep = items[k];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    keep = keep || items[0];
    items.forEach(function(p) {
      if (String(p.profileId) === String(keep.profileId)) return;
      sh.getRange(p.row, 7).setValue(false);
      sh.getRange(p.row, 8).setValue(false);
      sh.getRange(p.row, 14).setValue(new Date());
      sh.getRange(p.row, 15).setValue("'" + target);
      var oldNote = String(data[p.row - 1][15] || '');
      sh.getRange(p.row, 16).setValue(oldNote ? oldNote + ' | Auto-disabled duplicate profile' : 'Auto-disabled duplicate profile');
      p.isPrimary = false;
      p.isActive = false;
      changed = true;
    });
  });

  var activeProfiles = profiles.filter(function(p) { return p.isActive; });
  var selectedPrimary = null;
  if (activeProfileId) selectedPrimary = activeProfiles.filter(function(p) { return String(p.profileId) === activeProfileId; })[0] || null;
  selectedPrimary = selectedPrimary || activeProfiles.filter(function(p) { return p.isPrimary; })[0] || activeProfiles[0] || null;
  if (selectedPrimary) {
    activeProfiles.forEach(function(p) {
      var shouldBePrimary = String(p.profileId) === String(selectedPrimary.profileId);
      if (p.isPrimary !== shouldBePrimary) {
        sh.getRange(p.row, 7).setValue(shouldBePrimary);
        p.isPrimary = shouldBePrimary;
        changed = true;
      }
    });
    if (activeCol > 0 && activeProfileId !== selectedPrimary.profileId) {
      found.sheet.getRange(found.row, activeCol).setValue(selectedPrimary.profileId);
      changed = true;
    }
  }
  if (changed) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  return haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(phone, true);
}

function haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(phone, includeInactive) {
  var found = haosV729FindUser_(phone);
  var user = found ? haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet) : {phone:haosV729CleanPhone_(phone), role:'User'};
  var sh = haosV729ProfileSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = haosV729CleanPhone_(phone);
  var out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (haosV729CleanPhone_(data[i][1]) !== target) continue;
    var profile = haosV729ProfileFromRow_(data[i], user);
    profile.row = i + 1;
    if (includeInactive || profile.isActive) out.push(profile);
  }
  return out;
}

function haosV729AppendPrimaryProfile_(user, updatedBy) {
  var sh = haosV729ProfileSheet_();
  var now = new Date();
  var id = 'UPR-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var canApprove = haosV729EffectiveRole_(user.role, user.role === 'Head' ? 'Head' : 'Member', false) === 'Head';
  sh.appendRow([
    id, "'" + haosV729CleanPhone_(user.phone), user.fullName || '', user.department || '', user.position || '',
    user.role === 'Head' ? 'Head' : 'Member', true, true, canApprove, '',
    user.email || '', '', now, now, "'" + haosV729CleanPhone_(updatedBy || user.phone), 'สร้างอัตโนมัติจากข้อมูล Users เดิม'
  ]);
  return id;
}

function haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(phone) {
  var found = haosV729FindUser_(phone);
  if (!found) return [];
  var user = haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet);
  var profiles = haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(user.phone, true);
  var active = profiles.filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
  if (!active.length) {
    haosV729AppendPrimaryProfile_(user, user.phone);
  }
  return haosV729DeduplicateProfilesForUser_(user.phone);
}

function haosV729ChooseActiveProfileFromList_(found, profiles) {
  if (!found || !profiles || !profiles.length) return null;
  var activeCol = getColumnIndex_(found.sheet, 'Active Profile ID');
  var activeId = activeCol > 0 ? String(found.values[activeCol - 1] || '').trim() : '';
  var chosen = activeId ? profiles.filter(function(p){ return String(p.profileId) === activeId; })[0] : null;
  if (!chosen) chosen = profiles.filter(function(p){ return p.isPrimary; })[0] || profiles[0];
  if (activeCol > 0 && chosen && activeId !== chosen.profileId) {
    found.sheet.getRange(found.row, activeCol).setValue(chosen.profileId);
    found.values[activeCol - 1] = chosen.profileId;
  }
  return chosen || null;
}

function haosV729ActiveProfileForUser_(phone) {
  var found = haosV729FindUser_(phone);
  if (!found) return null;
  var profiles = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(phone).filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
  if (!profiles.length) return null;
  return haosV729ChooseActiveProfileFromList_(found, profiles);
}

function haosV729ApplyProfileToUserData_(userData, profile, profiles) {
  userData = userData || {};
  profile = profile || haosV729ActiveProfileForUser_(userData.phone);
  profiles = profiles || haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(userData.phone, false);
  userData.accountRole = userData.accountRole || userData.role || 'User';
  if (profile) {
    userData.activeProfileId = profile.profileId;
    userData.activeProfile = profile;
    userData.department = profile.department || userData.department || '';
    userData.position = profile.position || userData.position || '';
    userData.profileRole = profile.profileRole || 'Member';
    userData.canApproveProfile = !!profile.canApprove;
    userData.role = profile.effectiveRole || haosV729EffectiveRole_(userData.accountRole, profile.profileRole, profile.canApprove);
  }
  userData.profiles = profiles.map(function(p) {
    delete p.row;
    return p;
  });
  return userData;
}

function loginAccount(phone, pin) {
  try {
    var targetFast = haosV729CleanPhone_(phone);
    var pinTextFast = String(pin || '').replace(/^'/, '');
    var foundFast = haosV729FindUser_(targetFast);
    if (!foundFast || String(foundFast.values[1] || '').replace(/^'/, '') !== pinTextFast) {
      return {success:false, message:'ข้อมูลไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    }
    var shFast = foundFast.sheet;
    var rowFast = foundFast.values;
    var accountStatusFast = rowFast[10] || 'Active';
    if (accountStatusFast === 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้อยู่ระหว่างรอ Admin / Super Admin อนุมัติ กรุณารอการอนุมัติก่อนเข้าใช้งาน'};
    if (accountStatusFast === 'Rejected') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ไม่ได้รับการอนุมัติ กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
    if (accountStatusFast === 'Inactive') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ถูกปิดใช้งาน กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
    var lastLoginColFast = getColumnIndex_(shFast, 'Last Login');
    var prefColFast = getColumnIndex_(shFast, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
    if (lastLoginColFast > 0) shFast.getRange(foundFast.row, lastLoginColFast).setValue(new Date());
    var baseFast = {
      phone:haosV729CleanPhone_(rowFast[0]), fullName:rowFast[2], position:rowFast[3], department:rowFast[4],
      email:rowFast[5], role:rowFast[7] || 'User', accountRole:rowFast[7] || 'User',
      emailNotify:rowFast[9] || 'On', accountStatus:accountStatusFast,
      emailNotifyPreferences:prefColFast > 0 ? rowFast[prefColFast - 1] || '' : ''
    };
    var profilesFast = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(targetFast).filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
    var activeProfileFast = haosV729ChooseActiveProfileFromList_(foundFast, profilesFast);
    var auditPayloadFast = {activeProfileId:activeProfileFast ? activeProfileFast.profileId : ''};
    if (activeProfileFast) {
      auditPayloadFast.auditProfile = {
        profileId: activeProfileFast.profileId || '',
        department: activeProfileFast.department || '',
        position: activeProfileFast.position || '',
        profileRole: activeProfileFast.profileRole || '',
        accountRole: activeProfileFast.accountRole || '',
        effectiveRole: activeProfileFast.effectiveRole || '',
        canApprove: !!activeProfileFast.canApprove
      };
    }
    logAudit_(targetFast, rowFast[2], 'LOGIN', 'User', targetFast, 'เข้าสู่ระบบสำเร็จ', null, auditPayloadFast);
    return {success:true, userData:haosV729ApplyProfileToUserData_(baseFast, activeProfileFast, profilesFast)};

    var sh = haosV729UserSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var lastLoginCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Last Login');
    var prefCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
    var target = haosV729CleanPhone_(phone);
    var pinText = String(pin || '').replace(/^'/, '');
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (haosV729CleanPhone_(data[i][0]) === target && String(data[i][1] || '').replace(/^'/, '') === pinText) {
        var accountStatus = data[i][10] || 'Active';
        if (accountStatus === 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้อยู่ระหว่างรอ Admin / Super Admin อนุมัติ กรุณารอการอนุมัติก่อนเข้าใช้งาน'};
        if (accountStatus === 'Rejected') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ไม่ได้รับการอนุมัติ กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
        if (accountStatus === 'Inactive') return {success:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ถูกปิดใช้งาน กรุณาติดต่อผู้ดูแลระบบ'};
        if (lastLoginCol > 0) sh.getRange(i + 1, lastLoginCol).setValue(new Date());
        var base = {
          phone:haosV729CleanPhone_(data[i][0]), fullName:data[i][2], position:data[i][3], department:data[i][4],
          email:data[i][5], role:data[i][7] || 'User', accountRole:data[i][7] || 'User',
          emailNotify:data[i][9] || 'On', accountStatus:accountStatus,
          emailNotifyPreferences:prefCol > 0 ? data[i][prefCol - 1] || '' : ''
        };
        var profiles = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(target).filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
        var activeProfile = haosV729ActiveProfileForUser_(target);
        logAudit_(target, data[i][2], 'LOGIN', 'User', target, 'เข้าสู่ระบบสำเร็จ', null, {activeProfileId:activeProfile ? activeProfile.profileId : ''});
        return {success:true, userData:haosV729ApplyProfileToUserData_(base, activeProfile, profiles)};
      }
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ข้อมูลไม่ถูกต้อง'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getMyUserProfilesV729(phone) {
  try {
    var found = haosV729FindUser_(phone);
    var profiles = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(phone).filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
    var active = haosV729ChooseActiveProfileFromList_(found, profiles);
    profiles.forEach(function(p){ delete p.row; });
    return {success:true, profiles:profiles, activeProfileId:active ? active.profileId : '', activeProfile:active, version:'v70.29'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function setActiveUserProfileV729(phone, profileId) {
  try {
    var target = haosV729CleanPhone_(phone);
    var profiles = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(target).filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
    var profile = profiles.filter(function(p){ return String(p.profileId) === String(profileId); })[0];
    if (!profile) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบโปรไฟล์นี้ หรือโปรไฟล์ถูกปิดใช้งาน'};
    var found = haosV729FindUser_(target);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบบัญชีผู้ใช้งาน'};
    var activeCol = getColumnIndex_(found.sheet, 'Active Profile ID');
    if (activeCol > 0) found.sheet.getRange(found.row, activeCol).setValue(profile.profileId);
    var base = haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet);
    base.accountRole = base.role || 'User';
    var userData = haosV729ApplyProfileToUserData_(base, profile, profiles);
    logAudit_(target, base.fullName || '', 'SWITCH_PROFILE', 'UserProfile', profile.profileId, 'สลับโปรไฟล์เป็น ' + profile.department + ' / ' + profile.position, null, profile);
    return {success:true, message:'สลับโปรไฟล์แล้ว', profile:profile, userData:userData};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function updateUserProfile(data) {
  try {
    data = data || {};
    var found = haosV729FindUser_(data.phone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบบัญชีผู้ใช้งานนี้ในระบบ'};
    var sh = found.sheet;
    var active = haosV729ActiveProfileForUser_(data.phone);
    var profileId = String(data.activeProfileId || data.profileId || (active ? active.profileId : '') || '').trim();
    if (profileId) {
      var pFound = haosV729FindProfileById_(profileId);
      if (pFound && haosV729CleanPhone_(pFound.values[1]) === haosV729CleanPhone_(data.phone)) {
        pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 4).setValue(data.department || '');
        pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 5).setValue(data.position || '');
        pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 11).setValue(data.email || '');
        pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 14).setValue(new Date());
        pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 15).setValue("'" + haosV729CleanPhone_(data.phone));
      }
    }
    sh.getRange(found.row, 4).setValue(data.position || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 5).setValue(data.department || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 6).setValue(data.email || '');
    sh.getRange(found.row, 10).setValue(data.emailNotify || 'On');
    var prefCol = getColumnIndex_(sh, 'Email Notify Preferences JSON');
    if (prefCol > 0 && data.emailNotifyPreferences) {
      sh.getRange(found.row, prefCol).setValue(typeof data.emailNotifyPreferences === 'string' ? data.emailNotifyPreferences : JSON.stringify(data.emailNotifyPreferences));
    }
    return {success:true, message:'อัปเดตข้อมูลส่วนตัวและโปรไฟล์ที่ใช้งานอยู่เรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV729FindProfileById_(profileId) {
  var sh = haosV729ProfileSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0] || '') === String(profileId || '')) return {sheet:sh, row:i + 1, values:data[i]};
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV729CanManageProfiles_(adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (typeof haosV718CanAdminUsers_ === 'function') return haosV718CanAdminUsers_(adminPhone);
  } catch(ignore) {}
  var found = haosV729FindUser_(adminPhone);
  if (!found) return false;
  var u = haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet);
  return /^(Admin|Super Admin)$/i.test(String(u.role || '')) && !/Inactive|Pending|Rejected/i.test(String(u.accountStatus || 'Active'));
}

function getUserProfilesForAdminV729(targetPhone, adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV729CanManageProfiles_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var found = haosV729FindUser_(targetPhone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน'};
    var user = haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet);
    var profiles = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(targetPhone);
    profiles.forEach(function(p){ delete p.row; });
    return {success:true, user:user, profiles:profiles, version:'v70.29'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function saveUserProfileV729(adminPhone, targetPhone, payload) {
  try {
    if (!haosV729CanManageProfiles_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    payload = payload || {};
    var found = haosV729FindUser_(targetPhone);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน'};
    var user = haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet);
    var phone = haosV729CleanPhone_(targetPhone);
    var department = String(payload.department || '').trim();
    var position = String(payload.position || '').trim();
    var profileRole = haosV729NormalizeProfileRole_(payload.profileRole || 'Member');
    if (!department) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุกลุ่มงาน'};
    if (!position) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุตำแหน่ง'};
    var sh = haosV729ProfileSheet_();
    var now = new Date();
    var isPrimary = haosV729Bool_(payload.isPrimary, false);
    var isActive = haosV729Bool_(payload.isActive, true);
    var canApprove = haosV729Bool_(payload.canApprove, profileRole === 'Head' || profileRole === 'Acting Head');
    if (!payload.profileId) {
      var wantedKey = haosV729ProfileKey_({phone:phone, department:department, position:position, profileRole:profileRole, canApprove:canApprove});
      var existingMatch = haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(phone, true).filter(function(p) {
        return haosV729ProfileKey_(p) === wantedKey;
      })[0];
      if (existingMatch) payload.profileId = existingMatch.profileId;
    }
    if (isPrimary) haosV729ClearPrimaryProfiles_(phone, '');
    var before = null;
    if (payload.profileId) {
      var pFound = haosV729FindProfileById_(payload.profileId);
      if (!pFound || haosV729CleanPhone_(pFound.values[1]) !== phone) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบโปรไฟล์ที่ต้องการแก้ไข'};
      before = haosV729ProfileFromRow_(pFound.values, user);
      sh.getRange(pFound.row, 3, 1, 14).setValues([[
        user.fullName || '', department, position, profileRole, isPrimary, isActive, canApprove,
        payload.signatureId || pFound.values[9] || '', payload.notifyEmail || user.email || '', payload.notifyTelegram || pFound.values[11] || '',
        pFound.values[12] || now, now, "'" + haosV729CleanPhone_(adminPhone), payload.notes || ''
      ]]);
    } else {
      var id = 'UPR-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
      sh.appendRow([
        id, "'" + phone, user.fullName || '', department, position, profileRole, isPrimary, isActive, canApprove,
        payload.signatureId || '', payload.notifyEmail || user.email || '', payload.notifyTelegram || '', now, now,
        "'" + haosV729CleanPhone_(adminPhone), payload.notes || ''
      ]);
      payload.profileId = id;
    }
    if (isPrimary) haosV729SyncUserFromPrimary_(phone);
    var after = haosV729FindProfileById_(payload.profileId);
    var afterObj = after ? haosV729ProfileFromRow_(after.values, user) : payload;
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), before ? 'UPDATE_USER_PROFILE_SLOT' : 'CREATE_USER_PROFILE_SLOT', 'UserProfile', payload.profileId, 'จัดการโปรไฟล์หลายกลุ่มงานของ ' + (user.fullName || phone), before, afterObj);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกโปรไฟล์เรียบร้อยแล้ว', profile:afterObj};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV729ClearPrimaryProfiles_(phone, exceptProfileId) {
  var sh = haosV729ProfileSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = haosV729CleanPhone_(phone);
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (haosV729CleanPhone_(data[i][1]) === target && String(data[i][0]) !== String(exceptProfileId || '')) {
      sh.getRange(i + 1, 7).setValue(false);
    }
  }
}

function haosV729SyncUserFromPrimary_(phone) {
  var found = haosV729FindUser_(phone);
  if (!found) return;
  var profiles = haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(phone, true);
  var primary = profiles.filter(function(p){ return p.isPrimary && p.isActive; })[0] || profiles.filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; })[0];
  if (!primary) return;
  found.sheet.getRange(found.row, 4).setValue(primary.position || '');
  found.sheet.getRange(found.row, 5).setValue(primary.department || '');
  var activeCol = getColumnIndex_(found.sheet, 'Active Profile ID');
  if (activeCol > 0) found.sheet.getRange(found.row, activeCol).setValue(primary.profileId);
}

function setUserProfileActiveV729(adminPhone, targetPhone, profileId, isActive) {
  try {
    if (!haosV729CanManageProfiles_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var pFound = haosV729FindProfileById_(profileId);
    if (!pFound || haosV729CleanPhone_(pFound.values[1]) !== haosV729CleanPhone_(targetPhone)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบโปรไฟล์'};
    isActive = haosV729Bool_(isActive, true);
    if (!isActive) {
      var activeCount = haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(targetPhone, false).filter(function(p){ return p.profileId !== profileId; }).length;
      if (activeCount < 1) return {success:false, message:'ต้องมีโปรไฟล์ที่ใช้งานอย่างน้อย 1 โปรไฟล์'};
    }
    pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 8).setValue(isActive);
    pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 14).setValue(new Date());
    pFound.sheet.getRange(pFound.row, 15).setValue("'" + haosV729CleanPhone_(adminPhone));
    if (!isActive && haosV729Bool_(pFound.values[6], false)) {
      var profiles = haosV729ProfilesByPhone_(targetPhone, false).filter(function(p){ return p.profileId !== profileId; });
      if (profiles.length) {
        haosV729ClearPrimaryProfiles_(targetPhone, profiles[0].profileId);
        pFound.sheet.getRange(profiles[0].row, 7).setValue(true);
        haosV729SyncUserFromPrimary_(targetPhone);
      }
    }
    return {success:true, message:isActive ? 'เปิดใช้งานโปรไฟล์แล้ว' : 'ปิดใช้งานโปรไฟล์แล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getAllUsers() {
  try {
    if (typeof haosV730GetAllUsersFast_ === 'function') return haosV730GetAllUsersFast_();
    var sh = haosV729UserSheet_();
    var usersData = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var usersList = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < usersData.length; i++) {
      var status = usersData[i][10] || 'Active';
      if (status === 'Inactive' || status === 'Pending' || status === 'Rejected') continue;
      var base = haosV729UserObj_(usersData[i], sh);
      var profiles = haosV729EnsureProfilesForUser_(base.phone).filter(function(p){ return p.isActive; });
      profiles.forEach(function(p) {
        usersList.push({
          phone: base.phone,
          name: base.fullName,
          fullName: base.fullName,
          position: p.position || base.position,
          department: p.department || base.department,
          role: p.effectiveRole || base.role,
          accountRole: base.role,
          profileRole: p.profileRole,
          profileId: p.profileId,
          accountStatus: status,
          email: base.email || ''
        });
      });
    }
    usersList.sort(function(a,b){ return String(a.department || '').localeCompare(String(b.department || ''), 'th') || String(a.name || '').localeCompare(String(b.name || ''), 'th'); });
    return {success:true, data:usersList, version:'v70.29-user-profiles'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getUserDirectoryForPickerV721() {
  return getAllUsers();
}

function getUserProfileByPhone_(phone) {
  try {
    var found = haosV729FindUser_(phone);
    if (!found) return null;
    var user = haosV729UserObj_(found.values, found.sheet);
    var active = haosV729ActiveProfileForUser_(phone);
    if (active) {
      user.position = active.position || user.position;
      user.department = active.department || user.department;
      user.profileRole = active.profileRole || 'Member';
      user.accountRole = user.role || 'User';
      user.role = active.effectiveRole || user.role || 'User';
      user.activeProfileId = active.profileId;
      user.activeProfile = active;
    }
    return user;
  } catch(e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function getHeadOfDepartmentV68(department) {
  try {
    var target = haosNormalizeDeptV685_(department);
    if (!target) return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', position:'', department:'', message:'ยังไม่ได้ระบุกลุ่มงาน', version:'v70.29'};
    var profiles = haosV729ProfileSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < profiles.length; i++) {
      var pDept = haosNormalizeDeptV685_(profiles[i][3]);
      if (pDept !== target || !haosV729Bool_(profiles[i][7], true)) continue;
      var pRole = haosV729NormalizeProfileRole_(profiles[i][5]);
      var canApprove = haosV729Bool_(profiles[i][8], false);
      if (pRole === 'Head' || pRole === 'Acting Head' || canApprove) {
        return {success:true, phone:haosV729CleanPhone_(profiles[i][1]), name:profiles[i][2] || getUserNameByPhone_(profiles[i][1]), position:profiles[i][4] || 'หัวหน้ากลุ่มงาน', role:'Head', profileRole:pRole, department:profiles[i][3] || department, profileId:profiles[i][0], version:'v70.29'};
      }
    }
    var sh = haosV729UserSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var fallback = null;
    for (var j = 1; j < data.length; j++) {
      var status = data[j][10] || 'Active';
      if (status === 'Inactive' || status === 'Pending' || status === 'Rejected') continue;
      var dept = haosNormalizeDeptV685_(data[j][4]);
      if (dept !== target) continue;
      var role = data[j][7] || '';
      var position = data[j][3] || '';
      if (haosIsHeadRoleOrPositionV685_(role, position)) {
        return {success:true, phone:haosV729CleanPhone_(data[j][0]), name:data[j][2] || '', position:position, role:role, department:data[j][4] || department, version:'v70.29-legacy'};
      }
      if (!fallback) fallback = {phone:haosV729CleanPhone_(data[j][0]), name:data[j][2] || '', position:position, role:role, department:data[j][4] || department};
    }
    return {success:true, phone:'', name:'', position:'', department:department || '', message:'ยังไม่ได้กำหนดหัวหน้ากลุ่ม', fallback:fallback, version:'v70.29'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, phone:'', name:'', position:'', department:department || '', message:e.toString(), version:'v70.29'};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV729() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.29-user-multi-profiles', functions:[
    'getMyUserProfilesV729','setActiveUserProfileV729','getUserProfilesForAdminV729',
    'saveUserProfileV729','setUserProfileActiveV729','bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV729'
  ]};
}

// ========================= v70.30: login performance + database repair =========================
function haosV730HeaderMap_(sheet) {
  var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
  var headers = lastCol ? sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0] : [];
  var map = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
    var key = String(headers[i] || '').trim();
    if (!key) continue;
    if (!map[key]) map[key] = [];
    map[key].push(i + 1);
  }
  return {headers:headers, map:map};
}

function haosV730RepairAction_(result, action, sheet, row, col, label, detail) {
  result.items.push({action:action, sheet:sheet || '', row:row || '', col:col || '', label:label || '', detail:detail || ''});
  result.counts[action] = (result.counts[action] || 0) + 1;
}

function haosV730GetAllUsersFast_() {
  var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  if (!sh) {
    Setup();
    sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  }
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return {success:true, data:[], version:'v70.30-fast-user-directory'};
  var userRows = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, Math.min(sh.getLastColumn(), 12)).getValues();
  var profileRows = [];
  var pSh = getDB().getSheetByName('UserProfiles');
  if (pSh && pSh.getLastRow() > 1) {
    profileRows = pSh.getRange(2, 1, pSh.getLastRow() - 1, Math.min(pSh.getLastColumn(), 16)).getValues();
  }
  var rawProfilesByPhone = {};
  profileRows.forEach(function(row) {
    if (!haosV729Bool_(row[7], true)) return;
    var phone = haosV729CleanPhone_(row[1]);
    if (!phone) return;
    if (!rawProfilesByPhone[phone]) rawProfilesByPhone[phone] = [];
    rawProfilesByPhone[phone].push(row);
  });

  var usersList = [];
  userRows.forEach(function(row) {
    var status = row[10] || 'Active';
    if (status === 'Inactive' || status === 'Pending' || status === 'Rejected') return;
    var base = haosV729UserObj_(row, sh);
    var rawProfiles = rawProfilesByPhone[base.phone] || [];
    var seen = {};
    if (!rawProfiles.length) {
      rawProfiles = [[
        '', base.phone, base.fullName || '', base.department || '', base.position || '',
        base.role === 'Head' ? 'Head' : 'Member', true, true,
        haosV729EffectiveRole_(base.role, base.role === 'Head' ? 'Head' : 'Member', false) === 'Head',
        '', base.email || '', '', '', '', '', 'fallback directory profile'
      ]];
    }
    rawProfiles.forEach(function(pRow) {
      var profile = haosV729ProfileFromRow_(pRow, base);
      var key = haosV729ProfileKey_(profile);
      if (seen[key]) return;
      seen[key] = true;
      usersList.push({
        phone: base.phone,
        name: base.fullName,
        fullName: base.fullName,
        position: profile.position || base.position,
        department: profile.department || base.department,
        role: profile.effectiveRole || base.role,
        accountRole: base.role,
        profileRole: profile.profileRole,
        profileId: profile.profileId,
        accountStatus: status,
        email: base.email || ''
      });
    });
  });
  usersList.sort(function(a,b){ return String(a.department || '').localeCompare(String(b.department || ''), 'th') || String(a.name || '').localeCompare(String(b.name || ''), 'th'); });
  return {success:true, data:usersList, version:'v70.30-fast-user-directory'};
}

function haosV730DeleteRowsDesc_(sheet, rows) {
  rows = (rows || []).sort(function(a,b){ return b - a; });
  var deleted = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    if (rows[i] > 1) {
      sheet.deleteRow(rows[i]);
      deleted++;
    }
  }
