  var allowed = haosV745PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ ? haosV745PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
  allowed.bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV745 = true;
  return allowed;
};

// ==========================================================
// HAOS v70.48 - Booking owner change requests + OTP reset PIN
// ==========================================================
function haosV748CleanPhone_(phone) {
  try {
    if (typeof haosV718CleanPhone_ === 'function') return haosV718CleanPhone_(phone);
  } catch (ignore) {}
  return String(phone || '').replace(/'/g, '').replace(/\D/g, '');
}

function haosV748Norm_(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .replace(/^\s*\d{1,3}\s+(?=(?:กลุ่มงาน|สำนักงาน|นพ\.สสจ\.นย\.|รอง|ผู้ช่วย))/u, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

function haosV748IsAdmin_(phone) {
  phone = haosV748CleanPhone_(phone);
  try {
    if (typeof isITServiceStaff_ === 'function' && isITServiceStaff_(phone)) return true;
  } catch (ignore) {}
  try {
    var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
    if (!found) return false;
    var role = String(found.values[7] || '');
    return role === 'Admin' || role === 'Super Admin';
  } catch (ignore2) {}
  return false;
}

function haosV748BookingChangeSheet_() {
  var ss = getDB();
  var name = 'ITBookingChangeRequests';
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sheet) sheet = ss.insertSheet(name);
  ensureHeaders_(sheet, [
    'Request ID', 'Booking ID', 'Request Type', 'Owner Phone', 'Owner Name',
    'Payload JSON', 'Reason', 'Status', 'Created At', 'Reviewed At',
    'Reviewer Phone', 'Reviewer Name', 'Review Note'
  ], '#dbeafe');
  return sheet;
}

function haosV748BookingChangeMap_(sheet) {
  var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
  var map = {};
  headers.forEach(function(h, i) { map[String(h)] = i + 1; });
  return map;
}

function haosV748FindITBookingRow_(bookingId) {
  var sheet = getITBookingSheet_();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var map = itBookingHeaderMapV47_(sheet);
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(itGetByHeader_(data[i], map, 'Booking ID')) === String(bookingId)) {
      return {sheet: sheet, row: i + 1, values: data[i], map: map, booking: itBookingRowToObject_(data[i])};
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV748BookingChangeObject_(row, map) {
  function val(name) {
    var idx = map[name];
    return idx ? row[idx - 1] : '';
  }
  var payload = {};
  try { payload = JSON.parse(String(val('Payload JSON') || '{}')); } catch (ignore) {}
  return {
    requestId: val('Request ID'),
    bookingId: val('Booking ID'),
    requestType: val('Request Type'),
    ownerPhone: haosV748CleanPhone_(val('Owner Phone')),
    ownerName: val('Owner Name'),
    payload: payload,
    reason: val('Reason'),
    status: val('Status') || 'Pending',
    createdAt: val('Created At'),
    reviewedAt: val('Reviewed At'),
    reviewerPhone: haosV748CleanPhone_(val('Reviewer Phone')),
    reviewerName: val('Reviewer Name'),
    reviewNote: val('Review Note')
  };
}

function getITBookingChangeRequestsV748(actorPhone) {
  try {
    actorPhone = haosV748CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    var isAdmin = haosV748IsAdmin_(actorPhone);
    var sheet = haosV748BookingChangeSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var map = haosV748BookingChangeMap_(sheet);
    var requests = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var req = haosV748BookingChangeObject_(data[i], map);
      if (isAdmin || req.ownerPhone === actorPhone) {
        requests.push(req);
      }
    }
    requests.sort(function(a, b) {
      return new Date(b.createdAt || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt || 0).getTime();
    });
    return {success:true, requests:requests};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), requests:[]};
  }
}

function requestITBookingChangeV748(bookingId, requestType, payload, actorPhone) {
  try {
    actorPhone = haosV748CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    payload = payload || {};
    requestType = String(requestType || '').toLowerCase() === 'cancel' ? 'cancel' : 'edit';
    var found = haosV748FindITBookingRow_(bookingId);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอจองห้อง/Zoom'};
    var booking = found.booking;
    if (haosV748CleanPhone_(booking.ownerPhone) !== actorPhone) {
      return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะเจ้าของคำขอเท่านั้นที่ส่งคำขอแก้ไข/ยกเลิกได้'};
    }
    var statusText = String(booking.status || '');
    if (/ยกเลิก/.test(statusText)) return {success:false, message:'รายการนี้ถูกยกเลิกแล้ว'};

    var sheet = haosV748BookingChangeSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var map = haosV748BookingChangeMap_(sheet);
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var req = haosV748BookingChangeObject_(data[i], map);
      if (String(req.bookingId) === String(bookingId) && req.ownerPhone === actorPhone && req.status === 'Pending') {
        return {success:false, message:'มีคำขอที่รออนุมัติอยู่แล้ว กรุณารอเจ้าหน้าที่พิจารณาก่อน'};
      }
    }
    var id = 'ITBC-' + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 10).toUpperCase();
    var reason = String(payload.reason || payload.cancelReason || '').trim();
    var rowObj = {
      'Request ID': id,
      'Booking ID': bookingId,
      'Request Type': requestType,
      'Owner Phone': "'" + actorPhone,
      'Owner Name': booking.ownerName || getUserNameByPhone_(actorPhone) || '',
      'Payload JSON': JSON.stringify(payload),
      'Reason': reason,
      'Status': 'Pending',
      'Created At': new Date(),
      'Reviewed At': '',
      'Reviewer Phone': '',
      'Reviewer Name': '',
      'Review Note': ''
    };
    var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String);
    sheet.appendRow(headers.map(function(h){ return rowObj[h] !== undefined ? rowObj[h] : ''; }));
    try {
      createNotificationsV732_(
        getITServiceStaffPhones_(),
        (requestType === 'cancel' ? 'มีคำขอยกเลิก' : 'มีคำขอแก้ไข') + ' รายการจองห้อง/Zoom รออนุมัติ: ' + (booking.topic || bookingId),
        'IT Booking Change Request',
        id,
        'ด่วน',
        {module:'itBooking', action:'openItBooking', createdBy:actorPhone}
      );
    } catch (ignore) {}
    logAudit_(actorPhone, booking.ownerName || '', 'REQUEST_IT_BOOKING_CHANGE', 'ITBookingChangeRequest', id, 'ส่งคำขอ' + requestType + ' booking ' + bookingId, booking, payload);
    return {success:true, message:'ส่งคำขอให้เจ้าหน้าที่พิจารณาแล้ว', requestId:id};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function reviewITBookingChangeRequestV748(requestId, approve, note, adminPhone) {
  try {
    adminPhone = haosV748CleanPhone_(adminPhone);
    if (!haosV748IsAdmin_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์อนุมัติคำขอนี้'};
    var sheet = haosV748BookingChangeSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var map = haosV748BookingChangeMap_(sheet);
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var req = haosV748BookingChangeObject_(data[i], map);
      if (String(req.requestId) !== String(requestId)) continue;
      if (req.status !== 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'คำขอนี้ถูกพิจารณาไปแล้ว'};
      var found = haosV748FindITBookingRow_(req.bookingId);
      if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายการจองต้นทาง'};
      var before = found.booking;
      if (approve) {
        if (req.requestType === 'cancel') {
          itSetByHeader_(found.sheet, found.row, found.map, 'Status', 'ยกเลิก');
          itSetByHeader_(found.sheet, found.row, found.map, 'Cancel Reason', req.reason || note || '');
          itSetByHeader_(found.sheet, found.row, found.map, 'Updated At', new Date());
          try { haosV748UpdateLinkedBookingSchedule_(req.bookingId, 'cancel', adminPhone); } catch(ignoreSyncCancel) {}
        } else {
          var p = req.payload || {};
          var fieldMap = {
            topic:'Topic', bookingType:'Booking Type', joinOption:'Join Option',
            meetingId:'Meeting ID', passcode:'Passcode', meetingLink:'Meeting Link',
            resourceId:'Resource ID', roomName:'Room Name', otherLocation:'Other Location',
            startTime:'Start Time', endTime:'End Time', signatureData:'Signature Data'
          };
          Object.keys(fieldMap).forEach(function(k) {
            if (p[k] === undefined) return;
            var v = p[k];
            if (k === 'startTime' || k === 'endTime') {
              var d = new Date(v);
              v = isNaN(d.getTime()) ? v : d;
            }
            if (k === 'meetingId' || k === 'passcode') itSetTextByHeaderV794_(found.sheet, found.row, found.map, fieldMap[k], v || '');
            else itSetByHeader_(found.sheet, found.row, found.map, fieldMap[k], v || '');
          });
          itSetByHeader_(found.sheet, found.row, found.map, 'Updated At', new Date());
          try { haosV748UpdateLinkedBookingSchedule_(req.bookingId, 'edit', adminPhone); } catch(ignoreSyncEdit) {}
        }
      }
      var r = i + 1;
      sheet.getRange(r, map['Status']).setValue(approve ? 'Approved' : 'Rejected');
      sheet.getRange(r, map['Reviewed At']).setValue(new Date());
      sheet.getRange(r, map['Reviewer Phone']).setValue("'" + adminPhone);
      sheet.getRange(r, map['Reviewer Name']).setValue(getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone) || '');
      sheet.getRange(r, map['Review Note']).setValue(note || '');
      try {
        createNotificationV732_(
          req.ownerPhone,
          'ผลการพิจารณาคำขอ' + (req.requestType === 'cancel' ? 'ยกเลิก' : 'แก้ไข') + ' จองห้อง/Zoom: ' + (approve ? 'อนุมัติ' : 'ไม่อนุมัติ') + ' - ' + (before.topic || req.bookingId),
          'IT Booking Change Result',
          req.requestId,
          approve ? 'ปกติ' : 'ด่วน',
          {module:'itBooking', action:'openItBooking', createdBy:adminPhone}
        );
      } catch (ignore) {}
      logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), approve ? 'APPROVE_IT_BOOKING_CHANGE' : 'REJECT_IT_BOOKING_CHANGE', 'ITBookingChangeRequest', requestId, 'พิจารณาคำขอเปลี่ยนแปลง booking', before, req);
      return {success:true, message:approve ? 'อนุมัติคำขอเรียบร้อยแล้ว' : 'ไม่อนุมัติคำขอเรียบร้อยแล้ว'};
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอ'};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function haosV748UpdateLinkedBookingSchedule_(bookingId, mode, actorPhone) {
  try {
    if (typeof haosV745ScheduleIdForBooking_ !== 'function') return {success:true, skipped:true};
    var scheduleId = haosV745ScheduleIdForBooking_(bookingId);
    if (!scheduleId) return {success:true, skipped:true};
    var found = haosV748FindITBookingRow_(bookingId);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอจองต้นทาง'};
    var booking = found.booking;
    var start = typeof haosV745LocalDateTime_ === 'function' ? haosV745LocalDateTime_(booking.rawStartTime || booking.startTime) : (booking.rawStartTime || booking.startTime);
    var end = typeof haosV745LocalDateTime_ === 'function' ? haosV745LocalDateTime_(booking.rawEndTime || booking.endTime || booking.rawStartTime || booking.startTime) : (booking.rawEndTime || booking.endTime || booking.rawStartTime || booking.startTime);
    if (!start) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบเวลาเริ่มต้น'};
    var room = booking.roomName || booking.otherLocation || booking.resourceId || '';
    var link = booking.adminLink || booking.meetingLink || '';
    var details = [
      'ใบขออนุญาตใช้ระบบประชุมออนไลน์',
      'เรื่อง: ' + (booking.topic || '-'),
      'ผู้ขอ: ' + (booking.ownerName || '-'),
      'หน่วยงาน: ' + (booking.department || '-'),
      'ห้อง/สถานที่: ' + (room || '-'),
      'เวลา: ' + (start || '-') + (end ? ' ถึง ' + end : ''),
      'สถานะ: ' + (mode === 'cancel' ? 'ยกเลิกตามคำขอที่อนุมัติแล้ว' : 'อนุมัติ'),
      '[ITBooking:' + bookingId + ']'
    ].join('\n');
    return updateScheduleV2({
      eventEditId: scheduleId,
      eventName: 'ประชุมออนไลน์/จองห้อง: ' + (booking.topic || bookingId),
      startTime: start,
      endTime: end || start,
      meetingLink: link,
      chatId: '',
      leadTime: 30,
      workStatus: mode === 'cancel' ? 'ยกเลิกสถานะ' : 'อยู่ระหว่างการดำเนินการ',
      ownerPhone: booking.ownerPhone,
      ownerName: booking.ownerName || getUserNameByPhone_(booking.ownerPhone),
      resourceId: 'ITB-' + bookingId,
      fileUrl: '',
      details: details,
      location: room,
      scheduleScope: 'Department',
      ownerDepartment: booking.department || '',
      priority: 'ปกติ',
      tags: 'จองห้องประชุม/Zoom, Meeting Online'
    });
  } catch (e) {
    try { logError_(actorPhone || '', 'haosV748UpdateLinkedBookingSchedule_', e, {bookingId:bookingId, mode:mode}); } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

var haosV748PrevGetITBookings_ = typeof getITBookings === 'function' ? getITBookings : null;
if (haosV748PrevGetITBookings_) {
  getITBookings = function(phone, role, department) {
    var res = haosV748PrevGetITBookings_.apply(this, arguments);
    try {
      if (res && res.success) {
        var changes = getITBookingChangeRequestsV748(phone);
        var pendingMap = {};
        (changes.requests || []).forEach(function(req) {
          if (req.status === 'Pending') pendingMap[String(req.bookingId)] = req;
        });
        (res.data || []).forEach(function(item) {
          item.pendingChangeRequest = pendingMap[String(item.id)] || null;
        });
        res.changeRequests = changes.requests || [];
      }
    } catch (ignore) {}
    return res;
  };
}

function haosV748OtpSheet_() {
  var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName('PasswordResetOtp');
  if (!sheet) sheet = getDB().insertSheet('PasswordResetOtp');
  ensureHeaders_(sheet, ['Token', 'Phone', 'Email', 'OTP Hash', 'Status', 'Created At', 'Expires At', 'Used At', 'Attempts'], '#fee2e2');
  return sheet;
}

function haosV748Hash_(value) {
  return Utilities.base64Encode(Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, String(value || '')));
}

function haosV748EmailOk_(email) {
  return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(String(email || '').trim());
}

function haosV748ForgotUser_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var phone = haosV748CleanPhone_(payload.phone);
  if (!phone) return {ok:false, message:'กรุณาระบุเบอร์โทรศัพท์'};
  var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
  var active = haosV727ActiveUserOrMessage_(found);
  if (!active.ok) return {ok:false, message:active.message};
  var row = found.values;
  var checks = [
    {label:'ชื่อ-นามสกุล', input:payload.fullName, actual:row[2]},
    {label:'ตำแหน่ง', input:payload.position, actual:row[3]},
    {label:'กลุ่มงาน', input:payload.department, actual:row[4]},
    {label:'อีเมล', input:payload.email, actual:row[5]}
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < checks.length; i++) {
    if (haosV748Norm_(checks[i].input) !== haosV748Norm_(checks[i].actual)) {
      logAudit_(phone, row[2] || '', 'FORGOT_PIN_OTP_DENIED', 'User', phone, 'ข้อมูลยืนยันตัวตนไม่ตรง: ' + checks[i].label, null, {field:checks[i].label});
      return {ok:false, message:'ข้อมูลยืนยันตัวตนไม่ตรงกับระบบ กรุณาตรวจสอบ ' + checks[i].label + ' หรือ ติดต่อ Admin'};
    }
  }
  if (!haosV748EmailOk_(row[5])) return {ok:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ยังไม่มีอีเมลที่ถูกต้อง กรุณาติดต่อ Admin เพื่อเพิ่มอีเมลก่อนรีเซ็ต PIN'};
  return {ok:true, phone:phone, email:String(row[5] || '').trim(), name:row[2] || '', found:found};
}

function sendForgotPinOtpV748(payload) {
  try {
    var found = haosV748ForgotUser_(payload);
    if (!found.ok) return {success:false, message:found.message};
    var otp = String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000));
    var token = 'OTP-' + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '').substring(0, 18).toUpperCase();
    var now = new Date();
    var exp = new Date(now.getTime() + 10 * 60 * 1000);
    var sheet = haosV748OtpSheet_();
    sheet.appendRow([token, "'" + found.phone, found.email, haosV748Hash_(token + '|' + otp + '|' + found.email), 'Pending', now, exp, '', 0]);
    MailApp.sendEmail({
      to: found.email,
      subject: '[Health Assistant OS] OTP สำหรับรีเซ็ต PIN',
      body: 'เรียน ' + (found.name || 'ผู้ใช้งาน') + '\n\nรหัส OTP สำหรับรีเซ็ต PIN คือ: ' + otp + '\nรหัสนี้หมดอายุภายใน 10 นาที\n\nหากไม่ได้ดำเนินการเอง กรุณาติดต่อ Admin / Super Admin ทันที'
    });
    logAudit_(found.phone, found.name || '', 'SEND_FORGOT_PIN_OTP', 'User', found.phone, 'ส่ง OTP รีเซ็ต PIN ไปยังอีเมล', null, {email:found.email});
    return {success:true, otpRequired:true, otpToken:token, emailMasked:found.email.replace(/^(.{1,2}).*(@.*)$/, '$1***$2'), phone:found.phone, name:found.name, message:'ส่ง OTP ไปยังอีเมลที่ลงทะเบียนไว้แล้ว'};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function verifyForgotPinOtpAndResetV748(payload) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var token = String(payload.otpToken || payload.token || '').trim();
    var otp = String(payload.otp || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (!token || otp.length !== 6) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุ OTP ให้ครบ 6 หลัก'};
    var identity = haosV748ForgotUser_(payload);
    if (!identity.ok) return {success:false, message:identity.message};
    var sheet = haosV748OtpSheet_();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0] || '') !== token) continue;
      var row = i + 1;
      var status = String(data[i][4] || '');
      var expires = data[i][6] ? new Date(data[i][6]) : null;
      var attempts = Number(data[i][8] || 0);
      if (status !== 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'OTP นี้ถูกใช้หรือหมดอายุแล้ว'};
      if (expires && expires.getTime() < new Date().getTime()) {
        sheet.getRange(row, 5).setValue('Expired');
        return {success:false, message:'OTP หมดอายุแล้ว กรุณาขอรหัสใหม่'};
      }
      if (attempts >= 5) {
        sheet.getRange(row, 5).setValue('Locked');
        return {success:false, message:'กรอก OTP ผิดเกินจำนวนที่กำหนด กรุณาขอรหัสใหม่'};
      }
      var expected = String(data[i][3] || '');
      var actual = haosV748Hash_(token + '|' + otp + '|' + identity.email);
      if (expected !== actual) {
        sheet.getRange(row, 9).setValue(attempts + 1);
        return {success:false, message:'OTP ไม่ถูกต้อง'};
      }
      var newPin = haosV727NewPin_();
      identity.found.sheet.getRange(identity.found.row, 2).setValue("'" + newPin);
      sheet.getRange(row, 5).setValue('Used');
      sheet.getRange(row, 8).setValue(new Date());
      try {
        MailApp.sendEmail(identity.email, '[Health Assistant OS] รีเซ็ต PIN สำเร็จ', 'เรียน ' + (identity.name || 'ผู้ใช้งาน') + '\n\nระบบรีเซ็ต PIN ให้เรียบร้อยแล้ว\nเบอร์โทรศัพท์: ' + identity.phone + '\nPIN ใหม่: ' + newPin + '\n\nหากไม่ได้ดำเนินการเอง กรุณาติดต่อ Admin / Super Admin ทันที');
      } catch (ignore) {}
      logAudit_(identity.phone, identity.name || '', 'SELF_RESET_PIN_OTP', 'User', identity.phone, 'ผู้ใช้รีเซ็ต PIN ด้วย OTP ทางอีเมล', null, {pin:'******'});
      return {success:true, message:'รีเซ็ต PIN สำเร็จ', pin:newPin, name:identity.name, phone:identity.phone};
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ OTP กรุณาขอรหัสใหม่'};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

var haosV748PrevRequestForgotPinResetV727_ = typeof requestForgotPinResetV727 === 'function' ? requestForgotPinResetV727 : null;
requestForgotPinResetV727 = function(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  if (payload.step === 'verify' || payload.otp || payload.otpToken) {
    return verifyForgotPinOtpAndResetV748(payload);
  }
  return sendForgotPinOtpV748(payload);
};

var haosV748PrevRegisterAccount_ = typeof registerAccount === 'function' ? registerAccount : null;
if (haosV748PrevRegisterAccount_) {
  registerAccount = function(data) {
    data = data || {};
    if (!haosV748EmailOk_(data.email)) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุอีเมลที่ถูกต้อง อีเมลจำเป็นสำหรับ OTP รีเซ็ต PIN และการแจ้งเตือนระบบ'};
    return haosV748PrevRegisterAccount_.apply(this, arguments);
  };
}

function notifyUsersMissingEmailV748(adminPhone) {
  try {
    adminPhone = haosV748CleanPhone_(adminPhone);
    var foundAdmin = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(adminPhone);
    var adminRole = foundAdmin ? String(foundAdmin.values[7] || '') : '';
    if (adminRole !== 'Admin' && adminRole !== 'Super Admin') return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var users = getAdminUsersData();
    if (!users || !users.success) return {success:false, message:'โหลดรายชื่อผู้ใช้ไม่สำเร็จ'};
    var count = 0;
    (users.data || []).forEach(function(u) {
      if (u.accountStatus === 'Inactive' || u.accountStatus === 'Rejected') return;
      if (haosV748EmailOk_(u.email)) return;
      createNotificationV732_(u.phone, 'กรุณาเพิ่มอีเมลในตั้งค่าโปรไฟล์ส่วนตัว เพื่อใช้รับ OTP รีเซ็ต PIN และการแจ้งเตือนสำคัญของระบบ', 'Profile Email Required', u.phone, 'ด่วน', {module:'profile', action:'openProfile', createdBy:adminPhone});
      count++;
    });
    return {success:true, message:'ส่งแจ้งเตือนผู้ใช้ที่ยังไม่มีอีเมลแล้ว ' + count + ' รายการ', count:count};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

// ========================= v70.50: admin user management + current system tools =========================
function haosV750CleanPhone_(value) {
  return String(value || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
}

function haosV750IsAdminOrSuper_(phone) {
  phone = haosV750CleanPhone_(phone);
  if (!phone) return false;
  try {
    if (typeof isSuperAdmin_ === 'function' && isSuperAdmin_(phone)) return true;
  } catch (ignore) {}
  try {
    var found = typeof haosV718UserRowByPhone_ === 'function' ? haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone) : null;
    if (found && found.values) {
      var role = String(found.values[7] || '').trim();
      return role === 'Admin' || role === 'Super Admin';
    }
  } catch (ignore2) {}
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    var values = sh ? sh.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (haosV750CleanPhone_(values[i][0]) === phone) {
        var role2 = String(values[i][7] || '').trim();
        return role2 === 'Admin' || role2 === 'Super Admin';
      }
    }
  } catch (ignore3) {}
  return false;
}

function haosV750FunctionExists_(fn) {
  try {
    if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' && typeof globalThis[fn] === 'function') return true;
  } catch (ignore) {}
  try {
    if (typeof this !== 'undefined' && typeof this[fn] === 'function') return true;
  } catch (ignore2) {}
  try {
    return typeof eval(fn) === 'function';
  } catch (ignore3) {}
  return false;
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV750(adminPhone) {
  try {
    if (adminPhone && !haosV750IsAdminOrSuper_(adminPhone)) {
      return {success:false, version:'v70.50-admin-system-tools', message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Admin / Super Admin'};
    }
    var required = [
      'getAdminUsersData', 'superAdminUpdateUserProfileV719',
      'getMyUserProfilesV729', 'getUserProfilesForAdminV729', 'saveUserProfileV729', 'setUserProfileActiveV729',
      'loginAccountFastV730', 'repairDatabaseV730',
      'registerAutoLoginDeviceV731', 'getMyAutoLoginDevicesV731', 'revokeAutoLoginDeviceV731',
      'getNotificationCoverageReportV732', 'getNotificationCenter', 'markNotifAsRead', 'markAllNotificationsAsRead',
      'createSharedWorkspace', 'getMySharedWorkspaces', 'getSharedWorkspacePublicUrl', 'updateSharedWorkspaceConfigV737',
      'getITBookings', 'saveITBooking', 'requestITBookingChangeV748', 'getITBookingChangeRequestsV748', 'reviewITBookingChangeRequestV748',
      'sendForgotPinOtpV748', 'verifyForgotPinOtpAndResetV748', 'notifyUsersMissingEmailV748',
      'createSystemBackupV63', 'listSystemBackupsV63', 'restoreSystemBackupV63',
      'getPermissionMatrixV64', 'savePermissionMatrixV64',
      'getBrandingConfigV65', 'saveBrandingConfigV65', 'resyncAllGoogleCalendarV66'
    ];
    var allowed = typeof getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ === 'function' ? getAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
    var checks = required.map(function(fn) {
      return {name:fn, exists:haosV750FunctionExists_(fn), whitelisted:!!allowed[fn]};
    });
    var missing = checks.filter(function(x){ return !x.exists || !x.whitelisted; }).map(function(x){ return x.name; });
    var ss = getDB();
    var sheetNames = [
      SHEET_USERS, 'UserProfiles', SHEET_NAME, SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS, SHEET_IT_BOOKINGS,
      'ITBookingChangeRequests', 'AutoLoginDevices', 'PasswordResetOtp',
      SHEET_WORKSPACES, SHEET_MEETING_MINUTES, SHEET_BACKUP_LOGS, SHEET_PERMISSION_MATRIX,
      SHEET_SETTINGS, SHEET_AUDIT
    ];
    var sheets = sheetNames.map(function(name) {
      var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
      return {name:name, ok:!!sh, rows:sh ? Math.max(0, sh.getLastRow() - 1) : 0};
    });
    return {
      success:missing.length === 0,
      version:'v70.50-admin-system-tools',
      message:missing.length ? 'พบฟังก์ชันที่ยังไม่พร้อม ' + missing.length + ' รายการ' : 'เครื่องมือระบบขั้นสูงพร้อมใช้งานกับฟังก์ชันปัจจุบัน',
      checks:checks,
      sheets:sheets,
      missing:missing
    };
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, version:'v70.50-admin-system-tools', message:e.toString()};
  }
}

// ========================= v70.51: simplify forgot PIN identity check =========================
function haosV748ForgotUser_(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var phone = haosV748CleanPhone_(payload.phone);
  var email = String(payload.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!phone) return {ok:false, message:'กรุณาระบุเบอร์โทรศัพท์'};
  if (!haosV748EmailOk_(email)) return {ok:false, message:'กรุณาระบุอีเมลให้ถูกต้อง'};
  var found = haosV718UserRowByPhone_(phone);
  var active = haosV727ActiveUserOrMessage_(found);
  if (!active.ok) return {ok:false, message:active.message};
  var row = found.values;
  var storedEmail = String(row[5] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!haosV748EmailOk_(storedEmail)) {
    return {ok:false, message:'บัญชีนี้ยังไม่มีอีเมลที่ถูกต้อง กรุณาติดต่อ Admin / Super Admin เพื่อเพิ่มอีเมลก่อนรีเซ็ต PIN'};
  }
  if (email !== storedEmail) {
    logAudit_(phone, row[2] || '', 'FORGOT_PIN_OTP_DENIED', 'User', phone, 'เบอร์โทรศัพท์และอีเมลไม่ตรงกับระบบ', null, {email:email});
    return {ok:false, message:'เบอร์โทรศัพท์หรืออีเมลไม่ตรงกับข้อมูลในระบบ'};
  }
  return {ok:true, phone:phone, email:storedEmail, name:row[2] || '', found:found};
}

// ========================= v70.52: Remote Support Hub phase 1 + rollback-safe database =========================
function haosV752RemoteSessionHeaders_() {
  return [
    'Session ID', 'Session Code', 'Ticket ID', 'Requester Phone', 'Requester Name',
    'Department', 'Issue Type', 'Subject', 'Description', 'Preferred Mode',
    'AnyDesk ID', 'Agent Version', 'Status', 'Assigned To Phone', 'Assigned To Name',
    'Consent Status', 'Control Status', 'Created At', 'Expires At',
    'Started At', 'Ended At', 'Last Heartbeat', 'Join URL', 'Notes', 'Rollback Tag'
  ];
}

function haosV752RemoteEventHeaders_() {
  return ['Event ID', 'Timestamp', 'Session ID', 'Actor Phone', 'Actor Name', 'Event Type', 'Details JSON', 'Rollback Tag'];
}

function haosV752RemoteSignalHeaders_() {
  return ['Signal ID', 'Session ID', 'From Role', 'To Role', 'Payload JSON', 'Created At', 'Expires At', 'Consumed At', 'Rollback Tag'];
}

function haosV752RemoteSheet_(name, headers, color) {
  var sh = getOrCreateSheet_(name, headers, color || '#dbeafe');
  ensureHeaders_(sh, headers, color || '#dbeafe');
  return sh;
}

function haosV752SessionsSheet_() {
  return haosV752RemoteSheet_('RemoteSupportSessions', haosV752RemoteSessionHeaders_(), '#dbeafe');
}

function haosV752EventsSheet_() {
  return haosV752RemoteSheet_('RemoteSupportEvents', haosV752RemoteEventHeaders_(), '#e0f2fe');
}

function haosV752SignalsSheet_() {
  return haosV752RemoteSheet_('RemoteSupportSignals', haosV752RemoteSignalHeaders_(), '#ede9fe');
}

function haosV752Clean_(value, maxLen) {
  value = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  maxLen = maxLen || 300;
  return value.length > maxLen ? value.substring(0, maxLen) : value;
}

function haosV752CleanPhone_(value) {
  return String(value || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
}

function haosV752Enabled_() {
  return String(getSetting_('REMOTE_SUPPORT_ENABLED', 'ON') || 'ON').toUpperCase() !== 'OFF';
}

function haosV752IsRemoteAdmin_(phone) {
  phone = haosV752CleanPhone_(phone);
  if (!phone) return false;
  try { if (typeof isSuperAdmin_ === 'function' && isSuperAdmin_(phone)) return true; } catch(ignore) {}
  try { if (typeof isITServiceStaff_ === 'function' && isITServiceStaff_(phone)) return true; } catch(ignore2) {}
  try {
    var profile = getUserProfileByPhone_(phone) || {};
    return profile.role === 'Admin' || profile.role === 'Super Admin';
  } catch(ignore3) {}
  return false;
}

function haosV752SessionObject_(row, map) {
  function cell(name) {
    var idx = map[name] || 0;
    return idx ? row[idx - 1] : '';
  }
  return {
    sessionId: cell('Session ID'),
    sessionCode: cell('Session Code'),
    ticketId: cell('Ticket ID'),
    requesterPhone: String(cell('Requester Phone') || '').replace(/'/g, ''),
    requesterName: cell('Requester Name'),
    department: cell('Department'),
    issueType: cell('Issue Type'),
    subject: cell('Subject'),
    description: cell('Description'),
    preferredMode: cell('Preferred Mode'),
    anydeskId: cell('AnyDesk ID'),
    agentVersion: cell('Agent Version'),
    status: cell('Status') || 'Waiting',
    assignedToPhone: String(cell('Assigned To Phone') || '').replace(/'/g, ''),
    assignedToName: cell('Assigned To Name'),
    consentStatus: cell('Consent Status'),
    controlStatus: cell('Control Status'),
    createdAt: cell('Created At'),
    expiresAt: cell('Expires At'),
    startedAt: cell('Started At'),
    endedAt: cell('Ended At'),
    lastHeartbeat: cell('Last Heartbeat'),
    joinUrl: cell('Join URL'),
    notes: cell('Notes'),
    rollbackTag: cell('Rollback Tag')
  };
}

function haosV752HeaderMap_(sh) {
  var headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String);
  var map = {};
  headers.forEach(function(h, i){ map[h] = i + 1; });
  return map;
}

function haosV752AppendRemoteEvent_(sessionId, actorPhone, eventType, details) {
  try {
    var actor = getUserProfileByPhone_(actorPhone) || {};
    haosV752EventsSheet_().appendRow([
      'RSE-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 10),
      new Date(),
      sessionId || '',
      actorPhone ? "'" + haosV752CleanPhone_(actorPhone) : '',
      actor.fullName || '',
      eventType || 'EVENT',
      JSON.stringify(details || {}),
      'REMOTE_SUPPORT_V752'
    ]);
  } catch(ignore) {}
}

function haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey) {
  var sh = haosV752SessionsSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var map = haosV752HeaderMap_(sh);
  var key = String(sessionKey || '').trim();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var obj = haosV752SessionObject_(data[i], map);
    if (String(obj.sessionId) === key || String(obj.sessionCode) === key) return {sheet:sh, row:i + 1, map:map, data:data[i], item:obj};
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV752SessionCode_() {
  var sh = haosV752SessionsSheet_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var existing = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) existing[String(data[i][1] || '')] = true;
  for (var t = 0; t < 20; t++) {
    var code = String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000));
    if (!existing[code]) return code;
  }
  return String(new Date().getTime()).slice(-6);
}

function getRemoteSupportConfigV752() {
  return {
    success:true,
    enabled:haosV752Enabled_(),
    version:'v70.58-anydesk-support-hub',
    remoteUrl:getPublicAppBaseUrl_() + '/remote.html',
    agentName:'AnyDesk Portable',
    mode:'anydesk-ticket-dispatch',
    downloadUrl:'https://anydesk.com/en/downloads/windows',
    message:haosV752Enabled_() ? 'Remote Support ใช้ AnyDesk เป็นเครื่องมือช่วยเหลือทางไกลหลัก' : 'Remote Support ถูกปิดใช้งานชั่วคราว'
  };
}

function setRemoteSupportEnabledV752(adminPhone, enabled) {
  try {
    adminPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(adminPhone);
    if (!isSuperAdmin_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin'};
    var value = enabled === false || String(enabled).toUpperCase() === 'OFF' ? 'OFF' : 'ON';
    setSetting_('REMOTE_SUPPORT_ENABLED', value, 'เปิด/ปิดโมดูล Remote Support แบบ rollback-safe');
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'REMOTE_SUPPORT_TOGGLE', 'Settings', 'REMOTE_SUPPORT_ENABLED', 'Remote Support = ' + value, null, {enabled:value});
    return {success:true, enabled:value === 'ON', message:value === 'ON' ? 'เปิด Remote Support แล้ว' : 'ปิด Remote Support แล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function createRemoteSupportSessionV752(payload, actorPhone) {
  try {
    if (!haosV752Enabled_()) return {success:false, message:'Remote Support ถูกปิดใช้งานชั่วคราว'};
    payload = payload || {};
    actorPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(actorPhone || payload.requesterPhone || payload.phone);
    if (!actorPhone) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุเบอร์ผู้ขอรับบริการ'};
    var profile = getUserProfileByPhone_(actorPhone);
    if (!profile) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบผู้ใช้งานจากเบอร์นี้ในระบบ'};
    var subject = haosV752Clean_(payload.subject, 160);
    if (!subject) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุหัวข้อปัญหา'};
    var now = new Date();
    var sessionId = 'RS-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 10).toUpperCase();
    var code = haosV752SessionCode_();
    var joinUrl = getPublicAppBaseUrl_() + '/remote.html?code=' + encodeURIComponent(code);
    var rowObj = {
      'Session ID': sessionId,
      'Session Code': "'" + code,
      'Ticket ID': haosV752Clean_(payload.ticketId, 80),
      'Requester Phone': "'" + actorPhone,
      'Requester Name': profile.fullName || haosV752Clean_(payload.requesterName, 120),
      'Department': profile.department || haosV752Clean_(payload.department, 120),
      'Issue Type': haosV752Clean_(payload.issueType, 80) || 'Remote Support',
      'Subject': subject,
      'Description': haosV752Clean_(payload.description, 2000),
      'Preferred Mode': haosV752Clean_(payload.preferredMode, 80) || 'AnyDesk Portable',
      'AnyDesk ID': haosV752Clean_(payload.anydeskId || payload.anyDeskId, 80),
      'Agent Version': '',
      'Status': 'Waiting',
      'Assigned To Phone': '',
      'Assigned To Name': '',
      'Consent Status': 'Not Required',
      'Control Status': 'Handled in AnyDesk',
      'Created At': now,
      'Expires At': new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
      'Started At': '',
      'Ended At': '',
      'Last Heartbeat': '',
      'Join URL': joinUrl,
      'Notes': haosV752Clean_(payload.notes, 1000),
      'Rollback Tag': 'REMOTE_SUPPORT_V752'
    };
    var sessionSheet = haosV752SessionsSheet_();
    var sessionMap = haosV752HeaderMap_(sessionSheet);
    var row = new Array(sessionSheet.getLastColumn()).fill('');
    Object.keys(rowObj).forEach(function(name) {
      var col = sessionMap[name];
      if (col) row[col - 1] = rowObj[name];
    });
    sessionSheet.appendRow(row);
    haosV752AppendRemoteEvent_(sessionId, actorPhone, 'CREATED', {subject:subject, issueType:payload.issueType || '', anydeskId:payload.anydeskId || payload.anyDeskId || '', preferredMode:payload.preferredMode || 'AnyDesk Portable'});
    try {
      createNotificationsV732_(getITServiceStaffPhones_(), 'มีคำขอช่วยเหลือทางไกลใหม่: ' + subject + ' จาก ' + (profile.fullName || actorPhone), 'Remote Support', sessionId, 'ปกติ', {module:'remoteSupport', action:'openRemoteSupport', createdBy:actorPhone});
      createNotificationV732_(actorPhone, 'สร้างคำขอช่วยเหลือทางไกลแล้ว รหัส Session: ' + code, 'Remote Support Submitted', sessionId, 'ปกติ', {module:'remoteSupport', action:'openRemoteSupport', createdBy:actorPhone});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {success:true, message:'สร้างคำขอช่วยเหลือทางไกลแล้ว', session:{
      sessionId:sessionId, sessionCode:code, requesterPhone:actorPhone, requesterName:profile.fullName || '', department:profile.department || '',
      issueType:payload.issueType || 'Remote Support', subject:subject, anydeskId:payload.anydeskId || payload.anyDeskId || '', status:'Waiting', joinUrl:joinUrl, createdAt:now
    }};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getRemoteSupportSessionV752(sessionKey, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var found = haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ session'};
    var item = found.item;
    var clean = haosV752CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    var canManage = haosV752IsRemoteAdmin_(clean);
    if (clean && !canManage && item.requesterPhone !== clean) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ดู session นี้'};
    return {success:true, session:item, canManage:canManage};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getRemoteSupportSessionsV752(actorPhone, role, department, filters) {
  try {
    actorPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    filters = filters || {};
    var canManage = haosV752IsRemoteAdmin_(actorPhone);
    var sh = haosV752SessionsSheet_();
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var map = haosV752HeaderMap_(sh);
    var list = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var item = haosV752SessionObject_(data[i], map);
      if (!canManage && item.requesterPhone !== actorPhone) continue;
      if (filters.status && String(item.status) !== String(filters.status)) continue;
      if (filters.query) {
        var q = String(filters.query).toLowerCase();
        var hay = [item.sessionCode, item.anydeskId, item.requesterName, item.department, item.issueType, item.subject, item.description, item.status].join(' ').toLowerCase();
        if (hay.indexOf(q) === -1) continue;
      }
      list.push(item);
    }
    list.sort(function(a, b) {
      return new Date(b.createdAt || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt || 0).getTime();
    });
    return {success:true, sessions:list, canManage:canManage, enabled:haosV752Enabled_(), version:'v70.52-remote-support-phase1'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function updateRemoteSupportSessionV752(sessionKey, actorPhone, action, payload) {
  try {
    if (!haosV752Enabled_()) return {success:false, message:'Remote Support ถูกปิดใช้งานชั่วคราว'};
    actorPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    action = String(action || '').trim();
    payload = payload || {};
    var found = haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ session'};
    var item = found.item;
    var canManage = haosV752IsRemoteAdmin_(actorPhone);
    var isOwner = actorPhone && item.requesterPhone === actorPhone;
    var openByCode = !actorPhone && item.sessionCode === String(sessionKey || '').trim();
    if (!canManage && !isOwner && !openByCode) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์อัปเดต session นี้'};
    var sh = found.sheet;
    var map = found.map;
    function set(name, value) {
      var col = map[name];
      if (col) sh.getRange(found.row, col).setValue(value);
    }
    var actor = getUserProfileByPhone_(actorPhone) || {};
    var now = new Date();
    if (action === 'assign') {
      if (!canManage) return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะเจ้าหน้าที่ IT/Admin เท่านั้น'};
      set('Assigned To Phone', "'" + actorPhone);
      set('Assigned To Name', actor.fullName || '');
      set('Status', 'Assigned');
    } else if (action === 'setAnyDeskId') {
      if (!canManage && !isOwner) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์แก้ไข AnyDesk ID'};
      set('AnyDesk ID', haosV752Clean_(payload.anydeskId || payload.anyDeskId, 80));
      set('Last Heartbeat', now);
    } else if (action === 'agentReady') {
      set('Agent Version', haosV752Clean_(payload.agentVersion || payload.version || 'Portable Agent', 120));
      set('Last Heartbeat', now);
      if (item.status === 'Waiting') set('Status', 'Agent Ready');
    } else if (action === 'requestConsent') {
      if (!canManage) return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะเจ้าหน้าที่ IT/Admin เท่านั้น'};
      set('Consent Status', 'Requested');
      set('Control Status', haosV752Clean_(payload.controlStatus, 80) || 'View Only Requested');
    } else if (action === 'consent') {
      if (!isOwner && !openByCode) return {success:false, message:'ผู้ขอรับบริการต้องยืนยันเอง'};
      set('Consent Status', payload.approved === false ? 'Denied' : 'Approved');
      set('Control Status', payload.approved === false ? 'Blocked by User' : (haosV752Clean_(payload.controlStatus, 80) || 'View Only Approved'));
    } else if (action === 'start') {
      if (!canManage) return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะเจ้าหน้าที่ IT/Admin เท่านั้น'};
      set('Status', 'In Session');
      set('Started At', item.startedAt || now);
      if (!item.assignedToPhone) {
        set('Assigned To Phone', "'" + actorPhone);
        set('Assigned To Name', actor.fullName || '');
      }
    } else if (action === 'end') {
      if (!canManage && !isOwner) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์จบ session'};
      set('Status', 'Closed');
      set('Ended At', now);
      set('Notes', [item.notes, haosV752Clean_(payload.note, 1000)].filter(Boolean).join('\n'));
    } else if (action === 'cancel') {
      if (!canManage && !isOwner) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์ยกเลิก session'};
      set('Status', 'Canceled');
      set('Ended At', now);
      set('Notes', [item.notes, 'Cancel: ' + haosV752Clean_(payload.note, 800)].filter(Boolean).join('\n'));
    } else if (action === 'heartbeat') {
      set('Last Heartbeat', now);
    } else if (action === 'note') {
      if (!canManage && !isOwner) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์บันทึกหมายเหตุ'};
      set('Notes', [item.notes, haosV752Clean_(payload.note, 1000)].filter(Boolean).join('\n'));
    } else {
      return {success:false, message:'action ไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    }
    haosV752AppendRemoteEvent_(item.sessionId, actorPhone, action.toUpperCase(), payload);
    try {
      if (item.requesterPhone && canManage && action !== 'heartbeat') {
        createNotificationV732_(item.requesterPhone, 'Remote Support: ' + item.subject + ' — ' + action, 'Remote Support Update', item.sessionId, 'ปกติ', {module:'remoteSupport', action:'openRemoteSupport', createdBy:actorPhone});
      }
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return getRemoteSupportSessionV752(item.sessionId, actorPhone);
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function cleanupRemoteSupportDatabaseV752(adminPhone, options) {
  try {
    adminPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(adminPhone);
    options = options || {};
    if (!isSuperAdmin_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin'};
    var dryRun = options.dryRun !== false;
    var confirm = String(options.confirmText || '');
    if (!dryRun && confirm !== 'ROLLBACK_REMOTE_SUPPORT') return {success:false, message:'กรุณายืนยันด้วยคำว่า ROLLBACK_REMOTE_SUPPORT'};
    var ss = getDB();
    var targets = [
      {name:'RemoteSupportSessions', headers:haosV752RemoteSessionHeaders_(), color:'#dbeafe'},
      {name:'RemoteSupportEvents', headers:haosV752RemoteEventHeaders_(), color:'#e0f2fe'},
      {name:'RemoteSupportSignals', headers:haosV752RemoteSignalHeaders_(), color:'#ede9fe'}
    ];
    var result = {success:true, dryRun:dryRun, dropSheets:!!options.dropSheets, items:[], counts:{}, version:'v70.52-remote-support-rollback'};
    targets.forEach(function(t) {
      var sh = ss.getSheetByName(t.name);
      var rows = sh ? Math.max(0, sh.getLastRow() - 1) : 0;
      result.counts[t.name] = rows;
      result.items.push({sheet:t.name, rows:rows, action:options.dropSheets ? 'deleteSheet' : 'clearRowsKeepHeader'});
      if (!dryRun && sh) {
        if (options.dropSheets) {
          ss.deleteSheet(sh);
        } else {
          sh.clearContents();
          sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, t.headers.length).setValues([t.headers]);
          try { sh.setTabColor(t.color); } catch(ignore) {}
        }
      }
    });
    if (options.purgeNotifications) {
      var notif = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS);
      var removed = 0;
      if (notif && notif.getLastRow() > 1) {
        var map = haosV752HeaderMap_(notif);
        var typeCol = map['Type'] || 6;
        var moduleCol = map['Module'] || 11;
        var data = notif.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
          var type = String(data[i][typeCol - 1] || '').toLowerCase();
          var moduleName = String(data[i][moduleCol - 1] || '').toLowerCase();
          if (type.indexOf('remote support') !== -1 || moduleName === 'remotesupport') {
            removed++;
            if (!dryRun) notif.deleteRow(i + 1);
          }
        }
      }
      result.counts.RemoteSupportNotifications = removed;
      result.items.push({sheet:SHEET_NOTIFICATIONS, rows:removed, action:'deleteRemoteSupportNotifications'});
    }
    if (!dryRun && options.disableModule !== false) {
      setSetting_('REMOTE_SUPPORT_ENABLED', 'OFF', 'ปิดหลัง rollback Remote Support');
    }
    if (!dryRun) {
      logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'REMOTE_SUPPORT_ROLLBACK', 'Database', 'RemoteSupport', 'Rollback Remote Support database', null, result);
    }
    result.message = dryRun ? 'ตรวจ rollback Remote Support แล้ว ยังไม่ได้ลบข้อมูล' : 'Rollback Remote Support database สำเร็จ';
    return result;
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV752() {
  return {success:true, version:'v70.52-remote-support-phase1', functions:[
    'getRemoteSupportConfigV752', 'setRemoteSupportEnabledV752', 'createRemoteSupportSessionV752',
    'getRemoteSupportSessionV752', 'getRemoteSupportSessionsV752', 'updateRemoteSupportSessionV752',
    'cleanupRemoteSupportDatabaseV752', 'bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV752'
  ]};
}

var haosV752PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = typeof getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ === 'function' ? getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ : null;
getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = function() {
  var allowed = haosV752PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ ? haosV752PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
  [
    'getRemoteSupportConfigV752', 'setRemoteSupportEnabledV752', 'createRemoteSupportSessionV752',
    'getRemoteSupportSessionV752', 'getRemoteSupportSessionsV752', 'updateRemoteSupportSessionV752',
    'cleanupRemoteSupportDatabaseV752', 'bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV752'
  ].forEach(function(fn){ allowed[fn] = true; });
  return allowed;
};

// ========================= v70.53: Remote Support portable agent MVP + view-only + consent-gated control =========================
function haosV753RemoteFrameHeaders_() {
  return [
    'Frame ID', 'Session ID', 'Session Code', 'Image MIME', 'Image Base64',
    'Frame Width', 'Frame Height', 'Screen Width', 'Screen Height',
    'Captured At', 'Agent Version', 'Created At', 'Rollback Tag'
  ];
}

function haosV753FramesSheet_() {
  return haosV752RemoteSheet_('RemoteSupportFrames', haosV753RemoteFrameHeaders_(), '#dcfce7');
}

function haosV753SetSessionField_(found, name, value) {
  var col = found && found.map ? found.map[name] : 0;
  if (col) found.sheet.getRange(found.row, col).setValue(value);
}

function haosV753ActorName_(phone) {
  try {
    var p = getUserProfileByPhone_(phone) || {};
    return p.fullName || '';
  } catch(e) {
    return '';
  }
}

function haosV753AppendSignal_(sessionId, fromRole, toRole, payload, ttlMinutes) {
  var now = new Date();
  var expires = new Date(now.getTime() + (ttlMinutes || 5) * 60 * 1000);
  var signalId = 'RSS-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 10).toUpperCase();
  haosV752SignalsSheet_().appendRow([
    signalId,
    sessionId || '',
    fromRole || 'System',
    toRole || 'Agent',
    JSON.stringify(payload || {}),
    now,
    expires,
    '',
    'REMOTE_SUPPORT_V753'
  ]);
  return signalId;
}

function haosV753LatestFrame_(sessionId) {
  var sh = haosV753FramesSheet_();
  if (sh.getLastRow() < 2) return null;
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var map = haosV752HeaderMap_(sh);
  var sidCol = map['Session ID'] - 1;
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (String(data[i][sidCol] || '') === String(sessionId || '')) {
      return {
        frameId:data[i][map['Frame ID'] - 1],
        sessionId:data[i][map['Session ID'] - 1],
        sessionCode:String(data[i][map['Session Code'] - 1] || '').replace(/'/g, ''),
        mime:data[i][map['Image MIME'] - 1] || 'image/jpeg',
        imageBase64:data[i][map['Image Base64'] - 1] || '',
        frameWidth:Number(data[i][map['Frame Width'] - 1] || 0),
        frameHeight:Number(data[i][map['Frame Height'] - 1] || 0),
        screenWidth:Number(data[i][map['Screen Width'] - 1] || 0),
        screenHeight:Number(data[i][map['Screen Height'] - 1] || 0),
        capturedAt:data[i][map['Captured At'] - 1],
        agentVersion:data[i][map['Agent Version'] - 1] || '',
        createdAt:data[i][map['Created At'] - 1]
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function haosV753PruneFrames_(sessionId, keepRow) {
  try {
    var sh = haosV753FramesSheet_();
    if (sh.getLastRow() < 3) return;
    var map = haosV752HeaderMap_(sh);
    var sidCol = map['Session ID'];
    for (var r = sh.getLastRow(); r >= 2; r--) {
      if (r === keepRow) continue;
      if (String(sh.getRange(r, sidCol).getValue() || '') === String(sessionId || '')) sh.deleteRow(r);
    }
  } catch(ignore) {}
}

function getRemoteSupportConfigV753() {
  var cfg = getRemoteSupportConfigV752();
  cfg.version = 'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp';
  cfg.mode = 'portable-agent-view-only-control-mvp';
  cfg.phaseStatus = {
    phase2:'Portable Agent MVP ready as source/build scaffold',
    phase3:'View-only screenshot polling ready',
    phase4:'Remote control command queue ready with required user consent'
  };
  cfg.limits = {maxFrameBase64:45000, framePollSeconds:2, commandPollSeconds:2};
  cfg.security = [
    'No unattended access',
    'Remote control requires explicit user consent',
    'Only approved command types are queued',
    'All actions are written to RemoteSupportEvents'
  ];
  return cfg;
}

function setRemoteSupportEnabledV753(adminPhone, enabled) {
  return setRemoteSupportEnabledV752(adminPhone, enabled);
}

function createRemoteSupportSessionV753(payload, actorPhone) {
  var res = createRemoteSupportSessionV752(payload, actorPhone);
  if (res && res.success && res.session) {
    res.version = 'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp';
    haosV752AppendRemoteEvent_(res.session.sessionId, actorPhone || res.session.requesterPhone, 'REMOTE_MVP_READY', {phase:'2-4', mode:'portable-agent'});
  }
  return res;
}

function getRemoteSupportSessionV753(sessionKey, actorPhone) {
  var res = getRemoteSupportSessionV752(sessionKey, actorPhone);
  if (res && res.success && res.session) {
    var frame = haosV753LatestFrame_(res.session.sessionId);
    res.latestFrame = frame ? {
      frameId:frame.frameId,
      frameWidth:frame.frameWidth,
      frameHeight:frame.frameHeight,
      screenWidth:frame.screenWidth,
      screenHeight:frame.screenHeight,
      capturedAt:frame.capturedAt,
      agentVersion:frame.agentVersion
    } : null;
    res.version = 'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp';
  }
  return res;
}

function getRemoteSupportSessionsV753(actorPhone, role, department, filters) {
  var res = getRemoteSupportSessionsV752(actorPhone, role, department, filters);
  if (res && res.success) res.version = 'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp';
  return res;
}

function updateRemoteSupportSessionV753(sessionKey, actorPhone, action, payload) {
  try {
    action = String(action || '').trim();
    payload = payload || {};
    if (action !== 'requestControl' && action !== 'controlConsent' && action !== 'stopControl') {
      var base = updateRemoteSupportSessionV752(sessionKey, actorPhone, action, payload);
      if (base && base.success) base.version = 'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp';
      return base;
    }
    if (!haosV752Enabled_()) return {success:false, message:'Remote Support ถูกปิดใช้งานชั่วคราว'};
    actorPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(actorPhone);
    var found = haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ session'};
    var item = found.item;
    var canManage = haosV752IsRemoteAdmin_(actorPhone);
    var isOwner = actorPhone && item.requesterPhone === actorPhone;
    var openByCode = !actorPhone && item.sessionCode === String(sessionKey || '').trim();
    var now = new Date();
    if (action === 'requestControl') {
      if (!canManage) return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะเจ้าหน้าที่ IT/Admin เท่านั้น'};
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Consent Status', 'Control Requested');
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Control Status', 'Control Requested');
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Last Heartbeat', now);
      haosV753AppendSignal_(item.sessionId, 'Staff', 'Agent', {
        type:'requestControl',
        requestedByPhone:actorPhone,
        requestedByName:haosV753ActorName_(actorPhone),
        message:haosV752Clean_(payload.message, 300)
      }, 10);
      try {
        createNotificationV732_(item.requesterPhone, 'Remote Support ขออนุญาตควบคุมเครื่อง: ' + item.subject, 'Remote Support Control Request', item.sessionId, 'ด่วน', {module:'remoteSupport', action:'openRemoteSupport', createdBy:actorPhone});
      } catch(ignore) {}
    } else if (action === 'controlConsent') {
      if (!isOwner && !openByCode) return {success:false, message:'ผู้ใช้ปลายทางต้องยืนยันการควบคุมเอง'};
      var approved = payload.approved !== false;
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Consent Status', approved ? 'Approved' : 'Denied');
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Control Status', approved ? 'Control Approved' : 'Control Denied');
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Last Heartbeat', now);
      haosV753AppendSignal_(item.sessionId, 'Agent', 'Staff', {type:'controlConsent', approved:approved}, 10);
    } else if (action === 'stopControl') {
      if (!canManage && !isOwner && !openByCode) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีสิทธิ์หยุดการควบคุม'};
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Control Status', 'Control Stopped');
      haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Last Heartbeat', now);
      haosV753AppendSignal_(item.sessionId, canManage ? 'Staff' : 'Agent', canManage ? 'Agent' : 'Staff', {type:'stopControl'}, 10);
    }
    haosV752AppendRemoteEvent_(item.sessionId, actorPhone, action.toUpperCase(), payload);
    return getRemoteSupportSessionV753(item.sessionId, actorPhone);
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function agentRemoteSupportCheckInV753(sessionKey, payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  return updateRemoteSupportSessionV753(sessionKey, '', 'agentReady', {
    agentVersion:payload.agentVersion || payload.version || 'HealthAssistantRemoteAgent MVP'
  });
}

function pushRemoteSupportFrameV753(sessionKey, payload) {
  try {
    if (!haosV752Enabled_()) return {success:false, message:'Remote Support ถูกปิดใช้งานชั่วคราว'};
    payload = payload || {};
    var found = haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ session'};
    var item = found.item;
    if (String(item.status || '').match(/Closed|Canceled/i)) return {success:false, message:'session ปิดแล้ว'};
    var image = String(payload.imageBase64 || payload.dataUrl || '');
    var mime = String(payload.mime || 'image/jpeg');
    var m = image.match(/^data:(image\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+-]+);base64,(.*)$/);
    if (m) {
      mime = m[1];
      image = m[2];
    }
    image = image.replace(/\s/g, '');
    if (!image) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบภาพหน้าจอ'};
    if (image.length > 45000) return {success:false, message:'ภาพใหญ่เกินไปสำหรับ MVP กรุณาลดขนาด/quality ของ Agent'};
    var now = new Date();
    var sh = haosV753FramesSheet_();
    var frameId = 'RSF-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 10).toUpperCase();
    sh.appendRow([
      frameId,
      item.sessionId,
      "'" + item.sessionCode,
      mime,
      image,
      Number(payload.frameWidth || payload.width || 0),
      Number(payload.frameHeight || payload.height || 0),
      Number(payload.screenWidth || 0),
      Number(payload.screenHeight || 0),
      payload.capturedAt ? new Date(payload.capturedAt) : now,
      haosV752Clean_(payload.agentVersion || payload.version || '', 160),
      now,
      'REMOTE_SUPPORT_V753'
    ]);
    haosV753PruneFrames_(item.sessionId, sh.getLastRow());
    haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Last Heartbeat', now);
    if (payload.agentVersion) haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Agent Version', haosV752Clean_(payload.agentVersion, 160));
    if (item.status === 'Waiting') haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Status', 'Agent Ready');
    return {success:true, frameId:frameId, capturedAt:now, version:'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getRemoteSupportFrameV753(sessionKey, actorPhone) {
  try {
    var session = getRemoteSupportSessionV752(sessionKey, actorPhone);
    if (!session || !session.success) return session;
    var frame = haosV753LatestFrame_(session.session.sessionId);
    if (!frame) return {success:true, frame:null, session:session.session, message:'ยังไม่มีภาพจาก Agent'};
    frame.dataUrl = 'data:' + frame.mime + ';base64,' + frame.imageBase64;
    delete frame.imageBase64;
    return {success:true, frame:frame, session:session.session, canManage:session.canManage, version:'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function enqueueRemoteSupportCommandV753(sessionKey, staffPhone, command) {
  try {
    if (!haosV752Enabled_()) return {success:false, message:'Remote Support ถูกปิดใช้งานชั่วคราว'};
    staffPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(staffPhone);
    if (!haosV752IsRemoteAdmin_(staffPhone)) return {success:false, message:'เฉพาะเจ้าหน้าที่ IT/Admin เท่านั้น'};
    var found = haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ session'};
    var item = found.item;
    command = command || {};
    var type = String(command.type || '').trim();
    var allowed = {requestFrame:true, click:true, doubleClick:true, rightClick:true, key:true, hotkey:true, typeText:true, scroll:true};
    if (!allowed[type]) return {success:false, message:'ชนิดคำสั่งไม่ถูกต้อง'};
    if (type !== 'requestFrame' && String(item.controlStatus || '') !== 'Control Approved') {
      return {success:false, message:'ยังไม่ได้รับอนุญาตให้ควบคุมเครื่อง'};
    }
    var payload = {
      type:type,
      commandId:'RSC-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 10).toUpperCase(),
      xRatio:Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(command.xRatio || 0))),
      yRatio:Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(command.yRatio || 0))),
      key:haosV752Clean_(command.key, 60),
      text:haosV752Clean_(command.text, 500),
      hotkeys:Array.isArray(command.hotkeys) ? command.hotkeys.slice(0, 4).map(function(k){ return haosV752Clean_(k, 40); }) : [],
      amount:Number(command.amount || 0),
      requestedByPhone:staffPhone,
      requestedByName:haosV753ActorName_(staffPhone)
    };
    var signalId = haosV753AppendSignal_(item.sessionId, 'Staff', 'Agent', payload, 3);
    haosV752AppendRemoteEvent_(item.sessionId, staffPhone, 'REMOTE_COMMAND_' + type.toUpperCase(), {signalId:signalId, commandId:payload.commandId});
    return {success:true, signalId:signalId, commandId:payload.commandId, version:'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function pollRemoteSupportCommandsV753(sessionKey, agentId) {
  try {
    var found = haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ session'};
    var item = found.item;
    var sh = haosV752SignalsSheet_();
    var map = haosV752HeaderMap_(sh);
    var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    var now = new Date();
    var commands = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][map['Session ID'] - 1] || '') !== String(item.sessionId || '')) continue;
      if (String(data[i][map['To Role'] - 1] || '') !== 'Agent') continue;
      if (data[i][map['Consumed At'] - 1]) continue;
      var expires = data[i][map['Expires At'] - 1];
      if (expires && new Date(expires).getTime() < now.getTime()) continue;
      var payload = {};
      try { payload = JSON.parse(String(data[i][map['Payload JSON'] - 1] || '{}')); } catch(ignore) {}
      payload.signalId = data[i][map['Signal ID'] - 1];
      commands.push(payload);
      sh.getRange(i + 1, map['Consumed At']).setValue(now);
      if (commands.length >= 10) break;
    }
    haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Last Heartbeat', now);
    return {success:true, session:item, commands:commands, version:'v70.53-remote-support-portable-agent-mvp'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function ackRemoteSupportCommandV753(sessionKey, agentId, commandId, result) {
  try {
    var found = haosV752FindSession_(sessionKey);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบ session'};
    haosV752AppendRemoteEvent_(found.item.sessionId, '', 'REMOTE_COMMAND_ACK', {agentId:agentId || '', commandId:commandId || '', result:result || {}});
    haosV753SetSessionField_(found, 'Last Heartbeat', new Date());
    return {success:true};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function cleanupRemoteSupportDatabaseV753(adminPhone, options) {
  var res = cleanupRemoteSupportDatabaseV752(adminPhone, options || {});
  if (!res || !res.success) return res;
  try {
    var ss = getDB();
    var dryRun = (options || {}).dryRun !== false;
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName('RemoteSupportFrames');
    var rows = sh ? Math.max(0, sh.getLastRow() - 1) : 0;
    res.counts = res.counts || {};
    res.counts.RemoteSupportFrames = rows;
    res.items = res.items || [];
    res.items.push({sheet:'RemoteSupportFrames', rows:rows, action:(options || {}).dropSheets ? 'deleteSheet' : 'clearRowsKeepHeader'});
    if (!dryRun && sh) {
      if ((options || {}).dropSheets) {
        ss.deleteSheet(sh);
      } else {
        sh.clearContents();
        sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, haosV753RemoteFrameHeaders_().length).setValues([haosV753RemoteFrameHeaders_()]);
        try { sh.setTabColor('#dcfce7'); } catch(ignore) {}
      }
    }
    res.version = 'v70.53-remote-support-rollback';
  } catch(e) {
    res.frameCleanupError = e.toString();
  }
  return res;
}

// ========================= v70.54: Remote Support role-aware UX routing =========================
function getRemoteSupportPortalV754(phone) {
  try {
    phone = haosV752CleanPhone_(phone);
    var profile = {};
    if (phone) {
      try { profile = getUserProfileByPhone_(phone) || {}; } catch(ignore) {}
    }
    var canManage = haosV752IsRemoteAdmin_(phone);
    var canRollback = false;
    try { canRollback = !!(phone && isSuperAdmin_(phone)); } catch(ignore2) {}
    return {
      success:true,
      version:'v70.54-remote-support-role-aware-ux',
      phone:phone,
      canManage:canManage,
      canRollback:canRollback,
      defaultMode:canManage ? 'staff' : 'user',
      profile:{
        fullName:profile.fullName || '',
        department:profile.department || '',
        role:profile.role || ''
      },
      labels:canManage ? {
        title:'คอนโซลช่วยเหลือทางไกล',
        subtitle:'รับงาน ดูหน้าจอ ขอสิทธิ์ควบคุม และจบงานจากหน้าเดียว'
      } : {
        title:'ขอให้ IT ช่วยเหลือทางไกล',
        subtitle:'กรอกปัญหา เปิด Agent แล้วรอเจ้าหน้าที่ช่วยเหลือ'
      }
    };
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

  cfg.version = 'v70.55-remote-support-realtime-relay';
  cfg.relayUrl = String(getSetting_('REMOTE_SUPPORT_RELAY_URL', '') || '').trim();
  cfg.relaySecretEnabled = !!String(getSetting_('REMOTE_SUPPORT_RELAY_SECRET', '') || '').trim();
  cfg.realtimeMode = cfg.relayUrl ? 'websocket-relay' : 'not-configured';
  cfg.realtimeViewerUrl = getPublicAppBaseUrl_() + '/remote-live.html';
  cfg.message = cfg.relayUrl ? 'Remote Support realtime relay พร้อมใช้งาน' : 'ยังไม่ได้ตั้งค่า REMOTE_SUPPORT_RELAY_URL';
  return cfg;
}

function setRemoteSupportRelayConfigV755(adminPhone, relayUrl, relaySecret) {
  try {
    adminPhone = haosV752CleanPhone_(adminPhone);
    if (!isSuperAdmin_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin'};
    relayUrl = String(relayUrl || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
    if (relayUrl && !/^wss?:\/\//i.test(relayUrl)) return {success:false, message:'Relay URL ต้องขึ้นต้นด้วย ws:// หรือ wss://'};
    setSetting_('REMOTE_SUPPORT_RELAY_URL', relayUrl, 'Remote Support realtime relay websocket URL');
    if (relaySecret !== undefined && relaySecret !== null) {
      setSetting_('REMOTE_SUPPORT_RELAY_SECRET', String(relaySecret || '').trim(), 'Remote Support relay shared secret');
    }
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), 'REMOTE_SUPPORT_RELAY_CONFIG', 'Settings', 'REMOTE_SUPPORT_RELAY_URL', relayUrl ? 'Set relay URL' : 'Clear relay URL', null, {relayUrl:relayUrl, hasSecret:!!relaySecret});
    return {success:true, relayUrl:relayUrl, message:relayUrl ? 'บันทึก Relay URL แล้ว' : 'ล้าง Relay URL แล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

// ========================= v70.58: AnyDesk remote support mode, de-whitelist legacy realtime relay APIs =========================
function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV758() {
  var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
  var required = [
    'getRemoteSupportConfigV752', 'setRemoteSupportEnabledV752', 'createRemoteSupportSessionV752',
    'getRemoteSupportSessionV752', 'getRemoteSupportSessionsV752', 'updateRemoteSupportSessionV752',
    'cleanupRemoteSupportDatabaseV752', 'getRemoteSupportPortalV754', 'bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV758'
  ];
  var blocked = [
    'getRemoteSupportConfigV753', 'setRemoteSupportEnabledV753', 'createRemoteSupportSessionV753',
    'getRemoteSupportSessionV753', 'getRemoteSupportSessionsV753', 'updateRemoteSupportSessionV753',
    'agentRemoteSupportCheckInV753', 'pushRemoteSupportFrameV753', 'getRemoteSupportFrameV753',
    'enqueueRemoteSupportCommandV753', 'pollRemoteSupportCommandsV753', 'ackRemoteSupportCommandV753',
    'cleanupRemoteSupportDatabaseV753', 'getRemoteSupportConfigV755', 'getRemoteSupportRelayTokenV755',
    'getRemoteSupportAgentConfigV755', 'setRemoteSupportRelayConfigV755'
  ];
  var missing = required.filter(function(fn){ return !allowed[fn]; });
  var stillOpen = blocked.filter(function(fn){ return !!allowed[fn]; });
  return {
    success: missing.length === 0 && stillOpen.length === 0,
    version: 'v70.58-anydesk-remote-support-whitelist',
    required: required,
    missing: missing,
    blocked: blocked,
    stillOpen: stillOpen,
    message: missing.length || stillOpen.length ? ('Missing: ' + missing.join(', ') + ' Still open: ' + stillOpen.join(', ')) : 'AnyDesk remote support bridge whitelist is ready.'
  };
}

    'getRemoteSupportAgentConfigV755', 'setRemoteSupportRelayConfigV755', 'bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV753',
    'bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV755'
  ].forEach(function(fn){ delete allowed[fn]; });

    'getRemoteSupportConfigV752', 'setRemoteSupportEnabledV752', 'createRemoteSupportSessionV752',

// ========================= v70.60: External organization directory for Super Admin user management =========================
var HAOS_V760_EXTERNAL_ORG_SETTING_ = 'EXTERNAL_ORGANIZATIONS_V760';

function haosV760CleanPhone_(value) {
  return String(value || '').replace(/'/g, '').replace(/\D/g, '').trim();
}

function haosV760CleanText_(value, maxLen) {
  var text = String(value == null ? '' : value).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (maxLen && text.length > maxLen) text = text.substring(0, maxLen);
  return text;
}

function haosV760ExternalValue_(name) {
  name = haosV760CleanText_(name, 160);
  return /^หน่วยงานภายนอก\s*:/i.test(name) ? name : ('หน่วยงานภายนอก: ' + name);
}

function haosV760ReadExternalOrganizations_() {
  var raw = String(getSetting_(HAOS_V760_EXTERNAL_ORG_SETTING_, '[]') || '[]');
  try {
    var list = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
  } catch (e) {
    return [];
  }
}

function haosV760WriteExternalOrganizations_(list) {
  setSetting_(HAOS_V760_EXTERNAL_ORG_SETTING_, JSON.stringify(list || []), 'External organizations allowed for registration/user profiles');
}

function haosV760AdminRole_(phone) {
  phone = haosV760CleanPhone_(phone);
  try {
    var profile = getUserProfileByPhone_(phone) || {};
    var role = String(profile.accountRole || profile.role || '').trim();
    return role;
  } catch (ignore) {}
  try {
    var sh = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
    var values = sh ? sh.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (haosV760CleanPhone_(values[i][0]) === phone) return String(values[i][7] || '').trim();
    }
  } catch (ignore2) {}
  return '';
}

function haosV760IsAdminRole_(role) {
  return /^(admin|super\s*admin)$/i.test(String(role || '').trim());
}

function haosV760IsSuperAdminRole_(role) {
  return /^super\s*admin$/i.test(String(role || '').trim());
}

function haosV760CanManageExternalOrganizations_(phone) {
  phone = haosV760CleanPhone_(phone);
  if (!phone) return false;
  if (phone === '0868246621') return true;
  try {
    if (typeof haosV719CanSuperEditUsers_ === 'function' && haosV719CanSuperEditUsers_(phone)) return true;
  } catch (ignore) {}
  try {
    if (typeof isSuperAdmin_ === 'function' && isSuperAdmin_(phone)) return true;
  } catch (ignore2) {}
  return haosV760IsSuperAdminRole_(haosV760AdminRole_(phone));
}

function haosV760CanViewExternalOrganizations_(phone) {
  var role = haosV760AdminRole_(phone);
  return haosV760CanManageExternalOrganizations_(phone) || haosV760IsAdminRole_(role);
}

function getExternalOrganizationsV760(actorPhone) {
  try {
    var canViewAll = haosV760CanViewExternalOrganizations_(actorPhone);
    var list = haosV760ReadExternalOrganizations_().map(function(org) {
      var name = haosV760CleanText_(org.name || org.value || '', 160);
      return {
        id: String(org.id || ''),
        name: name,
        value: haosV760ExternalValue_(name),
        contact: haosV760CleanText_(org.contact || '', 160),
        note: haosV760CleanText_(org.note || '', 300),
        active: org.active !== false && String(org.active).toLowerCase() !== 'false',
        createdAt: org.createdAt || '',
        updatedAt: org.updatedAt || '',
        createdBy: haosV760CleanPhone_(org.createdBy || ''),
        updatedBy: haosV760CleanPhone_(org.updatedBy || '')
      };
    }).filter(function(org) {
      return org.name && (canViewAll || org.active);
    });
    list.sort(function(a, b) {
      if (a.active !== b.active) return a.active ? -1 : 1;
      return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name), 'th');
    });
    return {success:true, data:list, canManage:haosV760CanManageExternalOrganizations_(actorPhone), version:'v70.60-external-orgs'};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), data:[]};
  }
}

function getDepartmentDirectoryV760() {
  try {
    var external = getExternalOrganizationsV760('');
    return {success:true, external:external.data || [], version:'v70.60-external-orgs'};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString(), external:[]};
  }
}

function saveExternalOrganizationV760(adminPhone, payload) {
  try {
    adminPhone = haosV760CleanPhone_(adminPhone);
    if (!haosV760CanManageExternalOrganizations_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    payload = payload || {};
    var name = haosV760CleanText_(payload.name || payload.value || '', 160);
    if (!name) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อหน่วยงานภายนอก'};
    name = name.replace(/^หน่วยงานภายนอก\s*:\s*/i, '').trim();
    if (!name) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุชื่อหน่วยงานภายนอก'};
    var contact = haosV760CleanText_(payload.contact || '', 160);
    var note = haosV760CleanText_(payload.note || '', 300);
    var now = new Date().toISOString();
    var list = haosV760ReadExternalOrganizations_();
    var targetKey = name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    var foundIndex = -1;
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      var rowName = haosV760CleanText_(list[i].name || list[i].value || '', 160).replace(/^หน่วยงานภายนอก\s*:\s*/i, '').trim();
      if (rowName.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ') === targetKey || String(list[i].id || '') === String(payload.id || '')) {
        foundIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    var before = foundIndex >= 0 ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(list[foundIndex])) : null;
    var org = foundIndex >= 0 ? list[foundIndex] : {};
    org.id = org.id || ('EXT-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8));
    org.name = name;
    org.value = haosV760ExternalValue_(name);
    org.contact = contact;
    org.note = note;
    org.active = payload.active === false ? false : true;
    org.createdAt = org.createdAt || now;
    org.updatedAt = now;
    org.createdBy = org.createdBy || adminPhone;
    org.updatedBy = adminPhone;
    if (foundIndex >= 0) list[foundIndex] = org; else list.push(org);
    haosV760WriteExternalOrganizations_(list);
    logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), before ? 'UPDATE_EXTERNAL_ORG' : 'CREATE_EXTERNAL_ORG', 'Settings', HAOS_V760_EXTERNAL_ORG_SETTING_, 'จัดการหน่วยงานภายนอก: ' + name, before, org);
    return {success:true, message:'บันทึกหน่วยงานภายนอกแล้ว', org:org, data:getExternalOrganizationsV760(adminPhone).data};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function setExternalOrganizationActiveV760(adminPhone, orgId, active) {
  try {
    adminPhone = haosV760CleanPhone_(adminPhone);
    if (!haosV760CanManageExternalOrganizations_(adminPhone)) return {success:false, message:'อนุญาตเฉพาะ Super Admin เท่านั้น'};
    orgId = String(orgId || '').trim();
    var list = haosV760ReadExternalOrganizations_();
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      if (String(list[i].id || '') === orgId) {
        var before = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(list[i]));
        list[i].active = !!active;
        list[i].updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        list[i].updatedBy = adminPhone;
        haosV760WriteExternalOrganizations_(list);
        logAudit_(adminPhone, getUserNameByPhone_(adminPhone), active ? 'ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_ORG' : 'DEACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_ORG', 'Settings', HAOS_V760_EXTERNAL_ORG_SETTING_, (active ? 'เปิดใช้งาน' : 'ปิดใช้งาน') + 'หน่วยงานภายนอก: ' + (list[i].name || orgId), before, list[i]);
        return {success:true, message:active ? 'เปิดใช้งานหน่วยงานภายนอกแล้ว' : 'ปิดใช้งานหน่วยงานภายนอกแล้ว', data:getExternalOrganizationsV760(adminPhone).data};
      }
    }
    return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบหน่วยงานภายนอกที่ต้องการแก้ไข'};
  } catch (e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV760(adminPhone) {
  var allowed = getAllowedBridgeFunctions_();
  var required = ['getExternalOrganizationsV760', 'getDepartmentDirectoryV760', 'saveExternalOrganizationV760', 'setExternalOrganizationActiveV760'];
  var missing = required.filter(function(fn){ return !allowed[fn]; });
  return {
    success: missing.length === 0,
    version: 'v70.60-external-orgs',
    required: required,
    missing: missing,
    externalOrganizations: getExternalOrganizationsV760(adminPhone || '').data || [],
    message: missing.length ? ('Missing: ' + missing.join(', ')) : 'External organization directory is ready.'
  };
}

var haosV760PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = typeof getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ === 'function' ? getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ : null;
getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = function() {
  var allowed = haosV760PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ ? haosV760PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
  [
    'getExternalOrganizationsV760',
    'getDepartmentDirectoryV760',
    'saveExternalOrganizationV760',
    'setExternalOrganizationActiveV760',
    'bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV760'
  ].forEach(function(fn){ allowed[fn] = true; });
  return allowed;
};

// ========================= v70.66: per-workspace public branding =========================
var HAOS_V766_WORKSPACE_BRANDING_HEADER_ = 'Workspace Branding JSON';

function haosV766DefaultWorkspaceBranding_() {
  return {
    enabled:false,
    appName:'Health Assistant OS',
    organizationName:'สำนักงานสาธารณสุขจังหวัดนครนายก'
  };
}

function haosV766CleanBrandText_(value, maxLen) {
  return String(value == null ? '' : value).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, maxLen || 120);
}

function haosV766NormalizeWorkspaceBranding_(branding) {
  branding = branding || {};
  var enabled = branding.enabled === true || String(branding.enabled || '').toLowerCase() === 'true' || String(branding.enabled || '') === '1';
  var appName = haosV766CleanBrandText_(branding.appName || branding.systemName || branding.productName || '', 120);
  var organizationName = haosV766CleanBrandText_(branding.organizationName || branding.orgName || branding.departmentName || '', 160);
  if (!enabled) return haosV766DefaultWorkspaceBranding_();
  if (!appName) appName = 'Health Assistant OS';
  if (!organizationName) organizationName = 'สำนักงานสาธารณสุขจังหวัดนครนายก';
  return {enabled:true, appName:appName, organizationName:organizationName};
}

function haosV766ParseWorkspaceBranding_(raw) {
  if (!raw) return haosV766DefaultWorkspaceBranding_();
  if (typeof raw === 'object') return haosV766NormalizeWorkspaceBranding_(raw);
  try {
    return haosV766NormalizeWorkspaceBranding_(JSON.parse(String(raw)));
  } catch (e) {
    return haosV766DefaultWorkspaceBranding_();
  }
}

function haosV766WorkspaceBrandingColumn_(sheet) {
  var col = getColumnIndex_(sheet, HAOS_V766_WORKSPACE_BRANDING_HEADER_);
  if (col < 1) {
    col = sheet.getLastColumn() + 1;
    sheet.getRange(1, col).setValue(HAOS_V766_WORKSPACE_BRANDING_HEADER_);
    try { sheet.getRange(1, col).setBackground('#ccfbf1').setFontWeight('bold'); } catch (ignore) {}
  }
  return col;
}

var haosV766PrevWorkspaceSetup_ = typeof workspaceSetup_ === 'function' ? workspaceSetup_ : null;
workspaceSetup_ = function() {
  var headers = typeof haosV735WorkspaceHeaders_ === 'function' ? haosV735WorkspaceHeaders_() : [];
  var sheet = haosV766PrevWorkspaceSetup_ ? haosV766PrevWorkspaceSetup_() : getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_WORKSPACES, headers, '#ccfbf1');
  haosV766WorkspaceBrandingColumn_(sheet);
  return sheet;
};

  return ws;

function haosV766WriteWorkspaceBranding_(sheet, workspaceId, branding) {
  var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
  if (!found) return;
  var normalized = haosV766NormalizeWorkspaceBranding_(branding || {});
  var col = haosV766WorkspaceBrandingColumn_(sheet);
  sheet.getRange(found.row, col).setValue(normalized.enabled ? JSON.stringify(normalized) : '');
}

function haosV766ReadWorkspaceBranding_(workspaceId) {
  var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
  var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
  if (!found) return haosV766DefaultWorkspaceBranding_();
  var col = haosV766WorkspaceBrandingColumn_(sheet);
  return haosV766ParseWorkspaceBranding_(found.values[col - 1] || '');
}
