// ========================= v70.68: shared workspace section flow =========================
var HAOS_V768_WORKSPACE_FLOW_HEADER_ = 'Workspace Flow JSON';

function haosV768Bool_(value, defaultValue) {
  if (value === true || value === false) return value;
  if (value == null || value === '') return !!defaultValue;
  var s = String(value).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (/^(true|yes|y|1|on|active)$/i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^(false|no|n|0|off|inactive)$/i.test(s)) return false;
  return !!defaultValue;
}

function haosV768SplitOptions_(value) {
  if (typeof haosV744SplitOptions_ === 'function') return haosV744SplitOptions_(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(function(x) { return String(x || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
  return String(value || '').split(/[,|]/).map(function(x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
}

function haosV768DefaultWorkspaceFlow_() {
  return {
    enabled: false,
    version: 'v1',
    mode: 'sections',
    sections: [{ id: 's1', title: 'Section 1' }],
    logicRules: []
  };
}

function haosV768ParseWorkspaceFlow_(raw) {
  if (!raw) return haosV768DefaultWorkspaceFlow_();
  if (typeof raw === 'object') return haosV768NormalizeWorkspaceFlow_(raw);
  try {
    return haosV768NormalizeWorkspaceFlow_(JSON.parse(String(raw)));
  } catch (e) {
    return haosV768DefaultWorkspaceFlow_();
  }
}

function haosV768NormalizeWorkspaceFlow_(flow) {
  flow = flow && typeof flow === 'object' ? flow : {};
  var sourceSections = Array.isArray(flow.sections) && flow.sections.length ? flow.sections : [{ id:'s1', title:'Section 1' }];
  var sections = [];
  var ids = {};
  sourceSections.forEach(function(section, index) {
    section = section || {};
    var id = String(section.id || ('s' + (index + 1))).replace(/[^\w-]/g, '').trim() || ('s' + (index + 1));
    if (ids[id]) id = id + '_' + (index + 1);
    ids[id] = true;
    sections.push({
      id: id,
      title: String(section.title || ('Section ' + (index + 1))).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() || ('Section ' + (index + 1))
    });
  });
  if (!sections.length) sections = [{ id:'s1', title:'Section 1' }];

  var logicRules = [];
  (Array.isArray(flow.logicRules) ? flow.logicRules : []).forEach(function(rule) {
    rule = rule || {};
    var fieldIndex = Number(rule.fieldIndex);
    var option = String(rule.option || '').trim();
    if (!isFinite(fieldIndex) || !option) return;
    var action = String(rule.action || 'next').trim();
    if (['next', 'section', 'submit'].indexOf(action) < 0) action = 'next';
    var targetSectionId = String(rule.targetSectionId || '').trim();
    if (action !== 'section' || !ids[targetSectionId]) targetSectionId = '';
    logicRules.push({
      fieldIndex: fieldIndex,
      option: option,
      action: action,
      targetSectionId: targetSectionId
    });
  });

  return {
    enabled: haosV768Bool_(flow.enabled, false),
    version: 'v1',
    mode: 'sections',
    sections: sections.slice(0, 40),
    logicRules: logicRules.slice(0, 400)
  };
}

function haosV768WorkspaceFlowColumn_(sheet) {
  var col = getColumnIndex_(sheet, HAOS_V768_WORKSPACE_FLOW_HEADER_);
  if (col < 1) {
    col = sheet.getLastColumn() + 1;
    sheet.getRange(1, col).setValue(HAOS_V768_WORKSPACE_FLOW_HEADER_);
    try { sheet.getRange(1, col).setBackground('#bfdbfe').setFontWeight('bold'); } catch (ignore) {}
  }
  return col;
}

var haosV768PrevWorkspaceSetup_ = typeof workspaceSetup_ === 'function' ? workspaceSetup_ : null;
workspaceSetup_ = function() {
  var sheet = haosV768PrevWorkspaceSetup_ ? haosV768PrevWorkspaceSetup_() : getOrCreateSheet_(SHEET_WORKSPACES, [], '#ccfbf1');
  haosV768WorkspaceFlowColumn_(sheet);
  return sheet;
};

var haosV768PrevNormalizeFieldConfig_ = typeof haosV735NormalizeFieldConfig_ === 'function' ? haosV735NormalizeFieldConfig_ : null;
haosV735NormalizeFieldConfig_ = function(columns, config) {
  var out = haosV768PrevNormalizeFieldConfig_ ? haosV768PrevNormalizeFieldConfig_(columns, config) : [];
  config = Array.isArray(config) ? config : [];
  out.forEach(function(field, index) {
    var item = config[index] || {};
    if (!item && field && field.label) {
      for (var i = 0; i < config.length; i++) {
        if (String(config[i] && config[i].label || '').trim() === String(field.label || '').trim()) {
          item = config[i];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    var sectionId = String(item.sectionId || item.section || item.flowSection || '').replace(/[^\w-]/g, '').trim();
    if (sectionId) field.sectionId = sectionId;
  });
  return out;
};

  row = row || [];
  ws.workspaceFlow = haosV768ParseWorkspaceFlow_(row[25] || '');
  return ws;
};

function haosV768WriteWorkspaceFlow_(sheet, workspaceId, flow) {
  var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
  if (!found) return;
  var normalized = haosV768NormalizeWorkspaceFlow_(flow || {});
  var col = haosV768WorkspaceFlowColumn_(sheet);
  sheet.getRange(found.row, col).setValue(JSON.stringify(normalized));
}

function haosV768ReadWorkspaceFlow_(workspaceId) {
  try {
    var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
    var found = findWorkspaceRow_(sheet, workspaceId);
    if (!found) return haosV768DefaultWorkspaceFlow_();
    var col = haosV768WorkspaceFlowColumn_(sheet);
    return haosV768ParseWorkspaceFlow_(found.values[col - 1] || '');
  } catch (e) {
    return haosV768DefaultWorkspaceFlow_();
  }
}

var haosV768PrevGetMySharedWorkspaces_ = typeof getMySharedWorkspaces === 'function' ? getMySharedWorkspaces : null;
getMySharedWorkspaces = function(actorPhone) {
  var res = haosV768PrevGetMySharedWorkspaces_ ? haosV768PrevGetMySharedWorkspaces_(actorPhone) : {success:false, message:'getMySharedWorkspaces is not available'};
  if (res && res.success && Array.isArray(res.data)) {
    var flowById = {};
    try {
      var sheet = workspaceSetup_();
      var col = haosV768WorkspaceFlowColumn_(sheet);
      var rows = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
        flowById[String(rows[i][0] || '')] = haosV768ParseWorkspaceFlow_(rows[i][col - 1] || '');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      flowById = {};
    }
    res.data.forEach(function(ws) {
      if (!ws || !ws.id) return;
      var flow = flowById[String(ws.id)] || haosV768DefaultWorkspaceFlow_();
      ws.workspaceFlow = flow;
      ws.workspaceFlowEnabled = !!flow.enabled;
      ws.workspaceFlowSections = flow.sections.length;
    });
  }
  return res;
};

function haosV768FieldRequiredEmpty_(field, value) {
  field = field || {};
  if (field.type === 'checkbox') {
    if (haosV768SplitOptions_(field.options).length) return !haosV768SplitOptions_(value).length;
    return !/^(true|1|yes|y|on|done|checked|\u2713|\u2714)$/i.test(String(value || '').trim());
  }
  return String(value == null ? '' : value).trim() === '';
}

function haosV768RequiredMissingForVisible_(ws, row, visibleIndexes) {
  var visible = {};
  (Array.isArray(visibleIndexes) ? visibleIndexes : []).forEach(function(index) {
    visible[String(Number(index))] = true;
  });
  var fields = Array.isArray(ws && ws.fieldConfig) && ws.fieldConfig.length ? ws.fieldConfig : haosV735NormalizeFieldConfig_((ws && ws.columns) || [], []);
  var missing = [];
  fields.forEach(function(field, fallbackIndex) {
    var index = Number(field.index != null ? field.index : fallbackIndex);
    if (!visible[String(index)]) return;
    if (!haosV768Bool_(field.required, false)) return;
    var label = String(field.label || '').trim();
    if (index === 0 && /timestamp/i.test(label)) return;
    if (haosV768FieldRequiredEmpty_(field, row[index])) missing.push(label || ('Field ' + (index + 1)));
  });
  return missing;
}

var haosV768PrevSubmitPublicWorkspaceForm_ = typeof submitPublicWorkspaceForm === 'function' ? submitPublicWorkspaceForm : null;
submitPublicWorkspaceForm = function(workspaceId, token, row) {
  var visibleIndexes = null;
  if (row && typeof row === 'object' && !Array.isArray(row)) {
    visibleIndexes = Array.isArray(row.visibleIndexes) ? row.visibleIndexes : null;
    row = Array.isArray(row.values) ? row.values : [];
  }
  if (!visibleIndexes || !visibleIndexes.length || !haosV768PrevSubmitPublicWorkspaceForm_) {
    return haosV768PrevSubmitPublicWorkspaceForm_ ? haosV768PrevSubmitPublicWorkspaceForm_(workspaceId, token, row) : {success:false, message:'submitPublicWorkspaceForm is not available'};
  }
  var prevRequired = typeof haosV735RequiredMissingLabels_ === 'function' ? haosV735RequiredMissingLabels_ : null;
  if (prevRequired) {
    haosV735RequiredMissingLabels_ = function(ws, values) {
      var flow = haosV768NormalizeWorkspaceFlow_(ws && ws.workspaceFlow);
      if (!flow.enabled) return prevRequired(ws, values);
      return haosV768RequiredMissingForVisible_(ws, values, visibleIndexes);
    };
  }
  try {
    return haosV768PrevSubmitPublicWorkspaceForm_(workspaceId, token, row);
  } finally {
    if (prevRequired) haosV735RequiredMissingLabels_ = prevRequired;
  }
};

// v70.73 schedule public link performance override
// ==========================================
function haosV773ScheduleHeaderMap_(sheet) {
  var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 1)).getValues()[0];
  var map = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
    var key = String(headers[i] || '').trim();
    if (key && !map[key]) map[key] = i + 1;
  }
  return map;
}

function haosV773EnsureSchedulePublicHeaders_(sheet) {
  var required = [
    'ID', 'Event Name', 'Start Time', 'End Time', 'Meeting Link', 'Visibility',
    'User Email', 'Telegram Chat ID', 'Notify Lead Time', 'Status', 'Owner Phone',
    'Resource ID', 'Approval Status', 'Approver Phone', 'File URL', 'Details',
    'Location', 'Public Enabled', 'Public Expires At', 'Public Created At',
    'Schedule Scope', 'Owner Department', 'Priority', 'Tags',
    'Google Calendar Event ID', 'Google Calendar Sync', 'Last Calendar Sync'
  ];
  var map = haosV773ScheduleHeaderMap_(sheet);
  var missing = [];
  required.forEach(function(header) {
    if (!map[header]) missing.push(header);
  });
  if (missing.length) {
    var startCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 1) + 1;
    sheet.getRange(1, startCol, 1, missing.length).setValues([missing]);
    sheet.getRange(1, startCol, 1, missing.length).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#e8f0fe');
    map = haosV773ScheduleHeaderMap_(sheet);
  }
  return map;
}

var haosV773PrevGetSchedulePublicUrlServer_ = typeof getSchedulePublicUrlServer === 'function' ? getSchedulePublicUrlServer : null;
getSchedulePublicUrlServer = function(scheduleId, ownerPhone, expiryDays) {
  try {
    scheduleId = String(scheduleId || '').trim();
    if (!scheduleId) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบรหัสรายการที่ต้องการสร้างลิงก์' };

    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME);
    if (!sheet) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบชีตตารางงาน' };

    var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) return { success: false, message: 'ยังไม่มีรายการตารางงาน' };

    var map = haosV773EnsureSchedulePublicHeaders_(sheet);
    var idCol = map['ID'] || 1;
    var ids = sheet.getRange(2, idCol, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
    var rowIndex = -1;
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      if (String(ids[i][0]) === scheduleId) {
        rowIndex = i + 2;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (rowIndex < 0) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบรายการที่ต้องการสร้างลิงก์' };

    var ownerCol = map['Owner Phone'] || 11;
    var targetPhone = ownerPhone ? String(ownerPhone).replace(/'/g, '').trim() : '';
    var rowPhone = String(sheet.getRange(rowIndex, ownerCol).getValue() || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
    if (targetPhone && rowPhone !== targetPhone) {
      return { success: false, message: 'คุณไม่มีสิทธิ์สร้างลิงก์สาธารณะของรายการนี้' };
    }

    var days = Number(expiryDays);
    if (isNaN(days)) days = Number(getSetting_('PUBLIC_LINK_EXPIRY_DAYS', 30));
    if (isNaN(days)) days = 30;

    var expiresAt = '';
    if (days > 0) expiresAt = new Date(new Date().getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    sheet.getRange(rowIndex, map['Public Enabled']).setValue('TRUE');
    sheet.getRange(rowIndex, map['Public Expires At']).setValue(expiresAt || '');
    sheet.getRange(rowIndex, map['Public Created At']).setValue(new Date());

    var url = buildPublicPageUrl_({ publicId: scheduleId });
    try {
      logAudit_(targetPhone, '', 'CREATE_PUBLIC_LINK', 'Schedule', scheduleId, 'สร้าง/เปิดลิงก์สาธารณะ', null, { url: url, expiryDays: days, patch: 'v70.73' });
    } catch (ignoreAudit) {}

    return {
      success: true,
      url: url,
      expiresAt: expiresAt ? formatDateTime_(expiresAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : 'ไม่หมดอายุ',
      patch: 'v70.73'
    };
  } catch (e) {
    try { logError_(ownerPhone || '', 'getSchedulePublicUrlServer.v70.73', e, { scheduleId: scheduleId }); } catch (ignore) {}
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
};

var haosV773PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = typeof getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ === 'function' ? getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ : null;
getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = function() {
  var allowed = haosV773PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ ? haosV773PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
  allowed.getSchedulePublicUrlServer = true;
  allowed.bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV773 = true;
  return allowed;
};

  };

// ==========================================
// v70.74 schedule public link status + faster TextFinder path
// ==========================================
function haosV774FindSchedulePublicRow_(sheet, scheduleId) {
  scheduleId = String(scheduleId || '').trim();
  if (!sheet || !scheduleId || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return null;
  var map = haosV773EnsureSchedulePublicHeaders_(sheet);
  var idCol = map['ID'] || 1;
  var found = sheet.getRange(2, idCol, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1)
    .createTextFinder(scheduleId)
    .matchEntireCell(true)
    .findNext();
  if (!found) return null;
  return { row: found.getRow(), map: map };
}

function haosV774SchedulePublicUrl_(scheduleId) {
  return buildPublicPageUrl_({ publicId: String(scheduleId || '').trim() });
}

getSchedulePublicUrlServer = function(scheduleId, ownerPhone, expiryDays) {
  try {
    scheduleId = String(scheduleId || '').trim();
    if (!scheduleId) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบรหัสรายการที่ต้องการสร้างลิงก์' };

    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME);
    if (!sheet) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบชีตตารางงาน' };

    var found = haosV774FindSchedulePublicRow_(sheet, scheduleId);
    if (!found) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบรายการที่ต้องการสร้างลิงก์' };

    var map = found.map;
    var row = found.row;
    var targetPhone = ownerPhone ? String(ownerPhone).replace(/'/g, '').trim() : '';
    var rowPhone = String(sheet.getRange(row, map['Owner Phone'] || 11).getValue() || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
    if (targetPhone && rowPhone !== targetPhone) {
      return { success: false, message: 'คุณไม่มีสิทธิ์สร้างลิงก์สาธารณะของรายการนี้' };
    }

    var days = Number(expiryDays);
    if (isNaN(days)) days = Number(getSetting_('PUBLIC_LINK_EXPIRY_DAYS', 30));
    if (isNaN(days)) days = 30;

    var expiresAt = '';
    if (days > 0) expiresAt = new Date(new Date().getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    sheet.getRange(row, map['Public Enabled']).setValue('TRUE');
    sheet.getRange(row, map['Public Expires At']).setValue(expiresAt || '');
    sheet.getRange(row, map['Public Created At']).setValue(new Date());

    return {
      success: true,
      url: haosV774SchedulePublicUrl_(scheduleId),
      expiresAt: expiresAt ? formatDateTime_(expiresAt, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : 'ไม่หมดอายุ',
      enabled: true,
      patch: 'v70.74'
    };
  } catch (e) {
    try { logError_(ownerPhone || '', 'getSchedulePublicUrlServer.v70.74', e, { scheduleId: scheduleId }); } catch (ignore) {}
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
};

function getSchedulePublicLinkStatus(scheduleId, ownerPhone) {
  try {
    scheduleId = String(scheduleId || '').trim();
    if (!scheduleId) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบรหัสรายการ' };

    var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME);
    if (!sheet) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบชีตตารางงาน' };

    var found = haosV774FindSchedulePublicRow_(sheet, scheduleId);
    if (!found) return { success: false, message: 'ไม่พบรายการตารางงาน' };

    var map = found.map;
    var row = found.row;
    var targetPhone = ownerPhone ? String(ownerPhone).replace(/'/g, '').trim() : '';
    var rowPhone = String(sheet.getRange(row, map['Owner Phone'] || 11).getValue() || '').replace(/'/g, '').trim();
    if (targetPhone && rowPhone !== targetPhone) {
      return { success: false, message: 'คุณไม่มีสิทธิ์ดูสถานะลิงก์ของรายการนี้' };
    }

    var enabledRaw = sheet.getRange(row, map['Public Enabled']).getValue();
    var enabledText = String(enabledRaw || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    var enabled = enabledText === 'TRUE' || enabledText === 'ON' || enabledText === 'YES' || enabledText === 'PUBLIC';
    var expValue = sheet.getRange(row, map['Public Expires At']).getValue();
    var createdValue = sheet.getRange(row, map['Public Created At']).getValue();
    var expiresAt = '';
    if (expValue) expiresAt = formatDateTime_(expValue, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm');
    var createdAt = createdValue ? formatDateTime_(createdValue, 'dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm') : '';
    var expired = false;
    if (expValue) {
      var expDate = expValue instanceof Date ? expValue : new Date(expValue);
      expired = !isNaN(expDate.getTime()) && new Date() > expDate;
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        publicId: scheduleId,
        url: haosV774SchedulePublicUrl_(scheduleId),
        enabled: enabled && !expired,
        rawEnabled: enabledRaw,
        expired: expired,
        expiresAt: expiresAt || (enabled ? 'ไม่หมดอายุ' : '-'),
        createdAt: createdAt,
        patch: 'v70.74'
      }
    };
  } catch (e) {
    try { logError_(ownerPhone || '', 'getSchedulePublicLinkStatus.v70.74', e, { scheduleId: scheduleId }); } catch (ignore) {}
    return { success: false, message: e.toString() };
  }
}

  var allowed = haosV774PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ ? haosV774PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
  allowed.getSchedulePublicUrlServer = true;
  allowed.getSchedulePublicLinkStatus = true;
  allowed.revokePublicScheduleLink = true;
  allowed.bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV774 = true;
  return allowed;
};

    publicBaseUrl: getPublicAppBaseUrl_()
  };
}

// v70.78: Meeting minutes audio guard.
// Prevent old clients from sending oversized inline audio payloads to Gemini and crashing with Out of Memory.
var haosV778PrevAnalyzeMeetingAudio_ = typeof analyzeMeetingAudio === 'function' ? analyzeMeetingAudio : null;
var HAOS_V778_MEETING_AUDIO_MAX_BASE64_CHARS = 3.1 * 1024 * 1024;

function haosV778MeetingAudioMemoryMessage_() {
  return 'ไฟล์เสียงยาวหรือใหญ่เกินกว่าที่ Apps Script/Gemini จะวิเคราะห์ได้ในครั้งเดียว ระบบหยุดก่อนเพื่อไม่ให้โปรแกรมค้าง กรุณาแบ่งไฟล์เสียงเป็นช่วงสั้นลงประมาณ 5-10 นาที หรือวางข้อความถอดเสียงแล้วกด AI วิเคราะห์ข้อความแทน';
}

function haosV778IsMemoryError_(value) {
  return /out\s*of\s*memory|memory limit|maximum execution memory|exceeded memory/i.test(String(value || ''));
}

analyzeMeetingAudio = function(base64Data, fileName, mimeType) {
  try {
    if (!base64Data) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบไฟล์เสียงสำหรับวิเคราะห์' };
    var cleanBase64 = String(base64Data || '');
    if (cleanBase64.indexOf(',') >= 0) cleanBase64 = cleanBase64.split(',').pop();
    var mt = String(mimeType || '').toLowerCase();
    if (mt.indexOf('audio/') !== 0) return { success:false, message:'รองรับเฉพาะไฟล์เสียงเท่านั้น' };
    if (cleanBase64.length > HAOS_V778_MEETING_AUDIO_MAX_BASE64_CHARS) {
      return {
        success:false,
        message:haosV778MeetingAudioMemoryMessage_(),
        reason:'AUDIO_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',
        fileName:fileName || '',
        base64Chars:cleanBase64.length,
        maxBase64Chars:HAOS_V778_MEETING_AUDIO_MAX_BASE64_CHARS,
        patch:'v70.78'
      };
    }
    if (!haosV778PrevAnalyzeMeetingAudio_) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบฟังก์ชันวิเคราะห์เสียงเดิม' };
    var res = haosV778PrevAnalyzeMeetingAudio_(cleanBase64, fileName, mimeType);
    if (res && res.success === false && haosV778IsMemoryError_(res.message)) {
      res.message = haosV778MeetingAudioMemoryMessage_();
      res.reason = 'AUDIO_AI_OUT_OF_MEMORY';
      res.patch = 'v70.78';
    }
    return res;
  } catch (e) {
    var msg = e && e.toString ? e.toString() : String(e);
    return {
      success:false,
      message:haosV778IsMemoryError_(msg) ? haosV778MeetingAudioMemoryMessage_() : ('AI วิเคราะห์เสียงประชุมไม่สำเร็จ: ' + msg),
      reason:haosV778IsMemoryError_(msg) ? 'AUDIO_AI_OUT_OF_MEMORY' : 'AUDIO_AI_ERROR',
      patch:'v70.78'
    };
  }
};

var haosV778PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = typeof getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ === 'function' ? getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ : null;
getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = function() {
  var allowed = haosV778PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ ? haosV778PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
  allowed.analyzeMeetingAudio = true;
  allowed.bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV778 = true;
  return allowed;
};

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV778() {
  return {
    success:true,
    version:'v70.78-meeting-minutes-audio-safe',
    maxBase64Chars:HAOS_V778_MEETING_AUDIO_MAX_BASE64_CHARS,
    functions:['analyzeMeetingAudio']
  };
}

// v70.81: Meeting minutes text analysis modes.
// Adds Auto / Concise / Detailed output styles while keeping the original response schema.
var haosV781PrevAnalyzeMeetingMinutesText_ = typeof analyzeMeetingMinutesText === 'function' ? analyzeMeetingMinutesText : null;
var haosV781PrevBuildGeminiPayload_ = typeof buildGeminiPayload_ === 'function' ? buildGeminiPayload_ : null;

function haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode) {
  var raw = String(mode || 'auto').toLowerCase();
  if (/detail|long|ยาว|ละเอียด/.test(raw)) return 'detailed';
  if (/concise|short|สั้น|กระชับ/.test(raw)) return 'concise';
  return 'auto';
}

function haosV781MeetingMinutesModeLabel_(mode) {
  var normalized = haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode);
  if (normalized === 'detailed') return 'แบบยาวและละเอียด';
  if (normalized === 'concise') return 'แบบสั้นและกระชับแต่ได้ใจความ';
  return 'รูปแบบอัตโนมัติ';
}

function haosV781MeetingMinutesMarker_(mode) {
  var normalized = haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode);
  if (normalized === 'detailed') return 'HAOS_MEETING_MINUTES_DETAILED';
  if (normalized === 'concise') return 'HAOS_MEETING_MINUTES_CONCISE';
  return 'HAOS_MEETING_MINUTES_AUTO';
}

function haosV781MeetingMinutesStyleInstruction_(mode, text) {
  var normalized = haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode);
  var textLength = String(text || '').length;
  if (normalized === 'concise') {
    return [
      'โหมดสั้นและกระชับ:',
      '- สรุปให้กระชับแต่ต้องได้ใจความครบ ไม่ตัดมติหรือข้อสั่งการสำคัญ',
      '- ช่อง agenda ให้มีประมาณ 5-8 bullet/บรรทัด ครอบคลุมวัตถุประสงค์ ประเด็นหลัก และข้อมูลที่ใช้ตัดสินใจ',
      '- ช่อง conclusion ให้มีประมาณ 4-8 bullet/บรรทัด เน้นมติ ข้อสั่งการ ผู้รับผิดชอบ และงานติดตาม',
      '- หลีกเลี่ยงการเขียนยาวแบบบทความ'
    ].join('\n');
  }
  if (normalized === 'detailed') {
    return [
      'โหมดแบบยาวและละเอียด:',
      '- เขียนให้ใกล้เคียงรายงานประชุมทางการ ไม่ใช่สรุปสั้น',
      '- ช่อง agenda ให้จัดเป็นหัวข้อย่อยมีเลขลำดับ เช่น 1. วัตถุประสงค์การประชุม, 2. ประเด็นสำคัญ, 3. เกณฑ์/แนวทาง, 4. การอภิปรายหรือสถานการณ์จำลอง, 5. บทบาทหน่วยงาน, 6. ปัญหา/ข้อสังเกต, 7. ข้อเสนอแนะ',
      '- แต่ละหัวข้อควรมีคำอธิบาย 2-5 ประโยค หากข้อมูลในข้อความมีเพียงพอ',
      '- ช่อง conclusion ให้แยก มติที่ประชุม, ข้อสั่งการ, ผู้รับผิดชอบ, กำหนดติดตาม, ข้อเสนอแนะ และประเด็นที่ต้องปรับปรุง',
      '- ถ้าข้อความต้นฉบับยาว ให้ดึงรายละเอียดสำคัญให้มากที่สุด อย่าเหลือเพียงรายการคั่นด้วยจุลภาค',
      '- เขียนภาษาไทยสุภาพ อ่านเป็นรายงานได้ทันที'
    ].join('\n');
  }
  return [
    'โหมดอัตโนมัติ:',
    '- ถ้าข้อความสั้นหรือเป็นงานทั่วไป ให้ใช้รูปแบบสั้นและกระชับ',
    '- ถ้าข้อความยาวกว่า 4,000 ตัวอักษร หรือมีหลายวาระ/หลายประเด็น ให้ใช้รูปแบบยาวและละเอียด',
    '- ความยาวข้อความต้นฉบับประมาณ ' + textLength + ' ตัวอักษร',
    '- เป้าหมายคือให้รายงานไม่สั้นเกินไปเมื่อข้อมูลต้นฉบับมีรายละเอียดมาก'
  ].join('\n');
}

function haosV781BuildMeetingMinutesTextPrompt_(text, mode) {
  var normalized = haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode);
  var marker = haosV781MeetingMinutesMarker_(normalized);
  var modeLabel = haosV781MeetingMinutesModeLabel_(normalized);
  var inputLimit = normalized === 'detailed' ? 20500 : 18500;
  var sourceText = String(text || '').substring(0, inputLimit);
  return marker + '\n' +
    'คุณคือเลขานุการการประชุมและนักสรุปรายงานประชุมของหน่วยงานราชการด้านสาธารณสุขไทย\n' +
    'งานของคุณคืออ่านข้อความดิบจากการประชุม/ข้อความถอดเสียง แล้วจัดทำข้อมูลสำหรับรายงานการประชุมในระบบ Health Assistant OS\n' +
    'โหมดที่ผู้ใช้เลือก: ' + modeLabel + '\n\n' +
    haosV781MeetingMinutesStyleInstruction_(normalized, sourceText) + '\n\n' +
    'กติกาสำคัญ:\n' +
    '- ตอบเป็น JSON object เท่านั้น ห้ามมี markdown, code fence, หรือข้อความอื่นปน\n' +
    '- ใช้ข้อมูลจากข้อความเท่านั้น ห้ามแต่งชื่อคน วันที่ สถานที่ หรือมติที่ไม่มีในต้นฉบับ\n' +
    '- ถ้าข้อมูลใดไม่พบ ให้ใส่ \"ไม่ระบุ\" หรือ \"-\" ตามความเหมาะสม\n' +
    '- ถ้าพบวันที่ พ.ศ. ให้แปลงเป็น ค.ศ. ในรูปแบบ YYYY-MM-DD เช่น 12 มิถุนายน 2569 = 2026-06-12 ห้ามแปลงผิดเป็นปีอื่น\n' +
    '- attendees ให้แยกรายชื่อหรือกลุ่มผู้เข้าร่วมคนละบรรทัด ถ้าไม่มีชื่อชัดเจนให้สรุปเป็นกลุ่มผู้เข้าร่วม\n' +
    '- agenda และ conclusion ต้องขึ้นบรรทัดใหม่ให้อ่านง่าย ห้ามรวมทุกประเด็นเป็นประโยคเดียวคั่นด้วยจุลภาคยาว ๆ\n' +
    '- ถ้ามีคำศัพท์เฉพาะ เช่น PHEOC, PM2.5, BCP, Incident Command System ให้คงคำศัพท์ไว้และอธิบายตามบริบทที่พบ\n\n' +
    'schema ที่ต้องส่งกลับ:\n' +
    '{"title":"หัวข้อการประชุม","date":"YYYY-MM-DD ถ้าพบ ไม่พบให้ว่างหรือ -","location":"สถานที่","chairman":"ประธาน","attendees":"รายชื่อ/กลุ่มผู้เข้าร่วม แยกบรรทัด","agenda":"วาระ/สาระสำคัญแบบจัดหัวข้อ","conclusion":"สรุปผล/มติ/ข้อสั่งการ/งานติดตามแบบจัดหัวข้อ"}\n\n' +
    'ข้อความต้นฉบับ:\n' + sourceText;
}

function haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesResult_(data, mode) {
  var out = data || {};
  var keys = ['title', 'date', 'location', 'chairman', 'attendees', 'agenda', 'conclusion'];
  for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
    var key = keys[i];
    if (out[key] == null || out[key] === '') out[key] = key === 'date' ? '' : '-';
    out[key] = String(out[key]);
  }
  out.analysisMode = haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode);
  out.analysisModeLabel = haosV781MeetingMinutesModeLabel_(mode);
  return out;
}

analyzeMeetingMinutesText = function(text, mode) {
  try {
    if (!text) return { success:false, message:'ไม่พบข้อความสำหรับวิเคราะห์' };
    var normalized = haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode);
    var prompt = haosV781BuildMeetingMinutesTextPrompt_(text, normalized);
    var ai = fetchGeminiAI(prompt, null, null);
    if (!ai.success) return { success:false, message:ai.message };
    var parsed = parseGeminiJson_(ai.text);
    return {
      success:true,
      data:haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesResult_(parsed, normalized),
      raw:ai.text,
      modelUsed:ai.model,
      analysisMode:normalized,
      patch:'v70.81'
    };
  } catch(e) {
    if (haosV781PrevAnalyzeMeetingMinutesText_) {
      try {
        var fallback = haosV781PrevAnalyzeMeetingMinutesText_(text);
        if (fallback && fallback.success) {
          fallback.patch = 'v70.81-fallback';
          fallback.analysisMode = haosV781NormalizeMeetingMinutesTextMode_(mode);
          return fallback;
        }
      } catch (inner) {}
    }
    return { success:false, message:'AI วิเคราะห์ข้อความประชุมไม่สำเร็จ: ' + e.toString(), patch:'v70.81' };
  }
};

buildGeminiPayload_ = function(prompt, base64Data, mimeType) {
  var payload = haosV781PrevBuildGeminiPayload_ ? haosV781PrevBuildGeminiPayload_(prompt, base64Data, mimeType) : {
    contents:[{ role:'user', parts:[{ text:String(prompt || '') }] }],
    generationConfig:{ temperature:0.15, topP:0.8, maxOutputTokens:4096 }
  };
  try {
    if (!base64Data && payload && payload.generationConfig) {
      var rawPrompt = String(prompt || '');
      if (rawPrompt.indexOf('HAOS_MEETING_MINUTES_DETAILED') >= 0) {
        payload.generationConfig.maxOutputTokens = 8192;
      } else if (rawPrompt.indexOf('HAOS_MEETING_MINUTES_CONCISE') >= 0) {
        payload.generationConfig.maxOutputTokens = 3072;
      } else if (rawPrompt.indexOf('HAOS_MEETING_MINUTES_AUTO') >= 0) {
        payload.generationConfig.maxOutputTokens = 6144;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return payload;
};

var haosV781PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = typeof getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ === 'function' ? getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ : null;
getAllowedBridgeFunctions_ = function() {
  var allowed = haosV781PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_ ? haosV781PrevGetAllowedBridgeFunctions_() : {};
  allowed.analyzeMeetingMinutesText = true;
  allowed.bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV781 = true;
  return allowed;
};

    success:true,
    version:'v70.81-meeting-minutes-ai-modes',
    functions:['analyzeMeetingMinutesText'],
    modes:['auto', 'concise', 'detailed']
  };
}

function bridgeWhitelistHealthCheckV790() {
  return {

function haosV7114FindAssetById_(assetId) {
  var sh = getITAssetSheetV70_();
  var row = haosV70FindRowById_(sh, assetId, 1);
  if (row <= 1) return null;
  return {row:row, asset:itAssetRowToObjectV70_(sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, 29).getValues()[0])};
}

function haosV7114SanitizeAssetChangeFields_(fields) {
  fields = fields || {};
  var allowed = {
    assetCode:true, assetNumber:true, assetName:true, category:true, brand:true, model:true,
    serialNumber:true, spec:true, purchaseDate:true, fiscalYear:true, budgetSource:true, price:true,
    vendor:true, warrantyStart:true, warrantyEnd:true, ownerDepartment:true, currentUserName:true,
    currentUserPhone:true, location:true, status:true, condition:true, lastCheckedAt:true,
    remark:true, photoUrl:true
  };
  var out = {};
  Object.keys(fields).forEach(function(key) {
    if (!allowed[key]) return;
    var value = fields[key];
    if (key === 'purchaseDate' || key === 'warrantyStart' || key === 'warrantyEnd' || key === 'lastCheckedAt') value = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(value);
    if (key === 'currentUserPhone') value = haosV70Phone_(value);
    out[key] = value == null ? '' : String(value);
  });
  return out;
}

function requestITAssetChangeV7114(payload, auth) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var login = haosV7112PublicAssetLogin_(auth || {});
    if (!login.success) return login;
    var user = login.user || {};
    var assetId = String(payload.assetId || '').trim();
    var found = haosV7114FindAssetById_(assetId);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบทรัพย์สินที่ต้องการขอแก้ไข'};
    var fields = haosV7114SanitizeAssetChangeFields_(payload.fields || {});
    var message = String(payload.message || '').trim();
    if (!Object.keys(fields).length && !message) return {success:false, message:'กรุณาระบุข้อมูลที่ต้องการแก้ไขหรือเหตุผล'};

    var sh = getITAssetChangeRequestSheetV7114_();
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var requestId = haosV70Id_('ACR');
    sh.appendRow([
      requestId, found.asset.assetId || assetId, found.asset.assetCode || '', found.asset.assetName || '',
      "'" + haosV70Phone_(user.phone), user.fullName || '', user.role || '', user.department || '',
      'Update', JSON.stringify(fields), message, 'Pending',
      '', '', '', '', now, now
    ]);
    try {
      createNotificationsV732_(getITServiceStaffPhones_(), 'มีคำขอแก้ไขข้อมูลทรัพย์สิน IT: ' + (found.asset.assetName || found.asset.assetCode || assetId), 'IT Asset Change Request', requestId, 'ปกติ', {module:'itAsset', action:'openITAssetChangeRequests', createdBy:user.phone});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    try {
      logAudit_(user.phone || '', user.fullName || '', 'REQUEST_IT_ASSET_CHANGE', 'ITAssetChangeRequest', requestId, found.asset.assetName || assetId, found.asset, {fields:fields, message:message});
    } catch(ignore2) {}
    return {success:true, message:'ส่งคำขอแก้ไขข้อมูลแล้ว รอ IT ตรวจอนุมัติ', requestId:requestId};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function getITAssetChangeRequestsV7114(auth, status) {
  try {
    var login = haosV7112PublicAssetLogin_(auth || {});
    if (!login.success) return login;
    var user = login.user || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(user)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var wanted = String(status || 'Pending');
    var data = getITAssetChangeRequestSheetV7114_().getDataRange().getValues();
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!data[i][0]) continue;
      var item = haosV7114ChangeRequestRowToObject_(data[i]);
      if (wanted && wanted !== 'All' && item.status !== wanted) continue;
      out.push(item);
    }
    out.sort(function(a,b){ return String(b.createdAt || b.updatedAt).localeCompare(String(a.createdAt || a.updatedAt)); });
    return {success:true, version:'v70.114-itasset-change-request', requests:out.slice(0, 100)};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

function reviewITAssetChangeRequestV7114(payload, auth) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var login = haosV7112PublicAssetLogin_(auth || {});
    if (!login.success) return login;
    var user = login.user || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(user)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var requestId = String(payload.requestId || '').trim();
    var approve = String(payload.decision || '').toLowerCase() === 'approved' || payload.approve === true;
    var sh = getITAssetChangeRequestSheetV7114_();
    var row = haosV70FindRowById_(sh, requestId, 1);
    if (row <= 1) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบคำขอแก้ไขนี้'};
    var req = haosV7114ChangeRequestRowToObject_(sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, 18).getValues()[0]);
    if (req.status !== 'Pending') return {success:false, message:'คำขอนี้ถูกตรวจแล้ว'};
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    if (approve) {
      var found = haosV7114FindAssetById_(req.assetId);
      if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบทรัพย์สินต้นทางของคำขอ'};
      var updated = found.asset || {};
      Object.keys(req.fields || {}).forEach(function(key) { updated[key] = req.fields[key]; });
      var saveRes = saveITAssetV70(updated, user);
      if (!saveRes || !saveRes.success) return saveRes || {success:false, message:'บันทึกข้อมูลทรัพย์สินไม่สำเร็จ'};
    }
    var newStatus = approve ? 'Approved' : 'Rejected';
    sh.getRange(row, 12, 1, 7).setValues([[newStatus, "'" + haosV70Phone_(user.phone), user.fullName || user.phone || '', now, payload.note || '', req.createdAt || now, now]]);
    try {
      createNotificationV732_(req.requestedByPhone, 'คำขอแก้ไขข้อมูลทรัพย์สิน IT "' + (req.assetName || req.assetCode || req.assetId) + '" ' + (approve ? 'ได้รับอนุมัติแล้ว' : 'ไม่ผ่านการอนุมัติ') + (payload.note ? ': ' + payload.note : ''), 'IT Asset Change Result', requestId, approve ? 'ปกติ' : 'ด่วน', {module:'itAsset', action:'openITAsset', createdBy:user.phone});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    try {
      logAudit_(user.phone || '', user.fullName || '', approve ? 'APPROVE_IT_ASSET_CHANGE' : 'REJECT_IT_ASSET_CHANGE', 'ITAssetChangeRequest', requestId, req.assetName || req.assetId, req, {note:payload.note || ''});
    } catch(ignore2) {}
    return {success:true, message:approve ? 'อนุมัติและอัปเดตข้อมูลทรัพย์สินแล้ว' : 'ไม่อนุมัติคำขอแล้ว'};
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

// ========================= v70.115: Public IT Asset manager edit =========================
function haosV7115NormalizeAssetFieldsForSave_(fields, baseAsset) {
  fields = haosV7114SanitizeAssetChangeFields_(fields || {});
  baseAsset = baseAsset || {};
  if (fields.purchaseDate) {
    fields.purchaseDate = haosV7115CorrectAssetDateByFiscal_(haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(fields.purchaseDate), fields.fiscalYear || baseAsset.fiscalYear, haosV7116AssetDateHint_({
      assetCode:fields.assetCode || baseAsset.assetCode,
      assetNumber:fields.assetNumber || baseAsset.assetNumber,
      assetName:fields.assetName || baseAsset.assetName,
      category:fields.category || baseAsset.category,
      smartCategory:fields.smartCategory || baseAsset.smartCategory,
      spec:fields.spec || baseAsset.spec,
      fiscalYear:fields.fiscalYear || baseAsset.fiscalYear
    }));
  }
  if (fields.warrantyStart) fields.warrantyStart = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(fields.warrantyStart);
  if (fields.warrantyEnd) fields.warrantyEnd = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(fields.warrantyEnd);
  if (fields.lastCheckedAt) fields.lastCheckedAt = haosV7111NormalizeImportDate_(fields.lastCheckedAt);
  if (fields.condition !== undefined) fields.condition = haosV7124DisplayCondition_(fields.condition, fields.status || baseAsset.status || '') || 'ดี';
  if (fields.remark !== undefined) fields.remark = haosV7124CleanRemark_(fields.remark);
  return fields;
}

function savePublicITAssetV7115(payload, auth) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    var login = haosV7112PublicAssetLogin_(auth || {});
    if (!login.success) return login;
    var user = login.user || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(user)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var assetId = String(payload.assetId || '').trim();
    var found = haosV7114FindAssetById_(assetId);
    if (!found) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายการทรัพย์สินที่ต้องการแก้ไข'};
    var before = found.asset || {};
    var fields = haosV7115NormalizeAssetFieldsForSave_(payload.fields || {}, before);
    if (!Object.keys(fields).length) return {success:false, message:'ไม่มีข้อมูลที่ต้องแก้ไข'};
    var updated = {};
    Object.keys(before).forEach(function(key){ updated[key] = before[key]; });
    Object.keys(fields).forEach(function(key){ updated[key] = fields[key]; });
    updated.assetId = before.assetId || assetId;
    updated.createdAt = before.createdAt || updated.createdAt || '';
    updated.createdBy = before.createdBy || updated.createdBy || '';
    var saveRes = saveITAssetV70(updated, {
      phone:user.phone || '',
      name:user.fullName || user.name || user.phone || '',
      role:user.role || '',
      department:user.department || ''
    });
    if (!saveRes || !saveRes.success) return saveRes || {success:false, message:'บันทึกข้อมูลทรัพย์สินไม่สำเร็จ'};
    var refreshed = haosV7114FindAssetById_(assetId);
    try {
      logAudit_(user.phone || '', user.fullName || '', 'PUBLIC_MANAGER_UPDATE_IT_ASSET', 'ITAsset', assetId, before.assetName || assetId, before, {fields:fields});
    } catch(ignore) {}
    return {
      success:true,
      version:'v70.124-itasset-master-data-normalize',
      message:'บันทึกข้อมูลทรัพย์สินเรียบร้อย',
      asset:refreshed ? refreshed.asset : updated
    };
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

  var out = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {

function haosV7123FindOverrideRows_() {
  var sh = getITAssetCategoryOverrideSheetV7123_();
  var data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  var byAssetId = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var assetId = String(data[i][1] || '').trim();
    if (assetId) byAssetId[assetId] = i + 1;
  }
  return {sheet:sh, byAssetId:byAssetId};
}

function haosV7123BuildOverrideMap_() {
  var out = {};
  getAllITAssetCategoryOverridesV7123_().forEach(function(item) {
    if (item && item.assetId) out[String(item.assetId)] = item;
  });
  return out;
}

function haosV7123ApplyCategoryOverrides_(assets) {
  assets = Array.isArray(assets) ? assets : [];
  var overrides = haosV7123BuildOverrideMap_();
  return assets.map(function(asset) {
    asset = asset || {};
    var autoCategory = haosV7113SmartAssetCategory_(asset);
    var ov = overrides[String(asset.assetId || '')] || null;
    asset.autoSmartCategory = autoCategory;
    asset.manualCategory = '';
    asset.categoryOverrideNote = '';
    asset.categoryOverrideUpdatedAt = '';
    asset.categoryOverrideUpdatedBy = '';
    asset.categoryOverridden = false;
    if (ov && ov.isActive && ov.manualCategory) {
      asset.manualCategory = ov.manualCategory;
      asset.categoryOverrideNote = ov.note || '';
      asset.categoryOverrideUpdatedAt = ov.updatedAt || '';
      asset.categoryOverrideUpdatedBy = ov.updatedByName || ov.updatedByPhone || '';
      asset.categoryOverridden = true;
      asset.smartCategory = ov.manualCategory;
      asset.finalCategory = ov.manualCategory;
    } else {
      asset.smartCategory = asset.smartCategory || autoCategory;
      asset.finalCategory = asset.smartCategory || autoCategory;
    }
    return asset;
  });
}

function haosV7123CategoryOptions_(assets) {
  var defaults = [
    'คอมพิวเตอร์ตั้งโต๊ะ / PC','Notebook','เครื่องพิมพ์ / Printer','Scanner','จอภาพ / Monitor',
    'อุปกรณ์เครือข่าย','Server / Storage','UPS / ไฟฟ้าสำรอง','Projector','Software / License',
    'อุปกรณ์ต่อพ่วง / Accessory','อื่นๆ / ต้องจัดหมวดเพิ่ม'
  ];
  var seen = {};
  var out = [];
  function add(v) {
    v = String(v || '').trim();
    if (v && !seen[v]) {
      seen[v] = true;
      out.push(v);
    }
  }
  defaults.forEach(add);
  (assets || []).forEach(function(a) {
    add(a.smartCategory);
    add(a.autoSmartCategory);
    add(a.manualCategory);
    add(a.category);
  });
  return out;
}

getITAssetModuleDataV70 = function(phone, role, department) {

  try {
    res.assets = haosV7123ApplyCategoryOverrides_(res.assets || []);
    var licenses = res.licenses || [];

    res.executiveSummary = haosV7113BuildExecutiveSummary_(res.assets, licenses, alerts, tickets);
    res.filters = res.filters || {};
    res.filters.smartCategories = haosV7112Unique_(res.assets, 'smartCategory');
    res.filters.categories = haosV7112Unique_(res.assets, 'category');
    res.filters.statuses = haosV7112Unique_(res.assets, 'status');
    res.filters.departments = haosV7112Unique_(res.assets, 'ownerDepartment');
    res.filters.locations = haosV7112Unique_(res.assets, 'location');

  return res;
};

function saveITAssetCategoryOverrideV7123(payload, actor) {
  try {
    payload = payload || {};
    actor = actor || {};
    if (!haosV711ActorCanManageIT_(actor)) return haosV711ITNoManageResponse_();
    var ids = Array.isArray(payload.assetIds) ? payload.assetIds : [payload.assetId];
    ids = ids.map(function(id){ return String(id || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
    if (!ids.length) return {success:false, message:'ไม่พบรายการทรัพย์สินที่ต้องการแก้หมวดย่อย'};
    var manualCategory = String(payload.manualCategory || payload.smartCategory || '').trim();
    var note = String(payload.note || '').trim();
    var clearOverride = !!payload.clearOverride || !manualCategory;
    var foundRows = haosV7123FindOverrideRows_();
    var sh = foundRows.sheet;
    var now = haosV70Now_();
    var assetsById = {};
    getAllITAssetsV70_().forEach(function(a){ if (a && a.assetId) assetsById[String(a.assetId)] = a; });
    var updated = 0;
    var skipped = 0;
    var results = [];
    ids.forEach(function(assetId) {
      var asset = assetsById[assetId];
      if (!asset) {
        skipped++;
        results.push({assetId:assetId, success:false, message:'not_found'});
        return;
      }
      var autoCategory = haosV7113SmartAssetCategory_(asset);
      var rowNo = foundRows.byAssetId[assetId] || 0;
      var old = rowNo > 1 ? itAssetCategoryOverrideRowToObjectV7123_(sh.getRange(rowNo, 1, 1, 10).getValues()[0]) : {};
      var overrideId = old.overrideId || haosV70Id_('ITCAT');
      var row = [
        overrideId,
        assetId,
        asset.assetCode || '',
        autoCategory,
        clearOverride ? '' : manualCategory,
        note,
        clearOverride ? 'FALSE' : 'TRUE',
        now,
        "'" + haosV70Phone_(actor.phone || ''),
        actor.name || actor.fullName || actor.phone || ''
      ];
      if (rowNo > 1) sh.getRange(rowNo, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
      else {
        sh.appendRow(row);
        foundRows.byAssetId[assetId] = sh.getLastRow();
      }
      updated++;
      results.push({assetId:assetId, success:true, manualCategory:clearOverride ? '' : manualCategory});
      try {
        logAudit_(actor.phone || '', actor.name || actor.fullName || '', clearOverride ? 'CLEAR_IT_ASSET_CATEGORY_OVERRIDE' : 'SET_IT_ASSET_CATEGORY_OVERRIDE', 'ITAsset', assetId, asset.assetName || asset.assetCode || assetId, old, {autoCategory:autoCategory, manualCategory:clearOverride ? '' : manualCategory, note:note});
      } catch(ignore) {}
    });
    return {
      success:true,
      version:'v70.124-itasset-master-data-normalize',
      updated:updated,
      skipped:skipped,
      cleared:clearOverride,
      manualCategory:clearOverride ? '' : manualCategory,
      results:results
    };
  } catch(e) {
    return {success:false, message:e.toString()};
  }
}

/* v70.126: Workspace AI Designer Phase 1
 * Adds a normalized fieldConfig draft for the shared workspace AI helper.
 * Rollback: remove this block and assets/js/modules/shared-workspace-ai-designer.js.
 */
function haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceAiType_(value, fallback) {
  var raw = String(value || fallback || 'table').trim().toLowerCase();
  var map = {
    online_form: 'form',
    light_table: 'table',
    sheet: 'google_sheet',
    form_google: 'google_form',
    googleform: 'google_form',
    googlesheet: 'google_sheet',
    test: 'quiz',
    exam: 'quiz'
  };
  var normalized = map[raw] || raw;
  var valid = {table:true, checklist:true, form:true, quiz:true, google_sheet:true, google_form:true};
  return valid[normalized] ? normalized : (fallback || 'table');
}

function haosV7126Bool_(value, fallback) {
  if (value === true || value === false) return value;
  if (value === null || typeof value === 'undefined' || value === '') return !!fallback;
  var text = String(value).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (/^(true|yes|y|1|on|required|require|required field)$/i.test(text)) return true;
  if (/^(false|no|n|0|off|optional)$/i.test(text)) return false;
  return !!fallback;
}

function haosV7126SplitOptions_(value) {
  var arr = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value || '').split(/[,|]/);
  var seen = {};
  var out = [];
  arr.forEach(function(item) {
    var text = String(item || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    var key = text.toLowerCase();
    if (!text || seen[key]) return;
    seen[key] = true;
    out.push(text);
  });
  return out;
}

function haosV7126InferWorkspaceFieldType_(label, options, requested) {
  var raw = String(requested || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  var mapped = {
    select: 'dropdown',
    dropdownlist: 'dropdown',
    choice: 'radio',
    single_choice: 'radio',
    single: 'radio',
    multiple_choice: 'checkbox',
    multi_choice: 'checkbox',
    multiple: 'checkbox',
    checklist: 'checkbox',
    longtext: 'textarea',
    paragraph: 'textarea',
    photo: 'image',
    picture: 'image',
    file: 'image',
    upload: 'image'
  }[raw] || raw;
  var valid = {text:true, textarea:true, dropdown:true, radio:true, checkbox:true, image:true};
  if (valid[mapped]) return mapped;
  var lower = String(label || '').toLowerCase();
  if (/photo|image|picture|file|upload|attachment/.test(lower)) return 'image';
  if (/detail|description|note|comment|remark|feedback|opinion/.test(lower)) return 'textarea';
  if (options && options.length) return /multiple|many|checkbox/.test(lower) ? 'checkbox' : 'dropdown';
  return 'text';
}

function haosV7126ParseWorkspaceFieldSpec_(value) {
  var text = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!text) return {};
  var parts = text.split('|').map(function(part) { return String(part || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
  var label = parts[0] || text;
  var type = '';
  var options = [];
  var required = false;
  for (var i = 1; i < parts.length; i++) {
    var part = parts[i];
    if (/^(text|textarea|dropdown|radio|checkbox|image|select|choice|photo|file)$/i.test(part)) {
      type = part;
    } else if (/required|must|yes|true|1/i.test(part)) {
      required = true;
    } else if (part.indexOf(',') >= 0) {
      options = haosV7126SplitOptions_(part);
    }
  }
  var match = label.match(/^(.*?)\s*\((.*?)\)\s*$/);
  if (match) {
    label = String(match[1] || '').trim();
    if (!options.length) options = haosV7126SplitOptions_(match[2]);
  }
  return {label: label, type: type, options: options, required: required};
}

function haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceField_(field, index, workspaceType) {
  var source = {};
  if (typeof field === 'string') source = haosV7126ParseWorkspaceFieldSpec_(field);
  else if (field && typeof field === 'object') source = field;
  var label = String(source.label || source.name || source.title || source.question || source.header || source.column || ('Field ' + (index + 1))).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  var options = haosV7126SplitOptions_(source.options || source.choices || source.choiceList || source.values);
  var type = haosV7126InferWorkspaceFieldType_(label, options, source.type || source.fieldType || source.inputType);
  var required = haosV7126Bool_(source.required, workspaceType === 'form' || workspaceType === 'quiz');
  var notQuiz = workspaceType === 'quiz' ? haosV7126Bool_(source.notQuiz || source.skipQuiz || source.excludeFromScore, false) : true;
  var correct = source.correctAnswer || source.answer || source.correct || source.key || '';
  if (Array.isArray(correct)) correct = correct.join(', ');
  var points = Number(source.points || source.score || source.weight || 1);
  if (!isFinite(points) || points <= 0) points = 1;
  if (!options.length && (type === 'dropdown' || type === 'radio' || type === 'checkbox')) options = ['Option 1', 'Option 2'];
  if (workspaceType === 'quiz' && !notQuiz && !correct && options.length) correct = options[0];
  return {
    label: label,
    type: type,
    required: required,
    options: options,
    notQuiz: !!notQuiz,
    correctAnswer: correct,
    points: points,
    description: String(source.description || source.helpText || source.hint || '').trim(),
    section: String(source.section || source.group || '').trim(),
    logic: source.logic || source.branching || null
  };
}

function haosV7126WorkspaceFieldSpec_(field) {
  var spec = field.label || '';
  if (field.type && field.type !== 'text') spec += ' | ' + field.type;
  if (field.options && field.options.length) spec += ' | ' + field.options.join(', ');
  if (field.required) spec += ' | required';
  return spec;
}

function haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceAiDraft_(data, requestedType) {
  data = data || {};
  var workspaceType = haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceAiType_(data.workspaceType || data.type || requestedType, requestedType || 'table');
  var fields = [];
  if (Array.isArray(data.fieldConfig)) fields = data.fieldConfig;
  else if (Array.isArray(data.fields)) fields = data.fields;
  else if (Array.isArray(data.questions)) fields = data.questions;
  else if (Array.isArray(data.columns)) fields = data.columns;
  fields = fields.map(function(field, index) {
    return haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceField_(field, index, workspaceType);
  }).filter(function(field) { return !!field.label; });
  if (!fields.length) {
    fields = [
      haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceField_({label:'Name', type:'text', required:true}, 0, workspaceType),
      haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceField_({label:'Details', type:'textarea', required:false}, 1, workspaceType)
    ];
  }
  var columns = fields.map(function(field) { return haosV7126WorkspaceFieldSpec_(field); });
  var sampleRows = Array.isArray(data.sampleRows) ? data.sampleRows : [];
  if (!sampleRows.length && Array.isArray(data.rows)) sampleRows = data.rows;
  if (!sampleRows.length) {
    sampleRows = [fields.map(function(field) {
      if (field.type === 'checkbox') return field.options && field.options.length ? field.options[0] : 'Yes';
      if ((field.type === 'dropdown' || field.type === 'radio') && field.options && field.options.length) return field.options[0];
      if (field.type === 'image') return '';
      return '';
    })];
  }
  return {
    title: String(data.title || data.name || 'AI Workspace Draft').trim(),
    description: String(data.description || data.summary || '').trim(),
    workspaceType: workspaceType,
    columns: columns,
    fieldConfig: fields,
    sampleRows: sampleRows,
    logicTips: data.logicTips || data.logic || data.branching || '',
    rawAi: data
  };
}

function haosV7126BuildWorkspaceDesignerPrompt_(workspaceType, sourceKind) {
  return [
    'You are a form and collaborative workspace designer for a Thai public-health office system.',
    'Return JSON only. Do not use markdown. Use Thai text for user-facing titles, descriptions, field labels, options, and hints.',
    'Target workspaceType: ' + workspaceType + '. Source kind: ' + sourceKind + '.',
    'Design a practical workspace that can be edited by the user before saving.',
    'Supported workspaceType values: table, checklist, form, quiz, google_sheet, google_form.',
    'Supported field types: text, textarea, dropdown, radio, checkbox, image.',
    'Use dropdown/radio/checkbox when choices are useful. Checkbox means the respondent can select multiple choices.',
    'For quiz workspace, only scoring questions need correctAnswer and points. Non-scoring profile fields must set notQuiz=true.',
    'If branching logic is useful, put a short explanation in logicTips and optional per-field logic object. Do not require database changes.',
    'JSON schema:',
    '{"title":"","description":"","workspaceType":"' + workspaceType + '","fieldConfig":[{"label":"","type":"text|textarea|dropdown|radio|checkbox|image","required":true,"options":[],"notQuiz":true,"correctAnswer":"","points":1,"description":"","section":"","logic":null}],"sampleRows":[[]],"logicTips":""}'
  ].join('\n');
}

function analyzeWorkspaceText(text, workspaceType) {
  try {
    var cleanText = String(text || '').trim();
    if (!cleanText) return { success:false, message:'No text to analyze.' };
    var type = haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceAiType_(workspaceType || 'table', 'table');
    var prompt = haosV7126BuildWorkspaceDesignerPrompt_(type, 'text') + '\n\nInput:\n' + cleanText;
    var ai = fetchGeminiAI(prompt, null, null, { jsonMode:true, taskType:'text' });
    if (!ai.success) return ai;
    var parsed = parseGeminiJson_(ai.text);
    var draft = haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceAiDraft_(parsed, type);
    return { success:true, data:draft, raw:ai.text, modelUsed:ai.model, version:'v70.126-workspace-ai-designer-phase1' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success:false, message:'Workspace AI designer failed: ' + e.toString(), version:'v70.126-workspace-ai-designer-phase1' };
  }
}

function processWorkspaceDocument(base64Data, fileName, mimeType, workspaceType) {
  try {
    var type = haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceAiType_(workspaceType || 'table', 'table');
    var prompt = haosV7126BuildWorkspaceDesignerPrompt_(type, 'file') +
      '\n\nRead the attached file/image and design the workspace from it. File name: ' + String(fileName || '');
    var ai = fetchGeminiAI(prompt, base64Data, mimeType, { jsonMode:true, taskType:'file' });
    if (!ai.success) return ai;
    var parsed = parseGeminiJson_(ai.text);
    var draft = haosV7126NormalizeWorkspaceAiDraft_(parsed, type);
    return { success:true, data:draft, raw:ai.text, modelUsed:ai.model, version:'v70.126-workspace-ai-designer-phase1' };
  } catch(e) {
    return { success:false, message:'Workspace AI file designer failed: ' + e.toString(), version:'v70.126-workspace-ai-designer-phase1' };
  }
}

function haosV7126WorkspaceAiDesignerPatchStatus() {
  return {
    success:true,
    version:'v70.126-workspace-ai-designer-phase1',
    functions:['analyzeWorkspaceText', 'processWorkspaceDocument']
  };
}

function haosDB7132Id_(prefix){ return prefix + '-' + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g,'').substring(0,16); }
function haosDB7132Phone_(value){ return String(value || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g,''); }
function haosDB7132Json_(value, fallback){ try { return JSON.parse(value || ''); } catch(e) { return fallback; } }
function haosDB7132Text_(value, max){ return String(value == null ? '' : value).replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F]/g,'').trim().substring(0,max || 2000); }
function haosDB7132Bool_(value){ return value === true || String(value).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'; }

function haosDB7132ActorByPhone_(phone) {
  phone = haosDB7132Phone_(phone);
  var sheet = getDB().getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS);
  if (!sheet || !phone) return null;
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i=1;i<data.length;i++) {
    if (haosDB7132Phone_(data[i][0]) !== phone) continue;
    var status = String(data[i][10] || data[i][6] || '').toLowerCase();
    if (/inactive|disabled|rejected|ปิด/.test(status)) return null;
    return {phone:phone,name:haosDB7132Text_(data[i][2],160),position:haosDB7132Text_(data[i][3],160),department:haosDB7132Text_(data[i][4],220),email:haosDB7132Text_(data[i][5],220),role:haosDB7132Text_(data[i][7],80)};
  }
  return null;
}

function haosDB7132IsAdmin_(actor){ return !!actor && /super\s*admin|admin/i.test(String(actor.role || '')); }
function haosDB7132B64_(bytes){ return Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(bytes).replace(/=+$/,''); }
function haosDB7132SessionSecret_(){ var props=PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();var secret=props.getProperty('DASHBOARD_BUILDER_SESSION_SECRET');if(!secret){secret=Utilities.getUuid()+Utilities.getUuid()+getVercelApiSecret_();props.setProperty('DASHBOARD_BUILDER_SESSION_SECRET',secret);}return secret; }
function haosDB7132Sign_(body){ return haosDB7132B64_(Utilities.computeHmacSha256Signature(body,haosDB7132SessionSecret_())); }

function createDashboardBuilderSessionV7132(phone) {
  try {
    var actor = haosDB7132ActorByPhone_(phone);
    if (!actor) return {success:false,message:'ไม่พบบัญชีผู้ใช้งานที่เปิดใช้งาน'};
    var payload = {phone:actor.phone,iat:new Date().getTime(),exp:new Date().getTime() + 12*60*60*1000,nonce:Utilities.getUuid().substring(0,12)};
    var body = haosDB7132B64_(Utilities.newBlob(JSON.stringify(payload)).getBytes());
    return {success:true,session:body+'.'+haosDB7132Sign_(body),expiresAt:payload.exp,actor:actor,version:'v70.132-dashboard-builder-mvp'};
  } catch(e) { return {success:false,message:e.toString()}; }
}

function haosDB7132Session_(token) {
  var parts=String(token||'').split('.');
  if(parts.length!==2 || parts[1]!==haosDB7132Sign_(parts[0])) throw new Error('Dashboard session ไม่ถูกต้อง');
  var payload=JSON.parse(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64DecodeWebSafe(parts[0])).getDataAsString('UTF-8'));
  if(!payload.exp || new Date().getTime()>Number(payload.exp)) throw new Error('Dashboard session หมดอายุ กรุณาเปิดจากระบบหลักอีกครั้ง');
  var actor=haosDB7132ActorByPhone_(payload.phone);
  if(!actor) throw new Error('บัญชีถูกปิดหรือไม่พบผู้ใช้งาน');
  return actor;
}

function haosDB7132List_(actor) {

}

function finalizeDashboardDatasetV7132(token,datasetId,rowCount,schema,source) {
  try {

function haosDB7134PublicSecret_(){
  var props=PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var secret=props.getProperty('DASHBOARD_PUBLIC_LINK_SECRET');
  if(!secret){secret=Utilities.getUuid()+Utilities.getUuid()+getVercelApiSecret_();props.setProperty('DASHBOARD_PUBLIC_LINK_SECRET',secret);}
  return secret;
}
function haosDB7134Hash_(value){return haosDB7132B64_(Utilities.computeHmacSha256Signature(String(value||''),haosDB7134PublicSecret_()));}
function haosDB7134Token_(linkId,version){
  var body=haosDB7132B64_(Utilities.newBlob(JSON.stringify({lid:String(linkId),ver:Number(version||1)})).getBytes());
  return body+'.'+haosDB7134Hash_(body);
}
function haosDB7134TokenPayload_(token){
  var parts=String(token||'').split('.');
  if(parts.length!==2||parts[1]!==haosDB7134Hash_(parts[0]))throw new Error('ลิงก์ Dashboard ไม่ถูกต้อง');
  var payload=JSON.parse(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64DecodeWebSafe(parts[0])).getDataAsString('UTF-8'));
  if(!payload.lid||!payload.ver)throw new Error('ลิงก์ Dashboard ไม่สมบูรณ์');
  return payload;
}
function haosDB7134Iso_(value){if(!value)return '';var date=value instanceof Date?value:new Date(value);return isNaN(date.getTime())?'':date.toISOString();}

}

}

function haosDB7134Resolve_(token){
  var payload=haosDB7134TokenPayload_(token);var found=haosDB7134FindByLink_(payload.lid);
  if(!found||!found.link.active||Number(payload.ver)!==found.link.version)throw new Error('ลิงก์นี้ถูกปิดหรือถูกสร้างใหม่แล้ว');
  if(found.link.expiresAt&&new Date(found.link.expiresAt).getTime()<=new Date().getTime())throw new Error('ลิงก์ Dashboard หมดอายุแล้ว');
  var projectFound=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Projects_(),found.link.dashboardId);if(!projectFound)throw new Error('ไม่พบ Dashboard');
  var owner={phone:haosDB7132Phone_(projectFound.values[3]),name:String(projectFound.values[4]||''),department:String(projectFound.values[5]||''),role:'Owner'};var project=haosDB7132Project_(projectFound.values,owner);
  if(!project.active||project.status!=='active')throw new Error('Dashboard นี้ยังไม่เปิดใช้งาน');
  return {found:found,project:project,dataset:haosDB7134Dataset_(project)};
}
function haosDB7134PublicConfig_(config,visibleColumns){
  config=config||{};var allowed={};visibleColumns.forEach(function(name){allowed[name]=true;});var widgets=[];
  (config.widgets||[]).forEach(function(source){
    var widget=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(source||{}));if(!widget.type)return;
    if(widget.type==='table'){widget.fields=(widget.fields||visibleColumns).filter(function(name){return allowed[name];});if(!widget.fields.length)widget.fields=visibleColumns.slice(0,12);}
    if(widget.type==='kpi'&&widget.metric&&!allowed[widget.metric]){widget.metric='';widget.aggregation='count';}
    if(widget.type!=='table'&&widget.type!=='kpi'){if(widget.dimension&&!allowed[widget.dimension])return;if(widget.metric&&!allowed[widget.metric])return;}
    widgets.push(widget);
  });
  var filters=(config.filters||[]).filter(function(filter){return filter&&allowed[filter.field];}).map(function(filter){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(filter));});
  return {version:config.version||1,layoutVersion:Number(config.layoutVersion||1),widgets:widgets,filters:filters,theme:haosDB7132Text_(config.theme||'haos',30),density:config.density==='compact'?'compact':'comfortable',interaction:{crossFilter:!(config.interaction&&config.interaction.crossFilter===false),drilldown:false}};
}

    var schema=(resolved.dataset.schema||[]).filter(function(column){return visible.indexOf(String(column.name))>=0;});var rows=haosDB7132Rows_(resolved.project.datasetId).map(function(row){var clean={};visible.forEach(function(name){clean[name]=row[name];});return clean;});var project={id:resolved.project.id,title:resolved.project.title,description:resolved.project.description,department:resolved.project.department,tags:resolved.project.tags,status:resolved.project.status,config:haosDB7134PublicConfig_(resolved.project.config,visible)};
    resolved.found.sheet.getRange(resolved.found.row,12,1,2).setValues([[new Date(),link.accessCount+1]]);haosDB7132AuditWrite_(project.id,resolved.project.datasetId,'PUBLIC_VIEW',{phone:'PUBLIC',name:'ผู้ชมลิงก์สาธารณะ'},{columnCount:visible.length,rowCount:rows.length});
    return {success:true,project:project,schema:schema,rows:rows,public:{expiresAt:link.expiresAt,allowExport:link.allowExport},version:'v70.134-dashboard-public-sharing'};
  }catch(e){return {success:false,message:e.toString()};}
}

/* Dashboard Builder Google Sheet Sync v70.139
 * Phase 6.10 manual source sync and Phase 6.11 scheduled sync.
 * Every refresh stages a new dataset and switches the project pointer only after validation.
 * Rollback: see docs/ROLLBACK_DASHBOARD_BUILDER_GOOGLE_SYNC.md.
 */
var HAOS_DB7139_SYNC_HEADERS = ['Dashboard ID','Enabled','Interval Minutes','Last Sync At','Next Sync At','Last Status','Last Message','Last Row Count','Last Dataset ID','Notify On Failure','Updated By','Updated At','Consecutive Failures'];
var HAOS_DB7139_LOG_HEADERS = ['Sync ID','Dashboard ID','Mode','Status','Previous Dataset ID','New Dataset ID','Row Count','Duration Ms','Schema Changed','Message','Actor Phone','Actor Name','Created At'];
var HAOS_DB7139_INTERVALS = [15,30,60,360,1440];

function haosDB7139SettingsSheet_(){return getOrCreateSheet_('DashboardSyncSettings',HAOS_DB7139_SYNC_HEADERS,'#dbeafe');}
function haosDB7139LogSheet_(){return getOrCreateSheet_('DashboardSyncLog',HAOS_DB7139_LOG_HEADERS,'#e0f2fe');}
function haosDB7139Iso_(value){if(!value)return '';var date=value instanceof Date?value:new Date(value);return isNaN(date.getTime())?'':date.toISOString();}
function haosDB7139Interval_(value){value=Number(value||60);return HAOS_DB7139_INTERVALS.indexOf(value)>=0?value:60;}
function haosDB7139SettingFromRow_(row){return {dashboardId:String(row[0]||''),enabled:haosDB7132Bool_(row[1]),intervalMinutes:haosDB7139Interval_(row[2]),lastSyncAt:haosDB7139Iso_(row[3]),nextSyncAt:haosDB7139Iso_(row[4]),lastStatus:String(row[5]||''),lastMessage:String(row[6]||''),lastRowCount:Number(row[7]||0),lastDatasetId:String(row[8]||''),notifyOnFailure:row[9]===''?true:haosDB7132Bool_(row[9]),updatedBy:String(row[10]||''),updatedAt:haosDB7139Iso_(row[11]),consecutiveFailures:Number(row[12]||0)};}
function haosDB7139DefaultSetting_(dashboardId){return {dashboardId:String(dashboardId||''),enabled:false,intervalMinutes:60,lastSyncAt:'',nextSyncAt:'',lastStatus:'',lastMessage:'',lastRowCount:0,lastDatasetId:'',notifyOnFailure:true,updatedBy:'',updatedAt:'',consecutiveFailures:0};}
function haosDB7139FindSetting_(dashboardId){var sheet=haosDB7139SettingsSheet_();var found=haosDB7132Find_(sheet,dashboardId);return {sheet:sheet,found:found,setting:found?haosDB7139SettingFromRow_(found.values):haosDB7139DefaultSetting_(dashboardId)};}
function haosDB7139WriteSetting_(setting){var context=haosDB7139FindSetting_(setting.dashboardId);var row=[setting.dashboardId,!!setting.enabled,haosDB7139Interval_(setting.intervalMinutes),setting.lastSyncAt||'',setting.nextSyncAt||'',setting.lastStatus||'',haosDB7132Text_(setting.lastMessage,1000),Number(setting.lastRowCount||0),setting.lastDatasetId||'',setting.notifyOnFailure!==false,setting.updatedBy||'',setting.updatedAt||new Date(),Number(setting.consecutiveFailures||0)];if(context.found)context.sheet.getRange(context.found.row,1,1,row.length).setValues([row]);else context.sheet.appendRow(row);return haosDB7139SettingFromRow_(row);}
function haosDB7139Context_(token,dashboardId){var actor=haosDB7132Session_(token);var found=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Projects_(),dashboardId);if(!found)throw new Error('ไม่พบ Dashboard');var project=haosDB7132Project_(found.values,actor);haosDB7132CanEdit_(project);var datasetFound=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Datasets_(),project.datasetId);if(!datasetFound)throw new Error('ไม่พบ Dataset ปัจจุบัน');return {actor:actor,project:project,projectFound:found,dataset:haosDB7132Dataset_(datasetFound.values),datasetFound:datasetFound};}
function haosDB7139GoogleSupported_(dataset){return !!dataset&&String(dataset.type||'').toLowerCase()==='google'&&/\/spreadsheets\/d\/[a-zA-Z0-9-_]+/.test(String(dataset.url||''));}

function haosDB7139ReadGoogle_(dataset){
  var match=String(dataset.url||'').match(/\/spreadsheets\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9-_]+)/);if(!match)throw new Error('URL Google Sheets ต้นทางไม่ถูกต้อง');
  var book=SpreadsheetApp.openById(match[1]);var sheet=dataset.sheetName?book.getSheetByName(dataset.sheetName):book.getSheets()[0];if(!sheet)throw new Error('ไม่พบชีตต้นทางชื่อ '+String(dataset.sheetName||''));
  var values=sheet.getDataRange().getDisplayValues();var start=Math.max(0,Number(dataset.headerRow||1)-1);var header=values[start]||[];if(!header.length)throw new Error('ไม่พบแถวหัวตารางใน Google Sheet');
  var used={};header=header.map(function(value,index){var base=haosDB7132Text_(value,160)||('คอลัมน์ '+(index+1));used[base]=(used[base]||0)+1;return used[base]===1?base:base+' ('+used[base]+')';});
  var rows=[];for(var r=start+1;r<values.length&&rows.length<20000;r++){var item={};var has=false;for(var c=0;c<header.length;c++){var value=haosDB7132Text_(values[r][c],5000);if(value)has=true;if(/^[=+\-@]/.test(value))value="'"+value;item[header[c]]=value;}if(has)rows.push(item);}
  return {rows:rows,headers:header,sheetName:sheet.getName(),sourceName:book.getName(),truncated:values.length-start-1>20000};
}

function haosDB7139SchemaDiff_(schema,headers){
  var expected=[];(schema||[]).forEach(function(column){var name=String(column.sourceName||column.name||'');if(name&&expected.indexOf(name)<0)expected.push(name);});var actual=(headers||[]).map(String);var missing=expected.filter(function(name){return actual.indexOf(name)<0;});var added=actual.filter(function(name){return expected.indexOf(name)<0;});return {compatible:!missing.length&&!added.length,missing:missing,added:added};
}
function haosDB7139ApplySchema_(rows,schema){
  var active=(schema||[]).filter(function(column){return column&&column.enabled!==false;});return (rows||[]).map(function(row){var out={};active.forEach(function(column){var source=String(column.sourceName||column.name||'');var name=String(column.name||source);var raw=row[source];if(raw==null)raw='';if(column.type==='Integer'){var integer=parseInt(String(raw).replace(/,/g,''),10);out[name]=raw===''?'':(isNaN(integer)?'':integer);}else if(column.type==='Number'||column.type==='Decimal'){var number=Number(String(raw).replace(/,/g,''));out[name]=raw===''?'':(isNaN(number)?'':number);}else if(column.type==='Boolean')out[name]=/^(true|yes|1)$/i.test(String(raw));else out[name]=haosDB7132Text_(raw,5000);});return out;});
}
function haosDB7139Chunks_(rows){var chunks=[];var current=[];(rows||[]).forEach(function(row){var candidate=current.concat([row]);if(current.length&&(candidate.length>250||JSON.stringify(candidate).length>44000)){chunks.push(current);current=[row];}else current=candidate;if(JSON.stringify(current).length>44000)throw new Error('มีข้อมูลหนึ่งแถวใหญ่เกินขีดจำกัดของ Google Sheets');});if(current.length)chunks.push(current);return chunks;}
function haosDB7139CleanupStage_(datasetId,versionId){
  if(datasetId){haosDB7132DeleteChunks_(datasetId);var ds=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Datasets_(),datasetId);if(ds)haosDB7132Datasets_().deleteRow(ds.row);}
  if(versionId){var version=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Versions_(),versionId);if(version)haosDB7132Versions_().deleteRow(version.row);}
}
function haosDB7139Log_(dashboardId,mode,status,previousDatasetId,newDatasetId,rowCount,durationMs,schemaChanged,message,actor){haosDB7139LogSheet_().appendRow([haosDB7132Id_('DBS'),dashboardId,mode,status,previousDatasetId||'',newDatasetId||'',Number(rowCount||0),Number(durationMs||0),!!schemaChanged,haosDB7132Text_(message,1000),actor&&actor.phone?("'"+actor.phone):'',actor&&actor.name||'',new Date()]);}
function haosDB7139History_(dashboardId){var data=haosDB7139LogSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();var out=[];for(var i=data.length-1;i>=1&&out.length<10;i--){if(String(data[i][1])!==String(dashboardId))continue;out.push({id:String(data[i][0]||''),mode:String(data[i][2]||''),status:String(data[i][3]||''),previousDatasetId:String(data[i][4]||''),newDatasetId:String(data[i][5]||''),rowCount:Number(data[i][6]||0),durationMs:Number(data[i][7]||0),schemaChanged:haosDB7132Bool_(data[i][8]),message:String(data[i][9]||''),createdAt:haosDB7139Iso_(data[i][12])});}return out;}

function haosDB7139PerformSync_(dashboardId,actor){
  var projectSheet=haosDB7132Projects_();var projectFound=haosDB7132Find_(projectSheet,dashboardId);if(!projectFound)throw new Error('ไม่พบ Dashboard');var project=haosDB7132Project_(projectFound.values,actor);haosDB7132CanEdit_(project);var dsFound=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Datasets_(),project.datasetId);if(!dsFound)throw new Error('ไม่พบ Dataset ปัจจุบัน');var dataset=haosDB7132Dataset_(dsFound.values);if(!haosDB7139GoogleSupported_(dataset))throw new Error('Dashboard นี้ไม่ได้เชื่อมกับ Google Sheets');
  var fresh=haosDB7139ReadGoogle_(dataset);var diff=haosDB7139SchemaDiff_(dataset.schema,fresh.headers);if(!diff.compatible){var details=[];if(diff.missing.length)details.push('คอลัมน์ที่หาย: '+diff.missing.join(', '));if(diff.added.length)details.push('คอลัมน์ใหม่: '+diff.added.join(', '));throw new Error('SCHEMA_CHANGED: โครงสร้าง Google Sheet เปลี่ยน กรุณาเปิดแก้ไข Dashboard และอ่านข้อมูลใหม่ก่อนซิงก์ ('+details.join(' | ')+')');}
  var rows=haosDB7139ApplySchema_(fresh.rows,dataset.schema);var chunks=haosDB7139Chunks_(rows);var datasetId=haosDB7132Id_('DS');var versionId=haosDB7132Id_('DBV');var now=new Date();var version=Number(project.version||1)+1;
  try{
    if(chunks.length){var chunkRows=chunks.map(function(chunk,index){return [datasetId,index,JSON.stringify(chunk),chunk.length,now];});var chunkSheet=haosDB7132Data_();chunkSheet.getRange(chunkSheet.getLastRow()+1,1,chunkRows.length,chunkRows[0].length).setValues(chunkRows);}
    var schemaJson=JSON.stringify((dataset.schema||[]).slice(0,200));haosDB7132Datasets_().appendRow([datasetId,project.id,'google',haosDB7132Text_(fresh.sourceName||dataset.name,220),dataset.url,fresh.sheetName,dataset.headerRow,schemaJson,rows.length,chunks.length,"'"+project.ownerPhone,now,now]);
    haosDB7132Versions_().appendRow([versionId,project.id,version,JSON.stringify({action:'DATA_SYNC',previousDatasetId:project.datasetId,datasetId:datasetId,rowCount:rows.length,source:{type:'google',url:dataset.url,sheetName:fresh.sheetName,headerRow:dataset.headerRow}}),"'"+actor.phone,actor.name,now]);
    var projectRow=projectFound.values.slice();projectRow[11]=datasetId;projectRow[15]=now;projectRow[16]=version;projectRow[18]=actor.name;projectSheet.getRange(projectFound.row,1,1,projectRow.length).setValues([projectRow]);
    try{haosDB7132AuditWrite_(project.id,datasetId,'DATA_SYNC',actor,{previousDatasetId:project.datasetId,rowCount:rows.length,chunks:chunks.length,truncated:fresh.truncated});}catch(ignore){}
    return {datasetId:datasetId,previousDatasetId:project.datasetId,rowCount:rows.length,chunkCount:chunks.length,truncated:fresh.truncated};
  }catch(e){haosDB7139CleanupStage_(datasetId,versionId);throw e;}
}

function haosDB7139UpdateOutcome_(dashboardId,actor,result,error){var context=haosDB7139FindSetting_(dashboardId);var before=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(context.setting));var setting=context.setting;var now=new Date();var interval=haosDB7139Interval_(setting.intervalMinutes);setting.lastSyncAt=now;setting.nextSyncAt=setting.enabled?new Date(now.getTime()+interval*60000):'';setting.updatedAt=now;setting.updatedBy=actor&&actor.name||'System';if(error){setting.lastStatus='failed';setting.lastMessage=String(error.message||error);setting.consecutiveFailures=Number(setting.consecutiveFailures||0)+1;}else{setting.lastStatus='success';setting.lastMessage='ซิงก์สำเร็จ '+Number(result.rowCount||0)+' แถว';setting.lastRowCount=Number(result.rowCount||0);setting.lastDatasetId=result.datasetId||'';setting.consecutiveFailures=0;}return {before:before,after:haosDB7139WriteSetting_(setting)};}
function haosDB7139Execute_(dashboardId,actor,mode){
  var started=new Date().getTime();var lock=LockService.getScriptLock();if(!lock.tryLock(mode==='scheduled'?1000:5000))throw new Error('มีการซิงก์ Dashboard อื่นอยู่ กรุณาลองใหม่อีกครั้ง');
  try{
    var result;
    try{result=haosDB7139PerformSync_(dashboardId,actor);}catch(e){
      var schemaChanged=/SCHEMA_CHANGED/.test(String(e&&e.message||e));var failure=null;
      try{failure=haosDB7139UpdateOutcome_(dashboardId,actor,null,e);}catch(ignoreOutcome){}
      try{haosDB7139Log_(dashboardId,mode,'failed','', '',0,new Date().getTime()-started,schemaChanged,e.toString(),actor);}catch(ignoreLog){}
      if(failure&&failure.after.notifyOnFailure&&failure.before.lastStatus!=='failed'){
        try{var projectFound=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Projects_(),dashboardId);var ownerPhone=projectFound?haosDB7132Phone_(projectFound.values[3]):actor.phone;createNotificationV732_(ownerPhone,'Dashboard Sync ไม่สำเร็จ: '+String(e.message||e),'Dashboard Sync',dashboardId,'ด่วน',{module:'dashboardBuilder',action:'openDashboardBuilder',createdBy:actor.phone});}catch(ignoreNotification){}
      }
      throw e;
    }
    try{var outcome=haosDB7139UpdateOutcome_(dashboardId,actor,result,null);haosDB7139Log_(dashboardId,mode,'success',result.previousDatasetId,result.datasetId,result.rowCount,new Date().getTime()-started,false,outcome.after.lastMessage,actor);}catch(ignoreTelemetry){}
    return result;
  }finally{lock.releaseLock();}
}

function haosDB7139AnyEnabled_(){var data=haosDB7139SettingsSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();for(var i=1;i<data.length;i++)if(haosDB7132Bool_(data[i][1]))return true;return false;}
function haosDB7139EnsureTrigger_(){var triggers=ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().filter(function(trigger){return trigger.getHandlerFunction()==='runDashboardScheduledSyncV7139';});var needed=haosDB7139AnyEnabled_();if(needed&&!triggers.length)ScriptApp.newTrigger('runDashboardScheduledSyncV7139').timeBased().everyMinutes(15).create();if(!needed)triggers.forEach(function(trigger){ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(trigger);});return needed;}
function saveDashboardSyncSettingsV7139(token,dashboardId,payload){try{var context=haosDB7139Context_(token,dashboardId);if(!haosDB7139GoogleSupported_(context.dataset))throw new Error('Scheduled Sync ใช้ได้เฉพาะ Dashboard ที่เชื่อม Google Sheets');payload=payload||{};var current=haosDB7139FindSetting_(dashboardId).setting;var previous=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(current));current.enabled=payload.enabled===true;current.intervalMinutes=haosDB7139Interval_(payload.intervalMinutes);current.notifyOnFailure=payload.notifyOnFailure!==false;current.nextSyncAt=current.enabled?new Date(new Date().getTime()+current.intervalMinutes*60000):'';current.updatedBy=context.actor.name;current.updatedAt=new Date();var saved=haosDB7139WriteSetting_(current);try{haosDB7139EnsureTrigger_();}catch(triggerError){haosDB7139WriteSetting_(previous);throw new Error('สร้าง Scheduled Trigger ไม่สำเร็จ: '+triggerError.toString());}haosDB7132AuditWrite_(dashboardId,context.project.datasetId,'SYNC_SETTINGS',context.actor,{enabled:saved.enabled,intervalMinutes:saved.intervalMinutes,notifyOnFailure:saved.notifyOnFailure});return {success:true,settings:saved,triggerInstalled:haosDB7139HasTrigger_(),version:'v70.139-dashboard-google-sync'};}catch(e){return {success:false,message:e.toString()};}}

function runDashboardScheduledSyncV7139(){var started=new Date().getTime();var now=new Date();var data=haosDB7139SettingsSheet_().getDataRange().getValues();var due=[];for(var i=1;i<data.length;i++){var setting=haosDB7139SettingFromRow_(data[i]);if(!setting.enabled)continue;var next=setting.nextSyncAt?new Date(setting.nextSyncAt):new Date(0);if(next.getTime()<=now.getTime())due.push(setting);}due.sort(function(a,b){return new Date(a.nextSyncAt||0)-new Date(b.nextSyncAt||0);});var summary={success:0,failed:0,skipped:Math.max(0,due.length-3)};for(var d=0;d<due.length&&d<3;d++){if(new Date().getTime()-started>270000){summary.skipped+=due.length-d;break;}var syncStarted=false;try{var projectFound=haosDB7132Find_(haosDB7132Projects_(),due[d].dashboardId);if(!projectFound)throw new Error('ไม่พบ Dashboard');var actor=haosDB7132ActorByPhone_(projectFound.values[3]);if(!actor)throw new Error('บัญชีเจ้าของ Dashboard ไม่พร้อมใช้งาน');syncStarted=true;haosDB7139Execute_(due[d].dashboardId,actor,'scheduled');summary.success++;}catch(e){summary.failed++;if(!syncStarted){var systemActor={phone:'SYSTEM',name:'Scheduled Sync'};try{haosDB7139UpdateOutcome_(due[d].dashboardId,systemActor,null,e);}catch(ignoreOutcome){}try{haosDB7139Log_(due[d].dashboardId,'scheduled','failed','','',0,new Date().getTime()-started,false,e.toString(),systemActor);}catch(ignoreLog){}}}}return summary;}
